Absolute Regression
===================

Chapter 501: Even If the Bait Is Devoured.
------------------------------------------
Jugwol hurriedly arrived at the rear gate of the Chongqing Branch.

Just as the report that had flown in said, carriages were entering there.

The branch warriors guarding the rear gate were merely watching the scene, and the reason they had opened the gate was because of Jeong Hyeok (鄭革), a warrior of the Demon Extermination Squad.

The other warriors of the Demon Extermination Squad had left with Jin Hagun to rescue the Lord of Yumyeong Manor, and Jeong Hyeok had remained behind alone to bring the carriages into the branch.

“What is all this?”

At Jugwol’s question, Jeong Hyeok replied.

“They are external forces our squad requested as support.”

Jeong Hyeok answered exactly as Jin Hagun had ordered.

“You reinforced our forces without my knowledge?”

Jugwol could not understand it. Not only the branch’s own forces, but even outside martial artists coming as support were already gathering in an orderly fashion. And yet they had requested even more support here?

‘Are they planning to wage a war?’

The thought came to Jugwol naturally.

“You may be the Demon Extermination Squad, but this is still a place where I am the head. To bring in support troops without even my permission?”

Jeong Hyeok answered respectfully.

“This matter is an independent operation of our Demon Extermination Squad. Surely you know that, under the current state of emergency, the decisions of the Demon Extermination Squad take precedence over the decisions of the branch?”

Because it was true, Jugwol could not refute him, but he could not help feeling disappointed in Jin Hagun.

‘Fine, feeling slighted is one thing. But who on earth did he call?’

Jugwol’s gaze turned back to the carriages.

“It looks like they brought cargo, not troops.”

They were far too large to be carriages meant for transporting people.

Jugwol walked over and flung open the rear door of one of the transport carriages.

In that instant, Jugwol was startled.

There were over a dozen martial artists inside, and it was the first time he had ever seen so many people of such massive build gathered in one place. Those men were all looking at him, the one who had opened the door.

Of course, Murim Alliance Branch Leader Jugwol was not the sort of man to be intimidated simply because his opponents were large…

But right now, he was intimidated.

Normally, he would have immediately shouted for them to get down from the carriage and state their affiliation.

But now, pressed down by the force of those gazes, he could not say a word.

Especially after his eyes met those of one among them, he felt as though his heart had shriveled.

He had met all manner of martial artists in his life as a branch leader, but this was the first time he had ever encountered such a powerful impression.

In particular, the scar that cut across the man’s face made him feel that this was someone who had walked the path of an asura.

He was none other than the Demon Army Lord, Jang Ho.

Jang Ho was the first to step down from the carriage, and the other Demon Army troops followed after him. From the other carriages as well, the Demon Army troops got down all at once.

They were not merely large men with fierce features. They had already been elites to begin with, but after Jang Ho became the Demon Army Lord, they had become an organization of the finest elites.

Moreover, since all two hundred or so Demon Army troops could not come, those selected were the most experienced and strongest in martial arts among them. The elite of the elite had arrived.

Jeong Hyeok of the Demon Extermination Squad, who had already been given prior notice, could tell that the Demon Army was a far more powerful organization than what they had known.

And right now, they were even concealing their demonic energy. If they were not, their aura and presence would be even more overwhelming.

Jugwol tried hard to hide his shock as he asked.

“Who are you people?”

At Jugwol’s question, Jang Ho answered in a deep voice.

“We are people who have come to support the Demon Extermination Squad.”

Since he had to say the words “Demon Extermination” with his own mouth, Jang Ho was not in a good mood. But in the current situation, such things were nothing more than trivial details.

“Your affiliation?”

“None.”

Because he never imagined that the Demon Extermination Squad would have brought in demonic practitioners, Jugwol could not discern the true identities of the Demon Army troops.

‘Captain Jin, do you truly believe those bastards will come here to strike us?’

It was right then.

One of the Demon Army troops standing near the building suddenly flew up onto the roof.

Despite his massive build, he landed lightly on the rooftop. Once there, he surveyed the surroundings before nimbly descending.

“He was fast.”

At the report that someone had been there and fled, Jang Ho confirmed with Jugwol.

“Did you have a subordinate lying in ambush there?”

Jugwol felt as if this was some sort of trick, but for the moment, he answered truthfully.

“No.”

Jang Ho’s expression grew grave.

If that was the case, it meant someone was watching this place.

It was then that more Demon Army troops moved.

Three Demon Army troops near the wall rushed toward it all at once.

Crack! Pak! Paaak!

The swords they thrust out pierced through the wall.

Blood stained the blade of the third Demon Army soldier. The man who had gone up to the roof earlier was now standing atop the wall, surveying the other side.

There were traces of blood on the ground, but no corpse.

He reported to Jang Ho.

“We lost him again.”

Men with this level of skill were watching the surroundings?

Jang Ho spoke urgently.

“They’re already inside.”

When Jugwol tried to say something, Jang Ho gestured for him to be quiet with a shushing motion.

Sensing danger, Jang Ho drew up his qi and examined the surroundings.

What broke the silence that flowed for a moment was the sharp sound of wind slicing through the air.

Shaaaaaang!

A black rain of hidden weapons poured down upon them.

“Iron Wall (鐵壁)!”

At Jang Ho’s shout, the Demon Army troops all formed up at once, shoulders touching and backs braced against one another, drawing their weapons.

Just as the hidden weapons rained down on them, they spun their swords in unison.

Papapapapapak!

The Demon Army troops knocked away every hidden weapon flying toward them.

Jugwol and Jeong Hyeok, together with the branch warriors, struck down the hidden weapons. But the branch warriors, whose martial arts were comparatively inferior, could not deflect all of them in time.

Papapapapapak!

The one who threw himself before them and struck down the hidden weapons in their stead was Jang Ho.

“Thank you.”

Jugwol expressed his gratitude. But there was no leisure now to exchange polite greetings.

With that hidden weapon attack as the beginning, screams rang out from every direction, and the sounds of weapons clashing erupted. The surprise attack had begun in earnest.

Jang Ho quickly asked Jeong Hyeok of the Demon Extermination Squad.

“Which way is the dungeon?”

“That way.”

As he ran in the direction Jeong Hyeok pointed, Jang Ho gave an order.

“Half of you follow me. The rest, help the Murim Alliance warriors!”

Because the deaths of Murim Alliance warriors were also what the enemy wanted, Geom Mugeuk had ordered them to protect those people as much as they could.

At Jang Ho’s command, the Demon Army split into two groups.

Half followed Jang Ho, while the rest ran with Jugwol and Jeong Hyeok toward the main branch building.

Jang Ho ran at the lead.

He had already received word through the Tongcheon Pavilion about what was happening here.

That was why this operation was more special than any mission before. In truth, Jang Ho had no interest in the Murim Alliance warriors or the orthodox martial world. There was only one person he cared about.

Seo Daeryong.

He had to protect Seo Daeryong, who stood at the very center of this conspiracy.

In the distance, he could see masked martial artists standing at the entrance of the building. The warriors who had been guarding the front of the dungeon had already all fallen.

Sensing that there were masked men who had already gone inside, Jang Ho grew anxious.

Shiiiiiiik.

Those massive bodies began to accelerate.

* * *

Moonlight seeped faintly through the window in the corridor.

Seo Daeryong sat on the old, hard bed and looked beyond the bars.

Come to think of it, this was his first time being imprisoned in a dungeon. He had always looked inside from outside that dungeon.

He thought that when their drinking gatherings resumed later, he would have to tell them what he had experienced. He would brag about it for ages. Just imagining the endlessly thrilling bluster made him look forward to it.

Back when I was locked up in the Murim Alliance dungeon, do you know who was in the cell next to mine?

Drip.

At that moment, he heard the sound of water falling from somewhere.

‘Is that sound coming from outside?’

But in that instant, the smell of blood stabbed into Seo Daeryong’s nose.

The moment he realized it had not been the sound of water drops, but blood drops falling—

Shuuuu.

The next moment, a masked man appeared before the bars as if sliding into view.

Swish! Swish! Swish!

As though he had been given an order to kill, he hurled hidden weapons into the dungeon without a single word.

Pak! Pak! Pak!

The hidden weapons that flew through the bars embedded themselves in the bed. Seo Daeryong had already flipped the bed over and blocked the front of the bars.

‘They’re just going to kill me without a word?’

Seo Daeryong was dumbfounded. If they were going to kill someone, shouldn’t they at least look him in the face and say, “You are dying for this and that reason,” before killing him?

Sword qi flew toward the bed that had blocked the hidden weapons.

Shiiik!

The bed was split in half along with the iron bars.

And from between the falling halves of the bed—

Swaaaak!

A strand of saber qi surged out and flew.

Because the masked man had let his guard down, assuming Seo Daeryong’s internal energy had been suppressed and that he would have no weapon, he could not dodge the attack.

Puaaak!

The incoming saber qi split the masked man’s body apart, and he collapsed.

Seo Daeryong swung his saber once more and completely severed the bars.

The moment he stepped out of the dungeon—

Swaaaae! Swaaaak!

Sword qi filled the corridor, surging toward him.

Crunch.

The great saber embedded itself in the ground.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwak!

Some of the sword qi collided with the great saber, while the rest brushed past it.

Seo Daeryong had used the saber, infused with internal force, as a shield to block the sword qi. It was a defensive form often used by his master, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

‘These lunatics!’

Even when they had released his suppressed internal energy and returned his weapon, he had thought, surely they would not go so far as to attack a Murim Alliance branch. But they really had attacked. This enemy was beyond common sense.

The masked men who had launched the sword qi charged toward Seo Daeryong. Their momentum as they rushed in with their swords thrust forward was tremendous.

Seo Daeryong threw himself back into the dungeon. It looked as though he had been frightened, but that was not why he retreated. He had judged that fighting inside the dungeon would be more advantageous than fighting in the narrow corridor. In a confined space, a saber was more disadvantaged than a sword.

The three masked men who rushed in were skilled in coordinated attacks.

Clang! Clang! Clang! Clang!

Seo Daeryong swung his saber and knocked away their sharp thrusting attacks.

They pressed him hard without giving him a chance to counterattack, but Seo Daeryong blocked the torrent of attacks well.

Compared to the finest elite warriors, he could not be said to have much real combat experience, but Seo Daeryong had one thing that could stand in for that.

Desperate effort.

While training, Seo Daeryong had often made this vow. He could have any regret before he died, but this one regret, at least, he would never allow himself to have.

‘Ah, I should have trained harder.’

The result of that effort was now unfolding.

His body moved before his mind. The movements he had trained and trained came out on their own.

The moment Seo Daeryong staggered under the fierce assault, the masked man on the left did not miss the opportunity and rushed in, thrusting his sword.

Seo Daeryong displayed nimble footwork and regained his balance.

Shiiiiik!

Paaak!

Seo Daeryong’s saber cut off the masked man’s neck. It had been Seo Daeryong’s feint to draw his opponent in.

Seo Daeryong did not miss this chance now that their coordinated attack had collapsed.

He pressed forward fiercely and cut down the second masked man. In the process, blood sprayed from his arm, but from the beginning, it had been a move made with the resolve to give flesh in order to take bone. Once their coordination collapsed, the fight proceeded according to Seo Daeryong’s will.

Paaak!

Seo Daeryong cut through the torso of the third masked man.

Seo Daeryong in that moment was not the timid and cautious investigator. He was the intelligent and daring martial artist, Seo Daeryong.

After dealing with the three of them, Seo Daeryong stepped out of the dungeon.

In that instant, Seo Daeryong’s expression hardened. From the far end of the corridor, masked men were rushing toward him in a swarm. There were many of them.

If this dungeon had been breached so defenselessly, then outside, fighting must have broken out everywhere as well.

The masked men charged in with a momentum that disregarded death.

Seo Daeryong did not avoid them. He poured the internal force he had drawn up into his saber.

Seo Daeryong’s saber trembled once with great force, and the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s unique martial art unfolded.

Heaven-Extinguishing Demon Saber Art, Third Form.

Saber-Extinguishing Blood Wind (滅刀血風).

The saber qi extending from the blade flew forward in a whirl.

The masked men saw it: several separate strands of saber qi spinning as they flew toward them.

Whoooooong!

In the narrow corridor, it was a whirling attack that could never be avoided.

Papapapapapak!

The bodies of the charging men were shredded and scattered.

Even Seo Daeryong, who had unleashed the martial art, was shocked by its power. The Saber-Extinguishing Blood Wind he had used in actual combat for the first time was truly tremendous. But just as its power was great, its consumption of internal energy was extreme as well.

He had already consumed a great deal of internal energy blocking the sword qi earlier, and after unleashing Saber-Extinguishing Blood Wind as well, he felt the strength drain from his entire body.

“Whew.”

Seo Daeryong let out a sigh and examined the corpse nearest him.

‘Who in the world are you people?’

The reason he had made himself bait and allowed himself to be captured was to find a clue through them. Were they assassins? Hired vagabond warriors? Or…?

It was at that very moment.

Whoosh!

A dagger flew swiftly toward Seo Daeryong.

Clang!

Seo Daeryong’s blade trembled after knocking the dagger aside. The inner force imbued in the hidden weapon was tremendous.

Seeing the relaxed gaze of the masked man walking toward him, Seo Daeryong knew.

‘This one is the leader.’

Strength entered the hand gripping his blade.

The masked man who appeared to be the leader did not say a single word either. As if he were merely doing the task assigned to him, he rushed at Seo Daeryong.

Clang!

A heavy impact traveled through Seo Daeryong’s arm. The opponent’s inner force was overwhelming his own.

Clang, clang, clang, clang!

The only reason Seo Daeryong could hold out at all was because of his martial arts. The Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form, more refined and powerful than his opponent’s techniques, was making up for his lack of inner force.

But inner force was not the only thing in which his opponent was superior. His swordsmanship was precise, and his combat experience was abundant, so he did not easily reveal openings. On top of that, the corridor was narrow, putting Seo Daeryong, who used a blade, at an even greater disadvantage.

Spurt!

Blood sprayed from Seo Daeryong’s arm. But he had no time to tend to the wound. The masked man thrust his sword without pause, aiming for Seo Daeryong’s vital points.

But Seo Daeryong was not simply taking it.

Spurt!

Blood also sprayed from the masked man’s body under Seo Daeryong’s blade.

‘You bastard! Bait may get eaten, but it doesn’t die easily!’

Seo Daeryong was afraid even as he fought, but at the same time he felt a strange thrill. So this was why martial artists wanted to die in battle—that thought came to him.

Clang, clang, clang, clang!

Seo Daeryong was being pushed back little by little.

At last, he was driven all the way to a dead-end wall. Blood sprayed from his other arm and his side as well.

‘Even if I die, I should at least see Master before I go.’

And the person who came to mind at the final moment was Geom Mugeuk.

‘Thanks to you, Young Cult Leader, I lived a splendid life!’

Swoosh!

The instant the flying sword was about to pierce Seo Daeryong’s throat—

Kwahhhhng!

One side of the wall burst apart, and the masked man was slammed into the opposite wall.

Seo Daeryong saw it.

Someone had broken through the wall and sent the masked man flying.

In that fleeting instant, the masked man had tried to twist his body and block the attack, but the person who came crashing through the wall was far stronger and faster than him.

The one who had driven a sword into the masked man’s heart was twice the size of an ordinary person.

A person Seo Daeryong could never fail to recognize.

“My lord!”

The one who had saved him was none other than Jang Ho.

“Fortunately, I was not too late.”

Their gazes met in midair.

Seo Daeryong was so shocked and moved that he did not know what to say.

Never in his wildest dreams had he imagined that the person who said he would come would be Jang Ho. Behind the broken wall, massive figures stood lined up like a folding screen. Stalwart and dependable.

Who would have thought those huge frames could look so welcome!

Seo Daeryong said to Jang Ho with trembling eyes,

“You just missed your chance to become the right arm.”

At that, Jang Ho grinned. Never had that smile and those scars looked as impressive to Seo Daeryong as they did today.

“Being the right arm would be nice, but I could not lose my precious drinking companion for it.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 502

Chapter 502: Is It Really a Rescue Operation?
---------------------------------------------
Even if he had simply called him a drinking companion, he would have been deeply moved, but Jang Ho had added the word “precious.”

Seo Daeryong was deeply moved. Unexpected emotion from a taciturn person carried several times its usual strength.

It was truly an ill-suited thing to say in this situation, with the blood of dead masked men flowing past their legs.

“When we get back, I'll buy the drinks.”

He wanted to drink all night with Jang Ho at a courtesan house. Seo Daeryong had liked Jang Ho for a long time. Watching him sit quietly, drinking in silence, he had seemed so dashing. Even the scars on his face had made him seem more dashing.

Looking back, Jang Ho had been something of a guiding marker to him, who had wished to become a martial artist rather than an investigator.

*I want to become a martial artist like that.*

The Young Cult Leader, on the other hand, had been an unsuitable person for this. Geom Mugeuk was accomplishing things he wouldn't even dare imitate. The path he walked was one ordinary folk could never hope to follow. So for Seo Daeryong, Jang Ho could serve as a far more realistic goal.

“It would have been nice if this martial artist were with us too.”

When Jang Ho mentioned Ian, Seo Daeryong realized he missed her as well.

“You said it. Who knows where she is and what she's doing.”

Still, one thing was certain. She was likely growing stronger by the day.

“When this martial artist returns, we'll have much to talk about through the night. Both us and her.”

Their flights of fancy lasted only a moment before the two returned to reality.

From afar came the continuous sounds of weapons clashing and screams.

Seo Daeryong cried out in alarm.

“Shouldn't we hurry over?”

“Yes, let us go.”

“I'm worried the subordinates might be injured.”

Unlike Seo Daeryong, whose heart burned with urgency and wished to rush over immediately, Jang Ho was calm. He did not hurry. Today, the primary objective of the Demon Troops was to protect Seo Daeryong.

And Jang Ho had faith.

That the owners of those distant screams were all enemies.

Their massive physiques had not been forged simply by eating well. They were forged from bone-grinding effort and blood-soaked training, honed through real combat experience gained on countless battlefields.

“They'll be fine. We are the Demon Troops, after all.”

Pride in the Demon Troops, and absolute faith in his men.

Seo Daeryong was witnessing firsthand what the final mindset of a Hwangcheon Pavilion investigator or an enforcer martial artist ought to be.

“Our feeble Hwangcheon Pavilion lot will all begin hell training when we return.”

Naturally, he would take the lead in that training himself.

He had learned much fighting the masked men today. Using the Bloodwind Annihilation Blade, which consumed enormous internal energy, had felt good, but in the final fight, he had nearly died from insufficient internal energy. He had experienced firsthand how failing to regulate one's internal energy could lead to death.

*I'll train as if my life depends on it! I'll sleep when I'm dead!*

As they left that place and walked on, the Demon Troops surrounded Seo Daeryong. Standing on his toes between their massive frames, Seo Daeryong spoke to Jang Ho.

“Yesterday I was surrounded by Demon Extermination Squad martial artists; today I'm surrounded by Demon Troops. Have you ever seen such a life?”

* * *

Ju Gwol held nothing back.

He fought at the forefront against the masked men. Though he held grievances against Jinha-gun and was making faulty judgments, his loyalty to the Alliance and his care for his subordinates were second to none.

Splat!

Ju Gwol's sword pierced a masked man's throat. Without even time to wipe the blood from his face, he deflected a flying hidden weapon with his blade.

He was not a man who had secured the position of Martial Alliance branch leader through bribery or flattery.

“Watch the rear! Block the left flank!”

He roared commands at his subordinates and fought at the very front against the enemy.

Ju Gwol couldn't believe it. With so many martial artists on defense, how were they being attacked?

*Those crazy Demonic Cult bastards!*

Ju Gwol was convinced the enemies were demons. If not the Demonic Cult, there was no force that would dare commit such an act.

Furthermore, the Demonic Cult attacking a Martial Alliance branch was nothing short of an act of war.

Ju Gwol shouted to a branch martial artist who was fighting a masked man up ahead.

“Careful!”

Someone was stabbing him from behind. Surprisingly, it wasn't a masked man but a martial artist wearing the same Martial Alliance uniform.

It was too late to avoid it.

Crunch! Boom!

The ribs of the attacker shattered as he tumbled across the ground. A hulking warrior had instantly charged in, sending the masked man flying with his massive frame.

How could someone so large move so fast?

The Demon Troops were displaying movements that completely shattered the stereotype that lean men are the fast ones.

Far off, another Demon Trooper could be seen nimbly soaring into the air and swinging his blade.

The masked man who had been throwing hidden weapons from the trees plummeted along with the severed branches.

The attackers who ambushed the Central Capital branch today were of two types: those wearing masks, and those disguised in Martial Alliance uniforms.

*These cowardly bastards!*

They had killed Martial Alliance martial artists by ambushing them from behind while wearing Martial Alliance uniforms.

Such disguises had been possible because martial artists sent as support from various branches and sub-branches were all gathered together, making it difficult to tell who was who.

At first, they had continued to fall prey to these cowardly attacks, but the tide turned when the Demon Troops joined the fray.

The Demon Troops began swiftly eliminating the masked men first. Without bothering to root out the disguised allies, they simply killed the masked ones.

The martial arts of the Demon Troops were on a completely different level from ordinary branch martial artists.

Jeong Hyeok of the Demon Extermination Squad, fighting alongside them, confirmed that their martial prowess was in no way inferior to the elite warriors of the Demon Extermination Squad. Indeed, many displayed movements that were a cut above.

And for good reason—those Demon Troops who had come here were among the best of their kind.

Right when Ju Gwol was momentarily distracted watching the Demon Troops fight.

Shhhk!

Then, he twisted his body at the attack flying from behind. He despaired.

*Too late!*

Thud!

The tip of the enemy's sword, wielded by a martial artist in Martial Alliance uniform, stopped right in front of his face. As the attacker collapsed, Jang Ho stood revealed behind him.

“Thank you.”

Earlier, when hidden weapons had rained down, he had saved Ju Gwol's subordinates; this time, he had saved Ju Gwol himself.

To his deeply grateful thanks, Jang Ho merely nodded once.

The Demon Troops who had come with Jang Ho all leaped into action at once. With their addition, the battlefield was resolved even more swiftly.

Once the masked men rampaging throughout the area were all eliminated, flushing out the hidden enemies was no longer difficult.

“Gather by branch! Gather by sub-branch!”

As the Martial Alliance martial artists grouped together, the disguised infiltrators began to reveal themselves.

The Martial Alliance martial artists and the Demon Troops joined forces to swiftly eliminate them. Taking them alive proved difficult. Those who failed to escape slit their own throats in suicide.

Judging by the utter lack of hesitation in that decision, they were clearly well-trained operatives.

And so, all enemies were either killed or had escaped.

The Martial Alliance martial artists began clearing the battlefield. The wounded were taken to physicians, and the dead were gathered in one place.

Ju Gwol let out a sigh. Had it not been for these hulking martial artists, the damage would have been catastrophic; instead, they had stopped it with minimal losses.

Ju Gwol expressed his gratitude to Jang Ho.

“Thanks to you, we repelled the Demonic Cult's attack.”

Then Seo Daeryong stepped up.

“The attackers today weren't demons. Can't you tell by the fact that they tried to kill me?”

Ju Gwol looked at Jang Ho. At the questioning look in his eyes, Jang Ho nodded.

“They truly tried to kill him.”

Could it be a self-orchestrated stunt? He swallowed the words that had almost left his throat. He still believed it was the work of the Demonic Cult. The attack had been too cowardly and too daring for it to be anything else.

If not the Demonic Cult, who in the world could dare attack a branch teeming with Martial Alliance martial artists?

“Then who on earth are these people?”

Seo Daeryong answered Ju Gwol's question calmly.

“Those who wish to sow discord between your side and ours, I would assume.”

Ju Gwol let out a soft sigh and gave an order to his subordinate.

“Thoroughly verify the identities of the dead.”

“Understood.”

Ju Gwol spoke to Seo Daeryong.

“Until the mastermind behind today's incident is revealed, you cannot leave.”

“I have no intention of leaving, even if you asked me to.”

He had to stay here until Geom Mugeuk returned anyway.

Jang Ho spoke to him.

“We will remain here and stand guard until the Demon Extermination Squad leader returns.”

“I would be most grateful.”

Having incurred a life debt, Ju Gwol's gaze toward Jang Ho was gentle.

Ju Gwol spoke worriedly to Jeong Hyeok of the Demon Extermination Squad.

“I worry whether the squad leader is safe.”

They had proven bold enough to attack a Martial Alliance branch; Jinha-gun was the one he worried for.

Then Seo Daeryong formed a strange smile and spoke.

Naturally, it was something Ju Gwol could not understand.

“He has someone following him who has sacrificed even his right arm for his sake, so he should be fine.”

* * *

“There it is.”

In the direction Jinha-gun was looking stood a manor.

Currently, Geom Mugeuk and Jinha-gun stood side by side in a forest far from the manor.

It was so far that one could only make out the characters on the signboard hanging above the main gate by exerting vision-enhancing arts.

Nostalgia Mountain Villa (Xiangchou Shanzhuang).

It was a secluded mountain villa situated far from the city.

“We are currently conducting a covert investigation into that manor. The results will be delivered shortly.”

At Jinha-gun's explanation, Geom Mugeuk asked.

“How did you know the Master of Yumyeong is imprisoned here?”

“As you know, we are currently scouring Zhongjing in search of the missing Master of Yumyeong and his household. Not a single corpse was found at Yumyeong Estate, so we are holding out hope that they may still be alive. The number of people there that day was no small few. So I ordered all physicians in Zhongjing to be watched. I had reasoned that if they had been injured, they might need to purchase large quantities of medicinal herbs for treatment. In the course of that, we discovered that someone had purchased large quantities of medicinal herbs for treating injuries from a physician in this area. It was the very news his investigators had been waiting for. Judging by the types and quantities of herbs, it could have been to treat the people of Yumyeong. So we scoured the surrounding areas and discovered this manor. Furthermore, we learned that a martial artist who belonged to Yumyeong is present there.”

There was no reason to hesitate further. Whatever the circumstances, with a martial artist from Yumyeong having shown himself, they had no choice but to investigate.

“We can only hope the Master of Yumyeong and his household are still alive. Now, launch the rescue operation at once.”

“Is this operation truly a rescue, though?”

Geom Mugeuk's reason for doubting this situation was clear.

“Is it merely a coincidence that Yumyeong emerged right when Chief Seo was taken into your custody?”

Naturally, Jinha-gun harbored suspicions about this timing, but it was the only lead connected to the Master of Yumyeong. Even if it was a trap, they had to spring it.

Just then, a subordinate from the Demon Extermination Squad arrived and reported some news.

“The Central Capital branch has been ambushed.”

Jinha-gun was startled. He had expected as much, but he hadn't thought they would actually attack the branch.

“What happened?”

“Dozens on our side were killed or wounded, they say.”

Jinha-gun's expression hardened. He was enraged. An incident had erupted while he and the Demon Extermination Squad were away.

If this was a diversion? If there was nothing in that manor?

“What of the man held in the dungeon?”

“He is reportedly safe.”

Geom Mugeuk, listening in, felt relieved. Though he had sent Jang Ho and the Demon Troops, one never knew what variables might arise in murim affairs.

“The enemies?”

“They say all of them were killed.”

Jinha-gun looked at Geom Mugeuk. From the reports that Seo Daeryong had survived the ambush and that all enemies had been eliminated, he surmised the Demon Troops had distinguished themselves greatly.

“Your judgment was correct. My thanks.”

Without the support of the Demon Troops, the damage would have been incomparable. As always, the Young Cult Leader's insight proved trustworthy.

“Then this place must be a lure for us.”

He believed they had used the Yumyeong martial artist they kept alive to lure him here.

But Geom Mugeuk's thoughts differed.

“That may not be the case.”

Jinha-gun looked at Geom Mugeuk with a questioning expression.

“But what if this is the real scheme?”

Geom Mugeuk shared his thoughts.

“An attack on the branch won't trigger a war. The Main Cult and the Martial Alliance are not so fragile. But what about this? The one investigating the extermination of Yumyeong and Daejeong Gate...”

Geom Mugeuk paused briefly before adding.

“...the death of the Demon Extermination Squad leader.”

It was a matter of an entirely different magnitude from a simple branch attack. He was the grandson of the Martial Alliance Chief and the foremost candidate to succeed him.

“If the events up until now were all preparation for your death?”

The disciple of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon exterminates Yumyeong and Daejeong Gate, and the Demon Extermination Squad leader investigating the incident ultimately meets his death at the hands of the Demonic Cult. If that is the narrative they have crafted?

Jinha-gun looked at Geom Mugeuk in shock. Moreover, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would be furious as well. After all, it would mean the disciple of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had been dragged to a Martial Alliance branch to face investigation and ended up dead.

Tensions would spike between both sides, and if those people pulled further schemes?

“If my suspicion is correct...”

Geom Mugeuk's deep gaze turned toward the silent manor.

“Someone capable of killing both you and the entire Demon Extermination Squad is waiting there.”

Chapter 503: Even So, Nothing Changes.
--------------------------------------
What if Geom Mugeuk’s prediction was right?

It meant he would die today. Together with the elite Demon Extermination Squad of the Murim Alliance.

Would he have believed it if the one who said those words had not been Geom Mugeuk? That there were people who had laid a trap to kill him and the Demon Extermination Squad all in one place.

‘Absolutely not.’

But now, he believed it.

In all the times he had met Geom Mugeuk, had the man ever been wrong? No, he had not. His words had always become reality.

His prediction that the Chongqing Branch might be attacked had also proven accurate. If the Demon Lord had not come to help, the earlier report would have been far more horrific.

That was why Jin Hagun’s expression hardened on its own.

‘They’re even more vicious than the ones we dealt with last time.’

Jin Hagun looked at Geom Mugeuk.

“That’s fortunate. They couldn’t have calculated for a variable like you.”

But Geom Mugeuk did not let down his guard.

“That remains to be seen.”

Hyeolcheon Doma had become involved, and Seo Daeryong had been dragged in as well. How could he be certain he was an exception?

“What will you do now?”

At that question, Geom Mugeuk left the decision to Jin Hagun.

“You decide. The matter of Yumyeong Manor is yours.”

It was because he wanted Jin Hagun to take the lead in handling this matter.

Jin Hagun’s gaze turned toward the estate.

If danger lurked there, one option was to withdraw for now and return after mobilizing all the martial artists of the branch.

But then the sacrifices among ordinary martial artists could grow immense. These were people waiting to kill the Demon Extermination Squad; the losses among ordinary martial artists would truly be great.

Another option was to request masters from the Alliance.

But that would take time. Could they really afford to delay this matter like that?

Neither option appealed to him.

And so, the conclusion he reached was this.

“I want to break through head-on, now.”

He wanted to rescue the lord of Yumyeong Manor and his household in time.

If he had not known, that would have been one thing. But now that he knew they were here, he had to save them.

Because that was the reason the Murim Alliance’s Demon Extermination Squad existed.

Guessing his feelings, Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“I’ll watch your back, so take the lead.”

At words more reassuring than anything else in the world, Jin Hagun nodded.

Jin Hagun gathered all the martial artists of the Demon Extermination Squad.

“There is a very high possibility that people targeting us are inside that mountain villa. So keep your wits about you!”

Had they not known, they might have fallen for it. But if they prepared in advance, they would never be taken down easily.

Even if there was an absolute master capable of overwhelming them.

Jin Hagun’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

‘We have an absolute master too.’

And so Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun took the lead, with the martial artists of the Demon Extermination Squad following behind them.

Jin Hagun found it strange and new that, in a situation like this, he was walking alongside the young cult leader of the Demon Cult toward a place that might be a trap.

The turmoil he had felt from not being able to fully trust Geom Mugeuk had now completely vanished. He had decided that even nine-tenths trust was more than enough.

Perhaps that was why he could say something like this so easily.

“I have a favor to ask.”

It was a favor Geom Mugeuk truly had not expected.

“If I happen to die, please take good care of my younger sister.”

Jin Hagun had never imagined he would make such a request of Geom Mugeuk. But while he had the chance, he had wanted to say it. Whether as a friend’s younger sister, as one martial artist to another, or as a man and a woman—Geom Mugeuk was the only person he could trust and entrust his sister to.

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk answered.

“I can’t grant that favor.”

“Why not?”

“If you die here and I alone survive, then it will be as if I killed you. Even if they don’t think I killed you, your sister and the Alliance Leader will never forgive me for failing to keep you alive. So you absolutely must not die.”

Jin Hagun nodded. Now that he thought about it, that was true. His death would become the signal flare announcing the beginning of the Orthodox-Demonic War.

And so the two arrived before the estate.

“Are we really going in through the front gate like this?”

“On the contrary, they won’t have expected us to come knocking at the gate like this, will they? We’re striking where they’re not prepared.”

After saying that, Geom Mugeuk pounded on the gate.

The person who opened it immediately was a middle-aged martial artist. He looked like a martial artist belonging to the villa, and behind him stood three or four others dressed the same way.

The middle-aged martial artist was startled when he saw the Demon Extermination Squad lined up behind Jin Hagun. It felt as if dozens of sharpened treasured swords were being aimed at him.

One of the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists stepped forward.

“We have come to see the manor lord. Is he here?”

The middle-aged martial artist asked in a trembling voice,

“Where have you come from?”

“Tell him the captain of the Murim Alliance’s Demon Extermination Squad has arrived.”

At those words, the middle-aged martial artist was shocked. His gaze turned to Jin Hagun.

“Are you truly the captain of the Demon Extermination Squad?”

Jin Hagun asked him in return,

“If one impersonates the captain of the Demon Extermination Squad, one must spend a very long time locked in a prison. Do I look like such a foolish man to you?”

Even if Jin Hagun did not reveal his aura, the presence of the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists lined up behind him was enough to prove his identity.

“No, sir.”

When the middle-aged martial artist ordered one of the men standing behind him to inform them that the captain of the Demon Extermination Squad had arrived, the man ran inside.

From the look of it, they certainly had not expected this visit at all.

At their reaction, Jin Hagun sent a sound transmission.

—It doesn’t look like a trap, does it?

They were clearly flustered and startled by the sudden visit of the Demon Extermination Squad captain.

—The better the trap, the more it would look that way, no?

Geom Mugeuk was still suspicious of this place.

—That man’s martial arts are no ordinary thing. He’s probably someone who holds a fairly high position in this mountain villa. And yet he came to greet us himself. Isn’t that strange?

Jin Hagun understood what Geom Mugeuk’s sound transmission meant.

—You mean they were prepared for an intrusion from outside?

—Exactly.

A moment later, the martial artist who had run inside came back.

“He says to escort you in at once.”

The middle-aged martial artist spoke respectfully to Jin Hagun.

“This way, please.”

Jin Hagun followed him, and the martial artists of the Demon Extermination Squad spread out in all directions, keeping watch over their surroundings as they followed.

On the way into the inner court, they saw martial artists stationed here and there.

Jin Hagun tested the middle-aged martial artist with a question.

“The defenses are strict for an ordinary estate.”

The middle-aged martial artist answered calmly,

“Unsettling incidents have been occurring in Chongqing lately, so we strengthened our guard.”

Thus Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun arrived at the main hall.

There, the lord of Hyangsu Mountain Villa, Liu Fan, welcomed Jin Hagun.

“Welcome. What brings such an esteemed guest to this place?”

Standing a few steps behind Jin Hagun, among the Demon Extermination Squad, Geom Mugeuk observed the villa lord’s reaction. Liu Fan was an older man who had clearly experienced all sorts of hardships, but his reaction showed that he had not expected this visit at all.

“I came because I have something to say to you, Villa Lord.”

“Please speak.”

Liu Fan was extremely courteous. His opponent was a powerful candidate to become the next Alliance Leader, as well as the head of the Murim Alliance’s most elite Demon Extermination Squad.

“Do you know of the incident that occurred at Yumyeong Manor?”

“Of course.”

“A martial artist who was at that Yumyeong Manor was seen here at this mountain villa.”

“At our villa? That cannot be. It must have been someone who looked similar.”

Judging only from his startled reaction and expression, it truly seemed as though he knew nothing.

Whether he was flatly denying it or truly did not know, this was not a situation to resolve with words now.

“May we look around the estate?”

He did not seem inclined to allow it, but he could not bring himself to refuse.

“Of course. I will guide you myself.”

Liu Fan took the lead and guided the two of them.

He showed them various parts of the villa. Aside from the strict security, they could find nothing particularly strange. There were no martial artists lying hidden for an ambush, nor any traces of traps.

Geom Mugeuk sent a sound transmission to Jin Hagun.

—Ask to see the infirmary.

Jin Hagun understood Geom Mugeuk’s intention.

“Does the villa also have an infirmary?”

In that instant, Geom Mugeuk saw a faint hint of panic pass across Liu Fan’s face.

“We have one, though it is small.”

“I would like to see that place as well.”

And so he guided them to the infirmary.

There were several beds, but no one was there.

“Do you have a physician belonging to the villa?”

“Yes, one.”

“Where is he now?”

“He seems to have gone out.”

At that moment, a martial artist of the Demon Extermination Squad discovered something.

“Please look at this.”

Behind the infirmary building, herbs were piled up—the leftover scraps after the necessary parts of the medicinal herbs had been removed. Perhaps they had not yet been disposed of, for there was a considerable amount.

Jin Hagun said to Liu Fan,

“I know that recently, a large quantity of medicinal herbs was purchased from nearby clinics. Most were herbs used to treat external and internal injuries. Whom were you treating?”

This time, too, Liu Fan flatly denied it.

“My subordinates were injured during training.”

“Where are those injured subordinates?”

“I sent them to their hometowns so they could rest comfortably for a while.”

As Jin Hagun’s expression turned cold and he revealed a sharp aura, Liu Fan flinched.

The middle-aged martial artist who had guided them earlier stepped forward to protect his leader, but the martial artists of the Demon Extermination Squad also revealed their aura all at once.

Overwhelmed by that force, the middle-aged martial artist stepped back.

“Villa Lord Liu.”

“Speak.”

“I am someone trying to save the lord of Yumyeong Manor and his household. Why are you trying to hide things from me?”

Liu Fan denied it to the end.

“I do not know what you mean.”

“If I give the order, my subordinates will search this entire mountain villa from top to bottom. Given the nature of the matter, if there is something you are hiding, Villa Lord, you will have to pay the price for it.”

Sensing Liu Fan waver, Jin Hagun persuaded him gently.

“We are the Demon Extermination Squad. I do not know what you are worried about, but trust us.”

In the end, Liu Fan let out a sigh and said,

“Please follow me.”

Leaving his own subordinates there, Liu Fan took only Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Squad toward the rear garden.

There was a secret passage leading underground.

“This villa has had a secret refuge passed down from long ago.”

After descending the stairs, an underground passage appeared, and at the end of that passage was a large room.

When they opened the door and entered, an unfamiliar medicinal scent wafted out.

Dozens of beds were placed there, and people lay on every one of them.

Liu Fan sighed and said,

“They are the martial artists who survived Yumyeong Manor. The lord of Yumyeong Manor came to our mountain villa with the survivors.”

Jin Hagun was glad to learn that they were alive.

“Where is the lord of Yumyeong Manor?”

At that, Liu Fan let out a sorrowful sigh and delivered the tragic news.

“The lord of Yumyeong Manor died the day after he hid here. His wounds were too deep; there was nothing we could do.”

Jin Hagun sighed with him.

“Why did you not inform us sooner?”

“Before he died, the lord of Yumyeong Manor earnestly begged me. He told me not to tell anyone until his household had treated their wounds.”

“Did he also say why?”

Liu Fan nodded.

“He said that if it became known they were alive, they would come to kill them again. And soon after, news came that Daejeong Sect had been annihilated. That was when I realized this matter was not limited to Yumyeong Manor alone.”

Jin Hagun could roughly understand what had happened. That must have been why Liu Fan had been afraid—afraid that they, too, would be dragged into this matter and face annihilation. He had no intention of blaming him. After all, he had been treating these people.

“Did he say who did it?”

After hesitating for a moment, Liu Fan answered quietly,

“He said it was a young man who used a great saber.”

Judging only by that, the culprit was undoubtedly Seo Daeryong.

“I apologize for not telling you from the beginning.”

“No. I understand how you felt, Villa Lord Liu. For now, we will take these people with us.”

“Please do. In return, please keep the fact that they were hiding here a secret.”

“I will.”

If anything, he looked relieved.

Jin Hagun said to the martial artists of the Demon Extermination Squad,

“Carefully move the martial artists of Yumyeong Manor to the branch.”

The martial artists of the Demon Extermination Squad all possessed powerful martial arts, so they would be able to move the patients safely without causing complications. For this task, they were more suited than ordinary martial artists.

It was just as the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists were about to step forward. Geom Mugeuk, who had only been listening, suddenly spoke.

“How coincidental. The number of injured people is similar to the number of martial artists in the Demon Extermination Squad.”

In that instant, everyone’s gazes turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

“Now I understand.”

“What do you understand?”

Liu Fan looked at Geom Mugeuk in surprise. He had thought the man was a subordinate, but the two were speaking like friends.

A shocking statement flowed from Geom Mugeuk.

“I understand now how they intended to kill you and the Demon Extermination Squad.”

At the sudden words, Jin Hagun could not say a thing. The warriors of the Demon-Extermination Squad also stared at Geom Mugeuk with startled expressions.

“You and the Demon-Extermination Squad would all have been dead before you ever left this place.”

“!”

Jin Hagun looked silently at Geom Mugeuk. Geom Mugeuk loved all manner of mischief, but he had never once made sport of human lives. Nor was he the sort of man who would.

“What do you mean?”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the patients on the beds.

“You would have been ambushed while carrying them out.”

That meant all of them were enemies!

Everyone was shocked. Not only Jin Hagun, but none of the Demon-Extermination Squad warriors here had thought those patients could be enemies.

“Of course no one would suspect them. These people aren’t strangers you happened to meet on the road.”

The incident at Yumyeong Manor and Daejeongmun had occurred, they had searched for them, found evidence, and thus finally located these people. This was, at last, the moment they were going to rescue them.

“They are not warriors of Yumyeong Manor. The real ones likely all died that day. They were treated as missing precisely for this moment.”

A chill ran through Jin Hagun’s chest. If that was true, then this was a meticulously planned conspiracy to kill him and the Demon-Extermination Squad.

Yu Beom spoke with an expression of disbelief.

“What absurd nonsense is that?”

Ignoring his reaction, Geom Mugeuk said to Jin Hagun,

“The medicinal fragrance we smelled when we entered this place just now was the scent of burning Western Spirit Grass (西靈草).”

Geom Mugeuk walked toward the beds.

“Over here, I smell Untimely Death Grass (非命草). Each scent on its own does no harm to a person, but when the two combine, they momentarily produce an effect like taking qi-dispersing poison. It lasts only a very brief instant, but it is enough time to subdue an opponent.”

When it came to poisonous and medicinal herbs, Geom Mugeuk possessed knowledge and learning as profound as anyone’s.

Geom Mugeuk said to Jin Hagun,

“Everyone here was injured, yet the person caught in the Martial Alliance’s surveillance net was perfectly fine, wasn’t he? Isn’t that strange? And they left traces of medicinal herbs behind the infirmary as if they wanted us to see them?”

Jin Hagun nodded.

“It was to lure us here.”

The warriors of the Demon-Extermination Squad drew their swords all at once.

Then the people lying on the beds slowly raised themselves up. One could tell just from their eyes. They, too, were truly well-trained elite warriors.

Yu Beom, who had seemed flustered and frightened from beginning to end, changed in both gaze and voice, and even his aura became that of a different person.

“Even so, nothing changes.”

He looked behind him.

Drrrk.

Then a hidden door at the back opened, and a man walked out.

It was none other than the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber strapped to his back.

Chapter 504: Eight Defeats a Hundred
------------------------------------
After returning to the past, many things had happened.

He had been surprised many times while going through them, but had he ever been this surprised?

‘No, Elder, why are you coming out from there?’

Normally, he would have started with some banter like that. But Geom Mugeuk said nothing. No such words came out. First, he had to grasp what sort of situation this was.

‘Stay calm.’

The first thing that met was Geom Mugeuk’s gaze and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s.

Those eyes were colder than usual, but Geom Mugeuk knew.

‘It really is the Elder.’

He was not a fake disguised with a face mask, nor was he under sorcery. Everything else could be faked, but not that stiff, cantankerous gaze. Even if not for the look in his eyes, Geom Mugeuk’s instincts told him. It was definitely the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

Jin Hagun looked at Geom Mugeuk. He knew enough about the relationship between Geom Mugeuk and the Demon Venerables.

He also knew that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon should not be appearing here.

‘Was the master you said would kill us all the Blood Heaven Blade Demon?’

He did not send that thought through sound transmission. He could feel that Geom Mugeuk was even more shocked than he was. And with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s appearance, one suspicion naturally arose.

‘Don’t tell me it was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon who annihilated the Daejeong Sect?’

Moreover, what happened next only strengthened that suspicion. Yu Beom pointed at him and said this, as if telling them that he was the one they had to kill.

“That man is Jin Hagun, the commander of the Demon Extermination Unit.”

Even without being told, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and Jin Hagun had met before, so they knew each other.

Jin Hagun calmly greeted the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“It has been a long time.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon merely stared at him with an expressionless gaze and did not offer Jin Hagun a single word of greeting.

At that reaction, the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit grew tense. They were greater in number, but their opponent was a Demon Venerable.

On top of that, Yu Beom’s aura after revealing his true colors was anything but ordinary, and the warriors who had been disguised as patients on the beds were not to be underestimated either.

And above all, what they feared was Geom Mugeuk standing on the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s side. They knew his relationship with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was as deep as his relationship with Jin Hagun. No, an arm would inevitably bend inward.

If Geom Mugeuk did not stand on their side, Jin Hagun would die here today.

The warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit looked at one another and exchanged glances. The resolve to save Jin Hagun, even if all of them died, passed among them.

This time, Yu Beom looked at Geom Mugeuk. He knew that Geom Mugeuk was not a warrior of the Demon Extermination Unit, but the Young Cult Leader.

“I did not expect the Young Cult Leader to come here with them. He saw through our plan.”

If not for the Young Cult Leader, the Demon Extermination Unit and Jin Hagun would already be lying as cold corpses.

“Young Cult Leader.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon walked toward Geom Mugeuk as he spoke.

“I believe I told you once. This old man has lived his whole life with misfortune at his side, so it is best not to see me often.”

Geom Mugeuk silently watched him approach.

Jin Hagun watched the two of them, tense under an overwhelming pressure.

Sssrk.

The Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber was aimed at Geom Mugeuk’s neck. A sharp edge of killing intent flowed along the blade, as if his head would be lopped off with the slightest twitch of the hand.

His face was reflected in the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber.

Geom Mugeuk gazed for a moment at his own face reflected on the saber’s edge.

Suddenly, that day came to mind.

The day he first met the Blood Heaven Blade Demon after returning to the past. That day, too, the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber had been held to his neck just like this.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze slowly moved along the saber’s edge. Past the hand holding the blade, until it reached the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s face.

Looking at that face, Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly.

“I am truly glad you are safe.”

The corners of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s eyes twitched. His gaze remained cold.

Between the two of them flowed a suffocating tension, as if the saber might swing and sever his neck at any moment.

But Geom Mugeuk was no different from usual in the way he treated him.

“I was very worried!”

Though he had not shown it outwardly, inwardly he had been worried about the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. What if, by some chance, someone had gotten the better of him? What if he had been captured somewhere?

“Still, the person who knows you best really is Father. He said there was no need to worry about someone who wasn’t the sort to be defeated anywhere.”

Geom Mugeuk deliberately mentioned his father to see how the Blood Heaven Blade Demon would react. He showed no response at all.

“And Elder, how could you startle someone like this? When I saw you come through that door, my liver nearly dropped out!”

Even in a moment like this, Geom Mugeuk was talking as much as usual.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, on the other hand, responded with utter coldness.

“Does it not occur to you that I came here to kill you?”

One person spoke as though he were someone else, while the other remained unchanged. And so the conversation continued.

“Of course not.”

“Why?”

“Because you are not that kind of person, Elder.”

“What if I have been deceiving you all this time? What if I am that sort of person?”

“That cannot be.”

“How can you be so certain?”

“Because of this.”

Geom Mugeuk slowly reached into his robe and took something out. It was the poetry and painting collection he had received from Seo Daeryong.

“There are no villains among people who like books.”

“I may have pretended to like books in order to deceive you.”

“For that to be true, you liked books far too much.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon openly snorted.

“If you trust people so carelessly as you do, you will eventually be betrayed and die. That is the world, and that is the martial world.”

Cold demonic energy surged from the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s body. Goosebumps rose all over not only Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Unit, but also Yu Beom and his subordinates.

Only Geom Mugeuk looked calmly at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon without the slightest agitation.

“It is all right, even if you betray me, Elder.”

“Even if the result is death?”

“You have given me enough that I would not mind dying for it. With that much affection, I can give you this life.”

Shiiiik.

The Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber moved to cut his neck, and Geom Mugeuk twisted his body to avoid it by a hair. It had not been a true attempt to kill him, merely one meant to cut him enough for blood to spray from his neck.

“Why did you dodge?”

“Did I dodge? The saber’s edge was cold, so I moved away a little.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon merely gave him a look of contempt.

Yu Beom asked the Blood Heaven Blade Demon,

“We must finish what we started. Please hold the Young Cult Leader back for a moment.”

He naturally believed that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon would obey him.

As soon as those words ended, both sides moved at once. The Demon Extermination Unit all stepped forward to block the front and sides of Jin Hagun. The situation they had feared had finally occurred.

The warriors on the beds also stepped forward and drew their swords.

Yu Beom wore an expression that said he was confident as long as the Young Cult Leader did not intervene. And that confidence did not come from his own skill.

When Yu Beom touched a device protruding from the bed right beside him—

Clank.

Several small doors opened behind Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Unit. From the holes inside them—

Shiiiik.

The smoke that flowed out from there was the scent of Scream Grass. It had been arranged so that the scent of Scream Grass could also come out from behind, in case they did not approach the beds.

As the two scents merged, the flow of internal energy within Jin Hagun and the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit was momentarily severed.

Yu Beom’s order fell.

“Kill them all!”

Just as they were about to launch themselves all at once toward the Demon Extermination Unit!

Geom Mugeuk drew his sword and aimed it into the air between the two sides. It was a warning not to go toward the Demon Extermination Unit.

“Go through!”

At Yu Beom’s command, the warriors at the front flew straight toward Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Unit. They were certain the Blood Heaven Blade Demon would stop the Young Cult Leader.

Shiiiiik!

Slice!

A streak of sword energy shot from Geom Mugeuk’s sword.

The warriors in the lead were cut apart by the sword energy and came crashing down. Everyone following behind them stopped in place.

The smell of blood spread sharply through the hall, and blood began to stream across the floor.

Yu Beom looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon in shock.

“Why did you not stop the Young Cult Leader?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon withdrew the saber aimed at Geom Mugeuk’s neck and approached Yu Beom.

Smack!

Yu Beom, struck across the cheek, was sent flying into a corner. Fortunately, internal energy had not been infused into the blow, so he did not lose his life.

Yu Beom rose from where he had fallen and glared at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. Though his martial prowess was far below the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s, he openly displayed his anger.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at him and said coldly,

“He is the Young Cult Leader of our Cult. There is only one person who can obstruct his will.”

No matter how special the circumstances, the Young Cult Leader was still the Young Cult Leader.

It was then.

From behind, Geom Mugeuk spoke to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Even if someone is controlling you with gu poison, it does not matter. I will cure you. If you have a hidden child somewhere and that child is being held hostage right now, I will be sure to rescue them. Even if you are doing this simply because life suddenly became boring, that is fine too. From now on, I will visit you every day and keep you entertained.”

It was then. Since this meeting began, the thing Geom Mugeuk had so desperately wanted finally happened.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s sound transmission flew to him. And in his usual tone, at that.

—My ears hurt, you brat!

That one sentence was enough. Even without a long explanation, he could understand. Right now, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had no choice but to stand on that side. He was still the same Blood Heaven Blade Demon as ever.

—I knew it all along!

—How?

—Because you would trust Father and me. I don’t know what unavoidable circumstance sent you here, Elder, but you would trust that Father and I would resolve it. Trusting someone is also a form of intelligence, and you have better judgment and are smarter than anyone. You are the only Demon Venerable in the world who reads books, after all.

Because his back was turned, Geom Mugeuk did not see it. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s gaze softened at praise he had not heard in a long time.

—Still, you must have been surprised when you first saw me, no?

—You would say you were trying to show them that you were drawing a line between us by pretending to be cold, but I know. You did it to tease me, didn’t you? You wanted to enjoy watching me flustered and shocked. I have rubbed off on you. When an opportunity comes, you have to tease me somehow! It will not work on me. Go tease your disciple!

A smile formed on the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s lips. It was his original self.

But that fleeting smile lasted only an instant. When he turned his head to Yu Beom, his expression was colder than ever.

“We are going back!”

Yu Beom flinched in surprise.

“We must complete the mission.”

“Then stay here and die.”

Leaving Yu Beom and the warriors behind, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon walked toward the door he had come out of.

Geom Mugeuk sent one last sound transmission.

—I will move in my own way. I will see you again soon, Elder!

When the Blood Heaven Blade Demon left like that, Yu Beom looked at Jin Hagun, conflicted.

There were still traps left in this space. This place had been made as Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Unit’s grave, after all.

But there was the variable called Geom Mugeuk. With him having come along with them, every plan had gone awry. Even the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s departure was ultimately because of this Young Cult Leader.

Yu Beom’s deliberation did not last long. If they were going to leave, they had to get out together before the Blood Heaven Blade Demon got too far away.

“We are leaving too!”

Yu Beom and the warriors, swords drawn, backed toward the door through which the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had left.

The temporary effect of the internal-energy-dispersing poison had already disappeared, so the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit waited for Jin Hagun’s order to attack. Since the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had left first, if they attacked now, they might be able to annihilate them.

Jin Hagun instinctively felt it. They should not kill those people now.

If it were a situation where they had to die, Geom Mugeuk would have killed them first. The reason Geom Mugeuk was letting them go was probably because of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

Even in this situation, Geom Mugeuk was leaving the decision to him. Those people had tried to kill you and the Demon Extermination Unit, so I will follow your decision. If a fight broke out, Geom Mugeuk would certainly help them.

Because he sensed that consideration, Jin Hagun made a decision for Geom Mugeuk’s sake.

“Let them go.”

At Jin Hagun’s command, the Demon Extermination Unit withdrew their killing intent. Meanwhile, Yu Beom and the warriors all escaped.

“Thank you.”

Geom Mugeuk expressed his gratitude to Jin Hagun.

“What is there to thank me for? If not for you, we would all already be dead.”

After all, earlier they had almost carried those patients out without knowing they were enemies.

Even after being careful and careful again, one still encountered crises like this. Dealing with people who possessed only vicious killing intent and malice instead of a warrior’s honor was no easy thing.

The Demon Extermination Unit standing behind them all cupped their fists at once and paid their respects to Geom Mugeuk. It was an expression of gratitude for the grace of saving their lives.

They had not fought a single person today, but Geom Mugeuk saw the true face of the Demon Extermination Unit.

Right now, not one of them showed dissatisfaction on their face. They could certainly have been unhappy that those who had tried to kill them had simply been let go.

And even if the other party was the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult, they made their debts and grudges clear. This was why elites were elites.

Geom Mugeuk said to Jin Hagun,

“Even if I had not been here, Lord Blade Demon would never have let you die.”

Jin Hagun might find it hard to believe, but Geom Mugeuk had absolute faith.

“If I had not been here, Lord Blade Demon would have saved you by any means necessary. In fact, that may be the reason Lord Blade Demon was here today.”

“Why would the Blade Demon save us?”

Could that be? If he had to make a reason, was it because Jin Hagun would become the next Alliance Leader?

But that was not the reason Geom Mugeuk had in mind.

“Because he knows you are my friend.”

Jin Hagun knew what kind of moment this was.

It was the moment he saw the end of faith.

Geom Mugeuk was showing him now what it meant to trust a person.

‘Trust only up to eight, without burden? You are a liar.’

Geom Mugeuk’s faith in the Blood Heaven Blade Demon seemed insufficient even if called ten, no, a hundred. That eight of his was an eight that could beat a hundred.

“Now, let us leave as well.”

Geom Mugeuk took the lead and stepped outside.

He believed there was nothing in this world, aside from his father, that could bind the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

And yet something had entangled him? Then there was surely a reason he had to help the enemy, even though he did not wish to.

With the life he had lived before his regression, he could not know the reason. Among the Demon Venerables, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had died first.

‘In this life, I will change that fate.’

Chapter 505: This Enemy Is Different.
-------------------------------------
Demon Army soldiers were guarding one of the buildings within the Chongqing branch.

They had encircled the building at regular intervals, and their net of vigilance extended not only over its roof, but also across the rooftops of the neighboring buildings.

Even if ordinary martial artists had been mobilized, one might have called it an ironclad guard—and yet every last one of them was an elite Demon Army soldier. It was, quite literally, a cordon through which not even a single ant could slip.

Just then, a martial artist from the Chongqing branch came up to them and bowed his head.

“Thanks to you, I survived. Thank you.”

He was one of the martial artists who had been saved from the brink of death by the Demon Army.

Many martial artists of the Murim Alliance had been wounded or killed in this surprise attack, but if not for the Demon Army, it was impossible to imagine how high that number might have risen.

The Demon Army soldiers merely nodded once in acknowledgment and said nothing at all.

Seo Daeryong stood by the second-floor window, watching the scene. There was someone in this room, too, to whom he ought to offer his thanks. If not for Jang Ho, who sat at the desk reading a report, he himself would have been dead.

‘I really nearly died.’

But there was an emotion that came before that fear.

The thrill he had felt when he fought like a madman. Seo Daeryong could not forget that moment.

The training he did once he returned would surely be different now. He had gone all the way to the threshold of the afterlife and confirmed what he should train for, and when and how that training would shine.

After gazing out the window for a while, Seo Daeryong turned toward Jang Ho.

“What on earth do you think is happening over there?”

If anyone else had asked, Jang Ho would not have answered at all. Even if he had, he would likely have said that he was only moving according to orders.

But because it was none other than Seo Daeryong, he offered words of comfort for his sake.

“The Demon Venerable will be safe.”

It was a blunt, curt remark, but it was exactly what Seo Daeryong wanted to hear most right now.

“I believe so as well.”

Seo Daeryong forced a bright smile, and Jang Ho smiled too.

“By the way, how is the painting going?”

Jang Ho was a man who would not so much as blink if hundreds of enemies charged at him, but now his eyes widened as he asked,

“How did you know?”

“A certain tight-lipped person bragged so much about receiving a painting from Lord Jang. I imagine not only I, but everyone who would know already knows.”

Unable to hold back, Jang Ho laughed. Some time ago, he had gifted Geom Mugeuk a painting of him. He had wanted his first gifted painting to go to him no matter what.

Since then, he had painted many things. He had painted this and that. He painted his subordinates, then buildings, and the enormous stone statues within the cult. He painted fruit, and he painted swords and sabers. In truth, he had even painted Seo Daeryong and Ian. He simply had not been able to present them as gifts.

At first, he had thought that the act of painting was a good hobby because it allowed him to have time entirely to himself.

He had thought that, at least while he was painting, he would be able to forget all his worries.

But what he gained was something entirely different.

While painting, he came to realize something. That there was a side of him he had never expected.

He wanted to show his paintings to others. He found himself thinking that he wanted to gather the paintings he had done well and hold an exhibition. To show that he was someone who could paint such pictures. And to be praised for it.

To think he had such a desire within him. He had lived believing he was not the sort of person who wanted to show off and boast to others.

That was why painting became even more fun and fascinating. Though he painted others, what he discovered was himself.

“Pavilion Master Seo, you should try finding a hobby as well.”

“I don’t have the time.”

Jang Ho was about to tell him to make time even if he had to carve it out, but instead he smiled and said,

“You are still young.”

He knew that before the right occasion came, it would not be easy anyway. He himself had only put it off, again and again.

His own occasion had been this.

When he had once said that he wanted to paint someday, Geom Mugeuk had run out at once and bought paints, brushes, and the like.

His occasion had been becoming close to the young cult leader of the Demon Cult.

To paint even once, one had to become close to the young cult leader of the Demon Cult.

Seo Daeryong was watching. He was watching Jang Ho’s face as he smiled to himself, wondering what he was thinking. It was a look he could never have imagined in the past.

‘I have changed too, but Lord Jang has truly changed a great deal as well.’

It was then. The door opened, and the very occasion that had brought about all these changes entered.

When Seo Daeryong confirmed who had come in, he was startled, then immediately ran toward him.

“Young Cult Leader!”

Seo Daeryong threw himself into his arms. Normally, Geom Mugeuk would have lightly dodged aside and teased him, but today he did not avoid him.

“I’m glad you’re safe.”

After hugging him tightly, Geom Mugeuk patted Seo Daeryong on the shoulder.

“I nearly had this precious right arm of yours cut off, you know. I nearly died.”

“I wasn’t particularly worried.”

“Because Lord Jang was here?”

“No.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jang Ho and gave the reason.

“Because there was someone reliable enough to replace my right arm.”

At that, Seo Daeryong’s expression turned triumphant.

“What are you going to do? The person who was supposed to replace me saved the right arm, you know? It seems he has withdrawn from the candidacy!”

Geom Mugeuk walked over to Jang Ho.

“You worked hard.”

“I suspect that getting us into this Chongqing branch was harder than saving Pavilion Master Seo.”

Jang Ho shared the credit for saving Seo Daeryong with Geom Mugeuk. It meant that he had been able to save him because Geom Mugeuk had made it possible for him to come here.

“Don’t even mention it. I pleaded with Unit Leader Jin, pleaded and pleaded. I told him I’d grant all your requests from now on, that I wouldn’t force you to dance, that I wouldn’t tease you for being boring. Only after making every promise under the sun was I able to get you into the branch.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s banter, Seo Daeryong broke into a broad smile.

Then he bowed formally.

“Thank you, Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk relayed the news he must have been desperately waiting for.

“The elder is safe.”

Seo Daeryong asked in surprise,

“You met Master?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded. Not only Seo Daeryong, but Jang Ho as well looked relieved.

“Why did he not come with you?”

Geom Mugeuk answered with a meaningful look in his eyes.

“That is what we must find out from now on. Why he could not come with me.”

* * *

Half a double-hour later, a quiet farewell took place at the rear gate of the branch.

The carriage carrying the Demon Army soldiers departed first.

Branch Leader Ju Gwol expressed his gratitude to Jang Ho.

“I am grateful. I will not forget this debt.”

Not only had Jang Ho saved his subordinates, he had personally saved Ju Gwol’s life as well.

“If you ever need my help later, please be sure to come find me.”

Compared to his fervor, Jang Ho was calm.

“Thanks to you, I have stayed well. Let us meet again.”

And so the large carriages carrying the Demon Army soldiers quietly left the place. Whether the Demon Army returned to the main headquarters or stood by somewhere else, they would move according to the orders of Tongcheon Pavilion.

Geom Mugeuk, Seo Daeryong, and Jin Hagun were also there. Geom Mugeuk spoke to Jin Hagun about Seo Daeryong.

“I’ll be taking this man with me.”

Originally, it was not something he could permit. Since the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had appeared beneath Hyangsu Villa, it had become clear that this matter was truly connected to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

He still did not know about the sound transmission exchanged between Geom Mugeuk and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. Geom Mugeuk had kept that matter secret. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon clearly seemed to have a reason he had to stand on their side.

Even so, Jin Hagun readily accepted it.

“Do so.”

Ju Gwol, who had been listening, stepped forward.

“You cannot. The possibility remains that he is the culprit.”

He still had not let go of his suspicion that this incident was the work of demonic practitioners.

Geom Mugeuk said to him,

“If this man is revealed to be the culprit, I will take responsibility and bring him before you. I promise you as the Young Cult Leader.”

Jin Hagun had already granted permission, and Geom Mugeuk had gone this far in saying it—how could he insist further?

“Please do not forget that promise.”

When Ju Gwol withdrew from the place, Jin Hagun said to Geom Mugeuk,

“If you need help, tell me anytime.”

“The one who needs help isn’t me. It’s you.”

“Me?”

“You’re the one they’re targeting, aren’t you?”

As Geom Mugeuk said, they might continue to target him.

“So, with that in mind, how about staying for the time being at a Murim Alliance safe house and observing how the situation unfolds?”

Jin Hagun had not thought of going to a safe house. He had intended to confront the forces behind this here at the branch.

“Think of it not as hiding, but as lying in ambush. The less this side reveals itself, the harder it will be for the enemy. Think of it as removing one of their moves.”

Indeed, Geom Mugeuk was pointing out a part he had not considered. Now, in moments like this, Jin Hagun no longer felt jealousy toward Geom Mugeuk. There was only swift acceptance.

“I will.”

Thinking about it, if he stayed with them, the branch martial artists would only be in greater danger. Going to a safe house with the Demon-Extermination Unit might well be the better choice.

As he departed, Geom Mugeuk said,

“Once we smash them to pieces, call Sain and let’s have a drink.”

Jin Hagun nodded once, then turned and walked the opposite way. Seeing that he felt like seeing Bi Sain, someone he normally never even thought of, it seemed he truly wanted to smash the ones who had plotted this affair to pieces.

* * *

If one side had succeeded, there was also a side that had failed.

The person sitting before the old fortune-teller in a corner of the Chongqing marketplace was Yu Beom.

“Judging by your appearance, your luck today was not good.”

Yu Beom’s face was swollen, and crimson palm marks were imprinted on it. They were marks left by the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

Yu Beom lowered his head deeply.

He was tense. Depending on how the report he was about to give was received, his life could be forfeit.

While the old fortune-teller slowly shook the wooden tube, the two exchanged words by sound transmission.

—We failed to kill the Demon-Extermination Unit Leader.

At the report that the mission had failed, the old man’s hand, which had been shaking the tube, paused for a moment.

Yu Beom quickly gave the reasons why they could not help but fail.

—The Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult came there with him. And the Blood Heaven Blade Demon refused the order to stop the Young Cult Leader.

The old man slowly opened his eyes. His gaze was filled with nothing but coldness.

Both the plan to kill Seo Daeryong and the plan to kill Jin Hagun had failed.

—Please forgive me.

Fortunately, the old man’s anger did not turn into irritable killing intent that sought to hold him responsible.

—If the Young Cult Leader came, then it would have been so.
