He seemed to understand well the relationship between Geom Mugeuk and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

Soon, the sound transmission ended, and the old man shook the wooden tube with even greater vigor.

“If it does not resolve after one attempt, you try twice. If it still does not resolve after two attempts… then you must consult the fortune. Now, draw.”

The old man held out the divination tube filled with bamboo sticks.

As if he had been prepared for everything, there was once again a stick protruding slightly.

Yu Beom drew it out.

—Insufficient rage shall be filled with blood.

After checking the contents, Yu Beom looked at the old man with a tense expression. He knew what this meant. It meant they would proceed with the third annihilation.

Because this operation had failed, this third annihilation would become a major incident that would turn the murim upside down.

“Go and deliver the fortune.”

* * *

I returned to the safe house with Seo Daeryong.

I went straight to see Father and told him everything that had happened exactly as it was.

I told him everything from beginning to end. The only moment Father grew angry was when the Blood Heaven Blade Demon came up.

If they had previously touched the demonic path by leaving letters in blood, now it had become certain that they had touched the Demon Venerable.

Whoever they were, they had better hope they run into me rather than Father. Father was very angry.

“Do you perhaps have any idea what might be connected to Elder Blade Demon?”

I hoped that Father might know something. He had known the Blade Demon since his youth, after all.

But Father said nothing. I could not tell whether he did not know, or knew and simply would not tell me.

“This enemy is somehow different.”

I conveyed exactly what I had felt thus far.

“At first, I thought they were the forces behind everything. But now I have begun to think they may not be.”

“Why do you think that?”

“When we were cutting off their source of funds in Shaanxi, here in Chongqing they were drawing Elder Blade Demon in and plotting this affair. They were separate undertakings.”

At the very least, it meant this was not revenge for what had happened in Shaanxi.

And if Father had not turned the carriage around, this matter would have proceeded in an entirely different direction.

“Not only that, their methods are different as well. Until now, they hid in secret, built up their strength, and planned for the future.”

In our cult, Hwan Yeo had hidden as the master of the garden; in the Murim Alliance, as Jin Hagun’s master; and in the Evil Dao Alliance, as an instructor. It had been the same in the Pungcheon Sect, the Northern Sea Ice Palace, and the Golden Dragon Merchant Guild.

They had been weaving schemes and building their strength. Naturally, the Twelve Zodiac Kings were not the completed versions of themselves from the future.

“But this case is different. They boldly attacked the Chongqing branch of the Murim Alliance and tried to kill Pavilion Master Seo. At the same time, they tried to eliminate the Demon-Extermination Unit Leader and every member of the Demon-Extermination Unit. Father, you know what the result of that would have been, do you not?”

Their intention was clear.

“They are trying to start a war between our cult and the Murim Alliance.”

Father’s eyes gleamed coldly. Though Father dreamed of unifying the murim, there was no way he would wish for a war in which he was made to dance in someone else’s hands.

“It may simply be that they have suffered so much until now that they changed their strategy, but something about it is subtly different.”

Because it was a feeling born of instinct, I could not present a clear answer.

But though I did not know it,

“I believe that you, Father, know the answer.”

Chapter 506: If the Enemy Is Smart, We're at an Advantage.
----------------------------------------------------------
“Are you saying that I am deceiving you?”

Saying that Father knew the answer was certainly open to misunderstanding. It had sounded as though he knew the truth but had kept it from me.

“No. That is not what I meant.”

“Then what do you mean by saying that I know the answer?”

After a brief pause, I spoke to Father.

“I mean that somewhere in your past, there must be a clue to this matter.”

There was a clear reason I thought so.

“They are definitely people who hold a grudge or some history with Elder Doma. You can tell simply by the fact that Elder Doma follows them willingly.”

Father nodded with an expression showing he agreed.

“But is that all there is to it?”

“What do you mean?”

“If they held a grudge against Elder Doma, they would have killed him somehow. By poison, traps, or any other means. Yet they brutally annihilated an orthodox sect and pinned the crime on us. There is no greater infamy that could befall the Main Cult.”

Father’s eyes sharpened. He had assumed it was simply a matter involving Doma that had spread outward, but now the scope was expanding.

“And that is not all. They tried to kill the West Pavilion Master and attempted to kill the Myeolmadaeju to spark a war with the Main Cult. This is too great an undertaking to be merely for settling a grudge with Elder Doma.”

Father slowly nodded as if he had been thinking the same.

“Not merely resentment toward Elder Doma….”

This was the emotion my instincts had detected.

“I feel malice directed at the Main Cult.”

“!”

“A deep resentment that wishes to destroy not only Elder Doma, but the Main Cult as well.”

I could feel Father’s shock. I could tell by his reaction. He had not been thinking to that extent.

“That is why I spoke. The one who orchestrated this must be somewhere in Elder Doma’s past, somewhere in your past, Father. Even if you cannot remember right now.”

Silence flowed. Wondering if I had spoken too confidently about something uncertain, I added:

“Please simply keep it in mind. It is only a feeling, an intuition.”

“How accurate is that intuition?”

I smiled sheepishly and answered.

“It is almost always right.”

Father rose from his seat and slowly walked to the window. Then he looked outside and spoke in a low voice.

“Then the situation is even worse.”

It meant that, apart from the forces behind the scenes we had originally been facing, a new enemy had appeared.

And I viewed this situation positively.

“No, this could actually be good.”

Father’s gaze, which had been directed outside the window, turned to me. At the questioning look in his eyes asking why—

“Because now, while the forces behind the scenes have been somewhat weakened, is an opportunity to deal with the new enemy.”

There was another reason I felt it was a personal grudge.

The original forces behind the scenes would have acted more carefully, waiting for an opportunity. They would not have caused Jung-gyeong to be sealed off while Father and I were passing through. They would know how foolish it is to face us both at once.

But these people are different. Emotion can be felt in their actions.

“It feels as if they are saying this to us:”

I conveyed that feeling exactly to Father.

“We have returned!”

At that moment, I saw it. An emotion that flashed across Father’s face. It was clearly cold derision.

“If they wanted to say such things, they should have said them while opening the door and entering the Cheonma Hall.”

Because if it were Father, he would have done exactly that. Framing someone and seizing their weakness—such cowardly acts were what Father truly despised.

Father, I do not know who it is, nor for what reason.

“Send them back with me.”

* * *

When I came out of Father’s quarters, Seo Daeryong was pacing about in the inner courtyard.

“You must be tired. Why are you not resting?”

“I lay down to catch some sleep, but it would not come.”

It must have been because he was worried about Hyeolcheon Doma. I knew better than anyone how much affection had grown between them while learning martial arts.

“When you learned the Myeolcheonmadosik, did you not hear anything separately? That someone else knows it?”

“No, he said the Myeolcheonmadosik is passed down only to the successor of the Doma. It is a martial art that will absolutely never be leaked to outsiders.”

Then who on earth had used the Myeolcheonmadosik?

The previous Doma? He was known to be already dead.

Or perhaps Elder Doma had another martial brother? If so, there was no way Father would not know of it.

“Young Cult Leader.”

Seo Daeryong spoke with a more serious face than ever before.

“Please tell me whenever you need me. Even if it is dangerous.”

One does not become a martial artist capable of sacrificing for another simply by learning high-level martial arts. Because he was Seo Daeryong, who had hoped for change in the New Cult for the sake of his seniors who died unjustly, his gaze had always been fiery.

“If Elder Doma heard your words, he would be pleased.”

“Later, please make sure to take the credit on my behalf, Young Cult Leader.”

Just as we were smiling at each other like that, an inner courtyard martial artist came and handed me a thick stack of documents before leaving. The Tongcheon Pavilion had been sending information to the inner courtyard several times a day.

“What is this?”

“Documents on the orthodox sects of Jung-gyeong. I requested them from the Tongcheon Pavilion. To find out where their next target will be.”

Seo Daeryong looked at me with a surprised expression.

“Do you think they will commit another sect-annihilating disaster?”

“Because they failed to kill you and the Myeolmadaeju. Yes, I believe they will do it again. Not merely once, but twice, three times, until they achieve their goal. Even if I am wrong, being fully prepared is never a bad choice.”

Seo Daeryong’s expression darkened slightly. He was probably blaming himself, thinking that because he had survived, someone else’s death would follow. It was because he was this kind of person that I liked him.

“There is quite a clever person orchestrating this matter on their side. That is advantageous to us.”

“If the enemy is clever, why is that advantageous for us?”

“Because we also have a clever person who can figure out what they are thinking.”

“Who?”

“You.”

I handed the documents to Seo Daeryong.

“There must be something that clever people understand about each other. Find out where their next target is.”

“Then you should look at them, Young Cult Leader.”

I am not a clever person like Sima Gunsa, Go Wol, or Seo Daeryong. If I am regarded as clever, it is only the insight born of regression; after all, I could not have entered Hwangcheon Pavilion as a top student.

“What if I choose wrong?”

“If you choose wrong, then I will choose wrong too. Daeryong, believe in your cleverness.”

Strength entered Seo Daeryong’s eyes. This matter concerned his master’s very life, as well as the lives of innocents. In the past, he would have fled from such pressure.

“Yes, I will try.”

Seo Daeryong began spreading the documents on the floor. Rather than in a room or on a desk, this empty inner courtyard was better for surveying the materials all at once.

And so, documents on dozens of sects in Jung-gyeong were spread out in the inner courtyard.

Seo Daeryong immersed himself to a terrifying degree. He read quickly and moved aside what was unnecessary. He went here and there, reading this and then running over to read that.

I did not disturb him and simply watched quietly. I thought that just by being beside him, I would be a strength to Seo Daeryong. The thought that I was watching would make him focus even more.

Fortunately, Seo Daeryong did not know. That Father was looking down on us from the window.

Otherwise, instead of showing this terrifying concentration, he would have grown nervous and flustered and tripped over himself several times.

Do your best, Daeryong! So we can return to the Main Cult with the elder.

* * *

A person sat before the old fortune-teller.

The one silently staring at the old man with a massive greatsword resting on his knees was none other than Hyeolcheon Doma.

The old man showed no agitation, as if he had known Hyeolcheon Doma would come.

Was it because of those stubborn eyes? Though Hyeolcheon Doma revealed no intent whatsoever and merely sat there, he pressed down upon his opponent with tremendous weight.

“Where is it?”

“No matter how much of a fortune-teller I am, how would I know if you ask so abruptly?”

“I will not ask twice.”

Despite the clear threat, the old man was not frightened. And he knew exactly what Hyeolcheon Doma was asking.

“Were you always this sort of person? The Hyeolcheon Doma I know is someone who would not care in the slightest whether orthodox people live or die.”

Hyeolcheon Doma quietly placed his hand on the Myeolcheon Daedo resting on his knees. Though he still revealed not even a sliver of killing intent, let alone any small trace of intent, the pressure multiplied several-fold simply by touching the blade.

“Can you cut me?”

To the old man’s provocation, Hyeolcheon Doma answered in a languid tone.

“It is what I do best.”

A strange smile formed on the old man’s lips, and soon he picked up the divination tube.

“Even if I die for it, let me cast a fortune. As to why our Demon Lord is trying so hard to save orthodox people.”

Even in a situation where the Myeolcheon Daedo might fly and sever his neck in one blow, the old man calmly chose a bamboo stick from the tube.

—New ties surpass old ties.

The old man looked down at the words written on the divination slip and spoke.

“As I thought, it was because of the Young Cult Leader.”

Hyeolcheon Doma grasped the hilt of the Myeolcheon Daedo. He revealed no killing intent, but the old man could tell. That he truly intended to cut him down.

This time, the old man held out the tube to Hyeolcheon Doma.

“Draw one.”

As Hyeolcheon Doma, who had been staring at the old man, looked at the tube, one stick was drawn up into the air by itself.

—One who ties the knot must untie it.

“You have chosen a good fortune. Indeed, knots must be untied by those who tied them.”

The old man spoke to Hyeolcheon Doma.

“In three days, go to Bongsan Sword Gate.”

Hyeolcheon Doma knew. That the old man was not telling him this simply because the divination had come out this way. He did not know how it was done, but this divination too was likely one the old man had intended.

Perhaps it was even his will.

As Hyeolcheon Doma tried to rise from his seat, the old man shook the divination tube.

“Why don’t I have my fortune told as well?”

The old man pulled out a stick.

—Those fated to live shall live, and those fated to die shall die.

After glancing at the divination, Hyeolcheon Doma rose from his seat without a word.

“Why do you not ask? Which side I believe I am on.”

Hyeolcheon Doma answered as he walked away.

“I know which side I am on.”

* * *

“It has been narrowed down to two places.”

Two sects remained on the floor.

Ungang Sect.

Bongsan Sword Gate.

It was the result of all the agonizing Seo Daeryong had done. Documents of excluded sects were piled up beside him.

“Among the orthodox sects representing Jung-gyeong, both are sects with good reputations for their sect leaders. If it becomes known that even they were attacked by the Main Cult, the orthodox martial artists will not sit idly by.”

Geom Mugik examined the documents of those two sects.

“It is certainly one of the two, but I simply cannot choose.”

And no wonder, for numerous lives hung on this single choice.

“This is my limit. I need either Sima Gunsa or Go Gunsa.”

Then Geom Mugik said something surprising.

“I think I know where.”

Seo Daeryong looked at Geom Mugik with surprised eyes.

“You know? How?”

Seo Daeryong started in surprise and stepped back.

“If you think about it, the leader of all these dark schemes is—!”

Geom Mugik looked at Seo Daeryong with a sly gaze.

“Have you only now realized my secret?”

Seo Daeryong, who had been silently watching Geom Mugik, shook his head and sighed.

“I do not even want to treat this as a joke. The Young Cult Leader is the enemy? That would be the destruction of the martial world.”

“By my standards, it would be the unification of the martial world.”

“Ugh, please stop!”

Seo Daeryong came beside Geom Mugik and asked.

“Which sect is it?”

Geom Mugik picked up one of the two documents on the floor.

Bongsan Sword Gate.

“How on earth did you know?”

The reason Geom Mugik chose Bongsan Sword Gate was a point Seo Daeryong had never thought of.

“I chose the one that had bad relations with the previously annihilated Yu Myeongjang or Daejeongmun.”

The final candidate, Ungang Sect, was on very close terms with the Daejeongmun Sect Leader. It also had a friendship with Yu Myeongjang. On the other hand, Bongsan Sword Gate was in a competitive and opposing relationship with them even within the same orthodox faction.

“Why?”

Seo Daeryong could not understand at all. Then, an unexpected answer flowed from Geom Mugik.

“Because that would make the maximum number of orthodox factions furious.”

“!”

Even within the same orthodox faction, there are friendships and factions. The deeper the friendship, the greater the anger. The intention is to draw out the fury of the other faction.

“Ah! That makes sense!”

Seo Daeryong’s voice trembled involuntarily.

Just then, news arrived from the Tongcheon Pavilion. Geom Mugik checked the letter brought by the inner courtyard martial artist.

“It is certain that it is that place.”

The letter’s contents stated that in three days, the leaders of various sects in Jung-gyeong would gather at Bongsan Sword Gate for a meeting.

“It is that day.”

At Geom Mugik’s certainty, Seo Daeryong raised an objection.

“Wouldn’t it be the opposite? In the current atmosphere, if sect leaders move, they will bring many experts under their command…. Ah!”

At that moment, Seo Daeryong recalled one fact.

When they attacked the Murim Alliance’s Jung-gyeong Branch to kill him, the branch had been crowded with martial artists who had come as support from nearby branches and sub-branches.

And was that all? They were ones who tried to kill the Myeolmadaeju and the entire Demon Annihilation Corps. These people know no bounds.

“Yes, they will not miss the opportunity of several sects gathering.”

Geom Mugik patted Seo Daeryong’s shoulder.

“Good work. You are the one who found it.”

Worry and fear preceded joy for Seo Daeryong.

“Will Master come there that day?”

“That I do not know. What is certain is that someone capable of using the Myeolcheonmadosik will come.”

Seo Daeryong could feel it. Because he had learned the Myeolcheonmadosik, he too stood on the extension of this fate. The day he had drunkenly grabbed his master’s hem, he had stood upon the line for the first time.

And upon reflection, he had been walking upon it all along.

One step when his master took him as a successor, one step when gifting him the greatsword he had wielded in his youth, one step when teaching him martial arts, one step when scolding him, and one step when smiling at him.

And so, this time too, he must walk.

“If Master comes as well, then three people capable of wielding it will gather.”

Chapter 507: If Only Another Lifespan Could Increase.
-----------------------------------------------------
“I want to help too!”

That very feeling was the burning desire welling up from deep within Seo Daeryong’s heart.

“Of course, I know very well that I might only become a burden. Even so, I want to fight for my master.”

With his master possibly in danger, he could not simply wait around at the safe house.

It would have been nice if Geom Mugeuk had at least joked that he would make an excellent hostage for the enemy, but Geom Mugeuk only stared at him in silence.

‘I shouldn’t have said anything.’

He had nearly died even in the makeshift prison cell if not for Jang Ho. That was the level of his abilities at present.

His desire to save his master was genuine, but that did not mean he could become a burden in such an important matter.

Seo Daeryong smiled and changed his attitude.

“I was only saying. You know me, don’t you? Have you ever seen me go somewhere dangerous? I’ll wait at the safe house for the Young Cult Leader to return with my master.”

Having said it aloud, his pride was wounded and he was still reluctant to give up, so he added awkwardly,

“Isn’t there some way to suddenly become much stronger in three days?”

Geom Mugeuk suddenly thrust three fingers forward, then immediately shot away.

“Where are you going, Young Cult Leader?”

But Geom Mugeuk had already vanished from sight.

Seo Daeryong was puzzled.

‘Why did he hold up three fingers?’

Was it because he had asked whether there was a way to become stronger in three days? Or because the Bongsan Sword Sect gathering was in three days?

Whichever it was, did he really need to hold up his fingers like that?

‘Where did he suddenly go?’

The vanished Geom Mugeuk did not return after one hour, nor after two.

He still had not returned by the time Seo Daeryong let out this half-joking lament.

“You forgot about me, didn’t you? Now I understand. You meant that you’d return in three days, right?”

Just then, someone descended behind him with the sound of rushing wind.

“That would be difficult. My movement technique is too fast for that.”

Seo Daeryong spun around in surprise.

“Where have you been—huh?”

Seo Daeryong could not finish his sentence.

Geom Mugeuk was drenched in sweat. If he had sweated this much despite his level of martial prowess, then surely he had—

“Did you unify the martial world? Or at least the Central Plains?”

“If I had, I’d be drenched in blood, not sweat.”

As he spoke, Geom Mugeuk took a small box from inside his robe.

“What is this?”

“The answer to your question.”

“Huh? What did I ask?”

“Open it.”

Seo Daeryong took the box and opened it. Inside lay a single pill that glowed with a brilliant blue light. At a glance, it radiated an extraordinary energy.

“No! I don’t want it! I’m not eating it. I won’t eat it even if I die!”

“Do you even know what it is?”

“It’s a spiritual medicine, isn’t it? I’m not going to remain indebted to you, Young Cult Leader. Give this to Lord Jang instead.”

“It’s not a spiritual medicine.”

“……It isn’t?”

To the still-suspicious Seo Daeryong, Geom Mugeuk revealed the pill’s true identity.

“It’s an Explosive Qi Pill. It will let you unleash your demonic arts with greater power, but in exchange, it will shorten your lifespan. You asked, didn’t you? Whether there was a way to become stronger in three days. Here it is.”

Seo Daeryong stared down at the pill with trembling eyes.

“How much will it shorten my life?”

“It differs from person to person. It might take away one year, or it might take away ten.”

The brilliant blue light seemed to tempt him, as though it were trying to devour a human life.

Then the face of the Blood Heaven Demon Blade overlapped with the pill.

‘Master.’

If his strong master had ended up in such a situation, it meant that he was in danger.

‘Regardless of what anyone else does, I can’t just sit here.’

Locks between people were often fastened not by some grand event, but by things that seemed insignificant. That had been the case with the Blood Heaven Demon Blade as well.

The lock had been fastened the moment he realized that his neat and meticulous master had put him to bed while he was drunk.

His master could have thrown him into any room—or even the stable—after seeing him making a drunken mess of himself. Instead, he had put him in his own bed.

That was when the lock had been fastened.

I’m going to come to love this person.

Today, Seo Daeryong took another step along the thread of fate that bound him to his master.

“You never know, right? If my master is truly in danger, I might be able to save him because I took this pill.”

“It might shorten your lifespan by ten years. Or even twenty.”

“If another person’s lifespan can be extended in exchange, then that’s fine.”

Without hesitation, Seo Daeryong gulped down the pill. Why was this terrifying medicine so sweet?

In any case, since he had taken the medicine, Geom Mugeuk hurriedly had him circulate his qi.

“Use your cultivation method to dissolve the medicine.”

Seo Daeryong sat down and began circulating his energy.

Geom Mugeuk injected a strand of internal energy into his back, helping him dissolve the medicine.

Seo Daeryong circulated his true qi once, twice, then three times. With Geom Mugeuk’s help, the medicinal effects were completely absorbed into his body.

After finishing his circulation, Seo Daeryong opened his eyes. His gaze was far clearer and deeper than before he had absorbed the pill.

From his dantian, he could feel an internal energy that was incomparably more profound than before.

After examining his condition, the first thing he said was,

“It wasn’t some strange medicine, was it?”

Geom Mugeuk’s relaxed smile served as his answer.

“What medicine was it?”

Then an unexpected name emerged.

“The Azure Jade Divine Pill.”

Seo Daeryong was startled at the name. It was a spiritual medicine even more effective at increasing internal energy than the Demon Essence Pill he had taken earlier. It was not some pill that made him stronger by shortening his life.

“This precious thing!”

“I thought you wouldn’t take it if I simply gave it to you.”

“Of course I wouldn’t! Take the internal energy back!”

“Unfortunately, I haven’t learned the Star-Absorbing Demon Art.”

“Young Cult Leader!”

Seo Daeryong looked at Geom Mugeuk with trembling eyes.

“You were lucky. Fortunately, there was exactly one left in a single place.”

It was an era in which even money could not easily buy spiritual medicines, but Geom Mugeuk possessed the wealth of experience he had accumulated before his regression. He knew better than anyone where people sold what. Besides, he had earned money during this journey as well.

“The old man will be pleased when he hears that you tried to help him even at the cost of shortening your life. He’ll scold you as a fool on the surface, but he’ll be genuinely moved. I’ll make sure to take credit for telling him, too.”

Seo Daeryong felt embarrassed and burdened.

“To be honest, I thought it was a spiritual medicine. There’s no way you would give me something like that, Young Cult Leader. And you made up the name on the way here, didn’t you? What kind of name is Explosive Qi Pill? It’s so tacky. Don’t think you can fool me again! I was the top student admitted to Hwangcheon Pavilion! I’m not some pushover who gets tricked wherever he goes!”

Of course, to Geom Mugeuk, he was the biggest pushover in the world.

“I’ll have to be careful around that top student from now on. He just stuffs things into his mouth without even knowing what they are!”

“No, that’s not what happened!”

Seo Daeryong was delighted. His greatest weakness was that his internal energy was insufficient compared to the powerful Heaven-Slaying Demon Blade Form, and Geom Mugeuk had made up for that weakness.

‘How am I ever supposed to repay this kindness?’

Just then, Geom Mugeuk held up two fingers.

“By the way, what does that mean? This time there are two.”

“It means I’m going to show you the second way to become stronger in three days.”

Geom Mugeuk went into his quarters and came back out with something.

It was a protective armor. One of the pieces of armor he had obtained by putting on four layers of armor from the treasures the Silver-Han Merchant Guild Master had given his father as a gift.

The White Scaled Armor.

As its name suggested, it was a protective armor of pure white.

“I thought it would suit you. I had originally intended to give it to you anyway.”

It suited him even better today. This pure-white armor was a perfect match for someone willing to risk his life for his master.

“No, I’m really fine.”

Seo Daeryong waved his hands and backed away.

“Please don’t turn me into such a shameless person.”

“I’m lending it to you.”

Of course, Seo Daeryong did not believe him. He was going to give it to him anyway.

“How much?”

“About fifty years?”

Seo Daeryong wore an expression that said, Of course.

Then Geom Mugeuk said something that stopped him from taking another step back.

“You said you wanted to help the old man, didn’t you?”

Geom Mugeuk approached and handed him the White Scaled Armor. Then he opened his collar and showed him what he was wearing underneath. He was wearing the Black Dragon Hidden Armor.

“I’m wearing one too, so why are you being so impertinent and refusing?”

“No, that’s not the issue!”

“If you were sincere when you said you wanted to help your master, then put it on.”

How could Seo Daeryong refuse when Geom Mugeuk put it that way?

Seo Daeryong took off his clothes and put on the White Scaled Armor. Yes, staying alive was probably how he could help Geom Mugeuk.

The burden was the burden, but the White Scaled Armor was truly incredible.

“Ah, this is wonderful! It’s so comfortable that it feels like I’m not wearing anything at all.”

“Though it’s that thin and comfortable, it can block even fairly powerful sword qi.”

Seo Daeryong planted his great saber into the ground and gazed at his reflection in the blade. It felt so unfamiliar. The treasured garments, the disciple of the Blood Heaven Demon Blade wearing those garments, and even the great saber reflecting this image.

“It suits you.”

“I must have saved you in my previous life, Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled meaningfully.

‘Sorry, but Ian was the one who saved me. You must have saved me in the life before that.’

Geom Mugeuk looked far into the future.

“Fifty years from now, if you return this to me and say, ‘I used it well,’ won’t we laugh as we remember this day fifty years ago? We’ll spend that day reminiscing about the past. It’ll be a wonderful time.”

The thought of that moment filled Seo Daeryong’s heart to bursting.

“Will I still be alive then?”

“That’s why you should always wear it.”

And as though there were still one more thing remaining, Geom Mugeuk held up a single finger again.

“What is it this time?”

“The last of the three ways to become stronger in three days.”

The final gift of the day remained.

“Your internal energy has increased, and you’re wearing protective armor. Now, it’s time to fight.”

Seo Daeryong’s eyes widened. He realized that Geom Mugeuk intended to train him through actual combat.

“I won’t teach you martial arts while the old man is here. But there’s no problem with having an actual sparring match, is there?”

To receive actual combat training from the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Church—this was a fortuitous opportunity that could not be bought even with a fortune.

Geom Mugeuk announced the start of the sparring match with a frivolous remark.

“I’m supposed to be the incarnation of greed, yet I’ve given you too many precious things. Wouldn’t beating you up a little help me feel better?”

Seo Daeryong neither refused nor rejoiced.

He merely stared at Geom Mugeuk in silence.

When the first actual sparring match ended,

Seo Daeryong was crying. He had truly tried not to cry, not wanting to burden Geom Mugeuk, but the tears poured down anyway. Did he really deserve to be loved this much?

“Did it hurt that badly?”

Seo Daeryong answered without raising his head.

“Yes, it hurt terribly. Do you feel better now?”

With his head bowed, he heard Geom Mugeuk’s voice.

“No. This is only the beginning. Get up.”

The tears did not stop easily.

* * *

Geom Ujin stood by the window, watching his son and Seo Daeryong.

Hwi stood one step behind him, watching as well. He wondered what the Cult Leader was thinking at that moment.

‘If I were you, I would be proud, Cult Leader.’

If he had a son like that, anyway. It was a life Hwi could never have.

After silently gazing at his son, Geom Ujin slowly walked to the tea table, poured himself some tea, and drank it.

“You heard what I said—that the person who caused this incident might be someone from my past, didn’t you?”

“Yes, Cult Leader.”

After hearing those words, his son’s words kept circling in his mind.

“Did anyone come to mind?”

“No, no one.”

Then Geom Ujin suddenly spoke.

“There is one person who came to mind for me.”

Hwi looked at Geom Ujin in surprise.

“I had completely forgotten him, but while recalling the past, I remembered. There was one person who had always troubled me.”

Geom Ujin toyed with the empty teacup.

“He’s already dead.”

“Then how can you be thinking of him?”

Geom Ujin poured tea into the cup as he answered.

“Because we never found his corpse at the time.”

“!”

What if it truly was him?

“So much time has passed.”

Thinking of the person who might have returned through all those years, Geom Ujin quietly drank his tea.

* * *

The actual combat training continued until the morning of the third day.

They sparred while saving even their sleeping hours, except for the time spent eating.

This actual combat training was on an entirely different level from all the training Seo Daeryong had experienced until then.

Seo Daeryong passed the brink of death countless times. There were so many moments when he barely escaped disaster. It was a continuous succession of moments when he thought, Ah, so this is how I die.

But he did not die. His sparring partner was Geom Mugeuk, after all.

It was sparring solely for Seo Daeryong’s sake. It was training and instruction meant to pull him up to the next stage, and then the stage after that.

Geom Mugeuk did not say a single word, yet he corrected Seo Daeryong’s flawed postures and habits. He did not say a word, yet he taught him how to move in one situation and respond in another. As sword and saber clashed, Seo Daeryong learned through his body.

Because of that, Seo Daeryong felt his skills improving with every technique he used. He did not realize it, but his skills truly were improving.

He had only said it as a joke. He had asked whether there was any way to become dramatically stronger in three days.

The great saber strapped to Seo Daeryong’s back gleamed in the morning sunlight. The Seo Daeryong of three days ago and the Seo Daeryong of now were two different people.

“Daeryong.”

“Yes, Young Cult Leader.”

“What should we do now?”

Seo Daeryong knew what Geom Mugeuk was feeling when he asked that question. He knew why Geom Mugeuk had devoted himself so completely to him over the past three days.

So that he would not feel like a burden.

So that he would be the one to say these words.

“We’re going to rescue my master now. Will you come with me?”

Chapter 508: You Use a Broadsword Too.
--------------------------------------
The place where Geom Mugeuk and Seo Daeryong stood side by side was the roof of a building from which Bongsan Sword Sect could be seen in the distance.

Bongsan Sword Sect was not situated in some remote place, but built where many people came and went.

Looking down from above, its scale was considerable, and the fact that such a large compound stood adjacent to the marketplace alone was enough to show how great Bongsan Sword Sect’s influence was.

The sects attending the meeting were arriving at Bongsan Sword Sect one by one. There was still time before the appointed hour, but some had come early.

Seo Daeryong was flipping through the documents. The materials he was looking at now were information on Bongsan Sword Sect and on the sects invited today. Of course, they were materials sent by Tongcheongak.

“Bang Cheongmun (房淸紋), the sect leader of Bongsan Sword Sect, is someone who hates our cult very much. The purpose of today’s meeting is to unite against our cult as well. Ah, look over there.”

A sect had just arrived.

“The sect that just arrived is Namjung Sect (南中門). The expected number of attendees was twelve… yes, that’s right. Twelve. The sect leader, Won Hong (元泓), is favorably disposed toward our cult. He serves as a bridge between our cult and Chongqing’s orthodox murim, and he was invited to today’s meeting.”

“Outwardly, the banner they’re raising is unity against our cult, but in the end, it’s a political gathering.”

Nodding, Seo Daeryong continued speaking.

“Since their purpose in wiping out a sect is to enrage the orthodox factions, that Namjung Sect leader is the furthest thing from their target.”

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“If it were me, I would have targeted Namjung Sect.”

“What? Why?”

Seo Daeryong asked with a startled expression.

“If they strike Bongsan Sword Sect, which is usually hostile toward us, the orthodox factions will be furious, but they’ll also think it was an inevitable result. This place openly opposes us even in ordinary times.”

“I suppose so.”

“But what if they struck Namjung Sect? Would they really be pleased that a place friendly toward us had been annihilated?”

“They’re still part of the same orthodox faction, so they wouldn’t be pleased.”

What Geom Mugeuk was speaking of was the nature of anger.

“Exactly. Then what would their perception of us be? We annihilate a place that was friendly toward us? Wouldn’t they think we were truly insane? How would they feel looking at us committing such irrational and barbaric acts? I don’t think their hatred toward us would be any less. If anything, it would be greater.”

It was something Seo Daeryong had never considered at all. Hearing it, he realized the repercussions would be far greater if Namjung Sect were attacked.

“And you still say I’m smarter than you, Young Cult Master?”

Seo Daeryong was certain. If Geom Mugeuk became an enemy, he would be a hundred times—no, a thousand times—harder to deal with than he was now.

“You are smarter than me. You memorized all those materials, didn’t you?”

“I did memorize them.”

This time, Geom Mugeuk flinched in surprise.

“You memorized them? Really? I asked as a joke.”

“……”

“You’re joking too, right? You are, aren’t you?”

“…I’m joking.”

“Don’t say you’re joking with such confident eyes!”

There was definitely a sense of composure in Seo Daeryong that only someone who had memorized everything could show.

But that couldn’t be. There was no way he could have memorized all of this in such a short time.

Ah, come to think of it, when we first met, Seo Daeryong had come after memorizing all the personal information on the Demon Lords. Surely not? No, it couldn’t be.

“Let’s go.”

Before setting off, Geom Mugeuk burned all the documents he had read in his palm with Scorching Yang energy. The papers all turned to ash and scattered away.

Whoosh.

“When will I be able to do that?”

That was what Seo Daeryong envied most. Later, when he became able to do it, he would definitely try it in front of his subordinates. After reading a confidential document, he would burn it in his palm as if it were nothing. Casually, whoosh. Just thinking about it was cool.

Then Geom Mugeuk said something he had not expected.

“You have enough internal energy now too.”

“Really?”

“It won’t be long.”

At those heart-pounding words, Seo Daeryong realized anew just how great the fortuitous encounter he had obtained this time truly was.

“Now, let’s go!”

Geom Mugeuk launched himself lightly with qinggong, and Seo Daeryong followed after him.

With internal energy overflowing in his dantian, Seo Daeryong felt confidence in every movement he made.

Sects participating in the meeting were arriving one after another at Bongsan Sword Sect’s main gate.

As expected, the heads of each sect had brought subordinates with them—at least a dozen, and at most several dozen. Given the situation, they were taking every precaution for safety.

Since they could not enter through the main gate, Geom Mugeuk and Seo Daeryong climbed over the wall. Security was tight, but not to the point that the two of them could not infiltrate.

“Is it all right for us to walk around like this?”

After climbing over the wall, Geom Mugeuk openly revealed himself and walked.

“Straighten your shoulders. Act like invited guests.”

“Won’t my saber stand out?”

“It will only stand out the moment you worry about it.”

Indeed, the Bongsan Sword Sect martial artists they met while walking merely bowed their heads lightly in greeting and did not ask their identities. No one would imagine that the culprits would be walking around in broad daylight carrying a great saber.

“Look carefully. The scouts may already have come in.”

“Will a large number of them launch a surprise attack like when they attacked the Chongqing branch?”

“Who knows. It could be one person, or it could be a hundred. But one thing is certain: they’ll try to kill the leaders in the Myeolcheon Demon Saber style.”

When Seo Daeryong imagined someone killing people in the Myeolcheon Demon Saber style, his heart pounded as if it would burst. One thing was certain.

‘If we can’t stop them, countless people will die here today.’

If they told the people here that, no one would believe them.

With so many experts gathered here, who would dare come to kill them? And in broad daylight, no less. On top of that, Bongsan Sword Sect was not in some secluded place, so such an ambush would be even harder to imagine.

But they would come.

Just as they had come for him in the Thunder Prison.

“What do we do now?”

Geom Mugeuk had already thought of how to respond on the way here.

“We need to meet the leader of Bongsan Sword Sect.”

Since it was an unexpected method, Seo Daeryong was startled. He had thought they would hide somewhere, then appear and stop them when the enemies showed up.

“If we simply try to respond without knowing how they will attack, the damage could be great. I don’t know about anyone else, but we need to speak with the Bongsan sect leader beforehand.”

“Will he really believe us if we say we’re here to help?”

He probably would not even believe that he was the Young Cult Master to begin with.

“We have to make him believe.”

Seo Daeryong thought that this plan was only possible because it was Geom Mugeuk. He had faith that, if it were him, he could somehow persuade the leader of Bongsan Sword Sect.

“Let’s go. The sect leader’s hall is over there.”

“I’m going to meet the sect leader alone.”

Geom Mugeuk pointed at Seo Daeryong’s great saber.

“If you’re there, wearing that great saber, when I reveal that I’m a demonic practitioner, the sect leader will suspect that the culprits are playing some trick.”

“That makes sense. Then where should I be in the meantime?”

“Find out whether there are any suspicious people.”

“By myself?”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“Isn’t finding enemies in a dangerous place cooler than burning paper in your palm?”

Leaving Seo Daeryong behind, Geom Mugeuk headed toward the sect leader’s hall.

He judged that things were still safe for now. The enemies would move once the meeting began in earnest.

* * *

Bang Cheongmun, the leader of Bongsan Sword Sect, was in the sect leader’s hall.

He stood by the window, looking at the martial artists of each sect arriving at the sect.

A subordinate came to report to him.

“The invited sects are arriving one after another.”

“Has anyone informed us they will not attend?”

“Not a single one.”

“Go and thoroughly inspect the preparations for the meeting.”

“Yes!”

After sending his subordinate away, Bang Cheongmun wore a satisfied expression.

This meeting was an opportunity to leap forward.

One should not use another’s tragedy as an opportunity, but the destruction of Daejeong Sect was clearly an opportunity for Bongsan Sword Sect. They had competed with them, clashing over every little matter. They had naturally begun absorbing the various interests Daejeong Sect had held.

Today’s meeting had the purpose of demonstrating Bongsan Sword Sect’s power while uniting the sects close to him.

‘With this chance, we’ll rise to become the number one sect in Chongqing.’

That was his goal.

At that moment, someone opened the door and entered.

If it had been a subordinate, he would have announced himself before coming in.

When he turned around, the person who had entered was a young man he had never seen before.

Bang Cheongmun secretly drew up his inner force and asked calmly,

“Who are you?”

This was a place his subordinates were guarding like an iron wall. Moreover, even the guards who were supposed to reveal themselves in emergencies had sent no word. It meant they had all already been subdued.

“An uninvited guest, but likely the guest you need most today.”

Of course, the young man was Geom Mugeuk.

“What happened to my subordinates?”

“I merely sealed their acupoints, so don’t worry.”

Fortunately, there was no scent of blood anywhere.

“Who are you?”

Geom Mugeuk did not hide his identity.

“I am Geom Mugeuk, Young Cult Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

Bang Cheongmun’s eyes widened.

If not for the bloodshed that had recently occurred in Chongqing, if the public purpose of today’s meeting had not been to join forces against the Demon Cult, he would never have believed such an absurd statement.

“That is difficult to believe.”

Bang Cheongmun drew his sword. It was a sword he would have had to draw anyway. If he was the Young Cult Master of the Demon Cult, he could not have come with good intentions, so he had to draw it; if he was not the Young Cult Master, then all the more reason. He was a dangerous man impersonating the Demon Cult’s Young Cult Master.

Geom Mugeuk had no intention of persuading him through conversation. He knew well that words would not solve this.

Swoosh.

Geom Mugeuk charged toward Bang Cheongmun in an instant.

In the flash of a moment, Bang Cheongmun thrust his sword at Geom Mugeuk, who had closed in right before his nose.

But the tip of the sword he had thrust already met empty air.

Slide.

The Black Demon Sword was aimed at his neck from behind.

Though he was the leader of a large sect like Bongsan Sword Sect, he could not block Geom Mugeuk’s Myeongwang Step.

“I did not come to kill you.”

“Then why did you sneak in? Why are you pointing a sword at me?”

“I could hardly enter through the main gate of a place holding a meeting to stand against our cult, could I? The reason I pointed a sword at you was to confirm my identity.”

Geom Mugeuk withdrew his sword.

At that moment, Bang Cheongmun turned like a thunderbolt and swung his sword.

Shwing!

But the space behind him was empty once again.

Slide.

Once more, a sword was aimed at his neck from behind.

“I can understand twice, but there will be no third time. I have no intention of dealing with someone that foolish.”

When Bang Cheongmun nodded, Geom Mugeuk withdrew his sword.

Bang Cheongmun no longer had any intention of attacking. Even if the first time could be dismissed as having been caught off guard by a preemptive strike, the second had clearly been a surprise attack he launched first. And he had confirmed the overwhelming difference in their abilities.

“If I had come to kill you, you would already be dead, and I would now be writing words on that wall with your blood.”

Listening to Geom Mugeuk, Bang Cheongmun turned around.

“Someone who intends to do exactly that will come today.”

“!”

Bang Cheongmun’s heart sank. Geom Mugeuk’s clear, deep eyes were not the eyes of someone lying.

“Who is it?”

“I don’t know either. There are people trying to drive a wedge between us and the orthodox faction to start a war.”

Bang Cheongmun still wore an expression of disbelief.

“A wedge? I heard that the martial art used to kill the Daejeong Sect leader was the Demon Venerable’s martial art.”

The Murim Alliance would not have deliberately spread such rumors, so this rumor too must have been spread by them. Even if not, it was a rumor that could not help but spread.

“That is why I came personally today. To uncover what happened.”

Bang Cheongmun still did not cast aside his suspicion, but he seemed more relaxed than at first. Among martial artists, showing one’s skill once produced results faster than a hundred words.

“Honestly, I find it hard to believe. That the Young Cult Master of the Demon Cult would be moving around alone like this.”

“I understand.”

Geom Mugeuk looked out the window and said,

“Many more incomprehensible things will happen today.”

His gaze, which had been directed outside, returned to Bang Cheongmun.

“If you cannot trust me, then trust this sword that spared you twice. It is a sword that will spare you a third time as well.”

Bang Cheongmun had a definite premonition that the other man truly was the Young Cult Master.

To subdue him in a single move at such a young age?

Unless he was the Demon Cult’s Young Cult Master, it would be impossible.

Whether everything he said was true or not was still unknown, but first, he had to ask.

“What do you want?”

* * *

Seo Daeryong was roaming here and there, observing people.

‘Confidently.’

When he walked with his shoulders spread wide, no one suspected him.

He was going around, watching to see if there was anyone suspicious, when—

“An unfamiliar face.”

The white-haired martial artist with twin swords at his waist asked him, likely because the great saber on Seo Daeryong’s back stood out. Among those gathered, many carried sabers, but among them, Seo Daeryong was the only one carrying such a large great saber.

Just as even the gazes of the people nearby focused on him.

“I’m hurt, Martial Artist Yang. We met back then, and I even greeted you. You said we should have a drink sometime.”

“Ah, did I? My apologies. As I grow older, I keep forgetting things.”

When Seo Daeryong recognized him, the man called Martial Artist Yang no longer questioned him.

Seo Daeryong knew this old martial artist’s personal details. Fortunately, there was only one white-haired martial artist who used twin swords.

He had truly memorized all the distinctive traits of the people in the documents.

Just as Seo Daeryong felt relieved inwardly, someone spoke to him from behind.

“You use a great saber too, I see.”

When Seo Daeryong turned around, a young man was standing there.

He was a tall, handsome martial artist, and on his back he carried a saber as large as Seo Daeryong’s own.

‘Who was there among the young martial artists who used a great saber like that?’

There was no need to try to recall who it was. There had been no such person in the materials.

The man began walking toward Seo Daeryong with a pleasant smile.

Absolute Regression Chapter 509

Chapter 509: Kill the Fake and Kill the Onlookers
-------------------------------------------------
Seo Daeryong’s instincts told him.

‘It’s him.’

But at the same time, a doubt rose in his mind.

‘He’s this young?’

No, it was not because he was young. It was because he was bright. With such a handsome face and such an amiable smile suiting him so well—this man was the murderer?

If he asked ten passersby, not one of them would take this man for the fiend who had annihilated the Daejeong Sect, or for someone who had learned demonic arts. Even if he himself had been asked, he would have answered the same.

‘Is it not him?’

As all manner of thoughts flashed through his mind, the man drew close to Seo Daeryong. Seo Daeryong secretly raised his internal energy, ready to counterattack at any moment.

The tall man looked down at Seo Daeryong, still smiling with those amiable eyes.

“You’ve got nerve. In times like these, walking around with a great saber at your waist is bound to invite misunderstandings.”

Even his voice was calm and gentle, making him seem like a kind and decent person.

Seo Daeryong’s gaze turned to the great saber on the man’s back.

“You knew that too, yet you still wore one, did you not?”

The man looked at Seo Daeryong and smiled in a way whose meaning was impossible to read.

What was that look saying? That it was all right for him to carry one because he was strong enough? That it was fine because he had come here to kill everyone?

What if this man really was the murderer?

He had thought the rumor about a young man had been spread to frame him—but had the culprit truly been young after all?

Seo Daeryong grew tense. He felt he had to buy time somehow until Geom Mugeuk returned.

Should he handle it himself? Seo Daeryong had not raised that sort of confidence.

Though his increased internal energy and real-combat sparring with Geom Mugeuk had filled him with confidence, Seo Daeryong remained cautious, and cautious again.

He was not foolish enough to let his guard down against someone who had walked up to him with a smile.

“Which sect are you from?”

Seo Daeryong knew the other man did not belong to any of the sects invited today, but he asked as though he did not.

“Peerless.”

“You mean you are from the Peerless Sect?”

There was no such sect in Chongqing.

“No, I mean the characters written on your saber.”

The saber the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had used in his youth had the characters Peerless inscribed upon its blade. It seemed the man who had approached from behind had seen them.

“A fine saber.”

“My master gave it to me.”

How would he react to the word master? Seo Daeryong watched his response closely.

The man showed no particular reaction. Still wearing the same amiable smile he had shown at first, he instead asked about Seo Daeryong’s master.

“What sort of person is your master?”

He was asking about his master. Seo Daeryong could sense it instinctively. He had deliberately read the characters on the blade, and now he was asking about his master.

‘He knows who my master is, and he is asking anyway.’

And he was not even trying to hide the fact that he had approached while knowing. Seo Daeryong became more and more certain that this man was an enemy.

“He is exactly as the characters on the saber say. A man without equal in this murim.”

At that, the man denied Seo Daeryong’s words.

“There is no one in this world without an equal. Everyone has a destined adversary. The only difference is whether they meet them while they live, or die without ever doing so.”

The words destined adversary struck Seo Daeryong as meaningful.

‘Who did he have in mind when he said that? Me? Master? Or the Young Cult Leader?’

This time, Seo Daeryong asked about these adversaries.

“Are destined adversaries fated to fight each other?”

“Of course. They are fated to kill one another.”

“How can one tell if someone is their destined adversary?”

“You know when you see them.”

As he said that, he stared intently at Seo Daeryong. A taut tension naturally settled between the two of them.

‘This man considers me his destined adversary!’

That too was an instinctive judgment. He felt some entanglement of fate with the man before him.

Fear rose in him, but on the other hand, he also felt proud.

Seo Daeryong still recalled those gloomy days when he had been an investigator. No matter how strong he became, he believed his identity would always lie there. That way, he would not become arrogant.

Someone considered him a fated enemy? It frightened him, but it also pleased him. And beyond that, he even thought this:

Yes, then come after me instead of Master or the Young Cult Leader! I will face you.

The man’s gaze turned toward the distant main hall where today’s meeting was to take place. In front of the hall stood the warriors who had accompanied the leaders of each sect, and judging by their numbers, nearly all the invited guests seemed to have gathered.

The man asked Seo Daeryong,

“Do you know the purpose of today’s meeting?”

“I do not know much.”

“It is to unite against us.”

“Us?”

At Seo Daeryong’s question, the man nodded.

“That is right. Us.”

“Do you and I have anything in common that would make us ‘us’? Surely you do not mean this is a meeting to deal with saber-wielders?”

At that, the man answered without hesitation.

“We are demonic practitioners, are we not?”

“!”

Seo Daeryong was startled. The other man spoke so calmly that he thought he had misheard. Just as he had guessed, this man was one of those who had orchestrated this affair.

He’s revealing his identity like this? Then why did he approach me?

“It is a meeting hostile to us.”

Then he said something even more shocking.

“What do you say? Let us go in together and sweep them all away.”

The man’s eyes and tone were both serious, as though he meant it from the bottom of his heart.

Seo Daeryong laughed.

“Why are you laughing?”

“I was so tense that I laughed without realizing it.”

At Seo Daeryong’s honest answer, the man also laughed. He was certainly more composed than Seo Daeryong. It was clearly this man who was leading the atmosphere right now.

“Are you afraid?”

“How could I not be? Someone I have just met is asking me to commit suicide with him.”

“Why do you think we would lose? With your martial arts and mine, could we not sweep away those orthodox bastards?”

Instead of answering whether he would fight or not, Seo Daeryong subtly brought up his own story. He had no desire whatsoever to tell this man about himself, but he needed to buy time until Geom Mugeuk returned. Even if it was only enough time for a few words, he had to drag things out as much as possible now.

“There was a time, when I was gloomy, that I too wanted to sweep the whole world away.”

It was spoken sincerely. Before he met Geom Mugeuk, when the senior from Hwangcheon Pavilion who had cared for him died unjustly, his gloom had reached its peak. If he could have, he truly would have swept everything away.

“Why were you gloomy?”

He must surely have noticed that Seo Daeryong was stalling. Yet this man was accepting everything he said. Seo Daeryong felt an inexplicable unease, but for now this was the best he could do.

“As you can see, I am short, and my body is slight.”

“Did you perhaps learn martial arts using a great saber because of that inferiority complex?”

Seo Daeryong could feel it. The other man had intentionally provoked him by using the words inferiority complex.

If it had been long ago, he would have flared up. But now it was different.

‘Who do I have beside me? From whom did I learn?’

Seo Daeryong had grown by watching Geom Mugeuk face the Heavenly Demon and the Demon Venerables. He was not someone who would fall for a provocation of this level.

“When I learned that I would be studying martial arts using a great saber, I was so happy my heart felt like it would burst. Thinking about it, perhaps there really was such an inferiority complex, just as you say.”

Instead, Seo Daeryong asked him,

“Have you never felt something like an inferiority complex in your life?”

As if dumbfounded, the man laughed again. Seeing his smile up close as they conversed, Seo Daeryong finally understood. He was someone who smiled habitually, and that good-natured, amiable smile was not due to his personality, but to the way the man looked.

His handsome appearance merely made him seem affable; in his eyes, Seo Daeryong could sense a certain arrogance.

What he could not see before, he could see now.

“There is someone I would like to introduce to you. Someone I sincerely respect. He changed my life.”

At that, the man provoked Seo Daeryong again.

“I do not want someone else to change my life. Would that not be a rather insignificant life?”

Seo Daeryong stared at him and said,

“Are people not originally changed because of others? We read books, listen to what other people say, and watch how others live. Seeing you regard such things as insignificant, you truly must be remarkable.”

Perhaps he took it as sarcasm, for the smile vanished from the man’s face for the first time.

At that reaction, Seo Daeryong instead felt relieved.

‘Yes, if he is a villain, those are the eyes he should have. How am I supposed to recognize and deal with a villain if he has such kind-looking eyes?’

A villain who revealed himself like this was less frightening. Truly frightening villains were those who showed no sign of it at all.

But soon, the man returned to that amiable expression.

“Come to think of it, we have not even exchanged names. I am Seo Daeryong. Forgive my late greeting.”

At that, the man introduced himself.

“I am called Jagyeong.”

Jagyeong, are you my destined adversary?

“You said earlier that you were a demonic practitioner. What is your affiliation?”

Just what identity would he claim?

Jagyeong answered Seo Daeryong’s curiosity with astonishment.

“I am the disciple of the Saber Demon of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

“!”

Then he shamelessly asked Seo Daeryong,

“And what is your affiliation?”

Seo Daeryong stared at him in shock.

I knew he had learned the Heaven-Destroying Demon Saber Form, but he is the Saber Demon’s disciple? Which Saber Demon? Surely not Master’s disciple? Could he be a disciple Master accepted in the past? Seo Daeryong’s mind grew complicated.

“I too am the Saber Demon’s disciple.”

Seo Daeryong answered honestly. There was no need to bother lying to someone who had come seeking him out while already knowing.

Jagyeong smiled strangely and said,

“I never heard that he had a disciple like you.”

“The same goes for me.”

Srrrk.

The great saber on Jagyeong’s back moved on its own and slipped into his hand.

“Then one of the two of us must be a fake.”

Seo Daeryong was overwhelmed by the other man’s presence as he drew his saber. He himself could not draw his in such a stylish manner with telekinetic retrieval.

Instead of lifting his saber, Seo Daeryong quickly said,

“If we meet Master, will we not be able to tell who is real and who is fake?”

At the very least, he was certain the other man was not trying to kill him. If he had been, he would have attacked from the start. But as though that thought were a grave delusion—

Shwaaaaaak!

A streak of fierce saber energy flew toward Seo Daeryong.

Seo Daeryong threw himself aside and dodged the saber energy.

‘If I hadn’t dodged, I would be dead.’

When Seo Daeryong rolled across the ground and rose, the great saber was already in his hand.

‘Is he insane?’

They had been talking amiably with smiles, and then he had suddenly started a fight.

What was more, that killing intent had been sincere.

So he wanted to fight here? If a fight broke out, everyone would rush out.

He was an opponent whose thoughts could not be grasped at all. If he had wanted to kill Seo Daeryong, it would have been better to launch a secret ambush from the start.

“If the two of us fight here, everyone will gather.”

“I will kill the fake, and the spectators too.”

Shwaaaaaak!

Once again, Jagyeong’s saber energy came surging in.

This time, Seo Daeryong did not dodge and instead unleashed saber energy of his own. Since the other man had not used the Heaven-Destroying Demon Saber Form, Seo Daeryong also simply released saber energy and met the attack.

The two streaks of saber energy collided in midair.

Boom!

At the very least, Seo Daeryong was not being pushed back in terms of internal energy.

As the two of them began fighting, warriors gathered there.

At the news that a fight had broken out, even the leaders of each sect who had been waiting in the main hall all rushed over.

“You two, stop fighting!”

When they appeared, Jagyeong did not attack. Judging by his momentum just moments ago, it had seemed he would launch saber energy in earnest at everyone gathered here.

Jagyeong’s expression was as calm as though nothing had happened.

‘You said you would kill the fake and the spectators too, did you not?’

Then something Seo Daeryong had never imagined happened. Jagyeong said this to everyone:

“The man who committed the bloody massacre of the Daejeong Sect is this man right here.”

Everyone’s gaze turned toward Seo Daeryong, whom Jagyeong was looking at.

“This man is Seo Daeryong, disciple of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.”

Seo Daeryong looked at Jagyeong in astonishment. He had truly never imagined that the man would reveal his identity to the orthodox sect members and frame him.

‘What in the world are you doing?’

Did he think this would work? In the end, if they examined the rights and wrongs of the matter, it would be revealed that he too had learned demonic arts, would it not?

Even so, Jagyeong’s exposure continued.

“That man’s saber is the beloved weapon the Blood Heaven Saber Demon used in his youth. The characters Peerless on the blade prove it.”

The experts around them drew their weapons in unison and surrounded Seo Daeryong.

“He said he came here today to kill every expert present!”

Seo Daeryong was dumbfounded. The man was saying the very words Seo Daeryong should have said.

Seo Daeryong said to everyone,

“That is a lie! This man is the murderer! The one who committed the tragedy of the Daejeong Sect is him.”

At that, an aged expert among those surrounding him stepped forward. He was the master of Flying Sword Manor, who had attended today’s meeting.

“Are you indeed the disciple of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon?”

Seo Daeryong had already been imprisoned in the Chongqing Branch and then released. He could not hide his identity.

“That is correct. But the claim that I annihilated the Daejeong Sect is a lie. The one who committed that deed is that man. He also said he would eliminate all of you today.”

At that, the people standing around him all sneered at once. Some shook their heads, and others emitted direct killing intent toward Seo Daeryong.

‘What is this? Why are they acting like this?’

The master of Flying Sword Manor looked at Seo Daeryong on everyone’s behalf with an expression of disbelief.

“Do you even know who this man is?”

The Flying Sword Manor master’s next words were shocking.

“This young hero here is the Azure Cloud New Hero.”

Seo Daeryong looked at Jagyeong in astonishment.

The Azure Cloud New Hero.

He too had heard of him.

He was a young hero who had recently risen to prominence in the orthodox murim.

A rising chivalrous hero who had killed many villains and saved countless lives through various righteous deeds. Ah, come to think of it, he had also heard that the man was a master of saber arts.

‘This man has been posing as a chivalrous hero!’

Seo Daeryong understood. This affair related to his master was a conspiracy that had been prepared for a very long time.

And today’s gathering was clearly a trap meant to ensnare him.

‘I was not the one buying time!’

Jagyeong had been stalling while waiting for as many experts attending the meeting as possible to gather.

Moreover, the reason he had earlier told Seo Daeryong he would kill them all was so that Seo Daeryong would reveal it here.

The Azure Cloud New Hero tried to massacre orthodox experts? The Azure Cloud New Hero was a demonic practitioner who had learned demonic arts?

The moment he said something no one would believe, his credibility had already collapsed.

Jagyeong shouted to the assembled heroes,

“Let us join forces and eliminate this demonic practitioner!”

At the same time, he sent a voice transmission to Seo Daeryong.

—If you want to survive here, you will have to use the Heaven-Destroying Demon Saber Form!

Absolute Regression, Chapter 510

Chapter 510: The Problem Is That They Didn't Come
-------------------------------------------------
Where many people gather, there are bound to be those whose bodies move faster than their thoughts.

Shwiiiick!

A young martial artist charged forward, slashing with his sword.

As he moved, not to be outdone, another martial artist leaped after him.

Seo Daeryong calmly swung his great blade to block their attacks.

The onlookers were startled. He had moved that massive great blade as swiftly as a sword to deflect the attacks.

The first strike he simply parried, but for the second defense, he imbued his blade with vigorous internal energy.

Klang! Kang!

The two men’s grips tore open, and they lost their swords.

Having sent his opponents’ swords flying, Seo Daeryong shouldered his great blade and shouted,

“Calm yourselves and hear me out first!”

The martial artists who had lost their swords retrieved them and stepped back. Whether out of humiliation, the two glared at Seo Daeryong with terrifying eyes.

Dozens of sword blades gleamed, pointed at him, yet Seo Daeryong remained calm.

Part of it was because his martial prowess had greatly improved, but there was a greater reason than that.

He had not come here alone today.

No matter how deep a trap he was buried in, there was someone who would dig through the dirt to pull him out… No, where on earth was he!

If Geommugeuk was not here, he would have to get through this situation himself.

‘I must absolutely not unleash the Myeolcheonmado Form!’

If the Myeolcheonmado Form was unleashed, someone would definitely die or be gravely injured. Then this place would become a hell of killing and death, exactly as that bastard wanted.

‘I will never dance to your tune.’

Jagyeong was staring at him with a strange smile, as if thinking something. If that bastard charged in directly, it would become an uncontrollable fight.

‘Why isn’t he attacking?’

Judging by the methods he had used to corner him earlier, Jagyeong was not the type to miss an opportunity while enjoying the situation.

‘What the hell is he scheming?’

He was a man whose inner thoughts were utterly inscrutable. In any case, for now, he had to calm these excited and enraged martial artists.

‘What would the Young Cult Leader have done in this situation?’

He had to speak like Geommugeuk and act like Geommugeuk. As his right-hand man, he had to fully demonstrate everything he had seen and learned until now.

Seo Daeryong spoke to the martial artists surrounding him in a calm tone.

“I only found out afterward that that man is Cheongun Shinhyeop.”

After revealing that he had not known the man’s identity, he displayed his own standing once more.

“As I said before, I am the head disciple of Hyeolcheondoma, the one who will succeed the position of the Blade Demon.”

Whether he could truly become the next Blade Demon remained to be seen, but he deliberately mentioned being the successor. It was to prevent the opposition from acting rashly, even slightly.

Even so, the killing intent at the tips of the pointed swords did not soften. The moment they misunderstood that he had framed Cheongun Shinhyeop, he had become a murderer in their eyes.

Seo Daeryong sent a telepathic message to Jagyeong, provoking him.

—Does your master know? That his disciple goes around pretending to be a righteous hero.

—My master knows everything that is happening here today.

At least, those words sounded like the truth.

‘Is it really the Master?’

If his master had been Geommugeuk, he would never have believed Jagyeong’s words.

But the current master?

It wasn’t that he couldn’t trust his master, but that he didn’t know his master well yet. He had no way of knowing whether his master had truly taken in such a disciple in the past.

Jagyeong began to incite the gathered heroes once more.

“Whether you are a disciple of the Demonic Lord is unimportant. What matters now is that you are the culprit who annihilated Daejeongmun. No matter how much you try to intimidate us with the Demonic Cult at your back, you cannot break the great chivalrous spirit of us righteous faction members.”

A war of words unfolded between the two men with the martial artists between them.

“You are the one using the righteous faction as your backing to trap me. Evidence that I annihilated Daejeongmun! Produce that evidence first.”

Seo Daeryong spoke proudly, refusing to back down. The moment he lost in a battle of words, a real fight would break out. Then there would be no turning back.

“I am the evidence. Because I heard those words directly from you.”

The fact that Cheongun Shinhyeop had heard it directly was more credible than anything else. He would have no reason to lie to them.

Seo Daeryong’s rebuttal followed immediately.

“Do you believe I would say such a thing to someone I met for the first time today? I didn’t even know that man was Cheongun Shinhyeop.”

But there was no one who believed those words.

“Stop wasting time and kill him now!”

“Let’s attack together!”

“Filthy Demonic Cult bastards!”

As one person shouted, others shouted in succession.

But no one moved rashly. Among those gathered here were the leaders of sects invited here today. They were cautious. Rather than ordering an attack, they sent telepathic messages telling their people not to step forward first.

It wasn’t because they believed Seo Daeryong, but because experience had taught them that in fights like these, those who stepped out first suffered the greatest damage.

Moreover, there was no need to be the protagonist of “who attacked first?”

Jagyeong began to press Seo Daeryong again.

“Why is a demonic cultist like you here?”

“I judged that someone would target this place today.”

“Do you have evidence to support that judgment?”

“I cannot show it because all the documents were burned.”

Jagyeong openly sneered.

“Of course they were.”

It was right when the surrounding heroes were making similar expressions.

“I can tell you instead.”

Seo Daeryong quickly summarized why this place, the Bongsan Sword Gate, had become the target. He mentioned several sects from the documents he had received and explained why this place was chosen.

He even added Geommugeuk’s analysis. The analysis that because relations with Daejeongmun, which had previously been annihilated, were not good, they would target this place.

In this situation, Jagyeong could have cut off the explanation, asking what nonsense he was spouting, and incited an attack, but he remained silent and listened.

Because he remained still, the other martial artists also listened together. He was the one leading this situation right now.

Thus Seo Daeryong revealed his reasoning. The martial artists exchanged glances. It was a plausible analysis.

As the atmosphere shifted, this time Seo Daeryong pressed Jagyeong.

“Were you invited to today’s gathering?”

“I was not.”

“Then why did you come?”

The gazes of the righteous faction martial artists turned toward Jagyeong. They too were curious as to why Cheongun Shinhyeop had come to today’s gathering.

“I too obtained evidence that the Demonic Cult was targeting today’s gathering.”

“Can you show that evidence?”

“If I reveal the person who gave me the information, they will be targeted for revenge by your kind.”

When Jagyeong refused flatly, Seo Daeryong added a remark.

“Or perhaps such a person never existed in the first place.”

Some martial artists sent Jagyeong gazes that seemed to say: Reveal it clearly. After all, that man cannot escape from here today, can he?

But Jagyeong pressed Seo Daeryong in a different way.

“The martial art used to kill the Daejeongmun Sect Leader was the Myeolcheonmado Form that you have mastered. Now, how do you explain this?”

This was the greatest weakness in this matter.

“Surely you aren’t going to say that the Myeolcheonmado Form is a martial art that just anyone can learn?”

Jagyeong blocked even his retreat.

Seo Daeryong was at a loss for words at this question alone. Because it was something even he did not know.

Having seized the momentum, Jagyeong incited the heroes once more.

“Do you remember how this man tried to pin his crime on me earlier? Does that alone not show you what kind of person he is?”

Seo Daeryong was at a loss. He knew that in the current situation, even if he claimed that bastard was the real culprit, it would only backfire.

“The Daejeongmun Sect Leader must be unable to close his eyes even in death. Let us all avenge the Daejeongmun Sect Leader!”

Jagyeong raised his great blade high with a flash.

At that, the martial artists simultaneously released their qi, revealing their killing intent.

Jagyeong sent a telepathic message to Seo Daeryong, laughter contained in his eyes.

—Kill all these righteous faction bastards! Isn’t that why I’m helping you? Kill them all with the Myeolcheonmado Form!

The martial artists slowly approached with their swords pointed. The leaders, including Bigeomjangju, could not stay out of this fight either. Because becoming branded a coward was as fatal as suffering great damage by taking the lead.

Seo Daeryong stepped back.

‘If I don’t fight, I die.’

As that bastard said, to survive he had to unleash the Myeolcheonmado Form.

It was truly a hair-trigger situation!

Right then, someone’s voice was heard.

“Then the Daejeongmun Sect Leader will weep blood from his unclosing eyes.”

Everyone’s focus concentrated on the newly appeared person among the heroes. He was none other than Geommugeuk, whom Seo Daeryong had been waiting for all this time.

‘Young Cult Leader!’

Seo Daeryong felt as though a ray of light was descending upon him in the darkness. It was like someone reaching out their hand to him while dirt poured down from inside a pit.

Had it not been for the onlookers, he truly would have run straight to Geommugeuk.

‘I nearly died from the tension! You were watching on purpose, weren’t you? Making me suffer! Just so you could appear at the critical moment!’

Of course, thanks to that, he had gained precious experience. When would he ever have another chance to persuade others to clear his name while righteous faction martial artists’ swords were pointed at him?

Geommugeuk slowly walked forward and spoke.

“The Daejeongmun Sect Leader was a man who lived his entire life upholding chivalry. If you pin the false charge of killing him on an unrelated person and kill that person, do you think he would approve? That is what would truly make the Daejeongmun Sect Leader unable to close his eyes in death.”

Geommugeuk’s voice was low and calm, but it rang clearly in everyone’s ears.

Bigeomjangju asked Geommugeuk.

“Who are you?”

Geommugeuk did not hide his identity.

“I am Geommugeuk, the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult.”

Everyone was startled, their eyes widening wide. Who would have known the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult would appear here?

“Someone is sowing discord between our cult and you all, so I came to Jungkyeong myself to resolve this matter.”

Even though his opponent was the Young Cult Leader, Jagyeong showed no fear at all.

“Rather than resolving it, aren’t you the one carrying out this affair personally?”

Geommugeuk could have avoided unnecessary aura contests with Jagyeong or the martial artists here.

He could have released his qi to overwhelm them with crushing force. Crudely provoking the opponent would lead to a fight, but overwhelming qi would make them lose even their will to fight.

It was certainly an effective method, but Geommugeuk did not choose to do so.

It was precisely because of Jagyeong. There was no surprise or fear in Jagyeong’s eyes as he looked at him.

‘He knew I was here.’

That meant he had known Geommugeuk would appear before anything happened to Seo Daeryong. Then he would have also known there was no way Seo Daeryong would kill the righteous faction martial artists.

‘Don’t tell me… Is he trying to provoke me into killing these people instead of Seo Daeryong?’

It was because such doubt arose.

In any case, regardless of the bastard’s intentions, he had to calm the excited martial artists first.

Keeping his eyes on Seo Daeryong, Geommugeuk spoke to the gathered heroes.

“That man is not only the disciple of Hyeolcheondoma but also the pavilion lord of our cult’s Hwangcheon Pavilion.”

Geommugeuk began his persuasion by shifting Seo Daeryong’s status.

“In other words, he is someone appointed by the Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult.”

Just when they had been thinking of him only as a disciple of the Demonic Lord, the mention of the Cult Leader made everyone tense.

“Do you know what kind of place Hwangcheon Pavilion is? It is the place responsible for our cult’s discipline. It is the strictest among our cult’s countless organizations. That man is precisely the person in charge of such a place.”

Geommugeuk slowly walked toward Jagyeong.
