As the two men grew closer, a suffocating tension hung in the air. But Geommugeuk and Jagyeong themselves were both composed.

‘Why have you come here? You must know you could die by my hand.’

Even though he must have read the warning in Geommugeuk’s eyes, Jagyeong was instead smiling.

Stopping at a point slightly away from Jagyeong, Geommugeuk spoke calmly.

“You said that man told you he annihilated Daejeongmun? That an extraordinary person who became the youngest pavilion lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion said such a thing to you, whom he met for the first time today? Just how stupid do you think the people here are to pull such a trick?”

He had deliberately used the word “stupid.” If they were swayed by those words, it meant they were stupid.

“It is the same with leaving the Myeolcheonmado Form at the annihilation site. He has no intention of starting a war with you, yet the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord left his own martial art at the massacre site? If he had, the Cult Leader would not let it slide. Do you think that clever man would commit such a reckless act?”

Among the listening martial artists, some nodded slightly. They too had been suspecting that someone was sowing discord.

But when he heard that the Demon Venerable’s martial arts had been used, he had assumed it was the Demonic Cult’s doing. Hearing Geom Mugeuk say this directly made him consider that someone might have been sowing discord.

“Moreover, the Pavilion Lord of Hwangcheon was investigated by the Murim Alliance’s Chongqing Branch over this matter not long ago and then released. If the Pavilion Lord of Hwangcheon were guilty, would the Murim Alliance have let him go? If that is what you believe, you should be going to the Murim Alliance to argue, not here.”

Not a single word of it was wrong, so no one could offer any rebuttal.

Seo Daeryong, who had been listening, felt admiration and self-reproach at the same time.

‘Ah, I should have spoken like that.’

Just then, one man stepped out from among the gathered heroes. He was Bang Cheongmun, the sect master of the Bongsan Sword Sect.

“Sheathe your swords.”

At his command, everyone put away their swords without complaint. In any case, it was not as though they could fight the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult.

“I have heard the Young Cult Leader’s words well. Both the Young Cult Leader and the Divine Hero said you came after hearing that there might be bloodshed at our sect, but fortunately, nothing has happened.”

Bang Cheongmun regarded Geom Mugeuk with gentle eyes, but he was still half in doubt. Earlier, Geom Mugeuk could have killed him and yet had not, but even that could have been part of a scheme.

In any case, he had made a promise to Geom Mugeuk in the sect master’s hall. At least for today, he would not clash with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

At that moment, Jagyeong stepped forward and respectfully cupped his fist to Bang Cheongmun.

“Judging it to be an urgent situation, I came to see you like this without being able to contact you in advance. I apologize.”

“Not at all. You have no idea how reassuring it is that you came.”

Watching this, Geom Mugeuk felt a strange sense of incongruity.

No fight had broken out between Seo Daeryong and the righteous faction members, and the same was true for him. Yet that man did not seem disappointed in the least.

—His purpose is not to make you fight the righteous faction members.

Seo Daeryong was startled by Geom Mugeuk’s voice transmission.

—It’s not? Then why did he go to such lengths?

Standing among the righteous faction members, Jagyeong looked at Geom Mugeuk with the same open, amiable smile he had worn when he first saw Seo Daeryong.

You won’t be able to kill me while I’m among the righteous faction members, will you? It was not a smile born of such a thought. Then what was that smile?

—At any rate, he really is a bold bastard. If he was aiming for me, he must have expected the Young Cult Leader to come as well, yet he came here alone?

The instant he heard that voice transmission, Geom Mugeuk realized something.

—The problem is not that he came here.

—Then what is the problem?

—The problem is who didn’t come.

—Pardon?

—Why did the elder not come here?

He must certainly have known that an incident would take place here. If so, he surely would have come and tried to stop it somehow.

In that instant, Geom Mugeuk’s gaze deepened. He had realized what kind of smile Jagyeong’s was. It was fine if he managed to set them against each other, and it did not matter if he failed. The man’s objective lay elsewhere.

—That man’s purpose is to keep us tied down here.

—Why on earth?

The reason was simple.

—Because this is not where the real conspiracy is taking place.

Chapter 511: That’s Why Your Martial Arts Will Be Strong
--------------------------------------------------------
—The elder went there.

Geom Mugeuk thought of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. Of the sight of him walking alone with a great saber on his back.

He had believed he could deal with Jagyeong well enough and had gone to settle matters there.

—Master may be in danger.

Worry bloomed across Seo Daeryong’s face.

—How do we find out where that place is?

At Seo Daeryong’s question, Geom Mugeuk’s gaze naturally turned toward Jagyeong.

Jagyeong, too, glanced over while speaking with Bang Cheongmun, the master of the Bongsan Sword Sect.

Jagyeong smiled affably. In that expression, Geom Mugeuk could tell his hunch had been right. Jagyeong considered this situation a success.

Geom Mugeuk also gave him a broad smile. The moment Seo Daeryong saw that smile, he knew. Geom Mugeuk had begun opening the gates of hell.

Geom Mugeuk asked Jagyeong,

“There is something I would like to ask our Divine Hero.”

“Speak.”

The orthodox martial artists nearby held their breath and listened to the two men’s conversation.

Geom Mugeuk asked without preamble,

“Where?”

A faint smile brushed Jagyeong’s lips. He knew what Geom Mugeuk was asking.

Of course, since everyone was watching, Jagyeong flatly feigned ignorance.

“What do you mean?”

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes sank coldly.

“There is something you don’t know about me.”

When Geom Mugeuk’s attitude toward Jagyeong changed, all the orthodox martial artists around them grew tense.

Geom Mugeuk paid them no mind and continued, looking only at Jagyeong.

“The reason you’re alive right now isn’t because of the orthodox people here. Nor is it because of the power behind you.”

Each word from Geom Mugeuk was low, yet colder than anything.

“The reason you’re alive is because of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. Because I don’t know exactly how he is entangled with you people. So I’ll ask. Where is Elder Blade Demon right now?”

Jagyeong looked straight into Geom Mugeuk’s eyes and answered,

“I have no idea what you are talking about.”

In this situation, surrounded by orthodox martial artists, he was certain Geom Mugeuk would never be able to attack him. His eyes said as much.

—So? With so many orthodox experts here, you intend to attack me? Then this affair will be confirmed as something you people did.

“What is the meaning of this?”

Geom Mugeuk could tell. This was not an opponent who would obediently answer just because he was asked, and he was highly skilled at acting innocent. Then again, if he were not such a man, how could he have concealed his identity as a demonic practitioner and lived so flawlessly as a wandering hero?

Geom Mugeuk said to Bang Cheongmun,

“Please step back for a moment with the others present.”

Bang Cheongmun was flustered by Geom Mugeuk’s sudden action. Judging from what he had just said to Jagyeong, it was certain that the Young Cult Leader and the Lord of the Yellow Springs Pavilion truly considered the Azure Cloud Divine Hero the culprit.

“What are you planning to do?”

“I will determine who the culprit is in front of you all.”

Jagyeong shouted to Bang Cheongmun,

“Do not fall for the Demon Cult’s scheme. Please help me.”

Even if the Azure Cloud Divine Hero had not asked for help, this was not a situation in which they could simply withdraw.

Just as Bang Cheongmun was about to answer that they could not do so—

Plash.

Bang Cheongmun flinched in shock. Before he knew it, he was floating in the sky.

‘What in the world?’

But when he looked again, it was not the sky. He was standing on water, and the water was so clear that it reflected the sky perfectly, making him mistake it for the heavens.

The moment he realized that,

Splash.

Bang Cheongmun sank into the water. He struggled and resisted, but it was no use.

The surface of the water drifted farther and farther away.

His breath gradually choked off. When the terror of suffocation was joined by the pitch-black darkness of the abyss, Bang Cheongmun could not endure it.

‘…I’m going to die like this!’

At that very moment, everything vanished.

Geom Mugeuk was looking at him in silence.

Bang Cheongmun understood. What he had just felt was Geom Mugeuk’s aura.

Gasps flowed from those around them. It seemed everyone had felt the same aura.

But each person had felt something different. The stronger their martial arts, the greater the fear they felt.

That beautiful sky, water, and abyss.

Could a person possess such an aura? And could he incapacitate someone with nothing but his aura?

Bang Cheongmun could not believe it. He had heard rumors that the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult was powerful, but he had not known he was this powerful.

‘If he decides to, we will… be annihilated.’

Bang Cheongmun stared at Geom Mugeuk in fear.

His eyes remained clear and deep.

Perhaps he was the same kind of person as the aura he had just shown. A deep abyss hidden within those clear eyes.

Unlike that terrifying aura, Geom Mugeuk spoke calmly.

“I promise on the name of the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. If I have judged wrongly, I will take responsibility.”

What if this was a treacherous scheme of the Demon Cult? To kill that Azure Cloud Divine Hero…

At that moment, Bang Cheongmun realized it. A man this powerful would not go this far to harm a mere wandering hero, not the leader of the Martial Alliance or the captain of the Demon Extermination Squad. He would not do this while staking even his own name.

Bang Cheongmun said to the leaders around him,

“Since the Young Cult Leader of the Divine Cult has gone this far in his words, let us withdraw for a moment.”

The leaders of the invited sects looked at one another and exchanged glances.

Some thought they should step forward. But after the overwhelming aura Geom Mugeuk had just shown, they did not dare.

Even if problems arose later… Bang Cheongmun, who had made this decision, would have to take responsibility.

After making them step back, Geom Mugeuk turned to Jagyeong.

“You were trying to corner the Lord of the Yellow Springs Pavilion and force him to use the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form, weren’t you?”

Jagyeong denied it to the end.

“Is this how you people do things? Do you frame and eliminate anyone who gets in your way?”

“Yes, this is how we do things. You and I. Our way.”

“I, the Azure Cloud Divine Hero, will never yield even to the Demon Cult’s threats! Come at me, Young Cult Leader!”

Those words were not meant to provoke Geom Mugeuk, but to provoke the orthodox martial artists.

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“I am not the one who will face you.”

Geom Mugeuk said to Seo Daeryong,

“Lord of the Yellow Springs Pavilion.”

“Yes, Young Cult Leader.”

“Face this man.”

Seo Daeryong was inwardly startled, for he had not imagined Geom Mugeuk would make him fight.

But what startled him even more was the next sound transmission.

—Kill him with the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form! Use the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form from the start.

Seo Daeryong was shocked.

‘You really want me to kill him?’

Whether he could kill him or not was uncertain, but what worried him even more was this.

‘If I kill him, how will we find out where Master is?’

But Seo Daeryong immediately shook off his worry. If Geom Mugeuk told him to kill, then he would kill. There had to be a reason he was doing this.

“I will face him.”

Seo Daeryong stepped forward.

“You intend to make me exhaust my inner energy, then have the Young Cult Leader step in at the end.”

He blocked Geom Mugeuk from stepping forward, and Geom Mugeuk played along with his intentions.

“Even if the Lord of the Yellow Springs Pavilion loses, I will not step forward. I promise this, too, on the name of the Young Cult Leader.”

When Geom Mugeuk acted this way, Jagyeong no longer provoked the orthodox martial artists. If not, he would have done whatever it took to incite them into stepping forward. He knew well how to stir the pride and honor of the orthodox faction.

But if it was that Lord of the Yellow Springs Pavilion? He could kill him. He knew his martial skill well.

‘I’ll kill you with my own hands.’

Thus, the two men faced one another.

—Did I not tell you? In this world, destined opponents exist.

At Jagyeong’s sound transmission, Seo Daeryong replied. Seeing that Geom Mugeuk had put him forward instead of killing Jagyeong himself—

—It seems that destined opponent really is me.

With that sound transmission, the two clashed.

From the very first move, Seo Daeryong immediately unleashed the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form.

Shwaaaaaak!

An attack of tremendous power flew in.

Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form, First Form:

Annihilating Blade Single Strike.

If it had been Seo Daeryong from a few days ago, Jagyeong might have been able to block the attack with the orthodox martial arts the Azure Cloud Divine Hero used.

But Seo Daeryong had greatly increased his inner energy by taking the Blue Jade Divine Pill, and he had also received Geom Mugeuk’s teachings through real combat sparring.

It was not an attack that could be blocked with ease.

Kwaaaang!

Two bursts of force collided in midair.

Jagyeong was shocked and flustered. Seo Daeryong was far stronger than he had thought.

‘He’s this strong?’

The problem was that the attack was so fast and powerful that he had no choice but to use the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form to block it. Otherwise, his arm would have been blown away.

The orthodox martial artists watching could not hide their astonishment at that unexpected power.

Because the exchange had happened in an instant, they had not yet clearly realized that the Azure Cloud Divine Hero had used the same martial art.

Only Bang Cheongmun, the most skilled in martial arts among them, tilted his head slightly.

Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form, Second Form.

Annihilating Blade Wave.

Blade qi stretched out like waves. He had blocked even the First Form with all his might; how could he block the Second Form?

This time as well, Jagyeong met it with Annihilating Blade Wave.

Kwaaaaang!

With an earsplitting explosion, a rough blade wind spread in all directions, forcing those watching to stagger backward.

At the second clash, Bang Cheongmun’s eyes flew wide open, and the other leaders also wore expressions that said something was strange.

And when the Third Form of the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form, Annihilating Blade Blood Wind, collided with its counterpart, everyone understood.

“It’s the same martial art!”

“The Divine Hero has learned the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form!”

Everyone was horrified by the fact that Jagyeong had learned the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form.

Only now did Seo Daeryong understand Geom Mugeuk’s intention. Only if he displayed the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form would everyone recognize that the martial art Jagyeong used was the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form as well.

It was possible because Geom Mugeuk had seen through both his and Jagyeong’s abilities with precision.

As a result, Seo Daeryong had done what Jagyeong had intended to do.

If you want to live, you’ll have to use the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form!

Having been exposed as someone who had learned the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Form, Jagyeong openly exuded demonic energy and charged at Seo Daeryong.

Seo Daeryong also swung his saber and faced him.

Kwaang!

The two great sabers collided with a thunderous roar. The impact traveled into both their hands.

Surprisingly, what Seo Daeryong felt was this.

‘I can endure it!’

If he could not, he would have dropped his saber.

Though he had begun learning martial arts in earnest late, he had trained harder than anyone.

—I have too little time to train.

In response to his complaint, Geom Mugeuk had once told him this.

—That’s why your martial arts will be even stronger.

—Pardon?

—Because the experience of living your life diligently during the day can sometimes surpass training where all you do is swing a saber from morning to night. Believe in yourself! Believe in your experience, and believe in your clever mind!

Originally, Jagyeong certainly possessed superior skill.

But he, too, had an unexpected weakness.

The fact that he had used orthodox martial arts for a long time instead of demonic arts was holding him back.

Seo Daeryong, on the other hand, was honed to a razor edge.

That was why the two fought a neck-and-neck battle.

Chwaaaaak! Ttaeng! Ttaeaeng!

Lethal attacks flew in one after another.

Each time the sabers that came down as if cleaving the sky clashed against each other, sparks flew.

The ground was carved deep, and the rocks that had decorated the garden shattered and scattered.

Seo Daeryong was in a trance.

He did not even know that he was fighting a battle that was making the orthodox martial artists watching gape in astonishment. The training he had done by carving out time and cutting down on sleep was guiding him.

And at the decisive moment, his body moved in the way Geom Mugeuk had taught him.

He used his footwork, twisted his body, and lightly brought his saber down as if throwing it.

Shwaaaaaak!

Puaaak!

For an instant, both men’s movements stopped.

The one who spat a bowlful of blood from his mouth was Jagyeong.

Seo Daeryong’s saber had passed through his shoulder and embedded itself deep in his torso.

Seo Daeryong’s hand trembled. It was the first time he had fought a master of equal skill and killed his opponent.

When he had learned it, he had only thought, So there are techniques like this too, but in actual combat, that single move was truly a supreme technique that decided victory and defeat.

‘You taught me such an incredible technique so casually?’

Jagyeong looked up at Seo Daeryong and spoke with a vicious expression.

“You… you bastard.”

His face showed that he could not believe he had lost to Seo Daeryong. Not to the Young Cult Leader, but to this scrawny bastard?

Thinking that even getting angry was shameful, he forced a smile at Seo Daeryong.

“…If you kill me, you will never find where the Blood Heaven Blade Demon is.”

The moment Seo Daeryong heard him call him the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, he felt relieved.

‘This man is not Master’s disciple!’

Jagyeong had tried to survive by bringing out Hyeolcheon Doma if nothing else, but it did not work on Seo Daeryong.

“Since you told me to kill him, I’m sure you know what you’re doing.”

The moment those words ended, Seo Daeryong brought his great blade straight down.

Schwick!

Swhaaaack!

Jagyeong’s body split in half and spilled to either side.

The smell of blood wafted up thickly, and all the orthodox martial artists watched with shocked faces.

They did not even enter Seo Daeryong’s eyes.

The person he sought was Geom Mugeuk. Geom Mugeuk was looking at him with a smile.

—Impressive, my right arm.

At those words, Seo Daeryong’s chest welled up with emotion. He was so moved that he felt he could brag about this fight all night long—no, for the rest of his life whenever he drank. Seo Daeryong, whose list of bragging rights kept growing, had grown once more today.

Bang Cheongmun spoke to Geom Mugeuk.

“What the Young Cult Leader said was correct. The culprit was that man.”

He was convinced that the culprit was Jagyeong.

That a man renowned as a righteous hero of the orthodox faction was versed in the Myeolcheon Madosik? It was something that could never be unless he was one plotting a grand conspiracy.

Through today’s events, the truth would become known to the orthodox murim. That this matter was a conspiracy planned to drive a wedge between the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the orthodox faction.

Geom Mugeuk bade farewell to Bang Cheongmun and the orthodox martial artists.

“Thank you for believing me.”

In truth, they had believed him only because his overwhelming strength left them no other choice, but they were grateful that he said so.

“If you need help, speak anytime. We shall assist as well.”

“I shall. Then we shall take our leave for today.”

Geom Mugeuk and Seo Daeryong hurriedly left that place.

“You have some idea where Master might be, don’t you?”

“No.”

Seo Daeryong was startled by Geom Mugeuk’s answer.

“Then why did you tell me to kill him?”

“He was not someone who would have talked anyway. He would have only wasted time.”

Seo Daeryong agreed on that point.

“I still have a long way to go. Seeing how restless I am. You aren’t worried at all, Young Cult Leader?”

He had thought the Young Cult Leader was so relaxed because he believed in Hyeolcheon Doma.

“If you mean the Elder, I am worried. With that stubborn temperament of his, trying not to cause trouble for others—how could I not be worried?”

Then Seo Daeryong revealed his true feelings as well.

“Right? I feel like I’m going to die from worry. But why are you so relaxed?”

Because he had something he believed in.

“Even if I don’t believe in your master, I believe in my father.”

Geom Mugeuk believed in his father, and he believed in the relationship between his father and Hyeolcheon Doma.

When he had first met Hyeolcheon Doma after his regression, Hyeolcheon Doma had possessed the conviction that even if he killed the Young Cult Leader, his father would forgive him. Geom Mugeuk believed in the relationship between those two.

“Since this matter is related to the past between you two, Father will not simply stand by. He is not a man who would ever leave your affairs to another.”

* * *

Hyeolcheon Doma opened the main gate and entered.

The training ground, which would normally have been bustling with people, was completely empty.

Hyeolcheon Doma crossed the desolate training ground and entered the main hall.

A person was sitting in the main seat. Hyeolcheon Doma slowly walked toward him.

The person sitting in the main seat opened his mouth.

“Why do you not ask? Whether I have killed everyone who was here?”

His voice echoed through the main hall, hoarse as boiling phlegm, deep and desolate as the cracked, dry land of the frontier. Yet that voice clawed at people’s hearts like a hook, fierce and relentless.

“Because I know you have not killed them yet.”

Hyeolcheon Doma’s tone was calm yet respectful.

“Why is that?”

“This matter’s purpose was never to kill them, was it? From the beginning, the purpose was to draw me and the Cult Leader in.”

Hyeolcheon Doma, having arrived below the main seat, looked up.

An old man sat upon the main seat atop the stairs.

The dense wrinkles made it impossible to even guess his age. But the light radiating from his two eyes was as powerful as facing the sun.

Hyeolcheon Doma’s eyes trembled as he gazed at the old man.

“It has been a long time, Master.”

Chapter 512: Because of Feelings for Someone
--------------------------------------------
The old man was Qiao Tian, the master of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the Saber Demon of the previous generation.

A master he had believed dead. Truly, he had never once imagined the man might still be alive.

He had thought him someone he would only see after going to hell. Someone who no longer even appeared in his dreams.

When he received his master’s message telling him to come to Chongqing, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon was genuinely shocked.

If the message had not contained something known only to his master and himself, he would never have believed his master was alive.

“I did not know you were alive.”

At the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s words, Qiao Tian answered in a low voice.

“If you had, you would have come to kill me.”

Apart from the senility that could be felt in his voice, the voice itself struck sharply into the ears and scraped at a person’s heart.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon looked up at him. The shape of his eyes and his jaw still bore traces of his old face. But fine wrinkles had swallowed his appearance whole, and he thought that if they had passed each other on the street, he might not have recognized him.

Qiao Tian also looked down at his disciple. The Blood Heaven Saber Demon of his prime was nowhere to be found now.

“You have aged a great deal as well.”

“That much time has passed.”

Standing before his master, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon felt as though he had returned to those days long ago.

“Are you unwell anywhere?”

A strange radiance flowed from the two eyes buried among the fine wrinkles.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon could feel that his master’s martial realm had risen far higher than when he had once been a Demon Venerable. Of course, his body had grown old and feeble, but he could tell from those eyes alone that his inner power had become indescribably profound.

‘Could it be that he’s confident he can defeat the Cult Leader?’

Even if he had built upon his original strength with the power of time, could he truly withstand the Cult Leader’s Nine Flames Demonic Art? That supreme demonic art, the Nine Flames Demonic Art?

“If I am? Will you come and massage me?”

It was a curt response, but the Blood Heaven Saber Demon knew.

He had not even begun yet. He could not imagine what it would look like when the emotions he had stored away for all those long years began to burst forth.

“If you wish it, I will massage you.”

At the respectful answer, Qiao Tian beckoned him closer. The gesture carried the intent: Do you dare come near me?

Astonishingly, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon began walking toward him. Qiao Tian did not tell him to stop, and the Blood Heaven Saber Demon did not stop walking either.

The closer the two men grew, the greater the taut tension became.

Thirty paces, twenty paces, ten paces….

When he had approached to within ten steps, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon stopped.

Surely his master had not come alone, yet even after he had come this close, he could not sense anyone else’s aura nearby.

If someone was there, it meant they were that much of an expert; if his master had come alone, that in itself was astonishing.

“Why do you not come closer? Are you afraid of me?”

“I am afraid.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon answered readily. And the reason was unexpected.

“Because if it were me, I would not have endured this long. I would not have lasted even ten years before coming out for revenge.”

But his master had endured, and endured again, for more than thirty years. All for this very moment.

“Why have you returned?”

Then came his master’s low words.

“Because I am alive.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon let out a long sigh.

“If you survived, you should have simply lived.”

It was not mockery. He was speaking sincerely.

“You should have enjoyed your old age somewhere with good mountains and good waters. If that bored you, you could have become a hidden power with no connection to our cult and pursued authority and wealth.”

Then an unexpected answer came from his master.

“I tried. Both of them.”

For a moment, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon flinched.

“To forget. To forget it all, I went into the mountains, and I tried all manner of things. But I could not forget what happened that day.”

His voice was hoarse and cracked, but his emotions could be felt in every word.

“I realized that revenge was the only meaning left in my life.”

Knowing that the flame burning in his master’s heart could not be extinguished by anything, a heavy sigh escaped the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s lips.

“So you intend to borrow the strength of the righteous sects? You will start a war and kill the Cult Leader? Do you think that will kill him?”

“Wouldn’t those righteous bastards do everything they could to cut off the Demonic Cult Leader’s head?”

Was that truly his real intention? The Blood Heaven Saber Demon did not think so. He could not have spent all those long years merely for a scheme to sow discord with the righteous sects.

There was certainly something his master believed in. Something meant to kill the Cult Leader and himself. Something that would mark the end of his long revenge.

‘What on earth have you brought out?’

The one who broke the silence that briefly flowed between them was Qiao Tian.

“Are you sorry about that incident?”

Had his emotions surged as he recalled that day? A faint tremor could be felt in his hoarse, cracked voice.

“Do you regret it?”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon answered neither question, but Qiao Tian continued speaking.

“If you are sorry for what happened and regret it, then should you not help me now?”

At last, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon opened his mouth.

“How?”

Qiao Tian stared piercingly at the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

“Kill the Cult Leader with those hands.”

Then came an astonishing addition.

“With the very hands that killed me.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon silently looked down at his own hands. Though not as much as his master’s, the traces of deep years were now engraved layer by layer upon his hands as well.

As the wrinkles on those hands gradually faded and grew young, that day from long ago rose in the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s heart. That incident from long ago was as vivid as if it had happened yesterday.

“Master, did you call for me?”

The person who had run into the residence was the Blood Heaven Saber Demon in his youth. Having become the formal successor to the Saber Demon, he had received the Annihilating Heaven Demonic Saber Form and was in the midst of earnest training.

He was completely different from now. His face was rather handsome and even carried a hint of cuteness. And above all, he brimmed with the vitality of youth.

His master was seated in a taishi chair as he was today, looking down at him.

“I called you because I have something to tell you.”

“Please command me.”

Qiao Tian spoke to him as if making a declaration.

“From today onward, our Southern Saber Sect will officially support the First Young Master.”

At that moment, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s expression hardened.

“Go and inform all the Saber Ghosts of this fact.”

But instead of accepting the order, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon asked his master.

“Why the First Young Master?”

This time, Qiao Tian’s expression hardened.

“Because it is my decision.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon said something he should not have said.

“Please tell me a reason I can accept.”

He had never once disobeyed his master’s words before.

Even if he had to brace himself for a scolding, he had to hear the answer. That was how important this matter was to the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

Sure enough, Qiao Tian erupted in anger.

“Are you saying you will defy your master’s will?”

“No. That is not it.”

“Then why are you asking for a reason? Why are you still standing there?”

Qiao Tian’s fierce demonic energy blasted toward the Blood Heaven Saber Demon. Under the powerful demonic energy, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon raised his inner power and resisted, but naturally, it was not enough.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon gritted his teeth and endured the pain that felt as though his entire body would be torn apart. No matter how painful it was, he had to hear the answer.

Qiao Tian strode over and stood before the Blood Heaven Saber Demon. He already knew the reason.

“I know you are especially close to the Second Young Master. And I know that the cunning Second Young Master is trying to draw you in and use you.”

Even amid the pain, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon spoke his thoughts.

“The Second Young Master is not that kind of person.”

As his words were denied, Qiao Tian’s expression hardened even further, and the demonic energy became even stronger.

“Uuugh.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon felt as though his whole body would be ripped apart. If he endured even a little longer like this, he would suffer severe internal injuries.

Even so, Qiao Tian did not stop his demonic energy. He absolutely would not forgive the defiance of the disciple who would succeed him.

“Uuuuuugh!”

The demonic energy grew even stronger, and his scream grew louder.

If he continued to disobey, Qiao Tian intended to kill his disciple then and there. He could do it. There was a line of people waiting to become the next Saber Demon.

‘Dying like this would be a dog’s death.’

In the end, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon bent his will. If he had endured any longer, there was no doubt he would have suffered irreversible internal injuries or died.

“…I will follow Master’s will.”

Only then did the crushing demonic energy subside.

With an angry face, Qiao Tian ordered him to leave.

“Withdraw.”

As he turned weakly to go, Qiao Tian spoke from behind him.

“You asked me to give you a reason you could accept?”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon turned back to Qiao Tian.

“In this struggle for succession, the Second Young Master will certainly die.”

At that moment, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon felt it. The resolve that flashed through his master’s eyes.

‘Master intends to jump directly into this succession struggle.’

He felt that his master had resolved to kill the Second Young Master himself. For the sake of the First Young Master, he was a man who would stop at nothing.

“Do not stay close to the Second Young Master any longer. If you do, you will die with him.”

“I will keep that in mind.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon bowed respectfully, then began walking again.

When he left the residence, his fists were clenched tight.

As wrinkles formed upon that young fist, he returned to the present. The Blood Heaven Saber Demon of the present also had his fist clenched tightly, just as he had then.

After that day, he treated his master even better. He strove to gain even greater trust. Solely to find out when that time would come.

“I know. I know why Master chose the First Young Master rather than the Second.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon now spoke the words he had been unable to say then.

“It was not that the Second Young Master was cunning.”

After a brief pause, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon said,

“It was that the Second Young Master saw through Master’s cunning.”

Crack.

The armrest of the taishi chair shattered in Qiao Tian’s grip. The broken fragments turned to sand in Qiao Tian’s hand and scattered.

It was a move that showed just how profound Qiao Tian’s power was.

But the Blood Heaven Saber Demon continued speaking.

“From that young age, the Cult Leader was someone with clear likes and dislikes. If he disliked something, he disliked it; if he liked something, he liked it. Master knew. You knew that you were something the Cult Leader disliked.”

A beastly growl rumbled from Qiao Tian’s throat.

“You knew that if the Second Young Master became Cult Leader, you would be pushed to the bottom seat among the Eight Demon Venerables. That was something you could never endure.”

He had not known it then. It was better, perhaps, that he had not. If he had said these words in the hot blood of his youth back then, his master would likely have killed him on the spot.

“Your insolence and fearlessness have not changed from your youth to now.”

“It seems your memories have been distorted by your hatred of me. In my youth, I was more respectful to Master than anyone.”

Qiao Tian laughed. It was a laugh filled with both incredulity and deep hatred.

“You betrayed me. You betrayed me, who taught you martial arts!”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon said nothing.

“You call me cunning? Could I be as cunning as you? You knew it as well. You knew that if the First Young Master became Cult Leader, you could never become the next Saber Demon. The First Young Master would never leave alone someone like you, who had been so close to the Second Young Master.”

Yes, perhaps his master was right.

At the time, he had thought it was out of concern for the Cult Leader, but perhaps, deep in his subconscious, he had struggled to survive.

But at least his master had no right to say those words. Because he had given him no choice.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s gaze deepened, and he returned once more to a moment in the past.

The Cult Leader in his youth was standing before his eyes.

The Cult Leader from the days when he had been the Second Young Master, close enough with him to call each other brothers.

His atmosphere, too, was different from now. If nothing could be read from the Cult Leader’s eyes now, emotions could be read from his eyes in his youth.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon liked him. At least, it was not because he was someone who would become the next Heavenly Demon. He simply liked him. His actions, and his words. Though he was younger, there was much to learn from him.

He treated everyone with haughtiness and arrogance, but the Blood Heaven Saber Demon could feel that, in truth, he held something burning hot within his heart.

No, that reason alone was not enough.

He liked him enough to say these words to him, words that would change everyone’s fate from its very roots.

“The First Young Master and my master have joined hands and made a plan to kill you.”

Geom Ujin then had been different from now. Because the instant he heard those words, he clearly saw Geom Ujin’s pupils tremble.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon knew. He knew Geom Ujin wanted to end the succession struggle without killing his elder brother.

“The two of them will move during this hunt.”

Qiao Tian’s shout from then dragged the Blood Heaven Saber Demon back to reality.

“You betrayed your master, and made a younger brother kill his elder brother. That is the kind of man you are.”

If I had not, the Cult Leader would have died.

But he did not say those words to his master. Because the relationship between him and the Cult Leader would mean nothing to Qiao Tian. Because Qiao Tian would not believe in such a bond.

Yes, if he said he had betrayed him because of a pure liking for someone, it would instead wound his master. To Qiao Tian, it was better that he had betrayed him for the seat of Saber Demon.

And so the Blood Heaven Saber Demon nodded and readily admitted it.

“That is correct. I am that kind of man. Selfish and wicked, and so….”

With a sunken gaze, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon added in a low voice.

“Those who stand with me suffer great misfortune.”

Chapter 513: I’ll Have to Establish Discipline
----------------------------------------------
“If you do not kill the Eldest Young Master, you will die.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon still remembered it vividly. Geom Ujin’s answer when he said those words, and the look on his face.

“I don’t want to kill my brother.”

They were words one could never hear from the current Cult Leader. No one could even imagine the Cult Leader hesitating to kill someone.

Yes, even the Cult Leader had once had such days.

“I know.”

Because the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was someone who had felt the heat that dwelled in Geom Ujin’s heart.

Within the cult, Geom Ujin was more popular. Cold, composed, and proud as he was, all the demonic practitioners liked him. He was handsome, intelligent, and above all, possessed a natural talent for martial arts.

He had every quality demonic practitioners admired, and people said he was the greatest of all those who had ever inherited the Heavenly Demon’s bloodline.

The Eldest Young Master had joined hands with his master to kill Geom Ujin because he knew that if things continued as they were, he would have no chance of victory.

“Very well. Let us find a way to resolve this without killing the Eldest Young Master, no matter what.”

At that, Geom Ujin spoke firmly.

“Brother, stay out of it. This fight is mine.”

How could the Blood Heaven Blade Demon not know those words came from concern for him?

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon shook his head. Now that his master had become involved in this matter—

“The moment I told you of the Eldest Young Master and Master’s plan, this fight became mine as well.”

Their gazes tangled in the air. Eyes filled with a youthful fervor they could no longer even imitate.

“I want to live as a Demon Venerable of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult led by you.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon still remembered the tremor he had felt the moment he spoke those words.

He wanted to return to that time. To the youth of those days, hot as a midsummer night and refreshing as an autumn wind. To the days when he had risked his life without desiring anything, when he could read emotion on the Cult Leader’s face.

The one who dragged him back out of the past was Gyo Cheon.

“Meeting you was the greatest misfortune of my life.”

At the words of his master that reached his ears, the image of the young Geom Ujin vanished.

In its place stood the figure of his enraged master.

Even if his master had not said so, he had lived with that feeling.

After all, the words he had spoken to Geom Mugeuk—that his entire life had been accompanied by misfortune—had also come from his master.

Though it had been to save the Cult Leader, in the end he had never been able to escape the yoke of having betrayed his master.

“Do you know why I came after receiving your message, Master?”

“You came to kill me, I suppose.”

Despite the mocking reply, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon remained calm.

“If that had been my intention, I would have contacted the Cult Leader.”

But the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had quietly come alone to Chongqing.

Gyo Cheon looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon with eyes that asked for the reason.

And that reason was something Gyo Cheon had never imagined.

“I wanted to see you, Master.”

“!”

Gyo Cheon’s deep wrinkles twitched.

It was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s sincere feeling. Of course, it was not because of ordinary longing.

How should he express this desire to see him?

Was it like the feeling of his younger self extending a hand to him, now standing in the twilight of life?

Yes, it was not the master before his eyes who had called him, but his own self from his youth.

Gyo Cheon laughed. There were especially many wrinkles around his mouth, making it difficult to tell what he felt when he laughed. But the laugh now was clearly a sneer.

“You say such nonsense knowing why I came?”

The sneer soon turned into open laughter. Was it because of old age? Or had he contracted some illness? His cracked voice gave off the sound of scraping metal, and the laughter soon turned into a fit of coughing that would not stop.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon waited for him to stop coughing.

“You wanted to see me?”

Gyo Cheon slowly rose from his seat and loosened his robe.

His body was shriveled. It was a body that made one wonder whether he could truly perform martial arts with it. And for that reason, it was all the more frightening. When one thought of the tremendous power contained within that insignificant body.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s gaze went to the very center of his chest.

There was a large scar there. A scar far wider and deeper than one left by a sword thrust.

“Now, look.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon silently gazed at the wound.

And then he conveyed to him the words he had long held in his heart. Words he had never spoken to anyone, not even once.

“I am sorry, Master.”

“!”

He knew that even if it had not been him, his master had always been willing to take another disciple as his chief disciple. He knew that if he had continued to resist, his master would have killed him. Above all, it had been to save Geom Ujin.

Even so, he was sorry.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon bowed his head respectfully. The head he lowered only to Geom Ujin was lowered now.

As Gyo Cheon looked at him, a gleam burst from his eyes, and the fine wrinkles on his face deepened.

“You… were hypocritical even when you were young.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon understood his master. He was someone who would never believe that he had purely liked a man named Geom Ujin, and he was someone who would not believe his apology either.

But it did not matter. In the end, he had spoken those words for his own sake.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked up at the sky.

‘Thank you for allowing me to say the words I meant to convey only after going to hell.’

Gyo Cheon did not take the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s apology as sincere. And even if it were, what meaning would it have now?

“If you help me kill the Cult Leader, I will spare your life.”

“What lingering attachment could I possibly have to this old life?”

At that, Gyo Cheon threatened him with Seo Daeryong.

“If you do not listen to me, I will kill your disciple.”

“Is that why you called that child here as well?”

Of course, this threat did not work.

“Then he, too, will live a life without fortune in his master.”

In the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s gaze toward his master, there dwelled both love and hatred.

“You looked at me with those eyes in the past as well.”

“That too is a misunderstanding. Because you hate the present me, the past must all be distorted as well.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had a premonition. The end of this matter would be one of only two things: either he would die, or his master would. And so there was nothing he could not say. If what he had spoken earlier had been love, then what he would speak from now on was hatred.

“One might think that the older one grows, the wiser one becomes, but that was not so. Judgment grows clouded, and only stubbornness becomes stronger. I tried to prevent even a little of that by diligently reading books, but it was not easy to stop my mind from growing numb.”

After a brief pause, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon spoke.

“You are probably far worse, Master. If someone who did not know you saw you, they would think, How formidable must that old master be? He must possess tremendous wisdom. He must see through the hearts of people. How extraordinary must his insight be? They would think such things. But I know. Your mind must be filled with a hazy fog.”

Anger rose on Gyo Cheon’s twitching face.

“In that fog, only hatred must be shining. If even that were absent, Master, you would have lost your way.”

“Still putting on airs, I see.”

“You distort things again. I never once put on airs before you, Master. Then tell me. The Cult Leader, myself, and the events of that day. Aside from those, have you ever thought of anything else? Whether the history of the murim or matters of life, or at the very least, Ah, summer has gone, autumn is coming. Have you even had such interests?”

Gyo Cheon’s eyes grew colder, but he could not offer any rebuttal.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon continued to say what he wished to say.

“In the past, I would not have known either. But recently, through the Young Cult Leader, I realized something. Ah, I had been walking through the fog out of habit all this time. Because the path was so familiar, because the fog was so familiar, I merely walked without falling. Even though the fog had been growing thicker with every passing year.”

After meeting Geom Mugeuk, the stagnant waters of his heart had begun to flow. He trained in martial arts even more diligently, read more books, took in a disciple, and his relationships with the other Demon Venerables changed. He even tended a flower garden.

All of that had been an effort to clear away the fog. Though even with effort, it did not disperse easily.

“That is why I am not afraid of you, Master.”

And so he did not speak the other true feeling—that he did fear his master.

If Geom Mugeuk had met his master even a little earlier, could his master’s heart have changed?

He thought perhaps it might have. If it were Geom Mugeuk? Perhaps that clever fellow might know a way to unravel this deep grudge.

The fact that such thoughts came to him showed just how regretful he was. Even though his master had remained a wound deep within his heart.

This fate, in which they had met again like this and had to open the final chapter without any intervening process, was too regrettable.

“You insolent wretch!”

Gyo Cheon, on the other hand, was furious. How could his life be explained with such words?

Yet he could not think of anything to say in rebuttal. Surely countless words were circling in his mind.

But truly, as though it were filled with fog, he could not tell what he ought to say.

Within that place, only the emotion of hatred shone brightly.

“What if I said I would kill that great Young Cult Leader of yours?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon laughed. If you can kill him, then go ahead and try. You will likely collapse clutching the back of your neck while talking to him before that happens.

“That seems like a threat you should make to the Cult Leader, so save it.”

Threats did not work on the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“But just how do you intend to kill the Cult Leader? He is not someone who can be killed simply because one wishes to kill him.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was curious as to what move he had prepared.

“The Cult Leader is human too. All humans are bound to die.”

As he said that, Gyo Cheon took something from his robe.

It was a small box. Through Void-Seizing, it flew through the air and came to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

When he opened the box, what lay inside was a single small vial.

“Put that in the Cult Leader’s wine.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked down at the vial and asked.

“What is this?”

“You need not know. All you have to do is put it in the Cult Leader’s wine.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was truly dumbfounded. Did he think such a method would work? Was his master truly wandering in a fog?

“That is correct. He is someone who cannot be killed unless such methods are used. Sowing discord with the orthodox factions, threatening to kill a disciple, and now even poisoning him like this. But did you think I would do such a thing?”

“Did you not say you were sorry to me?”

As if there were no further value in speaking, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon tried to pour the contents of the vial onto the floor. Just as he was about to open the stopper, he flinched and stopped.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s gaze returned to Gyo Cheon.

“You must have expected that I would pour it out.”

An ominous thought flashed through the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s mind.

“Surely not?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon threw the vial far away.

Crash.

From the exposed liquid, poisonous smoke surged up at once. If he had poured it onto the floor, that smoke would have enveloped him in an instant.

“This was meant to kill me.”

Gyo Cheon’s eyes were filled with killing intent.

“I cannot let you die gently.”

From the way he spoke, the poison was no ordinary poison. It was a poison that inflicted extreme agony.

He had known that his master intended to kill him, but seeing him use poison so unhesitatingly like this left him feeling wounded. And such a vicious poison at that.

He realized anew that this was what human relationships were like—that even in a situation where they would kill and be killed by one another, one could still feel hurt.

“Do you hate me that much?”

“Did you not say it yourself? That only hatred remains inside my mind.”

Looking at his master, he truly felt as though he were staring at an impenetrable wall with whom words did not get through.

He had wanted to ease his heart somehow. And that desire had not been for his own sake, but for his master’s.

Of course, his master would not believe it. He would think he was scheming because he did not want to die.

‘Master, you do not give me a chance to choose this time either.’

Gyo Cheon slowly clapped his hands.

Then the door in the left wall opened, and someone walked out from within.

Clad in a blazing crimson robe, he was an old man of about the same age as the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, and he too wore a great saber. One could tell just from his steps. He had practiced the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Saber Form for many years.

It seemed likely that his master had taken him in as a new disciple and transmitted martial arts to him.

He was not the only one to appear. The door on the opposite side opened, and someone walked out from there as well.

This time it was an old man wearing a blue robe, and the skill he exuded was in no way inferior to that of the old man who had appeared first.

And the two of them possessed contrasting auras. If the first old man was fire, then this old man possessed an aura like water.

And the Blood Heaven Blade Demon could instinctively tell.

‘They are ones who have achieved mastery of the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Saber Form.’

They were truly experts with the strength of Demon Venerables. Since they were people his master had raised with vengeance, he could imagine their strength without even seeing it.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s eyes sank. He sensed that this would not be an easy fight.

Gyo Cheon looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and said,

“A betrayal suffered at the hands of a disciple should be repaid by disciples.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon did not lose his composure.

“It seems I will have to discipline my junior brothers.”

Gyo Cheon sneered and said,

“Who says they are your junior brothers? You were expelled from the sect long ago.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon answered calmly.

“Because I have not expelled you as my master.”

Gyo Cheon’s face twitched again. His master would misunderstand and grow angry, but it was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s tenderness toward him.

“It was sincere that I wanted to see you again, and my apology was sincere as well. My wish that you would simply withdraw even now and live quietly is also sincere. And this too is sincere.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon slowly drew the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber and said,

“For his sake, I can kill again.”

Chapter 514: We're Demon Venerables, So Why Ask for Help.
---------------------------------------------------------
“Shut your mouth!”

Gyo Cheon’s shout rang out thunderously.

Fwoooooosh.

The hem of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s robe snapped violently. It was a voice unlike any he had heard until now. His master’s bellow, laden with internal energy, crashed over him like a sudden gale.

Had he not drawn up his own inner power to resist it, he would have been blown backward and slammed down in an unsightly heap.

From that single shout, he could tell just how deep and weighty an internal force was hidden within his master’s aged body.

A fierce wind passed through.

One might have expected him to be cowed by that powerful internal energy, but instead, this time it was the Blood Heaven Saber Demon who roared.

“You shut your mouth, Master!”

Because he had treated his master with utmost courtesy until now, Gyo Cheon’s eyes went wide.

Now it was the Blood Heaven Saber Demon whose voice shook the entire hall.

“Why is your revenge so pathetic? Wiping out people who have nothing to do with this, trying to stir up a war through schemes and discord. Using poison. After all these long years, is this all your revenge amounts to?”

Crack.

The other armrest of the chair shattered. When he had broken the first one, it had crumbled like sand, but this time fragments rained down in a cascade. He was so furious that he had not even realized he had broken it.

Yet the Blood Heaven Saber Demon continued to raise his voice.

“You should have returned in glory. If you weren’t ready, you should never have come back!”

After shouting himself hoarse, he added one final sentence, his voice low and sorrowful.

“……Only then would I have gladly died for you.”

The gazes of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and Gyo Cheon tangled in midair.

Was it because of the grief and sadness contained in the eyes looking at him?

Gyo Cheon had believed even his disciple’s apology to be false, an act, but in this moment, that thought did not occur to him.

‘He came to find me prepared to die?’

But that thought, drawn out by sincerity, could not pass through the tightly sealed wall within him. After staring at his disciple for a moment, Gyo Cheon spoke to the two old men.

“Kill him. Kill him miserably and wretchedly!”

A smile of relief instead formed at the corner of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s mouth.

Yes. No matter what words passed between them, the ending of this meeting between master and disciple had been decided from the start.

The only difference was who would die.

If anything, he was grateful his master had come out like this. Now he could fight without lingering attachment.

“I shall obey your command, Master.”

The one who answered loudly was the Blood Heaven Saber Demon. It was a kind of jest and mockery, as though he had received the order to kill.

By contrast, the two old men showed no change in expression. His master must have raised them this way. Without any emotion, solely for this moment.

And those two could not be the only subordinates his master had. No one could know what might come out today from the pouch of resentment his master had carried for decades.

Wooooong!

The Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber, infused with inner power, let out one great cry. It was not a cry of fear. It was the trusting cry of a beloved weapon that had felt its master’s resolve.

‘Yes, I’m counting on you! Myeolcheon.’

The old man in the red long robe approached from the right, and the old man in the blue long robe from the left.

All three had reached great mastery in the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style.

At this level, the outcome would instead be decided in an instant. Moreover, they all knew one another’s martial arts far too well.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon imagined how his master must have trained them.

He would have trained them harshly. For this very moment. To kill him wretchedly not with some other martial art, but with the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style.

I will take back the martial art I passed down to you with that very same martial art—surely that was what he had intended.

‘In that case?’

The first to move was the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

The instant he kicked off the ground, he was already before the red-robed old man, swinging his saber.

Swaaaaaak!

The Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber flew in at a speed unbelievable for its size.

His opponent did not evade either, but swung his own great saber to block.

Just before saber met saber, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon twisted his blade. The saber’s angle shifted, sending up sparks as it slid along his opponent’s blade.

Whoooooom!

The Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber, having calculated even his opponent’s response, grazed the old man’s face.

The old man was inwardly greatly startled.

It was an unexpected variation. And because he had never imagined his opponent would use a variation aiming for his life on the very first move, his face had nearly been taken off.

It was a surprise move by the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, making use of the fact that masters of saber arts usually spent their first exchange testing one another with a clash of strength.

And with that momentum, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon did not let up and continued to press him.

He was not so weak that nearly being caught on the first move would throw his hands and feet into confusion, but the old man had clearly lost the initiative.

Thud! Thud-thud! Thud-thud-thud-thud!

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s attacks grew faster and fiercer. The ringing collisions of saber against saber deepened with each exchange.

The old man in the blue long robe could not step in to help.

Should it be compared to a carriage that had already set off?

They had already begun a fight that could not be entered. The two were pressed close together in a fierce struggle, and if he released the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style, even the red-robed old man would be swept up in the saber aura.

Nor could he simply leap in and fight alongside him. Their offense and defense were too intense, and this was not a fight of swords but of great saber against great saber. There was no gap for another great saber to enter.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was fighting with fundamentals.

His opponent must have polished only the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style for all those long years. Naturally, he would have expected the Blood Heaven Saber Demon to face them with that same style.

Compared to the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style, a battle of fundamentals should clearly be disadvantageous.

And yet the one leading the fight was indeed the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

His improvement was not due solely to training. There was one thing even more important.

His life had changed.

Only when one’s life changed did one’s martial arts change as well. That great principle of martial learning was being applied.

Gyo Cheon’s eyes darkened as he watched their battle.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was fighting better than he had expected.

The instant he realized that his disciple was stronger than he himself had been in his days as a Demon Venerable, jealousy and rage surged up within him.

At that moment, the words the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had said came back to him.

—Master, your head must be filled with fog.

Was that why only instincts like these came bursting out? Hatred, jealousy, rage.

But he could not wisely unravel the thought that had suddenly surfaced. He could not cross, by himself, the enormous wall he had built over all those years. If he had been able to, he would never have heard such words in the first place.

In the end, that jealousy led to another accusation.

‘Cunning bastard!’

Many of the words the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had spoken earlier had lodged in his chest.

Among them, the one that remained as the deepest sediment was that Geom Ujin was not cunning, but that Geom Ujin had seen through his master’s own cunning. That was why.

‘You cunning bastard! When you’re the more cunning one!’

In his heart, he hurled the word cunning again and again. To him, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s wise fight became cunning.

The red-robed old man, who had been pushed back by the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, soared upward and unleashed the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style. It was the Annihilating Saber Strike, the fastest technique to execute among the forms.

But that moment was the very moment the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had been waiting for.

That instant of vulnerability when a grand technique was unleashed, that brief moment when inner power was forcefully circulated.

Swaaaaaang!

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s great saber rushed in like a bolt of lightning, as though it had been waiting, and just as it was about to cleave the red-robed old man in two—

Shaaaaaang!

Saber aura flew in with a distinctive sound of wind. He could tell from the sound alone. It was dangerous.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon twisted his body and raised the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber upright, blocking the incoming attack.

Kwaaaaang!

As the saber aura struck, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon was pushed sliding backward together with the great saber he had raised like a shield.

It was tremendous power. If his inner power had been lacking even slightly, the attack would have broken his wrist.

The one who had saved the red-robed old man by sending out a palm strike was Gyo Cheon.

“Are you really going to do this?”

At the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s words, Gyo Cheon smiled pleasantly.

“You stop your cunning tricks first!”

At last, he had returned the word cunning to his disciple.

Their conversation could not continue.

Because the red-robed old man, having found room to breathe thanks to Gyo Cheon’s intervention, immediately unleashed the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style.

Swaaaaaaak!

Originally, he should have reorganized their formation and launched a combined attack, but his pride had been wounded by the fact that Gyo Cheon’s help had saved his life.

Saber-shaped aura surged toward the Blood Heaven Saber Demon in a storm.

It was an attack that could not be avoided. If it could be easily dodged, this martial art would not have been named the fourth form of the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style, Annihilating Saber Blood Wind.

The way to counter Annihilating Saber Blood Wind was with Annihilating Saber Blood Wind.

Saber qi extended from the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber, whirling as it flew out. Within both Annihilating Saber Blood Winds, small saber-shaped strands of aura were spinning inside the vortex.

The two blood winds collided.

With an earsplitting explosion, the saber auras canceled one another out—and at that moment.

Puuuk.

At the sound of flesh being torn, silence fell.

The red-robed old man looked down at his chest in disbelief. A strand of aura had flown through like a bird escaping a net and, as if in a lie, pierced him through.

Clearly, the same martial art had collided with similar power, but one thing had been different.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s Annihilating Saber Blood Wind contained a greater number of blades. As his martial realm had recently risen, his understanding of the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style had grown even deeper.

“……That number can increase?”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon answered him.

“How could this senior brother’s be the same as my junior brothers’?”

The red-robed old man asked with fading eyes.

“Then why did you not use it from the start?”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon slowly turned his head and looked at his master.

“I was saving it for Master.”

Gyo Cheon’s cheek trembled again. The Blood Heaven Saber Demon realized that it was not only his martial arts that had grown. He had also learned much from Geom Mugeuk about how to deal with his master. He was using on his master exactly what had once been used on him by Geom Mugeuk.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon now charged toward the old man in the blue long robe.

This time too, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon did not use the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style.

Seeing that, Gyo Cheon grew even more enraged.

Had he not just said so a moment ago? That he had not shown the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style because he had intended to use it on him? And yet even now he was not using it.

‘Are you determined to kill me with your own hands?’

It was ultimately because of this rage that he intervened in their fight, and this was also what the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had hoped for.

Whenever the blue-robed old man was in danger, Gyo Cheon sent finger winds to help him.

It should not have been easy to aid someone in a fight where two men were tangled together, yet Gyo Cheon’s attacks were sharp and precise.

Piit! Pit!

The finger winds his master sent grazed his arms and legs, while before him, a master who had reached great mastery in the Heaven-Extinguishing Demonic Saber Style was trying to kill him.

Normally, he would have grown excited and tried to kill his opponent as quickly as possible, but the Blood Heaven Saber Demon did not. He even managed his internal energy as he fought.

A handful of internal energy was worth trading for a drop of blood. He intended to survive until the very end and fight his master.

At last, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s calculated and thorough battle came to an end.

Puuuk!

The Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber pierced the heart of the blue-robed old man.

The old man’s resentful gaze was not directed at the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, but at Gyo Cheon.

Gyo Cheon’s aid in this fight had instead harmed him. If he was going to help, he should have stepped forward and helped properly. But that had not been the case.

Rather, he had disturbed his focus, and in the end, the old man had been unable to fully display his skill. Help tainted by emotion had not helped him at all.

Gyo Cheon looked at the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, whose entire body was soaked in blood. There was no word of mourning for the dead disciples, nor any regret for their deaths.

“Not bad.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon wiped the blood from his face and said,

“Among the titles of the Demon Venerables, why do you think I’m the only one with blood in mine?”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s eyes shone fiercely.

Meeting those eyes, Gyo Cheon spoke coldly.

“No one will come to help you. Because of your arrogance, you will die here alone.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon smiled forlornly, as if accepting it all, and asked in return,

“We are Demon Venerables. Whose help would we seek?”

Those words became thorns again and pierced Gyo Cheon’s heart. If his disciple was a Demon Venerable, then he too had once been one.

Demon Venerables were beings who lived by pride. In battle, they yielded to no one, sought help from no one, and stood tall alone. That was what it meant to be a Demon Venerable.

“I know you prepared much more. Now then, who is next?”

At the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s imposing figure, Gyo Cheon shouted in rage. He had thought of him as a brat of a disciple, yet now every word he spoke stabbed into his chest.

“You wretch, to the very end!”

Unable to endure it, Gyo Cheon unleashed his palm force.

Saaaaaaaang!

A terrifying palm force flew in. Since he could not evade it, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon used the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber as a shield, hiding his body behind it as he poured his inner power into it to the limit.

Kwaaaaang!

The impact made the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber shake violently. His wrist had nearly broken, and he had almost suffered internal injuries.

Saaaaaaaang!

Boom!

A second palm force flew in succession, followed by a third.

Bang! Kwaang!

The Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber shook as though it would break at any moment. Under the force of the incoming impacts, the blade gradually tilted.

His master’s voice came from beyond the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber.

“I’ll break that arrogant saber of yours!”

Swooooooooosh!

The sound was different from before. A palm force laden with tremendous internal energy was flying in.

‘The saber might break.’

But that was something that could not be allowed. The Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber was his symbol.

Instead of raising his protective qi barrier to the limit, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon poured his inner power into the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber to the utmost. Even if he suffered internal injuries, he intended to protect the saber.

Kwaaaaaang!

A tremendous explosion burst forth.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was still standing in that spot. He had not suffered internal injuries, nor had he been flung backward.

And the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber stood tall, unbroken.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon saw it.

A hand that had emerged from behind him, its palm pressed against the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber.

Startled, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon turned around.

The moment he confirmed who stood behind him, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon called out to him with a face full of emotion.

“Cult Leader!”

Geom Ujin was standing behind him.

Perhaps he had hoped the Cult Leader would come. Perhaps, deep down, he had believed he would.

But just as much as he had wished for it, there had also been a part of him that wished he would not come.

Because on the day he had killed his master, the Cult Leader had killed his elder brother. They had all desperately hoped it would not come to that, but it had.

He could only guess what wounds the Cult Leader carried with him. Since that day, he had never once asked about it. Neither of them had asked the other.

He had wanted to keep the Cult Leader from recalling that past again, and so he had resolved to handle this matter alone. Yet now, the Cult Leader had come.

And today, the look in the Cult Leader’s eyes resembled those young eyes that had looked at him on that day long ago.

“We were together that day, so we should be together today as well.”

Chapter 515: The Reason for Summoning Them.
-------------------------------------------
After that day, if anything, he grew distant from the Cult Leader.

He had thought that, having gone through such a thing together, they would see each other more often and grow closer. But reality was not so.

That was probably the reason.

Whenever they saw each other’s faces, they were reminded of that day. Perhaps both he and Geom Ujin had unconsciously wanted to avoid it.

Practical reasons were added to that.

Afterward, Geom Ujin became busy. He formally became the successor of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, and as he devoted himself to learning the Guhwa Demonic Art, it became difficult to even see his face.

Were they really going to drift apart like this?

One day, when that thought crossed his mind, Geom Ujin came to see the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

The moment he saw him, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon could feel it. That he had felt the same way. That he had wanted to see him too. Back then, emotions had still shown on Geom Ujin’s face.

But the form in which they treated each other had changed. Geom Ujin had become the Young Cult Leader, and he had risen to the seat of Demon Venerable.

“Have you been well, Young Cult Leader?”

“Thanks to your concern, Demon Venerable, I have been well.”

They were no longer in a relationship where they could call each other elder brother and younger brother. That incident had become a turning point in their relationship.

Geom Ujin held out the box he had brought.

“What is this, Young Cult Leader?”

“It is a gift for you, Demon Venerable.”

When he opened the box, what lay inside was a single pill with a bluish sheen. Astonishingly, it was the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill.

“This is my gift to you, Demon Venerable.”

Because he had become a Demon Venerable at a young age, a miraculous medicine like the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill was something he absolutely needed.

“I am all right. Please take it yourself, Young Cult Leader.”

At that moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon saw it. Geom Ujin’s eyes as he looked at him. It might have been his imagination, but at that moment Geom Ujin’s eyes seemed to be saying this.

Brother, take it.

As elder brother and younger brother. He remembered that gaze as probably being the last of its kind.

And in the end, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon did not take the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill. If he simply swallowed it, it felt as though what had happened between him and Geom Ujin that day would become over, just like that.

It was a day he had wanted so desperately to forget, and yet perhaps, to him, it had also been a day he never wanted to forget. The day he helped the Second Young Master and made him Young Cult Leader.

And so he kept the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill all that time, until he eventually gave it to Geom Mugeuk. As though by fate, it went to Geom Mugeuk.

Later, when Geom Ujin roamed the Central Plains with the Fist Demon and fought across it, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had wished that he, too, could be with them.

He was human as well; how could he not have felt that way?

But he did not express that feeling to Geom Ujin.

Even so, his fondness for the Cult Leader never changed.

And now, at this very moment.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon regretted it. This regret was one he had come to feel and understand as he built a deep relationship with Geom Mugeuk.

He should have talked with Geom Ujin.

Just as Geom Mugeuk came to him and spoke about everything from trivial matters to all manner of things, he too should have told Geom Ujin everything.

They should have deliberately spoken of that day, again and again. Instead of burying such a deep wound in their hearts, at the very least, the two of them should have talked.

If Geom Ujin had not thought of it, then he, as the older brother, should have guided him that way.

Had he done so, his relationship with Geom Ujin would have been different. Had he done so, the wound in their hearts would have grown lighter as well.

Now, back in reality, the two of them—Geom Ujin and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon—stared at each other.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon thought that he must tell Geom Ujin about the regret that had just come to mind. Because he had no intention of ever creating another regret born of words left unsaid.

“Are you all right?”

“I apologize, Cult Leader. I acted on my own without informing you in advance.”

“It was not an official situation, was it? I, too, was merely passing by.”

That, too, was true. He had been returning to the main headquarters when he turned his carriage around.

“I did not know Master was alive.”

“We did not see his corpse that day.”

Gyo Cheon had fallen beneath the ten-thousand-zhang cliff with his chest pierced through. Later, they had tried to find the body, but the corpse had been swept away by the current, and they could not locate it. Since his wound had been so fatal, they had thought he was dead, yet here he was, alive.

Watching the two of them converse, Gyo Cheon’s mood soured once more.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had his back turned completely to him as he looked at Geom Ujin and spoke. It made him feel ignored.

His mood had been foul ever since he met his disciple, and to the very end, the man was scraping at his insides. The fellow had always been like that, but the one who angered him even more was Geom Ujin.

If he saw him, should he not greet him first? More than cursing at him, seeing them talk among themselves like that made him feel even more disregarded.

In the end, unable to hold back again, he shouted.

“Geom Ujin!”

His voice rang out thunderously.

Only then did Geom Ujin’s gaze turn toward Gyo Cheon.

The moment their eyes met, Gyo Cheon was swept up in an even greater surge of emotion than when he had seen the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

In the end, the reason he had died was because of that Cult Leader.

Because he had chosen the First Young Master instead of him.

But soon, his rage turned to delight.

“We finally meet!”

He had been waiting only for this day. Now that he actually saw him like this, Gyo Cheon’s heart pounded, and he was exhilarated. He laughed by himself, making strange sounds.

He looked exactly like a madman, but Geom Ujin merely wore an indifferent expression and showed no reaction at all.

Seeing that response, Gyo Cheon laughed even louder.

A thought suddenly occurred to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

‘Because we never properly mourned that day, the three people who could not escape that day have met again.’

Yes, that was right. Both he and the Cult Leader, and even his master, laughing like that madman over there. None of them had properly mourned. Neither the death of the First Young Master, nor the fate of them all, who had collided like that.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon said to his master,

“The Cult Leader has arrived, so please come down now.”

It meant, Do not dare look down on him.

Naturally, the corners of Gyo Cheon’s eyes twitched. He could not say, Is the Cult Leader above your master? After all, he had proudly declared that he had disowned him.

Then Geom Ujin said,

“It is fine.”

It was not because he was going to kill him anyway. Geom Ujin had another reason for saying it was fine.

“This journey is one of coming down from the Taishi Chair. There is no need to be attached to seats.”

In the past, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon would have wondered what on earth that meant, but now he understood at once. Geom Mugeuk must have said that to the Cult Leader. It truly sounded like something Geom Mugeuk would say.

Looking at Gyo Cheon once more, Geom Ujin said,

“So this is what it feels like to look up.”
