Geom Mugeuk looked at his father and made one last jest.

“The victor makes his exit.”

His father pushed the dishes in front of him toward Geom Mugeuk. After landing that finishing blow, he went upstairs to the second floor.

“That’s too much!”

After giving Geom Mugeuk a smile, Hwi followed him upstairs, and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon also rose from his seat. He said one thing to Seo Daeryong.

“Still, keep reading diligently.”

“Yes, Master. Rest well.”

Geom Mugeuk silently watched his back as he went up to the second floor.

He believes in the beauty of poetry, and in the power of reading. Elder, you likely do not know just how magnificent that is.

With even the Blood Heaven Blade Demon gone upstairs, only Geom Mugeuk and Seo Daeryong remained there.

“Now then, let us have a drink among losers.”

He said it for no reason, but in truth, Seo Daeryong was perfectly fine. Whether he memorized a single poem or an entire poetry collection, how could he possibly be a match for these two Cult Leaders?

Rather, he was pleased, thinking he had gained something to talk about at future drinking gatherings. But the real thing to talk about began now.

The female warriors, having finished their meal, were about to leave when one of them came over to Seo Daeryong and said politely,

“I enjoyed the poem earlier. It was wonderful.”

At the unexpected words, Seo Daeryong was flustered. So flustered that he remained seated, not knowing what to say.

“Ah, yes.”

The woman gave a polite cupped-fist salute and bade him farewell.

“I hope I may be fortunate enough to hear another poem someday. Then, I’ll take my leave.”

Then she turned around and strode out of the inn.

Seo Daeryong looked half out of his wits. Ordinarily, he should have teased Geom Mugeuk, saying the loser had been the Young Cult Leader, but he had no presence of mind for that.

“What could that woman be trying to get out of me? Has there been some rumor that my poetry-and-painting album is a treasure map?”

He was referring to the woman who had intentionally approached him during the martial arts tournament in the past.

And that was not all. In his life, no woman had ever been the first to express pure goodwill toward him. The closest relationship he had ever had was that of a good senior.

After rising to the position of Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion, even those senior-junior relationships had all been severed. Not wanting to place any unnecessary burden on others, he had cut off all interest in the women within the cult. He had also been busy with work.

“Stop talking nonsense and go ask her name! Make plans to meet next time too. Hurry!”

When Geom Mugeuk pushed him along, Seo Daeryong ran out with a dazed expression.

He stood on the road and exchanged a few words with the woman, then returned to his seat. Seo Daeryong’s face was flushed.

“She was Miss Dan.”

Her name was Dan A.

“Was she the one you first took a liking to?”

“Not in my life up until now. Even if someone had come, it would have been a different woman. But today, yes.”

The woman he had taken a liking to had come and spoken to him.

“Congratulations, my right arm!”

Unlike the delighted Geom Mugeuk, Seo Daeryong was calm.

“You made a promise to meet again, right?”

“No.”

“You asked what group she belonged to?”

“No.”

“Why didn’t you?”

After a brief pause, Seo Daeryong said,

“I told her honestly. That I belong to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. She was surprised.”

“Why did you do that? Usually at times like this, you disguise yourself as being from the Seodo Sect.”

Seo Daeryong answered honestly.

“I don’t know either. In that moment, I thought I wanted to be honest with that woman. And she’s a woman I saw for the first time, so what do I know about her that I should like her? It’s just a passing connection.”

I’m a demon, so we can’t work out, can we?

He spoke as if he had given up on himself, but Geom Mugeuk could sense it. In fact, it was the opposite.

It seemed he truly had fallen for her at first sight. And on top of that, he had heard her say she had liked the poem he recited. Because it struck him too deeply, he had pushed it away instead. Because he was afraid.

“If you are fated with her, you will meet her again.”

“There’s no chance we’ll meet again, and even if we do, it won’t work out anyway.”

“If that truly becomes an obstacle between you two, then you can quit being Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion.”

Seo Daeryong now knew. Words like these were not a joke.

If he truly found someone he loved, he could leave the cult. Because that was the sort of person Geom Mugeuk was. Because he was someone who would gladly send him off. He would even put money in his hand to help with living expenses before letting him go.

“So that you can do this? ‘I’m sorry, but to protect the cult’s secrets, I have no choice. I’ll plant the flowers from the pot you were raising by your grave, Seo Daeryong—slash!’”

Geom Mugeuk acted as though he flinched.

“That is why I will not quit.”

If I must live alone, then I will live alone. I will never leave the Young Cult Leader, nor the cult.

* * *

Though he had resolved himself so, Seo Daeryong was tormented by nightmares all night.

It was a dream in which he recited poetry before countless people.

At times, the crowd listening would cheer and applaud. At other times, they jeered.

She was among them too. She only jeered.

After being tormented by dreams like that, he opened his eyes to find it was morning.

Seo Daeryong looked up at the ceiling and sighed.

“How pathetic, how truly pathetic. You even dreamed about that?”

Seo Daeryong got up from the bed and walked to the window.

As if it had never rained so hard, the sun had risen brightly.

A carriage was waiting at the entrance of the inn, and Hwi was preparing to depart.

“I’ll come down right away as well.”

At Seo Daeryong’s words, Hwi said calmly,

“It’s all right. The preparations are finished, so please come down at your leisure.”

Seo Daeryong quickly got ready and went downstairs.

Fortunately, the Cult Leader and his master had not yet come down.

What was he thinking? Hwi made an unexpected suggestion.

“Would you like to take the reins today?”

They were reins he had never once handed over before.

“Yes, I’ll try.”

Seo Daeryong sat in the driver’s seat and took the reins. His heart trembled for fear that he might make a mistake. Then a voice came from beside him.

“The poem was pleasing to hear for me as well.”

At first, he thought he had heard wrong. Hwi had not once spoken to him about anything personal on the way here.

“Th-thank you.”

Flustered, Seo Daeryong even stammered as he looked at Hwi, but Hwi said nothing more.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk leaned out of the carriage and said,

“Let us go slowly, Pavilion Lord.”

“Understood.”

A short while later, Geom Ujin and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon came down and boarded the carriage.

“We will depart.”

Before setting the carriage in motion, Seo Daeryong looked back at the inn one last time.

Sensing that deep regret, Geom Mugeuk sent a voice transmission.

—It was a poem that even Uncle Hwi responded to. This connection is not yet certain.

As if declaring that he would no longer harbor futile hope, Seo Daeryong set the carriage off with vigor.

—It is a connection that has already ended.

* * *

The ended connection was walking along the road.

They were the three women who had met Geom Mugeuk’s group at the inn just earlier. There was a name by which they were known in the jianghu.

The Three Dan Sisters.

The three sisters were wandering warriors. They were escort mercenaries who mostly took on guard missions, and because they were skilled and responsible, they had a very good reputation in the world of wandering warriors.

Moreover, work never ran out. When women traveling alone felt uneasy about male escorts, the brokers would invariably recommend the Three Dan Sisters.

That was why they had given off such a cheerful impression at the inn earlier. They were not women belonging to a sect who valued the honor of a family, but wandering warriors who freely roamed the jianghu. On top of that, the three of them were so close that it was an everyday sight for them to put their heads together and whisper among themselves.

“Sister, your taste in men really is unusual. He’s short and slight too. Could he even use any strength properly?”

The third sister, Dan Yeon, could not understand her older sister’s taste. Knowing that she was originally the type to speak frankly without beating around the bush, Dan A merely smiled.

“There was a handsome young master beside him, you know.”

“Then why did you tell them to send that dish to that gentleman? You’re the youngest among us.”

The dish had gone to Geom Ujin entirely because of the third sister Dan Yeon’s insistence.

“He was impressive, wasn’t he?”

Dan Yeon’s ideal type had always been a man who gave off such an intense feeling.

“He seemed impressive to me too.”

“Compare what can be compared.”

Dan A had felt Seo Daeryong’s gaze as soon as she entered the inn.

What had drawn her heart was nothing else but the shyness contained in his eyes.

When one met martial artists, they usually tried by any means to appear strong. She had grown tired, utterly tired, of those eyes that strove to look strong. Eyes filled with shyness had been truly rare and refreshing.

And most decisively,

“Have you ever seen someone carrying a sword recite poetry?”

She could tell that he had memorized the poem because of her. That pure feeling had been very nice.

“You’ve always liked kind people, Sister.”

At the second sister Dan Bi’s words, the youngest, Dan Yeon, shook her head.

“Kind? Didn’t he say he was a demon?”

At those words, the three sisters were silent for a moment. Among hired martial artists, there was a saying passed down through generations: nothing good ever came of getting entangled with demons.

“Why are you all making such a fuss? I simply told him I liked the poem.”

“You went out of your way to do that?”

“We’re here. Let’s work now.”

The place the three arrived at was a small hermitage in the mountains.

Since it was a location designated by the brokers, there should not be any particular problem, but the three women scattered and examined the surroundings.

Once they began working, they became different people. A sharpness that made it impossible to think they were the same ones who had been chatting earlier could be felt from them.

They had been doing this since the youngest was fourteen, so it was already their tenth year in this line of work.

During that time, they had crossed the line between life and death several times.

They knew. No matter how careful they were, this was work where death could come in a single instant. And so, knowing the preciousness of each moment, they strove to live as cheerfully as possible.

When one lived a life where tomorrow might bring death, how could one spend today fighting? They could not let their last moment with someone become a quarrel.

The client waiting at the appointed place was a woman.

A young and beautiful woman.

She was trying to remain calm, but Dan A sensed her tension and fear.

‘Someone is threatening her.’

With long experience, she could tell at a glance.

“We are the Three Dan Sisters.”

The three women greeted her with light cupped-fist salutes. The woman also lowered her head and accepted their greeting.

“We heard the place you need to go is Guizhou.”

The woman nodded. She was someone who kept her words to a minimum.

“May we ask what business takes you there?”

“Must I reveal my purpose as well?”

“Where exactly is your destination?”

“I will tell you once we enter Guizhou.”

From the woman’s caution, Dan A could guess. It was not that she did not trust them; rather, she was this careful because she feared those pursuing her.

Dan A felt an inexplicable unease. When working, she could usually tell at once: this job is dangerous, this job will be easy. This time, it fell on the dangerous side.

“I’m sorry, but unless you reveal exactly who you are and where you are going, we will refuse this commission. Find someone else.”

In ordinary cases, they often proceeded without asking. But when the feeling was bad, one had to be careful.

Sure enough, the woman was greatly shaken. From that alone, one could tell. She was in danger.

If so, they needed to learn even more about this job. She could not put her younger sisters in danger.

The two younger sisters left all decisions to their elder sister and quietly drew their swords, keeping watch over the surroundings.

“I will contact the broker again and tell them to send someone new. Until then, we will protect you.”

At that, the woman took a step back. There was no more time to delay.

“Very well. I will tell you who I am. I am a courtesan of Cheonhwa Pavilion in Fenghuang, Hunan Province.”

There were seven Cheonhwa Pavilions in Hunan alone. She was a courtesan of one of them, the Fenghuang Cheonhwa Pavilion.

Dan A could tell. Judging by the woman’s beauty, she was a very high-ranking courtesan among courtesans.

Was she perhaps eloping for love?

But the place the woman intended to go was unexpected.

“Please take me to Cheonhwa Pavilion’s main headquarters in Guizhou.”

From her mouth flowed the words that would reconnect a bond severed in the rain.

“I must see the Cheonhwa Pavilion Master as soon as possible.”

For the Cheonhwa Pavilion Master was none other than Yeo Jeong, the woman who loved the Extreme Evil Young Demon.

Chapter 526: For That Kind of Request, Go to the Martial Alliance.
------------------------------------------------------------------
Dana wanted to refuse.

She had a strong premonition that it would be best to somehow get out of this commission.

Of course, refusing a commission was something brokers hated most. Naturally, they wouldn't give work to wanderers who frequently refused commissions.

But the three of them had a reputation built steadily over the past ten years, so she could refuse this commission.

Yet there was one thing that held her back.

Dana's gaze turned to the woman who was frightened, only waiting to see what would become of her.

*If we give up, this woman dies.*

That premonition hit her strongly. It was the thick scent of death wafting from this that made her want to back out.

Dana looked at her younger sisters. Having been together for so long, they knew just from her hesitation what dilemma their elder sister was facing.

They showed no reaction. In moments like this, they followed their elder sister's will completely.

Soon, Dana made her decision.

"We will escort you to the Cheonhwaru in Gui Prefecture."

A sense of relief flashed across the woman's face as she waited for the decision.

"Thank you."

"Let's depart at once."

As she turned and walked away, Chohui spoke from behind.

"My name is Chohui (草熙)."

Without turning around, Dana answered.

"It's a beautiful name."

Just as the four of them were descending from the hermitage—

Swish!

A hidden projectile flew swiftly toward Chohui.

Chang!

Dana swung her sword, knocking away the hidden projectile as she shouted.

"Left tree, above!"

But before her shout even came out, the youngest, Danyeon's throwing dagger was already lodged between the branches.

A masked man struck by the dagger fell from the tree.

From the opposite tree, hidden projectiles flew with sharp whistling sounds.

"Run!"

Dana scooped up Chohui and leaped down the stairs.

Thud, thud, thud!

The flying projectiles embedded themselves where Chohui had been standing.

Danbi and Danyeon's, who had leaped down from both sides, deflected the incoming projectiles with their swords. The area around the hermitage was already ambushed by enemies. The situation was grave. The fact that the rendezvous point had been exposed meant the broker had been compromised.

While knocking away the hidden projectiles, the three women threw their own daggers. Especially the youngest Danyeon's blade-throwing arts were of a high level, and masked men fell one after another to her daggers.

"Behind that rock!"

The four of them dove behind the rock.

Papapapapak!

At the same time as their narrowly timed cover, the flying projectiles bounced off the rock.

Despite the quick judgment, blood was flowing from Danbi's shoulder.

"Are you alright?"

"It just grazed me. I'm fine."

But Danyeon's, who examined the wound, was startled. The wounded area was swelling up blue-black.

"It's poison."

At Danyeon's shout, a curse burst from Dana's lips.

"These bastards!"

She was the one best at cursing among the three sisters.

Danyeon's quickly took a medicine bottle from her bosom and applied it to the wound. It was a temporary medicine to stop the poison from spreading.

"We can't hold out long! Hurry and get out—"

Right then, masked men who had leaped over the rock attacked.

Sssssk! Puk!

Dana's sword pierced the side of the masked man lunging at Chohui, then tore through his flesh.

Shhhrrrrk!

The sword that tore through his side then stabbed the neck of another masked man who charged in after. Blood splashed across her face, but she had no leisure to wipe it away. Sword clashed against sword, emitting sparks, and hidden projectiles crossed each other, drawing two lines in the air.

Danyeon's throwing dagger flew and embedded itself in the neck of the masked man attacking from behind her elder sister. The descending sword lost its strength and split the empty air.

Behind the falling masked man, more masked men charged. Throwing herself toward the sword blades reflecting in the sunlight, Dana shouted.

"Dodge!"

* * *

Rumble.

Passing over a torn-up part of the road, the carriage shook violently.

"My apologies!"

The urgent cry was from Seo Daeryong.

From inside the carriage, Geom Mugeuk's voice was heard.

"Father spilled tea on his face while drinking."

Of course, it was Geom Mugeuk's prank. He hadn't forgotten the joke made when they first departed—that if the carriage rattled, heads would roll—after Seo Daeryong said he would drive.

Seo Daeryong, whose mind had snapped to attention, asked Hwi.

"Would you like to take the reins yourself?"

Having made a mistake, it wouldn't be strange for him to take the reins, but Hwi shook his head and continued to let Seo Daeryong drive the carriage.

"The road is treacherous, so focus."

At first, he had driven the carriage with extreme concentration. But human minds are fickle, and as he grew a bit accustomed, his thoughts wandered. Before he knew it, he had recalled the woman he met at the inn.

*To think I'm driving the carriage that the Cult Lord is riding in. I've lost my mind.*

Seo Daeryong shook off his stray thoughts with effort and gripped the reins tightly.

Geom Mugeuk poked his head out the window.

As the carriage ran through an open area and entered a narrow trail, the scenery that had been slow—as if wielding a heavy sword—sped up, as if now wielding a swift sword.

"Why does the return journey feel faster than when we departed?"

Then Hyeolcheon Doma spoke, his gaze still fixed on the book he was reading.

"What is the Young Cult Lord finding so regrettable?"

Facing the blowing wind, Geom Mugeuk answered.

"Indeed. What is it that I find so regrettable?"

Geom Mugeuk's gaze turned to Seo Daeryong on the driver's seat.

At least, the regret of these past two days was certainly not solely due to memories with his father.

"Branch Lord Seo, watch out for that pothole ahead!"

* * *

Rumble.

Closing the stable door, Dana was covered in blood.

Fortunately, the blood belonged to the enemies, but the problem was the second sister, Danbi.

The wound struck by poison earlier was continuing to swell.

Dana grabbed Chohui by the collar.

"You! Just what kind of trouble have you gotten tangled in?"

Run when told to run, lie down when told to lie down. Having mindlessly come all this way, Chohui came to her senses.

"I'm sorry."

She had asked for escort while knowing she was in grave danger. It had been an unavoidable choice, but because of her, they had fallen into great danger.

"Do you even know who attacked us!"

Dana had despaired when she realized who they were facing.

The Seven Paths Assassination Squad (七路追殺隊).

The Seven Paths Assassination Squad were also wanderers. They were those who mainly took on pursuit and killing, and among assassination squads, they were notorious and vicious.

If they were wanderers on the side of light, the assassination squads belonged to darkness. And among them, the Seven Paths Assassination Squad was profound darkness.

Getting through even a single squad of the Seven Paths Assassination Squad was a miraculous feat. But this matter wouldn't end with killing a dozen or so of them.

They would swarm endlessly like ants and eventually harm their target.

The fact that the Seven Paths Assassination Squad had been mobilized meant a huge sum was being spent to hunt Chohui.

Just why, for a mere courtesan?

But now was not the time to argue with her over such reasons.

Soon those bastards would come charging here.

"How is Biya?"

At Dana's question, Danyeon's answered worriedly.

"I've barely managed to stop the poison from spreading, but if we move too recklessly again, it'll spread throughout her entire body."

Then Dana's gaze turned to Danyeon's side. Pushing aside the blood-soaked hem of her clothes, a sword-cut wound was revealed.

"When did you get stabbed?"

"Earlier. I had stopped the bleeding, but it reopened when I ran."

She hadn't even realized her younger sister had been stabbed while fighting frantically.

"You!"

"I'm fine. I won't die, I'm fine!"

But the fact that there was no color in her face meant the youngest's condition was not good either.

Dana pressed the acupoint at her younger sister's wounded area again to stop the bleeding, then took out golden wound medicine and applied it to the wound.

"Lie down and rest for now."

"Yeah, let me lie down for a bit."

Watching her two younger sisters lying down, Dana regretted. She shouldn't have taken this commission. The premonition had been that bad.

"I'm sorry."

At Chohui's words, Dana didn't vent her anger on her. Chohui was merely the client, the customer. The one who decided to accept the job was herself.

"It's alright."

"Shouldn't we find a doctor for your sister first?"

If only they could. They couldn't take her to a doctor. When a situation like this occurred, those bastards would search the doctors first. That was the basics of pursuit.

"If the Seven Paths Assassination Squad is on us, we absolutely cannot reach Gui Prefecture. We need to ask someone for help first."

She took out a map from her bosom.

It was a map she had made herself for emergencies. Recorded on it were sects from whom they could request help. Sects with whom they had even small connections.

But they couldn't ask ordinary sects for help while being chased by the Seven Paths Assassination Squad. The Seven Paths Assassination Squad were ones who would storm into ordinary sects and massacre them to kill their target.

"We need to ask the Martial Alliance for help."

But it was too far. They had to leave this village and go another thirty ri before a Martial Alliance branch would appear. Taking her injured younger sisters, and moreover protecting Chohui while getting there, was impossible.

Furthermore, they might be guarding that route expecting them to head for the Martial Alliance.

Then a certain place caught Dana's eye.

The Heavenly Demon Cult's Jeongan Branch.

Not far from here was a branch of the Demonic Cult.

But if they recklessly went to the Demonic Cult and asked for help, they might incur an even greater disaster than falling to the Seven Paths Assassination Squad bastards.

Then she suddenly thought of the man she saw yesterday.

—I belong to the Divine Cult.

The characters engraved on the great blade he carried as he turned to leave after speaking those words.

Musou.

It was a phrase that suited the pure, bashful gaze with which he had looked at her far too little.

Then Danyeon's, who was lying down, asked quietly.

"What are you thinking?"

Dana bit her lip slightly and answered.

"A crazy thought."

* * *

The Heavenly Demon Cult's Jeongan Branch was a small sub-branch with barely twenty permanent members.

With no war occurring, what business could there be at a demonic cult branch? The person in charge of that peaceful place was the branch leader, Gangdal.

"Branch Leader, I think you need to come out."

At his subordinate's report, he came out to see something unexpected happening.

At the entrance of the Jeongan Branch, four women were surrounded by numerous masked men, their backs to the gate.

The women were Dana's group, and the Seven Paths Assassination Squad had pursued them all the way here.

"Please open the gate! I have something to tell the branch leader."

Because of the demonic cultists guarding the entrance, the Seven Paths Assassination Squad had only surrounded them and couldn't charge in.

Then the gate opened and Gangdal came out.

With him, more than ten branch cultists who had been waiting at the entrance followed out.

Gangdal looked at Dana's group first.

Only Dana stood straight, holding a sword dripping with blood.

Chohui was tending to the unconscious Danbi, her head laid on her lap, and Danyeon's was lying asleep beside her. They were in a state that made one marvel at how they had managed to get here, wounded all over.

Before the four women, more than ten masked men stood surrounding them in a semicircle.

Gangdal looked up at the plaque above the entrance. Had the plaque fallen off, causing this commotion? But the plaque reading "Heavenly Demon Cult Jeongan Branch" was firmly attached.

Then a subordinate spoke quietly.

"The masked ones are Seven Paths Assassination Squad bastards."

At the words "Seven Paths Assassination Squad," Gangdal frowned. He knew them well. They were vicious bastards who would kill even friends and family for money.

"Those bastards are crazy to begin with, but what are you lot?"

At Gangdal's question, Dana answered.

"We are wanderers known as the Three Dan Sisters. Please help us."

"If you're going to ask for that kind of favor, you should do it in front of a Martial Alliance branch."

Gangdal spoke while looking at the masked men.

"Clean them up and leave. If you leave so much as a single bloodstain, you will die."

He was telling them to kill them on their own and get lost.

As Gangdal turned to go back in—

"A master from the Divine Cult sent us here."

For an instant, Gangdal flinched and stopped. His eyes turning toward her were cold.

"Which master?"

Naturally, Gangdal thought the woman was lying to save her life. He hadn't received such word.

"He left the Yuyeong Inn yesterday. A short man wielding a large blade. The characters 'Musou' are engraved on that blade."

A moment of silence passed.

Gangdal asked again.

"And who is that?"

After a brief pause, she answered.

"I don't know who he is, either."

Then a cold word flowed from Gangdal's lips.

"You insane wench."

Yes, this was a crazy act even by her own reckoning. But she had already chosen, and now there was no way out. Ahead were the Seven Paths Assassination Squad, and behind was the Demonic Cult.

"That person said if a problem arose, we should receive help here."

Having lied to a Demonic Cult branch leader, it was truly a life-or-death gamble.

Even if they could get help, if the man she had seen at the inn was not a demon cultist, or was merely some low-ranking martial artist, then they would die at the hands of these demon cultists.

“I’m certain he’s someone higher-ranking than you. If you save us, you’ll live too. If we die here, you’ll die as well.”

It was just then. More members of the Chilro Chusaldae arrived. With over ten more added to their number, there were now more than twenty of them. From the look of it, they were all gathering here.

Even when the bastards saw him and the demon cultists of the branch, they showed not the slightest sign of fear. Instead, they bared their killing intent, as if saying, Just wait until we’re all here.

‘These bastards, do they have a death wish?’

But Gangdal turned around and said,

“Close the door.”

Then came a desperate plea.

“Please let at least these three people in.”

Gangdal turned back to Dan-a.

“I’ll be fine, so please let at least these three people in. Later, when that person comes, you should at least have something to say, shouldn’t you?”

Dan-a was prepared to fight alone and die.

Gangdal stared at her quietly, then said something unexpected.

“If you sisters are wanderers, then the one the Chilro Chusaldae chased here must be that woman.”

Gangdal looked at Chohui.

“In any case, that woman must be their target. Leave her outside, and I’ll take the three of you in.”

Dan-a looked at Chohui. Holding Danbi in her arms, Chohui nodded. Her eyes said to save the lives of even just those three.

Dan-a said to Gangdal,

“Please take only my younger siblings in. That woman and I will stay.”

If she could save even just her younger siblings, that was enough. Chohui shook her head, telling her not to, but Dan-a was not the sort of person who would abandon a client in a situation like this, even if it meant throwing away her life.

Gangdal looked at Dan-a, then abruptly said,

“If you had tried to come in by yourselves, none of you would have gotten in.”

Which meant—

“Everyone, come in.”

The Jeongan Branch had accepted them.

“Thank you.”

“Thank you so much.”

Dan-a and Chohui looked at each other in relief. Chohui bowed her head to Dan-a in gratitude.

Her younger siblings were carried inside, and Dan-a and Chohui entered as well.

Gangdal sealed Dan-a’s internal energy and took her sword. She accepted it meekly without resisting. His suspicion was only natural.

Gangdal gave an order to his subordinate.

“There’s no telling what those lunatics outside might do. Sound the emergency alarm and wake everyone who’s sleeping.”

“Understood.”

“And send an urgent message to Tongcheongak! Tell them the current situation.”

At that moment, Dan-a stepped forward. It meant she wanted him to convey what she had said as well.

“Add what that woman said earlier too. Short, uses a great saber with the word Musang written on it. What inn was it?”

At his question, Dan-a answered quickly.

“It was Yuyeong Inn.”

“Say we urgently need to find the person who left Yuyeong Inn yesterday.”

“Yes!”

The demon cultists began moving busily. Martial artists armed with weapons came out of the buildings, and hidden weapons were distributed from the concealed-weapon boxes.

“If you were spouting nonsense just to avoid those bastards, you’d better be prepared.”

Even at Gangdal’s threat, Dan-a did not shrink back at all. The wheel of fate had already begun to turn.

“Please return my sword and release my internal energy. If a fight breaks out, you’ll need me.”

She was dignified even before the branch leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. In truth, it was this dignity that had moved Gangdal’s heart. The courage to remain alone and protect her client.

And then she added a line Gangdal could never understand.

“I also want to live so I can hear that poem again.”

Chapter 527: She Is Looking for You.
------------------------------------
It was when they had stopped the carriage and were letting the horses rest that the urgent letter arrived.

“An urgent letter has arrived from Tongcheongak.”

Hwi handed the letter to Geom Ujin.

After checking the contents, Geom Ujin gave the letter to his son.

Geom Mugeuk, having read the letter, broke into a bright smile. When he showed it to Hyeolcheon Doma, the latter donned the same smile.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Seo Daeryong. Seo Daeryong was walking beside the horses grazing in the distant field.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to Geom Ujin and Hyeolcheon Doma.

“We should go on ahead.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, the two men nodded.

Geom Mugeuk leaped lightly toward where Seo Daeryong was.

“Let’s go, Right Arm!”

As he suddenly started running, Seo Daeryong hastily followed suit.

“What is the matter?”

“An urgent letter has arrived.”

Then, from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth flowed words that Seo Daeryong would never forget for the rest of his life.

“She is looking for you.”

“!”

At those words, Seo Daeryong overtook Geom Mugeuk and took the lead. His instantaneous speed was faster than a galloping horse.

However, Seo Daeryong, who had been running far ahead, stopped.

“Where is it?”

“You fool, you’ll burst your heart like that.”

Seo Daeryong shouted with a flushed face.

“I asked where!”

* * *

“Are you all right?”

When the second sister, Danbi, opened her eyes, her older sister Dana was before her.

“What happened?”

“You lost consciousness for a while due to poison on a hidden weapon.”

Fortunately, Danbi had been treated here at the branch, and her fever had subsided.

“Where is this?”

“The Jeongan Branch of the Cheonma Singyo.”

“What?”

Dana gave the surprised girl a smile.

“That’s how it is. I’ll explain later.”

“What about Danyeon?”

“She woke up earlier and is eating. Do you want something to eat too?”

“I’m thirsty.”

Dana brought her water. Only then did the now-conscious Danbi worry about her sister.

“Are you okay?”

She had heard the sounds of ceaseless fighting while drifting in and out of consciousness. She could tell without asking how her sister had protected her.

“I’m fine.”

“You always say you’re fine.”

She was someone who had devoted her entire youth to her younger sisters. Always saying she was fine, always fine.

Then, Danbi’s eyes caught the blood soaked all over her sister’s clothes. Seeing that worried gaze, Dana smiled and answered.

“I’m fine. It’s not my blood, so stop worrying. Want something to eat?”

“I’m not hungry.”

“Then sleep a little more.”

“I’m getting up.”

As she tried to rise, she staggered. Her body was not yet in a state to move.

Dana laid her sister back down on the bed.

“If you have to fight, wake me up too.”

“I will.”

“Promise me!”

Having been promised so, Danbi fell asleep again.

Dana silently looked down at her sister. With her eyes full of worry and affection, fighting was something that should be left to dreams.

Into that place came Chohui.

“I’ll stay with her.”

“Then I will leave her to you.”

As she turned to leave, Chohui spoke.

“Thank you.”

At her sincere gratitude, Dana gave a slight bow and then stepped outside.

In the courtyard, all the Main of the branch had come out.

Their gazes were focused on her. Though they must have roughly known because of whom today’s events had occurred, not a single person cursed her or picked a fight with her, perhaps because the branch leader, Gang Dal, was sitting astride the wall looking outside.

Gang Dal leaped down from the wall.

The Chilro Chusaldae gathered over there already numbered over a hundred. On this side, barely twenty. If they rushed in with suicidal intent, it would be no easy fight.

“No matter how crazy they are, if they strike us, they won’t be able to handle the aftermath.”

Gang Dal spoke as if he could not understand, but Dana was guessing at the reason.

The reason they were going this far, even against the Demonic Cult, was because they had to kill Chohui no matter what.

Why on earth? Just how much money had they been paid?

Just then, the youngest, Danyeon, came outside. She respectfully cupped her hands and pleaded with Gang Dal.

“Could I obtain some throwing knives?”

Dana stopped her.

“You can’t in that condition. Rest.”

“This happened because of us. I should fight too.”

Gang Dal ordered a subordinate.

“Give this young lady a box of throwing knives.”

“Yes, sir.”

A Main brought the throwing knives and gave them to Danyeon. Having collected them, she expressed her gratitude to Gang Dal.

“Thank you.”

“Don’t thank me. I’m giving them to you so you can die in my place out there.”

When Danyeon widened her eyes, Gang Dal put on an expression that said, “What other reason is there?”

Just then, a subordinate came and reported.

“The bastards have requested a face-to-face meeting with the branch leader.”

“Just tell them all to piss off.”

“They say the leader of the Chilro Chusaldae has come personally.”

“What?”

Gang Dal leaped onto the wall. Dana went up with him.

A man was walking out from among the Chilro Chusaldae, who had formed a battle formation.

He was none other than the leader of the Chilro Chusaldae, Yao Hong.

He was a figure known to possess tremendous martial prowess despite being a wanderer.

He was one who rarely appeared outside, yet he had revealed himself here today. His appearance confirmed once more how important today’s matter was to them.

It wasn’t just Yao Hong. The elites known as the Sainbang, who usually followed him, had come as well. Those four were far above the ordinary Chilro Chusaldae in skill.

As Yao Hong and the Sainbang appeared, the morale of the Chilro Chusaldae reached its peak. Everyone shouted war cries and surged with murderous intent.

Gang Dal’s face hardened.

“These bastards are looking to die!”

But his eyes were grave.

If Yao Hong had come, it meant they truly might cross this wall; the bastards were dead set on it.

“What on earth did that woman do to cause this mess?”

Dana looked at the Chilro Chusaldae, filled with malice, and spoke quietly.

“It wasn’t anything bad, at least?”

Then, a voice rang out.

“Branch leader, let us see each other face to face.”

It was Yao Hong, displaying his martial might through the internal energy in his thunderous voice.

Gang Dal, who had watched him with a complicated gaze for a moment, leaped down outside. The Main followed him out through the gate.

Dana watched this meeting in silence. With Yao Hong’s appearance, the situation had rapidly deteriorated. Could they really not hand them over now that Yao Hong and even the Sainbang had appeared? Everything depended on Gang Dal’s decision.

Yao Hong and Gang Dal stood facing each other, about thirty paces apart.

“I apologize for the disturbance.”

Gang Dal knew there was no sincerity in that polite apology.

He also well knew what a wicked bastard Yao Hong was. A man who enjoyed burning people alive.

“I heard that the people we are pursuing have taken refuge on your side. Hand them over, and we will leave immediately.”

Gang Dal replied calmly in a gentle tone.

“They did not come here to take refuge; they are our guests.”

He was an opponent he did not want to exchange a single word with, but there was no need to deliberately provoke him in front of his subordinates.

“Guests are guests, but uninvited ones, are they not? Hand them over.”

Just as the murderous intent of the Chilro Chusaldae lined up behind Yao Hong, Gang Dal had his pride—no, his guts—as one of the Demonic Cult’s Main.

“And if I refuse?”

Tension stretched tight between their gazes.

“Even if they leave, will you and your subordinates not remain here?”

That was a clear threat of private retaliation.

Of course, it did not work on Gang Dal.

“Who knows? Perhaps I’ll subdue those who look down on the main cult, get promoted, and transfer to the main hall.”

Had his opponent been anyone other than a branch leader of the Demonic Cult, he would have drawn his sword several times over by now. The patience that had rarely surfaced in Yao Hong’s life was being properly exercised today.

Gang Dal looked up at the branch plaque behind him.

“If you cross this plaque, there will be no turning back. Do not forget who we are.”

From behind came Yao Hong’s proposal.

“Why else would we attempt to cross your wall? If the branch leader yields just this once, I will be certain to repay that grace.”

What else could those words mean? It was a promise to certainly compensate with money later.

“I will give you ample time to think. I shall wait.”

Gang Dal did not answer and came back inside. The moment he entered, he spoke to his subordinates.

“They’ll be storming in soon. Prepare to meet them.”

“Yes, sir!”

He felt that the enemy had realized he would not hand over the women. And more than anything, he had never seen bastards like them keep their word.

The Main moved with perfect discipline.

Iron pillars were erected on both sides of the courtyard, and in front of them, something like near-invisible fishing line was installed. It was the Duan Xue Si used in warfare.

Other Main wheeled out mobile mechanisms that unleashed hidden weapons all at once. As they say, an organization follows its leader, and the Main of this Jeongan Branch were no pushovers.

Watching that sight, Dana was inwardly surprised.

‘So this is why the martial world fears the Demonic Cult. If a mere small branch is like this…’

What would happen if the true Demonic Cult went to war?

Gang Dal glanced back at Dana and Danyeon.

The place where the two women stood guard was in front of the building where Danbi and Chohui were. As their eyes met, Dana spoke to Gang Dal.

“We might die and be unable to tell you later, so I’ll say it now. Thank you for taking us in.”

Danyeon, beside her, bowed her head.

Gang Dal said gruffly.

“I didn’t take you in for your sake, so no need to thank me. I threw in my lot by my own choice. So that expert you mentioned had better be of higher standing than me.”

Dana thought of Seo Daeryong. Could that innocent-looking man really be someone of higher standing than these rough men? She didn’t think so.

‘I’m sorry. In the end, it seems I’ve deceived you.’

If she hadn’t met him at the inn, if she hadn’t accepted this commission, if he hadn’t taken them in—to make an excuse, she could only say that fate had pushed them.

It was at that very moment. A Main keeping watch outside the wall shouted.

“They’re coming!”

Cha-ang.

The Main drew their swords in unison from their positions.

At the same time, the bastards all leaped over the wall. It was a surprise attack, but this side was prepared.

Puak! Paaak! Puaaaak!

The limbs of the masked men who threw themselves at the installed Duan Xue Si were severed.

But they kept coming. Those who leaped over a second time cut the blood-revealed Duan Xue Si with their swords.

But mobile mechanisms fired hidden weapons at them.

Shwik! Shwik! Shwik! Shwik! Shwik!

Those who had cut the Duan Xue Si were all struck by the hidden weapons and tumbled down in heaps.

But the enemy gave them no time to readjust their mechanisms and continued crossing the wall.

A chaotic battle broke out within the grounds. The bastards seemed to have received orders. They pushed against the Main with numbers and quickly looked for the women.

Their intent was clear: minimize collisions with the Main, kill the women quickly, and withdraw.

Over ten masked men surged toward Dana and Danyeon.

Dana fought without leaving her sister’s side.

Though her skill was superior to theirs, the bastards pushed with numbers.

But Dana gritted her teeth and fought like a madwoman. Fighting with the conviction that if she died, her sisters would die, the bastards could not easily kill her. Danyeon too felled enemies by throwing knives.

It was then.

Yao Hong and the Sainbang were walking toward them. As they appeared, the martial artists of the Chilro Chusaldae fell back.

Gang Dal saw this situation as well. But he could not leave his post to help her. The bastards were pushing so desperately that if he moved, his subordinates would die. He chose to protect his men.

Danyeon threw knives at Yao Hong in succession. Her purpose was not to kill but to gauge the opponent’s skill through how he blocked them.

Chaeng! Chaeng!

Yao Hong did not even draw his sword. The Sainbang on either side lightly swung their blades and knocked the knives away.

‘Experts on the same level as big sister!’

Four such experts. And their leader, Yao Hong, on top of that.

Dana and Danyeon bravely charged, thrusting their swords.

Yao Hong leaped lightly and engaged them.

Though every move they made contained a strong will to protect each other, they could not overcome the clear gap in skill with Yao Hong.

They could not last even five moves. Danyeon was the first to take a palm strike and was flung backward. At the sound of her coughing up blood, Dana cried out.

“Danyeon!”

In the moment her hands and feet went numb with worry that her sister was badly hurt, Dana too took a blow and rolled on the ground. Both women had fallen to Yao Hong without him even drawing his sword.

Dana, coughing up a mouthful of blood, picked up a sword from the ground and stood before her sister.

“Are you all right?”

“…I’m fine.”

She could tell by her voice. Both she and her sister had sustained internal injuries.

Her sister rose again as well. Because if they fell, behind them were Danbi and Chohui.

Yao Hong’s gaze as he looked at them was cold as a snake’s.

“Because of you wretched bitches, things have become complicated. Get rid of them quickly and bring me that woman’s head!”

At the order, the Sainbang stepped forward.

“These bitches have decent looks; it’s a waste to just kill them.”

The Sainbang looked at Yao Hong with eyes asking if they couldn’t subdue and take them instead. These bastards were crazed scum who revealed their lust even in this situation.

Yao Hong coldly commanded.

“Stop your nonsense and tear them to shreds!”

The Sainbang pointed their swords at the two women in unison.

Dana tightly grasped Danyeon’s hand.

She felt her younger sister’s trembling hand grip hers tightly. She was so sorry she couldn’t protect her.

‘I’m sorry.’

The instant the Sainbang unleashed their sword qi in unison!

Swaaaaaak!

At the same time, a whirling blade storm came flying in from the other side. Its target was the Sainbang.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa!

The blades of the whirling Gang Qi rotated at a terrifying speed and swept over them.

Thud! Thwack! Thududududud!

Without even the time to dodge, the Sainbang’s bodies were shredded and exploded in all directions.

But the sword qi they had already launched was flying toward the women.

Swaaaaaak!

Watching the sword qi flying in, Dana squeezed her eyes shut. Due to her internal injuries, she could neither dodge nor block. In her final moment, she pulled her sister behind her.

Kwa-ang!

An ear-splitting explosion was heard. It was the sound of the sword qi crashing into something and bursting apart.

Silence followed.

Their bodies should have been torn apart.

‘Am I alive?’

When Dana slowly opened her eyes, the grounds were silent.

Everyone had stopped fighting and was looking in their direction.

Yao Hong stared in shock at the corpses of the Sainbang, scattered and turned to bloody pulp. Gang Dal too was looking their way with a surprised face, bleeding from his arm. Both the Main and the Chilro Chusaldae were all looking this way.

Before her, someone’s back was visible.

“Haa, haa, haa.”

He was catching his rough breath, having run so frantically.

And the greatsword planted before him.

Beyond the back of the diminutive man, a single character was visible.

Musang.

Chapter 528: The Man Trusted by the Young Cult Leader
-----------------------------------------------------
“……You really came.”

Dana’s startled voice trembled. Had she ever been this surprised in her life?

“Haa, haa, haa…….”

The rough breathing gradually subsided.

The back she was staring at was no longer a small, frail back. It was the back that had blocked the terrifying sword qi of the Four as it came pouring down upon them. It was the back that had closed again the gates of hell that had been flung wide open before her and her siblings.

Seo Daeryong slowly turned to look at her.

Relief that he had fortunately not been too late passed through his trembling gaze.

Overwhelmed by the fact that he had managed to save her by a hair’s breadth, Seo Daeryong could not say a word.

“……How did you get here?”

At Dana’s question, Seo Daeryong finally opened his mouth.

“……I heard you were looking for me.”

The moment she heard those words, Dana’s chest tightened. Not from emotion, but from guilt. Because she had thought he would not come. Because she had not trusted him.

And yet this man had run here until he was nearly out of breath.

The one as surprised as Dana by those words was Gangdal.

‘So he really existed!’

A master with a great saber and a small build.

He, too, had been half in doubt. No, he had thought it was a lie. Even so, he had accepted her because of the admirable way she had tried to protect her siblings, to protect her client, even at the cost of her life.

But the person she had spoken of truly existed?

If that man was indeed a master from the main cult as she had said, he should have stepped forward to greet him first, but they were still surrounded by the Seven Roads Pursuit Kill Squad, locked in a standoff with swords pointed at one another.

Of course, not only his own subordinates, but even the members of the Pursuit Kill Squad had their entire attention fixed on the newly appeared man.

And the person most shocked in this situation was Yo Goeng.

‘What is this? Who is this bastard?’

He was confused. What was contained in the face looking at the woman was innocence and embarrassment.

The one who had sent the Four flying with a single strike was making an expression like that? He was so bewildered that he even looked again at the corpses of the Four.

Yo Goeng secretly drew up internal energy into both hands. If the opportunity came, he intended to launch a surprise attack with his unique martial art, the Blood Hand Palm.

“Where have you come from?”

He asked as politely as possible.

Because the dozens of pieces of the Four scattered about so that one could not tell whose limbs were whose were telling him to be careful.

Seo Daeryong slowly turned toward him.

At that moment, Dana saw it. The shy look in his eyes when he had been looking at her turned colder than anything. For an instant, her heart pounded.

“Who are you?”

At Seo Daeryong’s calm question, Yo Goeng answered.

“I am called Yo Goeng, the one in charge of the Seven Roads Pursuit Kill Squad.”

He did not lower himself completely, nor did he act arrogantly. A chaotic battle would soon break out again, so he had to consider the morale of his subordinates, and he also had to consider his opponent’s skill.

“And who might you be?”

Instead of answering, Seo Daeryong drew the great saber planted before him. He handled the saber, which was as large as his own body, with ease. Because he was of small stature, the sight was all the more impressive.

Seo Daeryong was one who joked and blustered in front of Geom Mugeuk, but the Seo Daeryong standing before Yo Goeng had become a completely different person.

“The Seven Roads Pursuit Kill Squad. I’ve heard of you.”

Though he appeared calm on the outside, Seo Daeryong was furious to the top of his head. Had he been just a little later, she would have died at the hands of trash like these men.

“I am moved that you know of insignificant people like us.”

“It likely won’t be a good thing that I know of you.”

For the villains who fell into his hands, the end was either the prison or execution.

“When I heard the rumors about you, I had this thought.”

Yo Goeng tensed, wondering what Seo Daeryong would say. If possible, he hoped something favorable would be said so the atmosphere might flow in a good direction.

“Just let me get my hands on them once.”

Yo Goeng’s expression hardened coldly.

“However, if you answer this question, I may forgive you.”

At the word forgive, the corners of Yo Goeng’s eyes quivered with killing intent. He forcibly endured the urge to unleash a palm strike at once.

“What question?”

“Who commissioned this job?”

Yo Goeng answered coldly.

“If it were a client we could tell you about, would we have climbed over this wall?”

His determination never to speak could be felt clearly.

Rather, a relieved smile appeared at the corner of Seo Daeryong’s mouth.

From that smile, Yo Goeng could tell.

‘He wants to kill me.’

Instead, Yo Goeng softened his expression.

“We’ve seen enough blood, so I will withdraw here. What do you say? If you simply let us go…….”

The palm technique burst forth from both of Yo Goeng’s hands as he looked for an opening.

Puaaaaaang!

A palm technique of red energy shot from Yo Goeng’s hands and flew toward Seo Daeryong. It was a move of terrifying power.

Swaaaaaaak!

At the same time, Seo Daeryong’s great saber cut across the air.

Kwaaang!

With an explosive roar, the two energies collided. Yo Goeng had expected that his Blood Hand Palm might be blocked by the opponent’s attack. So he had been preparing to unleash his next move in succession.

But he had not expected the opponent’s saber qi to cleave through his palm technique and continue flying straight toward him.

‘No!’

The young master standing before him had opened the Ren and Du meridians and had even consumed two spiritual medicines. The martial art such a man displayed was none other than the demonic art of the Demon Sovereign. And above all, she was watching.

No matter how successful a wandering master Yo Goeng was, he could not block the Heaven-Annihilating Demonic Saber Form that such a Seo Daeryong unleashed.

Heaven-Annihilating Demonic Saber Form, First Form: Annihilating Saber Strike.

The saber qi split the red palm technique and flew on, striking Yo Goeng’s body.

Peooooong!

Yo Goeng’s body shattered and burst apart before their eyes.

Seeing the pieces of the corpse patter down in all directions, the seventy or so members of the Seven Roads Pursuit Kill Squad there were appalled. To them, Yo Goeng had been a terrifying man whose eyes they could scarcely meet.

They truly had never dreamed he would die like this in a single strike.

A suffocating silence flowed.

The one who broke the silence was Gangdal.

He spoke to those who had their swords pointed at him.

“Your squad leader is dead. Lower your swords.”

At his words, the members of the Seven Roads Pursuit Kill Squad looked at one another, at a loss.

Shwik! Puk! Shwik! Puuuk!

Gangdal thrust his sword and stabbed two of the Pursuit Kill Squad members in front of him in succession.

The two, who had not been focused, collapsed one after another, spraying blood.

“If you don’t throw away your swords, you die!”

At Gangdal’s ferocious momentum, a dozen or so nearby lowered their swords.

But there were even more who became excited and ran wild instead.

“Kill that bastard!”

“Kill him no matter what!”

Over twenty members of the Pursuit Kill Squad charged toward Gangdal. It was clear why these men were called madmen. Even in a situation like this, they were the sort to rush in while swinging their swords.

“Protect the branch leader!”

Just as the demonic practitioners were about to step forward to block them.

Swaaaaaaang!

Seo Daeryong’s great saber once again split the air.

Heaven-Annihilating Demonic Saber Form, Second Form: Annihilating Saber Wave.

Peopeopeopeopeopeok!

The charging Pursuit Kill Squad members were swept up in a wave of saber qi and burst apart. With that single move, all those who had been running forward were reduced to bloody pulp and scattered in every direction.

Dealing with villains was Seo Daeryong’s usual work. More than anyone, he knew their nature well. Strong against the weak and weak against the strong, if you showed even the slightest opening, they would bite. Today, there would be no mercy bestowed upon them.

Seo Daeryong pointed his saber at those who still had not thrown away their swords.

Even madmen had their limits. The remaining members of the Pursuit Kill Squad could no longer hold out and flung their swords away.

The demonic practitioners of the branch suppressed their internal energy and made them all kneel. It was not something to rejoice over, thinking they had saved their lives. Once they were imprisoned in the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s prison, agony worse than death would be waiting for them anyway.

Gangdal approached Seo Daeryong.

“Thank you truly for your help. I am Gangdal, the branch leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s Jeongan Branch.”

Everyone’s eyes focused on Seo Daeryong.

Only then did Seo Daeryong reveal his identity.

“I am Seo Daeryong, Pavilion Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s Hwangcheon Pavilion.”

In that instant, Gangdal was startled out of his wits. It was truly a position he had never imagined. It was not only Gangdal who was shocked; the other demonic practitioners were the same.

There was an enormous difference between a branch leader and a pavilion master. And he was not just any pavilion master.

‘The Hwangcheon Pavilion Master?’

Hwangcheon Pavilion was the inspection organization that investigated treason, misconduct, and corruption within the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. By the nature of the organization, it was the most powerful organization, taking precedence over every other organization within the cult.

The ghosts who dragged away demonic practitioners, the Hwangcheon Pavilion that made demonic practitioners tremble—it was truly one of the real powers among real powers of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult!

Gangdal, who had been standing blankly, quickly cupped his fist and bowed his head.

“I greet the Hwangcheon Pavilion Master.”

Chak, chak, chak.

At that, the demonic practitioners of the branch also straightened their posture all at once and paid their respects in disciplined unison.

“We greet the Pavilion Master!”

Their voices rang out thunderously.

Gangdal was delighted. It was surprising enough that her words had truly been true, but that master was the Hwangcheon Pavilion Master?

‘Something like this is happening to me?’

Perhaps because of this incident, bright sunlight might shine upon his life.

‘Perhaps I may even be sent to the main cult!’

He could only earnestly hope that it would truly happen.

When Seo Daeryong revealed his identity, Dana and Danyeon were also startled. In truth, they did not know much about Hwangcheon Pavilion.

But judging from the reactions of those fearsome branch demonic practitioners, he was certainly someone of high status.

Having revealed his identity, Seo Daeryong turned back toward Dana.

The fearsome man who had blown enemies apart with a single stroke, the man who held a high position in the Demon Cult, was smiling as he looked at her. He was embarrassed, just as he had been that day. His eyes were pure, just as they had been that day.

Seo Daeryong asked her,

“You seem to be hurt. How is your body?”

“I’m all right.”

At that moment, Danbi and Chohui came out of the building. Danbi, who had awakened late, had heard the sounds of fighting outside and come out to help her older sister despite Chohui’s attempts to stop her.

But they were shocked by the scene unfolding in the courtyard. Corpses were strewn about, and the Seven Roads Pursuit Kill Squad were all kneeling.

Her startled gaze turned toward Seo Daeryong, who stood in the center of everyone.

‘That man?’

She naturally recognized Seo Daeryong. How could she not know the man who had followed her sister out and revealed himself to be a demonic practitioner?

Before her younger sister could misspeak, Dana quickly said,

“He is the Hwangcheon Pavilion Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. He saved our lives, so greet him at once.”

At the words Hwangcheon Pavilion Master, Danbi was startled. He really had been a demonic practitioner, and on top of that, someone of high rank?

Danbi bowed her head respectfully.

“I will not forget this grace.”

At that moment, the youngest, Danyeon, whispered to Dana,

“I take back what I said about your standards in men being low, Sister.”

Dana’s face reddened.

The moment the tension loosened like that, she suddenly vomited blood. The internal injury she had been suppressing had flared up.

“Are you all right? Are you all right?”

The startled Seo Daeryong rushed over and seated her on the spot. He was so shocked and flustered that Dana was the one who became even more flustered.

“I’m fine.”

“Fine, my foot! Please come out and help quickly.”

Gangdal said urgently,

“I will call a physician.”

“I was not speaking to you.”

Seo Daeryong looked up into the air and said,

“Please help quickly!”

Then someone leapt lightly down from the roof.

It was Geom Mugeuk, who had been watching the situation.

He had deliberately not stepped forward so Seo Daeryong could finish this matter alone. Because he had wanted today’s protagonist to be Seo Daeryong until the end.

Even while vomiting blood, Dana looked after her younger sister first.

“My sister is hurt too. Please look at my sister first.”

“Sister, what are you saying! Please look at my sister first.”

Geom Mugeuk first injected a strand of internal energy into Dana’s back and examined the state of her body. When it came to the internal injuries of martial artists, he was superior to most physicians.

“Because the blood was not bursting inside and was instead vomited out, you avoided a serious injury.”

Fortunately, both women had avoided fatal internal injuries. They were injuries that would heal if they rested and recovered for a while.

“Thank you. I will not forget the grace of your treatment.”

When Dana expressed her gratitude, Geom Mugeuk passed the credit to Seo Daeryong.

“Give your thanks to Pavilion Master Seo. He ran here without rest to save you.”

Dana looked at Seo Daeryong. The sound of his rough breathing from earlier was vivid in her ears. If not for him, she and her sister would have died on the spot.

At her gaze, Seo Daeryong made an awkward expression. Just like the shy gaze that had first appealed to her, his expression now appealed to her as well.

Seeing the looks passing between the two of them, Geom Mugeuk smiled.

Seeing that, the youngest, Danyeon, asked Geom Mugeuk,

“And who are you, young hero? I would like to know your name.”

Everyone naturally thought this young martial artist was a subordinate of the Hwangcheon Pavilion Master.

In that instant, Seo Daeryong thought, Ah.

‘I got so caught up in a woman that I didn’t even introduce the Young Cult Leader.’

Seo Daeryong spoke loudly to the branch demonic practitioners, including Gangdal.

“He is the Young Cult Leader of our cult. Everyone, pay your respects!”

Young Cult Leader?

At first, Gangdal thought he had heard wrong. It made sense for the Hwangcheon Pavilion Master to have come because he had sought him out by letter, but the Young Cult Leader?

“What are you all doing?”

At Seo Daeryong’s shout, the demonic practitioners, including Gangdal, cupped their fists in unison and bent at the waist.

“We greet the Young Cult Leader.”

With even that Young Cult Leader of many rumors appearing, none of them could collect themselves.

The women were just as shocked. They, too, knelt together.

‘That person was the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult!’

Geom Mugeuk had them all rise.

Seo Daeryong deeply bowed his head to Geom Mugeuk.

“My heart was in haste, and I committed a discourtesy.”

No matter how urgent the situation had been, he should have introduced Geom Mugeuk first and then had him treat them. The closer one was, the more courtesy one had to show. That was what Geom Mugeuk always said.

Looking at Seo Daeryong, who was full of apology, Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly.

How could he not know what Seo Daeryong was feeling? He was simply happy that the bond between Seo Daeryong and Dana had continued.

“I understand, Pavilion Master.”

Because Dana was watching, Geom Mugeuk also cupped his fist respectfully and paid him courtesy.

“Pavilion Master, you are the person I trust and cherish most. The person you seek to protect is someone I, too, must protect.”

Geom Mugeuk was treating him with the greatest courtesy he had shown since meeting Seo Daeryong.

How could Seo Daeryong not know the heart of this man, who was treating him so honorably because he was before the woman he liked?

‘……Thank you, truly.’

Dana looked at Seo Daeryong with eyes that felt even more renewed. This man was trusted by the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult.

Gangdal’s heart was pounding in his own way. It was astonishing enough that the Hwangcheon Pavilion Master had come, but he simply could not believe the Young Cult Leader was at his branch.

Because as a demonic practitioner, he was undergoing an experience difficult to have even once in a lifetime.

Even if he could not go to the main cult, he would be able to live boasting about this memory alone for a long, long time.

At that moment, his subordinates, on guard, all drew their swords at once.

Gangdal’s gaze turned outside.

Far in the distance, a carriage was racing toward them.

Chapter 529: This Is a Dream, a Dream.
--------------------------------------
Gangdal lightly launched himself up and landed atop the wall.

“Only one carriage is approaching. There’s one man seated on the driver’s bench…”

In that instant, his gaze turned down below the wall.

Looking down, Gangdal was startled. Someone was standing beneath the wall, where no one should have been.

And it was not just one person. At intervals of three or four steps, massive martial artists stood surrounding the wall with their backs to it.

Gangdal flung himself backward and shouted.

“Get away from the wall!”

The branch martial artists standing near the wall promptly withdrew.

“There’s someone outside the wall!”

Gangdal assumed they were people who had come to assist Yogweng and the Chilro Chusal Squad.

‘When on earth did they get here? I didn’t notice them at all until they had surrounded the wall.’

No matter how much his attention had been taken by the Young Cult Leader and the Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion, to have been this oblivious…

Then a natural doubt arose.

Even if he had failed to notice them, the Young Cult Leader or the Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion should have noticed, shouldn’t they?

Could it be that those men were greater masters than those two? That couldn’t be.

Gangdal looked at Geom Mugeuk and Seo Daeryong.

Even after receiving his report, the two of them were not surprised in the slightest. From that reaction, he understood.

‘Ah! They’re the Young Cult Leader’s subordinates!’

It was only natural. There was no way the Young Cult Leader would move alone. Come to think of it, they had their backs to the wall. If they had been aiming for them, they would have been facing the wall.

‘If they had launched a surprise attack, we would all be dead.’

Once again, Gangdal marveled at the abilities of the demonic practitioners from the main cult.

‘Well, of course they’re strong. They’re the ones who protect the Young Cult Leader. If you only live once, this is how you ought to live.’

Meanwhile, the carriage arrived at the entrance.

‘Whoever it is, they picked the wrong day.’

Gangdal walked a few steps forward. Even if the Young Cult Leader and the Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion were here, he was the master of this branch.

Gangdal spoke to the man on the driver’s bench who had brought the carriage.

“Esteemed guests are visiting our branch today. We are receiving no visitors whatsoever, so please return and visit another time.”

But the carriage showed no intention of leaving.

Gangdal observed the man seated on the driver’s bench. He did not reveal any particular aura, but Gangdal instinctively knew he was no ordinary man. If so, the person inside the carriage must also be of considerable status. Even so.

‘You bastards, the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult is here right now. If you don’t want to die, get lost!’

If he shouted that, they would probably flee without looking back, wouldn’t they?

“Leave while I am still asking politely.”

Still, the carriage did not move.

The one who moved was one of the huge martial artists standing beneath the wall.

‘So the Young Cult Leader’s guards are finally moving.’

That was what Gangdal thought.

But the martial artist did not head toward the carriage. Instead, he entered the branch.

The moment they saw the martial artist entering, filling the doorway completely, Gangdal and the branch’s demonic practitioners all held their breath. The scar cutting across his face, the fierce gaze sweeping over his surroundings—when their eyes met his, their entire bodies trembled.

He was none other than the Demon Army Lord, Jang Ho.

The Demon Army had been returning to the cult, following the carriage Geom Ujin was riding in at a slight distance. Then, after receiving word from Tongcheon Pavilion, they had come here to attend upon the Heavenly Demon.

Since the Cult Leader was visiting a branch of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, how could they fail to observe proper formality? Thus, all the Demon Army soldiers were wearing the formal uniforms that revealed their status.

At that moment, Gangdal saw the characters embroidered on Jang Ho’s chest.

Demon Army.

Gangdal was shocked.

‘Could it be?’

Then Seo Daeryong said,

“Pay your respects. This is the Demon Army Lord, who leads our cult’s Demon Army.”

At the words “Demon Army Lord,” Gangdal was so astonished that his mind went blank for a moment. Truly, he had believed there could be nothing left today to surprise him.

The eyes of the branch’s demonic practitioners shone as they looked at Jang Ho. To ordinary demonic practitioners, the Demon Army held a special meaning.

The Demon Army, the finest elite organization of the main cult.

They were deployed on the most dangerous missions, and when war broke out, they stood at the vanguard. They were the dream and object of admiration for ordinary demonic practitioners.

Gangdal and the branch’s demonic practitioners all cupped their fists at once and bowed their heads.

“We greet the Demon Army Lord.”

Gangdal still believed the Demon Army had come forward to guard the Young Cult Leader. As far as his mind could conceive regarding today’s visit to the branch, the highest possible person involved was the Young Cult Leader.

With Jang Ho at the lead, the Demon Army soldiers began entering one after another.

Their overwhelming presence, with their demonic energy unconcealed, made not only the demonic practitioners but even the kneeling members of the Chilro Chusal Squad shrink back. Merely looking at them was intimidating.

Receiving gazes of awe, the Demon Army soldiers filed inside. Even one of them would have been chilling, yet they kept entering without end.

The four women stared at them in bewilderment. They did not know much about the Demon Army, but their massive physiques and fierce aura filled them with fear.

After looking at Geom Mugeuk and Seo Daeryong, then looking at these men, they felt as though they were seeing true demonic practitioners.

The Demon Army soldiers lined up on both sides from the entrance, forming a path.

‘They aren’t trying to block the carriage?’

Gangdal’s gaze turned toward the carriage. Just as he watched with a sinking suspicion, the carriage door opened.

The first person to step down from the carriage was the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

Jang Ho said to the demonic practitioners,

“This is the Demon Sovereign of our cult, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.”

At those words, the demonic practitioners were once again struck with shock. When would they ever have had the chance to see a Demon Sovereign in a small rural branch like this?

For Gangdal and the branch demonic practitioners alike, this was their first time seeing a Demon Sovereign in person.

“We greet the Demon Sovereign!”

They shouted thunderously as they paid their respects. Gangdal’s heart trembled.

‘To think the day would come when I’d see a Demon Sovereign with my own eyes!’

He had heard all sorts of terrifying rumors about Demon Sovereigns. From now on, if he so much as breathed wrong, he would die. He disciplined his subordinates with his gaze.

‘Be careful! Watch your words and actions! Don’t even look at him!’

His well-trained subordinates answered with their eyes.

‘Do not worry!’

But their shock was only just beginning.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon, after stepping down from the carriage, stood respectfully beside it and waited for someone to descend.

At last, Geom Ujin stepped down from the carriage.

The moment he saw Geom Ujin, Gangdal froze. He had seen many martial artists in his life, but this was the first time he had felt such a presence. The Blood Heaven Saber Demon, who had descended first, had also displayed a tremendous presence, but it could not be compared to this man before him.

Geom Ujin slowly moved forward and walked between the rows of Demon Army soldiers.

Watching that sight, Gangdal’s eyes grew wider and wider. Who was it that a Demon Sovereign would treat with such utmost respect? Who in this martial world could walk upon a path formed by the Demon Army?

Jang Ho declared in a loud voice,

“All of you, show respect to the Cult Leader!”

One of the demonic practitioners let out a scream of terror, then clamped a hand over his own mouth. But no one paid him any attention.

Gangdal and the demonic practitioners all prostrated themselves at once and shouted,

“The Divine Cult is undefeated! The Heavenly Demon endures forever! We greet the august Cult Leader.”

Gangdal was half out of his mind. The Cult Leader had visited his branch?

‘Is this a dream?’

The Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion had come, the Young Cult Leader had come, the Demon Army had come, a Demon Sovereign had come. Even that much was already like a dream, and now the Cult Leader as well?

‘Yes, this is a dream. A dream.’

Even though he knew it was not a dream, for that instant, he thought so.

The women, if anything, were not as surprised as they were.

The Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult was like someone from another world to them.

In the martial world, there was the Heavenly Demon, the Martial Alliance Leader, and the Evil Alliance Leader? That felt similar to saying there was a sun, a moon, and stars in the sky.

What surprised them was not that the Demon Cult Leader had come, but that this Demon Cult Leader was someone they knew.

The youngest, Danyeon, gasped.

“It’s him!”

When the three women saw Geom Ujin’s face, they were truly shocked. He was the man they had seen at the inn that day. And more than anything else—

‘The person we served food to was the Heavenly Demon?’

The three women looked at one another in astonishment.

The youngest’s eyes said this:

‘It was me! Me! I was the one who said we should send him food.’

The youngest’s taste in men was the Heavenly Demon?

Chohui stood behind them.

Unlike the bewildered women, Chohui knew very well just how astonishing and impossible this situation was.

She was a courtesan of Cheonhwaru. The person who appeared most often in the stories drunken martial artists blustered about was that man over there.

From all the boasts that began with “When I met the Heavenly Demon,” to the endless terror of the Heavenly Demon’s descent and the destruction of the martial world.

After hearing all those countless stories, there was at least one thing she knew for certain.

He was someone she would never see even once in her lifetime.

And yet that person was now walking toward her. That was why, more than anyone else, she understood just how unreal this moment was.

That unreal man walked between the Demon Army soldiers. The rugged Demon Army soldiers, who would look perfectly suited standing guard before the gates of hell, wore expressions more respectful than ever. Everything about the sight was unreal.

In that way, Geom Ujin reached the end of the formation and turned around.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon stood beside Geom Ujin as though guarding him.

Step, step, step.

The Demon Army soldiers who had stood in formation moved with discipline, taking positions to guard all directions.

Several Demon Army soldiers stood in front of the Chilro Chusal Squad, who were kneeling with their internal energy suppressed. It was not to protect them, but to block those filthy wretches from the Cult Leader’s sight.

Under the terrifying gazes of the Demon Army soldiers looking down at them, the men could not even breathe loudly and lowered their heads.

Jang Ho had the branch’s demonic practitioners and the women, who had been kneeling and prostrating themselves, all rise. They did not dare meet his eyes and kept their heads lowered.

Geom Mugeuk went before his father and greeted him.

“You have arrived.”

Geom Ujin nodded once, then looked at Seo Daeryong.

His gaze asked what had happened.

Seo Daeryong respectfully reported to Geom Ujin.

“Thanks to your concern, we safely rescued Lady Dana and her younger sisters.”

When their names were mentioned, Dana’s heart began to pound.

Her name was being mentioned in a conversation between the Demon Cult Leader and the Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion.

It did not feel real. Like Gangdal, this moment felt like a dream to her.

Then Geom Ujin’s calm question sounded, as if to tell her it was no dream.

“Which heroine is the one called Dana?”

Dana’s heart dropped as though she had fallen from a great height. The Demon Cult Leader was looking for her!

Seo Daeryong was even more flustered. He had never imagined the Cult Leader would ask who she was.

Seo Daeryong’s gaze turned toward Dana.

‘Will you be all right?’

But she was braver and bolder than Seo Daeryong had thought. Being surprised was one thing. When she spoke, she did so clearly, without faltering.

“My name is Dana. It is an honor to meet one so noble. Under the protection of the Divine Cult, and with the help of the Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion, my sisters and I were able to preserve our lives. I sincerely thank you.”

Geom Ujin’s deep gaze seemed almost as if it could peer into a person’s heart, but the reason he had asked to see her was not to judge what sort of person she was.

“I am glad you are safe.”

Dana opened her eyes wide and looked at Geom Ujin. How could she not be shocked?

The Heavenly Demon was telling her he was glad she was safe.

“Thank you for your concern.”

At her trembling words, Geom Ujin merely nodded and said nothing else. That was enough.

Geom Mugeuk could tell. This was his father’s consideration for Seo Daeryong. Just as he himself had shown her the utmost courtesy, his father had shown his trust in Seo Daeryong by asking after her safety.

How could Seo Daeryong not understand that?

‘I will repay this grace with my life.’

It was the moment a wall of loyalty, like the Great Demonic Wall of the Nine Flower Demonic Art, was completed—one that nothing could bring down.

If it was for a Cult Leader and Young Cult Leader like these, then even if they cursed and hated him for the rest of his life, he could die for them.

Seo Daeryong silently bowed his head to Geom Ujin, expressing his gratitude.

There was also consideration from his drinking friend, Jang Ho.

Jang Ho was the first to pay proper respects to Seo Daeryong as well.

“I greet the Pavilion Lord.”

“I greet the Army Lord.”

Dana was surprised once more to see that terrifying Demon Army Lord show Seo Daeryong such respectful courtesy. Today, what kind of person Seo Daeryong was was being properly engraved upon her heart.

If anything, it felt too burdensome. The fact that she had drawn the attention of someone so remarkable.

And yet, in Seo Daeryong’s eyes as he continued to look at her, then hastily turned away whenever their eyes met, there was still only bashfulness.

Gangdal and the branch demonic practitioners lined up before Geom Ujin. Since the Cult Leader had visited, they offered him their formal greetings.

Standing before the Cult Leader, Gangdal’s heart swelled. He could die now with no regrets.

‘I am the only branch leader to have received the Cult Leader at his branch!’

This was truly something he could boast about after gathering every branch leader in the vicinity.

At that moment, unexpected praise flowed from the Cult Leader’s mouth.
