“It may not measure up to that fish dish, but I can cook a thing or two myself.”

It was her way of asking them to take her along on that journey too.

Even after being told that she might become the central figure in an incident that would turn the entire murim upside down, such a joke came naturally to her. It was a moment that made her feel anew just how solid a fence these two people before her formed around her life.

“Of course. Naturally, you must come with us.”

When Geugak Soma shook his head as if to say they would go alone, Geom Mugeuk gave her a look that said, I will escort her myself. Just trust me.

It was at times like this that Yeojeong felt Geugak Soma had changed. In the past, it would have been unimaginable for him to shake his head in jest in a situation like this.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Yeojeong and greeted her formally.

“Have you been well, Tower Mistress?”

Earlier, he had let her reunite with Chohui first while he exchanged greetings with Geugak Soma. Only now was he properly greeting her.

“How could I merely have been well? Thanks to the Young Cult Leader’s advance warning, my life was saved. Not only mine—you saved everyone in this tower.”

“That was because Soma-nim came and protected you.”

“Orabeoni gives the credit to the Young Cult Leader, and the Young Cult Leader gives the credit to Orabeoni. Then shall I receive help from both of you this time?”

With that, she naturally relayed exactly what Chohui had told her moments ago.

After hearing the story, Geom Mugeuk’s expression hardened.

Now that it had been revealed that the enemies were targeting Cheonhwaru and its mistress, this matter would have to be handled more carefully and decisively than any other.

“If they say the one who will commit that incident is you, Tower Mistress, it likely means they intend to frame you for the crime.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, all three nodded.

“How did you obtain the information?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Chohui answered.

“Even heavy secrets that overcome the fear of death and even torture sometimes fail to overcome drunkenness.”

She did not explain in detail. But the men who had pursued her had already proven that the information she had obtained was true.

“I sincerely ask that the two of you please protect the Tower Mistress.”

Chohui bowed respectfully to Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma. When she had first fled here, her heart had been filled with unbearable unease. Even if she managed to convey this information to the Tower Mistress, she had been trapped in the ominous feeling that, in the end, the Tower Mistress might still die at their hands.

But now it was different.

She looked around. Terrifying Faceless Guests stood guard here and there, frightening merely to behold.

Then her gaze turned back to Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma. On the way here, she had experienced enough to know what kind of person Geom Mugeuk was.

And when she looked at the cold smile on that pure white mask of Geugak Soma’s, the thought naturally came to her.

‘Shouldn’t you people be more afraid now?’

Geom Mugeuk said to Chohui,

“Leave the matter to us now and rest well.”

“Yes! I will.”

After greeting the two of them cheerfully, she looked at Yeojeong one last time.

Yeojeong said to her,

“If I live, it will be thanks to you.”

Then Chohui returned the words Yeojeong had once said to her.

“No, it will be fate that saved you, Tower Mistress. The warm fate that led you to save a little girl being sold away.”

She bowed deeply and withdrew.

After walking some distance away, she turned back and asked,

“May I drink? I’ve held back for far too long.”

“You may drink it all.”

At Yeojeong’s words, Chohui’s steps as she turned away were light. Now everything she had to do was finished.

Watching her back, Geom Mugeuk said to Yeojeong,

“She is a good bond.”

“As you can see, I’m rather blessed with people.”

As she said that, she looked at Geugak Soma. Geugak Soma stood there without a word. Feeling that she alone had perhaps been flaunting her affection too openly, Yeojeong asked after one person.

“How is the pretty heart?”

“She should be well.”

“You must have set your heart far away.”

“I wanted her to become a heart that is both pretty and strong.”

Come to think of it, Yeojeong had told Ian that she would become someone precious.

Ian, are you doing well?

After they had exchanged greetings for the first time in a long while, Geom Mugeuk asked her,

“Do you have any guesses as to who might be behind this matter?”

Yeojeong shook her head.

“No.”

She had quietly grown Cheonhwaru and had never become entangled in any affair of the murim.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze naturally turned toward one person. It was Jaein, lying collapsed with his acupoints sealed.

“Who is that man?”

At Geugak Soma’s question, Geom Mugeuk answered,

“He is a subordinate of the man tasked with pursuing Lady Cho. We’re in a situation where we need someone very frightening to make him open his mouth.”

A cold laugh flowed from the empty eyeholes of Geugak Soma’s mask.

“You need not look far.”

* * *

Jaein regained consciousness and opened his eyes.

‘Ah! I was captured by the Young Cult Leader, wasn’t I?’

He remembered his acupoints being sealed by Geom Mugeuk.

‘He was rambling about some strange Young Alliance Leader business.’

As Jaein looked around, he flinched in surprise. Only then did he realize that someone was standing in the room where he had awakened, back turned, looking out the window.

“Where have you brought me?”

Jaein thought the man was Geom Mugeuk.

“No matter how much you pressure me, there are things that can be done and things that cannot. The one I serve places great meaning on protecting the client’s secret. So even if you go to him, you will not be able to learn who the client is…”

Just then, the door opened and Geom Mugeuk entered. When Jaein saw him, he was startled.

“What are you talking so much for? Have you already become friends?”

Then the man who had been standing with his back to the window slowly turned around.

The instant Jaein saw him, his eyes flew wide open. That mask, together with the Young Cult Leader? He could tell at a glance who it was.

“Geugak Soma!”

After blurting out the name without realizing it, he was frightened and quickly covered his mouth with his hand. The hand over his mouth trembled. A chill ran through his entire body.

No matter how much one acted strong, to a martial artist, the existence known as Geugak Soma evoked a primal terror. The wicked fear that if it were Geugak Soma, he would not simply kill you even if he killed you.

He remembered what Geom Mugeuk had said just before he lost consciousness. That a truly frightening person had come to Cheonhwaru. So that person had been Geugak Soma!

Geugak Soma slowly approached, stared at Jaein, and asked coldly,

“Was it you who sent the Ghost Killing Squad to Cheonhwaru?”

Jaein felt a sense of danger, as if his entire body would be torn apart on the spot if he said yes.

“No.”

“Then who was it?”

For a moment, Jaein could not say anything.

Then his eyes met Geugak Soma’s. The instant he met the eyes within those eyeholes, Jaein felt a terror he had never experienced before. It was not frightening simply because the other party was Geugak Soma; those eyes themselves, as they appeared now, were far too terrifying. His will collapsed in an instant, and only his survival instinct wrapped around him.

“The person I serve sent them!”

Geugak Soma had not pointed a finger at his eyes, nor had he inflicted any pain, but in the end, Jaein could not overcome the fear inspired by the being called Geugak Soma.

Geugak Soma still said nothing, waiting for his next words.

“I will take you to that person!”

Only then did Geugak Soma walk back to the window and stand with his back turned.

Geom Mugeuk came over to Jaein.

“I understand. Even I, the Young Cult Leader, find him frightening, so how much more must it be for you?”

Jaein let out a long sigh.

“That person will absolutely never reveal the client. He is pathologically obsessed with upholding that principle.”

Then Geugak Soma, who was looking out the window, suddenly said,

“No matter what kind of man he is, he will speak in the end.”

After a brief pause, absolute faith was added.

“Because the Young Cult Leader will be the one to make this plan.”

* * *

Seo Danmyeong did not stay in one place.

He kept moving by carriage, and he always decided where meetings would take place.

Today, the carriage was stopped in an alley from which Cheonhwaru could be seen, in the opposite direction from yesterday.

Jaein climbed into the carriage and reported in a low voice,

“We failed.”

Seo Danmyeong’s face twitched at once.

“You mean Goeak failed to kill him?”

“No. He did not try to fight him in the first place. They knew each other.”

Jaein told him the truth.

“The man guarding the courtesan was none other than the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult.”

The emotion that came to Seo Danmyeong first was relief rather than surprise.

‘Of course!’

It felt as though all the questions that had been weighing him down throughout this matter had been resolved at once. When he had heard that even Goeak had failed, everything before his eyes had truly gone dark.

But upon hearing the words “Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult,” light descended into that darkness.

‘I’m not a failure! I was simply disgustingly unlucky.’

Suspicion naturally followed.

‘There’s no way the Young Cult Leader would have simply let him go.’

When a cold glint flickered in Seo Danmyeong’s eyes as he looked at him, Jaein spoke honestly.

“Yes, I have brought the God of Death with me. This carriage is being watched even now.”

“You mad bastard!”

Killing intent flashed in Seo Danmyeong’s eyes. Jaein had judged that the suspicious Seo Danmyeong would never believe him if he said he had come alone, so he had been honest even about that.

“Do you want to die by my hand?”

“No. It is because I trust you, Elder.”

“Because you trust me?”

“Because I trust that you will somehow find a way to escape.”

Slap!

Seo Danmyeong struck Jaein mercilessly across the cheek.

“You should have died there!”

He wanted to drive a dagger into Jaein’s lowered neck at once.

But he could not. If he killed him, the Demonic Cult bastards would rush in immediately and either capture him or kill him.

Then the door opened and Geom Mugeuk climbed into the carriage.

“You seemed to be getting emotional, so I came early. Ah, and all the subordinates hiding nearby have been put to sleep, so don’t harbor any useless expectations.”

Seo Danmyeong knew at once that the man who had appeared was the Young Cult Leader.

“Young Cult Leader.”

“I hear you are called the Netweaver?”

“That is so.”

“A person who weaves nets. A splendid name.”

Geom Mugeuk treated him with courtesy. Even kindness with transparent motives was still better than rudeness. Especially to a man who took pride in the name Netweaver.

“I can guess why you have come, but I cannot reveal who the client is. I may die by your hand, but my death will remain an honorable one.”

Half of that was his resolve not to let his spirit be broken, and half was sincerity. He did not want to die. But to exactly the same degree, he did not want to reveal the client.

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“You will die not by my hand, but by the client’s. And it will not be a particularly honorable death.”

Seo Danmyeong shook his head firmly.

“As long as I try to keep the secret, the client will not try to kill me. From the very beginning, that promise at least was made clear.”

“That is not why.”

Geom Mugeuk pointed out something he had not even considered.

“You nearly ruined their work.”

Not only Seo Danmyeong but Jaein as well wanted to know what he meant.

“They are trying to make use of the Mistress of Cheonhwaru to carry out their scheme. That is why the courtesan risked her life to reach the Tower Mistress. But you attacked Cheonhwaru and tried to kill everyone there.”

“That was—!”

It had been a stratagem to prevent the courtesan from making contact. He had not known that their objective was the Tower Mistress and Cheonhwaru itself.

“I didn’t know that!”

“Will they truly understand? No, even if they do, what of it? You have already ruined everything. I will leave here and announce that the Mistress of Cheonhwaru is dead. The enraged client will surely try to kill you.”

Then came Geom Mugeuk’s decisive card.

“I will wait, and through those who come to kill you, I will find out who made the request. Since they will think everything is over because the work has been ruined, they will try to erase all traces using their own people.”

“!”

“The life you have built up your whole life will end with you being killed by your client. Since you laid hands on us, you should at least be prepared for that much.”

For a moment, Seo Danmyeong was so flustered that no thoughts came to him.

Geom Mugeuk gave him no time to think and pressed him hard.

As Geom Mugeuk was about to get out of the carriage, Jaein shouted,

“Wait! If we tell you who the client is… will you spare us?”

Of course, it was a prearranged interruption and a prearranged persuasion.

“Elder, you cannot let it end like this. You must live and plan for the future.”

But the words that he would reveal the client did not readily come from Seo Danmyeong’s mouth.

Geom Mugeuk delivered the powerful blow he had prepared.

“No one except us will know that you revealed it.”

Geom Mugeuk’s—no, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s—solution, added coldly, powerfully tempted him.

“Because we intend to wipe out that client completely.”

Chapter 536: Grudges Suit People Like Us
----------------------------------------
That powerful temptation did not work.

Because Seo Danmyeong answered in a low voice.

“You cannot sweep them away.”

Geom Mugeuk was curious. Just who was the client, for him to say such a thing?

In any case, he had pushed him hard enough; now it was time to gently draw him back in.

“True. Force cannot solve every problem.”

Geom Mugeuk persuaded him with a gentle gaze.

“Justification is good, and principles are good, but shouldn’t you live first? Net-Knotter. I quite like that name. I hope you’ll continue weaving splendid nets in the future. And when you have time, help us with our work as well.”

The choice on the other side of this was death, so how could Seo Danmyeong’s heart not waver?

Jaein, who had been listening, stepped in to support Geom Mugeuk.

“How can we trust that you’ll spare us?”

That question was surely the very thing Seo Danmyeong most wanted to ask.

“Because it is the Young Cult Leader’s promise.”

Seo Danmyeong looked at Geom Mugeuk. He knew better than anyone that there was nothing more foolish than trusting a demon.

But if he refused even after the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult had gone this far, he would end up dying by the Demonic Cult’s hand first.

Jaein, too, looked at Seo Danmyeong with desperate eyes. Shouldn’t we live first?

When Seo Danmyeong still said nothing.

“Then just die.”

Leaving behind that low remark, Geom Mugeuk was about to open the carriage door and leave.

In the end, the name of one place slipped from Seo Danmyeong’s mouth.

“Baekgye Merchant Guild.”

The moment he heard those words, Jaein tilted his head. He had expected a far more grandiose name, but it was merely a merchant guild? Then why had he said the Demonic Cult could not sweep them away?

“Who are they, for you to say such a thing?”

Geom Mugeuk answered his question for him.

“Because the Sado Alliance is behind them.”

Baekgye Merchant Guild was a place Geom Mugeuk knew as well. He had come to know of them when he first left the cult with Geugak Soma in the past. They had advanced into Guizhou with the Sado Alliance’s full support.

Come to think of it, the place where he had first met Bi Sain had also been Baekgye Merchant Guild.

Afterward, once he had told Bi Sain what he wanted to know, they had withdrawn from Guizhou, where they had been making money in Geugak Soma’s territory.

And now that Baekgye Merchant Guild had appeared again.

“That’s right. I knew full well the Sado Alliance was behind Baekgye Merchant Guild, so I could not refuse their request.”

At Seo Danmyeong’s words, Jaein asked with a startled expression.

“Then does that mean the Sado Alliance plotted this affair?”

Seo Danmyeong did not answer, but that silence was clearly an affirmation.

Now they could understand why Seo Danmyeong had tried to carry out this matter even while overreaching himself. And why he had hesitated until the end to reveal the client.

They could also guess why he had mobilized Goeak, one of the Seven Great Masters of the Sado Alliance, at the very end. He must have thought they were on the same side.

“Who came from Baekgye Merchant Guild? Did the guild master, Seo Jeongtae, come in person?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Seo Danmyeong shook his head.

“The vice guild master came. He and I know each other.”

Seo Danmyeong spoke to Geom Mugeuk with a tense gaze.

“This is all I know. Will you keep your promise to spare me?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“Of course.”

The very moment the tension began to ease from Seo Danmyeong’s face.

Someone’s question came from outside.

“Is it done?”

Seo Danmyeong’s eyes widened, wondering what was going on. Jaein had heard that voice before, so he hunched in on himself.

“It’s done.”

The moment Geom Mugeuk’s answer ended.

A sharp sound of wind came slicing through the air.

Thud!

A Blood Calamity Finger pierced through the carriage wall and blew a hole through Seo Danmyeong’s face.

Whoosh! Whoosh! Whoosh!

The finger winds that kept flying in struck his body one after another.

Thud! Thud! Thud! Thud!

The Blood Calamity Fingers that continued to fly in and embed themselves even in a body already dead showed Geugak Soma’s rage.

When the finger winds stopped, the carriage was riddled with holes. Through those holes, they could see Geugak Soma standing ahead with his back turned.

Thinking that he would be next, Jaein, who had been clutching his head, raised it. His face was filled with fear and anger.

“You promised you’d spare him!”

“I did.”

The moment he sent the Ghostslayer Unit to Cheonhwaru, his fate had already been decided. There was no way Geugak Soma would ever spare someone who had tried to kill the master of Cheonhwaru.

Jaein looked at Geom Mugeuk in terror.

“Are you going to kill me too?”

Beyond the holes, Geugak Soma still stood with his back turned, looking up at the sky.

“Do you want to die?”

Jaein answered in a trembling voice.

“No. I want to live.”

“Then live.”

Geom Mugeuk opened the carriage door and went outside. The one who was more surprised was actually Jaein.

“Why are you sparing me?”

His was a life that could be ended with the slightest twitch of a finger.

Geom Mugeuk looked back at him and said,

“I told you I’d spare you if you helped me, didn’t I?”

If Geugak Soma had not been satisfied unless he killed him too, there would have been nothing he could do, but Geugak Soma ended the punishment with Seo Danmyeong’s death.

“And you said you were still learning, didn’t you?”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward Seo Danmyeong’s corpse.

“If you live like that man, you’ll end up like that someday too. Learn how to weave nets directly from the world. Only then will you weave your own net.”

Jaein could feel it. The Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult was giving him one chance.

Then Geugak Soma, who had been standing a distance away, turned toward them. The moment Jaein’s eyes met Geugak Soma’s, he lowered his head deeply. His heart pounded as if it would burst.

How much time had passed?

When Jaein raised his head again, the two of them had vanished before he knew it.

With the relief that he had survived, Jaein let out a long sigh. Only then did he feel as though he had been freed from the other man’s net, but soon he thought.

Perhaps the chance the Young Cult Leader had given him again was itself part of an even larger net.

* * *

When Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma returned, Cheonhwaru was operating normally.

Aside from a small number keeping watch over the surroundings, the Faceless Guests rested in the guest hall in the rear garden and did not show themselves.

“Baekgye Merchant Guild commissioned it?”

Yeo Jeong, too, looked surprised, as if it was unexpected.

As the one who ran Bihwa, she was well informed about many things. Naturally, she also knew about Baekgye Merchant Guild, which had been connected to Geugak Soma.

“Something is strange.”

On the way back, Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma had discussed the situation, and Geom Mugeuk had told Geugak Soma that Yeo Jeong, being a sharp-minded person, would surely see through the circumstances.

“At a glance, it looks like Baekgye Merchant Guild is taking revenge for the losses they suffered when they withdrew from Guizhou, but something is strange.”

The point she focused on was this.

“If they were an ordinary martial household, perhaps that would make sense. There may be no one who knows as well as I do how much martial artists stake their lives on empty pride. But Baekgye Merchant Guild are merchants. The losses they suffered then were monetary losses, so they would accept even greater losses just to take revenge for that?”

At that, Geom Mugeuk looked at Geugak Soma and said,

“As expected! Sister-in-law is wiser than anyone.”

At the word “sister-in-law,” Yeo Jeong’s face flushed slightly. Even after forming a bond with Geugak Soma, she had never once heard that word. Who would she have heard it from? This relationship itself was a secret. To be honest, she felt pleased.

Geugak Soma was also slightly flustered. But he knew very well with what intention the Young Cult Leader had said those words. The Young Cult Leader was continuing to be considerate of her and think of her. How could he not know for whose sake that consideration was?

Breaking the brief silence, Yeo Jeong continued.

“It’s not because I’m particularly wise, but because I know something about Baekgye Merchant Guild. Seo Jeongtae, the master of Baekgye Merchant Guild, is a man who values making money above all else. The reason he joined hands with the Sado Alliance was to maximize their profits. I cannot understand people like that spending such an enormous sum to settle an old grudge.”

Yeo Jeong smiled as she looked at the expressions of the two men.

“You have already discussed this part, haven’t you?”

Geugak Soma looked at her and said with a smile,

“Do not forget who we are with.”

It meant that, being with the Young Cult Leader, how could he have missed something like that?

In truth, when Geugak Soma first heard the name Baekgye Merchant Guild, he had been unable to think properly because of guilt and anger.

But through his conversation with Geom Mugeuk, he was able to calm himself, and he came to understand that this matter was not so simple.

Geom Mugeuk agreed with her words, giving a straightforward reason.

“That’s right. If the murim is thrown into an uproar, merchants suffer losses.”

And yet they had said that Cheonhwaru was plotting something that would throw the murim into an uproar.

“There seems to be some other hidden story behind this matter.”

Yeo Jeong revealed her honest feelings.

“To tell the truth, I had been greatly troubled, wondering who could harbor such a deep grudge against me.”

At that, Geugak Soma opened his heavy mouth.

“How could that be? Grudges are things suited to people like us.”

Geugak Soma hoped that the ultimate target of this grudge would be himself. He hoped Cheonhwaru or Yeo Jeong would merely be something that brushed past briefly within a great conspiracy, literally a fleeting journey.

“First, we must find that vice guild master of Baekgye Merchant Guild. I will send word to Tongcheongak and have them discover his whereabouts.”

It was then. From outside, one of Yeo Jeong’s subordinates announced that a guest had arrived.

“A guest has come and wishes to see the tower master.”

A guest had come at a time like this, of all times? After glancing once at Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma, Yeo Jeong asked her subordinate,

“Who did he say he was?”

“He says he is the vice guild master of Baekgye Merchant Guild.”

At the subordinate’s report, all three were startled.

Yeo Jeong asked the two men with her eyes what she should do.

Geom Mugeuk left the decision to Geugak Soma. After all, this matter was one in which he was protecting his woman.

Geugak Soma gave Yeo Jeong a single nod. The person they were looking for had come on his own feet; he was not someone to avoid him.

“Escort him to the main hall.”

* * *

Ping Wei, the vice guild master of Baekgye Merchant Guild, entered the main hall where the master of Cheonhwaru was waiting.

Ahead of him sat the master of Cheonhwaru, and about twenty paces away from her, a seat had been prepared for him. He thought that somewhere in the space between them, a martial artist protecting her was likely hidden.

“You are quite cautious.”

At his first words, which sounded somehow curt, Yeo Jeong welcomed him with a smile.

“Being a woman dealing with drunken guests has made me timid. I ask for your understanding.”

Their gazes passed back and forth, each probing the other.

Yeo Jeong knew the basics about the vice guild master of Baekgye Merchant Guild.

He was said to be the person Seo Jeongtae, the master of Baekgye Merchant Guild, trusted most, and he was known to possess considerable martial prowess. She also knew him to be a man whose pride was as strong as his martial skill.

Indeed, he displayed sharp eyes and an air that would not be easily dealt with.

“To what do we owe the visit of such an honored person?”

She now knew that Ping Wei before her was the one who had commissioned the Net-Knotter to kill Chohui, but Yeo Jeong showed none of that emotion.

“I came because I have a proposal for Cheonhwaru.”

“Please speak.”

“At the beginning of next month, an honored guest will come. We wish to rent out all of Cheonhwaru to entertain that person.”

It was common for murim sects to entertain martial artists at pleasure houses. But renting out an entire pleasure house as large as Cheonhwaru was not common.

“To rent our entire establishment would cost a great deal of money.”

Ping Wei’s eyes intensified, and the corners of his mouth rose slightly.

“I misspoke.”

Yeo Jeong politely bowed her head in apology. Only then did Ping Wei soften his gaze.

“Do not worry about money.”

“What sort of guest do you intend to entertain?”

“I cannot tell you.”

It was a firm answer.

She could sense it instinctively. This was the very event that would throw the murim into an uproar.

Yeo Jeong spoke to him politely.

“I am sorry, but as a matter of principle, we cannot rent out our entire establishment for a guest whose identity we do not know. If you truly wish to keep it secret, we will receive them in a special room.”

For an instant, Ping Wei’s expression twitched. He might have emitted killing intent to frighten her, but instead, he brought out something more powerful than killing intent.

“I will pay ten times Cheonhwaru’s daily income.”

Does he know how much we earn, to offer ten times that?

In any case, it did not work on Yeo Jeong.

“There are things more important than money.”

Ping Wei’s gaze sank.

Yeo Jeong could read his thoughts from that gaze. A mere woman running a pleasure house is talking about things more important than money?

“If the secret leaks, your life may be in danger. Do you still wish to hear?”

Even at Ping Wei’s threat, Yeo Jeong silently nodded.

“A principle is a principle.”

When he sensed that Yeo Jeong would absolutely not back down, Ping Wei finally yielded.

“Very well. Then I will tell you. But the secret must be kept without fail.”

“Do not worry. Did you not come here in the first place because you trusted our establishment’s credibility?”

After staring at her in silence for a moment, an astonishing person’s name came from his mouth.

“We will be receiving the young alliance leader of the Sado Alliance.”

Yeo Jeong was shocked. She did not hide that shock. To Ping Wei, it looked as though she was shocked by the fact that the young alliance leader of the Sado Alliance was coming, but Yeo Jeong’s shock was different.

‘They intend to kill the young alliance leader of the Sado Alliance that day!’

The murim would be thrown into an uproar, and she would be made to bear the blame for it.

“Are you satisfied now?”

“No, I regret it. I should not have asked the name and should have refused to the end.”

Ping Wei smiled faintly. Her feeling burdened was only natural. And the moment she heard the name, refusing became impossible.

“If you need anything, contact us.”

“I will.”

Ping Wei rose from his seat and left the main hall.

Once he had completely departed, Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma opened the secret door at the rear and walked out.

“No wonder that frightening face has kept coming to mind lately.”

Geom Mugeuk was speaking casually, but Geugak Soma and Yeo Jeong could tell. His gaze had grown cold.

“We were planning to gather everyone together once anyway.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and said to Yeo Jeong,

“Well then, are you ready to turn the murim upside down?”

Chapter 537: Where Death Awaits
-------------------------------
When Geom Mugeuk, returning from an outing, entered the inner courtyard of Cheonhwaru, Geukak Soma stood on the roof with arms crossed.

"I don't see the faceless guests?"

"I sent them all to the nearby residences."

Since they had already realized those fellows' intentions, there was no longer any need to guard this place unnecessarily.

Geom Mugeuk lightly leaped onto the roof.

"Where did you go?"

"I went around sending urgent messages here and there."

"The Young Alliance Leader of the Heretic Alliance was among them, I presume."

"I'll go meet the Young Alliance Leader in person."

Geukak Soma knew how Geom Mugeuk handled affairs. He did not try to accomplish things alone. He informed whomever needed to be informed in advance, discussed, and prepared.

What mattered to him was everyone's safety. In a situation like this, Bisain's safety would become the most important task. Precisely because he harbored no desire to become a hero alone, he often ended up becoming one.

"They failed to kill young lady Chu, yet they're still pushing this matter. Those fellows must have another objective."

Geukak Soma said, looking at Geom Mugeuk.

"They may have changed their target to the Young Cult Lord."

"Perhaps."

Then everything would truly be turned upside down.

Geukak Soma knew. He knew what the relationship between the Cult Lord and Geom Mugeuk had been like recently. Should something happen to the Young Cult Lord, unimaginable things would occur.

"What should I do?"

Geukak Soma knew that the more difficult the situation, the wiser it was to trust Geom Mugeuk's judgment.

"Those fellows always plot schemes from behind, sow discord, and attempt assassinations. What choice do we have? We have no choice but to become stronger. We must make all those petty tricks become opportunities for growth and experience."

Geom Mugeuk added with an intense gaze.

"I know you've grown stronger lately. Become even stronger."

Geukak Soma's eyes also shone intensely.

"Yes, I understand."

"In that sense, teach me a move when I return."

Geukak Soma could guess Geom Mugeuk's intention. Even though his own martial realm had risen recently, he still could not match Geom Mugeuk's martial arts.

In the end, that match would be one that made him stronger.

"Very well."

Geukak Soma gratefully accepted that sentiment. These days, with changes in his martial realm, training with Geom Mugeuk would greatly help his martial arts. Geom Mugeuk proposed it precisely because he knew that as well.

Just then, the owner of Cheonhwaru could be seen walking with entertainers in the distance. She was preparing for that day in her own way.

Geukak Soma spoke in a low voice.

"Thank you for looking after her."

Geukak Soma could tell that Yeojeong had been greatly moved, especially by the word sister-in-law.

"Of course I should. She's my first sister-in-law."

Geukak Soma asked with his eyes.

When will I get to see my first sister-in-law?

Geom Mugeuk's eyes answered.

Who knows. Will I really be able to see her in this lifetime?

And so the two stood on the windy roof, looking down at Cheonhwaru.

* * *

A man was walking down an extravagantly decorated corridor.

There were rooms on both sides of the corridor, and peculiarly, small windows were installed so one could see inside. Women were confined there.

From middle-aged women to children who could be called little ones, all were cowered and frightened.

When the unfamiliar man looked into the rooms, they lowered their heads with fearful faces.

The man resumed walking.

At the end of the corridor, an old man in luxurious, expensive silk clothes stood against the wall.

The old man was using a young girl as a shield, pressing a dagger to her throat. The desperation of the cornered old man looked extremely dangerous.

"Stop! Come any closer and this child dies!"

The man who had been approaching halted at the old man's threat.

"Drop your sword!"

The old man's dagger looked poised to pierce the child's throat at any moment.

"Now!"

The man set his sword down on the floor and spoke calmly.

"Calm down. If the child is hurt, you die too."

Despite the urgent situation, the man remained composed.

The old man asked urgently.

"If this child dies, you're finished too. You'll be passed over for promotions for the rest of your life."

"That would be troublesome. I still have places to rise to."

Standing away from the lantern light, the man's face was not clearly visible.

"Which group do you belong to? Blue Dragon Division? White Dragon Division?"

"What if I told you?"

"Let me go, and I'll make your career."

The old man tried to butter him up by any means.

"You kidnapped women and sold them to men, didn't you? Even little children. How could I release such a criminal?"

It was a capital crime even among grave offenses, so execution was certain. That was why the old man could never allow himself to be taken away.

"Among those who bought young girls are people with money and power."

"Yes, and thankfully, it was all recorded in the ledger."

"Then you must know. How could a mere martial artist handle them? In less than a day, you'll be assassinated, and that ledger serving as evidence will disappear."

Threats were followed by temptations.

"Send me away, and your success is guaranteed. What position do you want? Squad Leader? Great Leader? Guild Leader? Name it."

Then the man spoke from the darkness.

"Alliance Leader."

The old man's face twitched. But now was not the time to get angry; it was the time to entice.

"Yes, you could become Alliance Leader someday. So untie me..."

At that moment, the old man stopped speaking.

The man had stepped forward. The characters written on his chest.

Demon Extermination.

He was Jin Hagun, the Demon Extermination Great Leader.

"He's shifted two steps to the left, and since he's stepping on a stone on the floor, one span higher!"

The moment the old man made a face wondering what that meant.

Craack!

Thud!

The sword that burst through the rear wall accurately pierced the disheveled old man's throat.

It was the sword of a Demon Extermination Squad subordinate who had approached behind the wall. Jin Hagun had been buying time until his subordinates reached the rear wall.

"Don't look back. Come here."

The child walked to Jin Hagun. Jin Hagun crouched down to meet eyes with the child. To the child still frightened, Jin Hagun spoke in a gentle tone.

"Uncle is a high-ranking person in the Martial Alliance. There's only one person who can do anything to uncle, and that's the Alliance Leader."

He said it to reassure the child, as she had heard the old man's threat together with him.

"There are bad people in the world, but most uncles are good. And this time, this uncle will lock all those bad guys in the infernal prison."

Only then did relief bloom on the child's face.

Jin Hagun thought that the relief he saw now was the very identity of the Martial Alliance.

He believed that the Martial Alliance should become a name one could call right after mother when scared and having a hard time.

Jin Hagun extended his hand to the child.

"Shall we go out with uncle?"

The child nodded and took Jin Hagun's outstretched hand. Though he had soothed her to reassure her, the child's hand was trembling. Jin Hagun held that hand tightly.

"Let's go home now."

Members of the Demon Extermination Squad entered and led all the confined women outside.

Jin Hagun handed the ledger he had obtained to a subordinate and ordered.

"Arrest every single one of the bastards here without exception!"

"Yes, sir!"

Jin Hagun reassured the women.

"I am Jin Hagun, the Demon Extermination Great Leader. Not only those who captured you, but all related parties will be punished. Do not worry about their revenge. They won't be getting out of the infernal prison before you grow old and die."

Believing they would now survive, the women shed tears. The Demon Extermination Squad members comforted them and helped them into carriages.

Then a subordinate of the Demon Extermination Squad approached and handed Jin Hagun a message.

"It's an urgent message."

The subordinates' gazes focused on Jin Hagun.

"What is it?"

Jin Hagun folded the message and tucked it into his chest, answering.

"It seems a friend is in danger."

* * *

Three people sat at a round table set up in a warehouse on the outskirts.

The two people glaring fiercely at each other were the leaders of the Black Spider Sect and the East Tiger Sect, respectively.

As the struggle over interests intensified, dozens had died or been injured. Since both martial sects belonged to the Heretic Alliance, the Heretic Alliance had eventually stepped in to mediate.

The person sitting between them was Bisain.

"Since this fight broke out over interests, I will resolve this dispute by looking only at the numbers."

Rumors had circulated that the Young Alliance Leader had been quite active within the unorthodox faction lately, but no one had expected him to personally come to resolve this matter.

"From now on, the two of you will divide it like this."

The two leaders were not satisfied with the ratio Bisain mediated, but they dared not refute it.

"No matter how we divide it, I know I can't satisfy both of you. Because this was a fight to take everything."

In the past, he would have overwhelmed the two with killing intent. Obey, or die. Because he had believed that was the power that maintained the unorthodox faction.

But now he was Bisain, who had become Geom Mugeuk's friend. Yes, if he was Geom Mugeuk's friend, he ought to be capable of at least this much, shouldn't he?

"Let me fill the greed that was cut away."

The two leaders looked at Bisain.

"I will remember you. Even after I become Alliance Leader, I will remember you. At least, it seems like greater profit than the money you think you're losing now? Or am I mistaken?"

At that final cold question, an extremely cold killing intent was imbued.

The two sprang to their feet and answered with cupped fists.

"Not at all!"

"How could that be?"

And the final words were, as expected, agreeable ones.

"I will surely remember you both."

After those two withdrew, Bisain leaned back in his chair and spoke.

"Stop hiding and come out."

Then Geom Mugeuk revealed himself from atop the stacked crates.

"I didn't know you had such an intellectual side."

Geom Mugeuk lightly jumped down from the crates. Since Bisain already knew he had arrived, he had sent a secret transmission to Illang to have everyone leave.

"I thought you'd just say 'Fight it out, and the winner takes all!'"

"Isn't that your way?"

Geom Mugeuk answered with a smile.

"Let's just call it my father's generation's way."

Geom Mugeuk had come running like the wind, using Swift Step. The speed of Swift Step had not yet reached its limit. It was growing faster and faster. In this manner, the Central Plains grew ever smaller for Geom Mugeuk.

The two went outside the warehouse. They walked together along a trail and talked. The Heretic Alliance Thirteen Wolves followed them from afar.

"What is it this time?"

Geom Mugeuk stopped walking and spoke to him.

"The Young Alliance Leader of the Heretic Alliance was assassinated by a peerless evil woman! That's the rumor about you that will soon circulate through the streets."

Bisain silently stared at Geom Mugeuk. He had spoken as if joking, but Bisain could tell it was the truth. At least Geom Mugeuk was not someone who joked about matters of life and death.

Geom Mugeuk told Bisain everything that had happened up until now without omitting a single detail.

"It seems they are trying to kill you at Cheonhwaru."

That fearsome face of Bisain twitched. No matter how bold a person was, they could not help but be enraged by a conspiracy to kill them.

"Actually, we had also been keeping an eye on the Baekgye Merchant Group."

"What for?"

"A report came in that though it was not yet known externally, the conflict between the Leader and Vice Leader had intensified."

"Yet you accepted the Vice Leader's invitation?"

"He's fighting with the Leader, so I figured he must want something from me. I thought he had invited me seeking mediation."

Then Geom Mugeuk guessed the deeper intentions of the Baekgye Merchant Group.

"Perhaps they deliberately showed an appearance of division in order to invite you without arousing suspicion."

Bisain made an expression of disbelief. If so, it would mean they had truly set their minds to it.

"But how do they intend to kill me? By poison?"

The Heretic Alliance Thirteen Wolves protecting him were on high alert against poisoning. All alcohol and food were inspected, so poisoning was nearly impossible.

"They may have developed a poison capable of fooling the Thirteen Wolves. Or something we could never imagine may appear."

At those words, Bisain's gaze deepened. It meant something capable of killing him and the Thirteen Wolves even without poison.

Bisain's gaze upon Geom Mugeuk was heated. Geom Mugeuk had once again rushed here to warn him of danger.

"Thank you."

"When you come to save me later, run twice as fast."

"I shall."

"Oh, and I called for Great Leader Jin as well. I told young lady Jin to come too."

Bisain asked, looking taken aback.

“Why did you call them?”

The enemy’s identity had yet to be revealed. If anything, Jin Hagun or even Jin Haryeong might be exposed to danger as well.

Then Geom Mugeuk said in a whispering voice.

“We can’t be the only ones in danger, can we?”

“…….”

After staring at him in disbelief for a moment, Bisain raised his voice.

“Stop joking!”

Yes, this was what it meant to meet Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk offered another reason.

“If they lay a hand on us, see who steps forward! I called them to show that we aren’t people they can dare to touch so carelessly.”

That might be part of it, but Bisain felt there had to be another, real reason.

“Tell me the real reason!”

Only then did Geom Mugeuk reveal the true reason.

“If a friend is in danger, isn’t it only natural to let them know?”

“!”

It was an unexpected answer. So obvious that he had failed to think of it.

“If they’re your friends, shouldn’t you keep them from coming to a dangerous place instead?”

Bisain had lived believing that was what a friend was. Geom Mugeuk thought differently.

“And what if they die somewhere I’m not there? What am I supposed to tell others? My friend died without asking for my help. Hahaha. I suppose he didn’t call me because it was dangerous. I can’t stand the sight of that. I’m the sort of person who looks back on what’s passed. The sort who can’t sleep all night over something that happened in the day. I’d be thinking about it for the rest of my life. If that’s the case, I’d rather die together!”

At least one thing was clear to Bisain. He, too, would be angry for the rest of his life if Geom Mugeuk died without even asking him for help.

“I’m going to tell everyone from now on. If Captain Jin is in danger, I’ll tell everyone. If I’m in danger, I’ll tell everyone too. I’ll tell them to come save me. I’ll even threaten them, saying I’ll spread rumors that they’re cowards if they don’t come. At least on this matter, it’s not something to boast about by saying, I handled it all by myself! I didn’t want to put my friend in danger—let me say in advance, I won’t accept that kind of excuse.”

Bisain could tell. This was Geom Mugeuk’s sincerity. This was the kind of friendship Geom Mugeuk wanted.

“Oh, and I called a new face too, so look forward to it.”

“Who on earth did you call this time?”

Of course, Geom Mugeuk was not someone who would answer so easily. He launched himself lightly into the air, flying far away as he said,

“So don’t wear those dreary martial robes. Come in stylish clothes that women like. In a color that goes well with white! Then I’ll see you that day!”

Before Bisain could ask anything more, Geom Mugeuk became a dot in the sky and vanished. With him suddenly arriving and suddenly leaving, the surroundings fell quiet. It truly felt as though he had dreamed of a storm passing through.

Illang approached him.

Bisain summarized Geom Mugeuk’s words and relayed them to him.

“He says an unparalleled villainess is trying to kill me, so I should come wearing stylish clothes that women like.”

“Pardon?”

Bisain’s gaze left the bewildered Illang and turned toward the sky where Geom Mugeuk had disappeared.

Thinking that his friends would be there with him at the place where death awaited, a broad smile appeared on his rugged face.

“In a color that goes well with white.”

Chapter 538: A Battle Impossible Without Trust
----------------------------------------------
Yeojeong strolled through the rear garden of Cheonhwaru with Geukak Soma.

Walking between the well-tended flowers and trees, she stopped when she reached the pond. Carp splashed droplets of water beside the lotus flowers floating serenely.

This time spent with Geukak Soma, after so long, was incredibly joyful and precious to Yeojeong.

She decided not to worry at all about the Baekgye Sangdan’s conspiracy. She didn’t even concern herself with why they had tried to use her.

“Where did the Young Cult Leader go? I haven’t seen him these past few days.”

“He went to meet the Vice Leader of the Sado Maeng.”

Yeojeong asked with a smile.

“Is the relationship between the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader and the Sado Maeng’s Vice Leader such that you can meet so easily?”

At her jest, the two eyes behind Geukak Soma’s mask smiled pleasantly.

“He’s the one who summoned the Martial Alliance Leader and the Sado Maeng Leader to our cult’s headquarters. Something like this is nothing.”

There was a side of Geom Mugeuk that Yeojeong saw and felt in truth.

“When I look at the Young Cult Leader, I think this.”

Geukak Soma was curious what she would say. He had expected some grandiose words like, *I sense a special destiny.*

“I feel he is someone who knows things to their very core.”

Had anyone else said it, he would have taken it as describing merely a smart man or someone well-informed. But Geukak Soma knew.

He knew that Yeojeong possessed a special ability. She was someone who could read people’s destinies. Of course, it was very rare for her to tell anyone what she had read.

“It’s somewhat different from knowing a lot, or from knowing deeply. It’s hard to explain, but you have a thoroughness uniquely your own, Young Cult Leader. My words are a bit strange, aren’t they?”

Geukak Soma’s eyes showed that he understood what she meant. How could he not, when it concerned the two people closest to him in his life?

“Not at all.”

Geukak Soma knew better than anyone that Geom Mugeuk was not a man easily understood.

“That is why I do not try to understand the Young Cult Leader. I merely feel him.”

Just then, something flew swiftly toward where the two stood.

What had been a dot in the distance tore across the space in an instant and rushed toward where the two stood. It seemed like an arrow loosed from a bow, yet also like a bird gliding at incredible speed.

Geukak Soma positioned Yeojeong behind him and raised his qi.

And only when it had nearly arrived did Geukak Soma relax. The one who had flown here was Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk landed in the courtyard. The reason Geukak Soma was surprised was not that Geom Mugeuk’s lightness skill had grown faster. It was the manner in which he set foot on the ground.

He stopped as lightly as if he had simply hopped down from a roof. Considering the speed at which he had flown in, the landing was impossibly soft and gentle.

Geukak Soma knew. Once lightness skill reached the supreme pinnacle, stopping became harder than running. The level of one’s lightness skill depended on how one stopped.

He had just seen that answer.

“Your lightness skill has grown faster.”

“I ran without rest because I wanted to see you both.”

Geom Mugeuk had truly run without rest, his sprints faster than a bird. Breaking through his own limit speed again and again as he ran gave him a pleasure distinct from growing stronger through martial arts.

“Did you meet the Vice Leader of the Sado Maeng?”

“Yes, I met him. He had already accepted the Baekgye Sangdan’s invitation.”

“So they truly were targeting the Vice Leader.”

“On my way, I also sent word to Eunwol and Tongcheon Gak. I told them to investigate everything about the Baekgye Sangdan. They will surely uncover something.”

As intelligence organizations were mentioned, Yeojeong excused herself from the spot.

“You must be hungry from running. Let me show you my skills. Please have a home-cooked meal from me, for the first time in a while!”

She was a woman who knew better than anyone when to step in and when to step away. Though the backdrop of this conspiracy was her own Cheonhwaru, the protagonists of this fight were these two men who had gladly run here for her sake.

Once Yeojeong left the rear garden, Geukak Soma asked cautiously,

“Could it be those fellows behind it?”

“Perhaps. Even if not, we must proceed cautiously, keeping in mind that they may be behind it.”

A conspiracy to kill the Vice Leader of the Sado Maeng was not something just anyone could orchestrate.

“Now, shall we do what we must do?”

Geukak Soma knew what he meant. Before leaving to meet the Vice Leader, he had said they would spar once he returned.

“Where shall we fight?”

Geom Mugeuk already looked as though he had something in mind.

“I know the best place. Ah, but let’s have that home-cooked meal first, for the first time in a while.”

* * *

Geom Mugeuk and Geukak Soma stood within a dense forest of trees.

“Truly amazing.”

Geukak Soma stretched out his hand. Sunlight shone upon the back of it. The sunlight piercing between the leaves gleamed as though a blade of sword qi had flown in. It was warm.

He ran his hand over a tree. He felt its rough surface, and the air was cold and fresh.

“I never knew the Spacetime Illusion Technique could be this vivid.”

This place was inside the Spacetime Illusion Technique unfolded by Geom Mugeuk.

Geukak Soma had known that Geom Mugeuk could unfold the Spacetime Illusion Technique.

In the past, when fighting the Ghost Demon and the Iron Shackle One, he had saved Geukak Soma from crisis by unfolding this technique. He had said then that he had learned the Spacetime Illusion Technique.

“I was too preoccupied then to see it properly. So it was such a vivid place.”

To think he created such a spacious place from within his room?

There was nothing mystical about the surroundings. It felt exactly like visiting a rear mountain. That made it all the more astonishing and incredible.

Geom Mugeuk wished from the bottom of his heart to teach the Spacetime Illusion Technique to his father or his demonic followers, but he could not. This martial art was the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s secret art. Therefore, at least within the Spacetime Illusion Technique, he treated him as his master.

“I deliberately made it a dense forest.”

Geukak Soma used Jipung and palm techniques as his secret arts. If they fought in an open field, the martial arts he used would vanish without a trace, but in a place full of trees like this, the traces of his techniques would remain.

This duel would become a bloodbath, but it was ultimately training.

Moreover, while this fight was training for Geukak Soma, it was also for himself.

Geom Mugeuk drew his Black Demon Sword and spoke.

“Shall we begin?”

Geukak Soma fixed his gaze on Geom Mugeuk. Today’s fight would hinge on whether he believed in one single overarching premise, and upon that belief, whether its effect could be maximized or not.

*No matter what method I use, I cannot kill the Young Cult Leader.*

If he believed this premise, he could display his skills to the absolute limit.

But… what if the Young Cult Leader made even the slightest mistake?

Geom Mugeuk spoke as though guessing Geukak Soma’s concern.

“You saw my lightness skill. You should know. I will dodge well.”

Geukak Soma nodded.

*Then I shall believe you, Young Cult Leader.*

The two respectfully cupped their hands toward each other.

And so the fight began.

Swish!

First, Hyeorangji shot forth from Geukak Soma’s hand.

It streaked straight out and in an instant pierced a distant tree. Geom Mugeuk, who had originally stood in its path, was already in mid-air bringing his sword down.

Swoosh!

Geukak Soma twisted his body to dodge and fired Hyeorangji again.

Could the Young Cult Leader really dodge Hyeorangji fired from such close range?

He certainly worried. But even so, Geukak Soma unleashed the best move he could.

Shhhhk!

The Hyeorangji shot like light, splitting into three branches aimed at Geom Mugeuk’s head, chest, and legs.

Geom Mugeuk’s figure moved like the wind and exquisitely slipped free. He had displayed the Wind God Steps.

The moment he saw that move, Geukak Soma released the final lock in his heart.

*Worrying about the Young Cult Leader is arrogance.*

The same was true for Geom Mugeuk. His strikes were as fierce as those of a man trying to kill Geukak Soma.

Swoosh!

Geom Mugeuk charged as fast as lightning, swinging his sword so fast it was invisible to the eye.

Geukak Soma scrambled up a tree like a squirrel.

Shk! Shk! Shk!

In an instant, the tree was cut continuously from its base upward.

The very moment Geukak Soma burst through the branches and soared upward.

Swish swish swish swish swish!

Hyeorangji poured down from the sky. From between the branches Geukak Soma had just burst through, Jipung rained down upon Geom Mugeuk like a storm.

Swish swish swish swish swish swish swish!

The Hyeorangji was as fast as hidden weapons fired continuously from a mechanism. The previous Hyeorangji had been fast and powerful, but it could not compare to this.

Papapapapapapapapak!

Creating over twenty holes in the ground, the Hyeorangji narrowly missed Geom Mugeuk by a hair’s breadth.

Both knew well that the more dangerous the brink, the more it helped their martial advancement, so heart-stopping moments continued one after another.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwang!

The surroundings were overturned by the Demonic Fury Palm.

A palm technique of tremendous might swept through the surroundings. Where the palm technique passed, it turned earth, trees, and even rocks to dust, carving a new path far into the distance.

“Are you alright?”

In response to Geukak Soma’s question, Geom Mugeuk’s voice was heard from afar within the cloud of dust.

“Do I look alright? It’s not an exaggeration. I truly almost died.”

But it was an exaggeration.

Swoosh.

A blade of sword qi pierced through the dust and grazed Geukak Soma’s shoulder. Had he not instinctively dodged, his shoulder would have been severed. Though he had passed through a moment of crisis, Geukak Soma’s eyes were smiling brilliantly.

The duel was fierce. The two fought as fiercely as though facing true mortal enemies.

This was a perilous fight impossible without trust in each other’s skills, and an adventure utterly impossible to attempt without a sincere desire to help one another.

Moreover, it was a violent and rough, yet incomparably wise fight.

They stopped fighting intermittently. At moments when words needed to be exchanged, Geom Mugeuk unfailingly halted the battle. He accurately grasped the moment when one word would demonstrate greater effect than a thousand blade strikes.

Just as his father had taught him, Geom Mugeuk imparted teachings to Geukak Soma. Of course, it was completely different from when he was with his father. Then, he had been the one learning; now, he was the one teaching. Geom Mugeuk did not miss even the enlightenment that came from that difference.

“Please show me that move from a moment ago once more.”

Geom Mugeuk snapped his fingers, and the surroundings changed—the damaged trees and rocks returned to their original state.

And the exchange of offense and defense unfolded anew.

It was different from the Hyeorangji displayed earlier. That much was clear from the traces left in the surroundings.

Before a hole left by Hyeorangji in a different location than before, Geom Mugeuk spoke.

“If you had fired here first, this hole would be in my body.”

But Geukak Soma did not think so.

“Even then, the Young Cult Leader would have found a way to dodge.”

The advantage of fighting within the Spacetime Illusion Technique was not limited to that alone.

Snap!

When Geom Mugeuk snapped his fingers, this time the surroundings grew dark as pitch.

“This time, it is night.”

A more dangerous battle began.

When the fight ended thus, a bright full moon revealed itself from within the clouds. Beneath the bright moonlight, the two reviewed the fight and conversed again.

When they had first begun the duel, Geukak Soma had truly never imagined it would become such a fight. This fight was one where the Young Cult Leader poured everything out for him. Even staking his life. Because of that, Geukak Soma also exerted his full strength. He would not let the Young Cult Leader’s goodwill go to waste.

“This time, it rains.”

Sssssshhhhhh.

The two fought in the rain. In the rain, it was even harder to dodge the Hyeorangji.

Even so, Geom Mugeuk was not hit by the Hyeorangji. He desperately dodged and counter-attacked. The moment he was injured, this training would end.

For his own sake, as well as for Geukak Soma’s sake, he absolutely must not be injured.

When Geukak Soma’s internal energy was completely depleted, he would circulate his qi again and then resume fighting.

Amazingly, the two fought for two days and nights. They fought without sleeping or eating.

And in this very moment when that desperate yet brilliant fight had ended.

Geukak Soma sat in a meditative posture, lost in thought.

Geom Mugeuk sat beside him, waiting for him to emerge from his profound trance.

Geom Mugeuk was certain that his father, too, had gained many realizations while teaching him. Because he himself had gained several enlightenments from this fight.

The bloody battle over the past two days was an opportunity he himself could not easily obtain.

How much time had passed?

Geugak Soma opened his eyes. Through this single fight, he had firmly consolidated the level of martial attainment he had recently risen to. Even when one’s realm advanced, it took time before it became wholly one’s own.

It depended on what sort of experiences one had, but it could take years, or one might fail to solidify that realm for an entire lifetime.

But through the bloody battles of the past two days, Geugak Soma had passed beyond that process.

“Thanks to you, I can now move toward the next stage.”

The fighting over these two days had given him greater enlightenment than all the battles he had fought in his entire life combined. It was possible only because the level of the two men was so high.

“Congratulations.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly and offered him sincere congratulations.

Geugak Soma looked at Geom Mugeuk’s arm with concern.

“Is your arm all right?”

There were traces of blood on his arm. At that, Geom Mugeuk grimaced and made a show of exaggerating.

“How could it be all right? My arm was nearly torn off.”

Of course, it was nothing that serious. The wound was only a little deeper than a light graze.

In truth, when the blood had spurted, what had nearly fallen out was not Geom Mugeuk’s arm but Geugak Soma’s liver. The moment it went thwack, he had been so startled that he had even let out a short scream.

“I do not know what I should say.”

“You don’t have to say anything.”

Yes, Geugak Soma said nothing instead. How could he possibly convey such immense gratitude with words?

The two sat side by side, looking at the place that had been their final battlefield.

Geugak Soma was struck anew with amazement. Geom Mugeuk had maintained this place for the entire two days. Even while fighting those insane battles.

“But can your internal energy still hold out? To think you’re still maintaining this space.”

“It’s on its last legs now.”

It had been possible because he had not used martial arts that consumed enormous internal energy, like the Nine Flames Demonic Art, and had fought only with pure swordsmanship and sword qi.

“Even so, I saved the last bit of internal energy to use where it mattered most.”

Snap!

When Geom Mugeuk flicked his fingers, their surroundings transformed into a new place.

“Ah!”

Geugak Soma’s eyes widened beneath his mask.

“You must have missed it after seeing nothing but green foliage for two whole days, no?”

What spread out before them was a snow-white landscape.

It was a scene that put Geugak Soma’s heart more at ease than anything else.

These days, his thoughts and feelings had changed enough that he had even placed furniture in his room, but even so, for him, this pure white scenery remained the space that set his heart most at ease.

What could he say to such thoughtfulness? Geugak Soma only gazed in silence at the vast snowfield stretching before him.

“Lord Soma.”

“Yes, Young Cult Leader.”

“Next time, you must travel with me.”

“When that time comes…”

Geugak Soma hesitated for a moment. Unlike him, what was he hesitating to say?

Just then, from outside the Spacetime Transposition Art—in other words, outside the residence—Yeo Jeong’s voice was heard.

“Young Cult Leader, are you there? Someone has come looking for you.”

Chapter 539: Have You Ever Served Wine at a Brothel?
----------------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk and Geukak Soma emerged from the Space-Time Transposition Art.

It was the moment they returned from illusion to reality, and for both of them, it had been two days they would never forget.

When Geom Mugeuk opened the door, Yeojeong was standing there.

“Has someone come?”

Yeojeong answered, her face slightly flushed.

“The Demon-Extermination Unit Leader and Miss Jin Haryeong.”

Of course, Yeojeong knew. She knew that the Demon-Extermination Unit Leader was the grandson of the Martial Alliance Leader and the strongest candidate to succeed him.

But she had never imagined that Jin Hagun would come to Cheonhwaru. Now the young alliance leader of the Sado Alliance would come as well. Once that happened, the heirs of all three powers—demonic, orthodox, and unorthodox—would be gathered in one place.

Yeojeong felt that this matter was the greatest crisis of her life, but at the same time, it was also a chance to experience something she would never experience again.

“A friend has fallen into danger, so I called him.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Yeojeong expressed her regret.

“I envy you. In my life, I never gained a friend like that.”

At her words, Geom Mugeuk spoke as if hurt.

“You have no friends? Then what am I?”

As if his words were too much for her to bear, Yeojeong quickly lowered her head.

“I will bring the two of them here.”

As Yeojeong turned away, she thought about it and realized the Young Cult Leader had every reason to feel hurt. When she had fallen into danger, had he not brought Chohui and rushed here without rest? It was no different from the Demon-Extermination Unit Leader rushing over.

‘Now that I think about it, I did have a friend.’

She had the heaven-like Geukak Soma, and another heaven-like friend who came running in times of crisis. It was a life with nothing more to wish for.

Once Yeojeong withdrew, Geom Mugeuk immediately sat down and began circulating his qi. Since he had no internal energy at all, he intended to seize even this brief opening to complete at least one Heavenly Cycle.

While Geom Mugeuk circulated his qi, Geukak Soma stood beside him as his protector. He recalled the snowy landscape he had seen within the Space-Time Transposition Art.

With his eyes closed, circulating qi, Geom Mugeuk spoke. He had reached a realm where he could speak freely even while circulating his energy.

“Soma-nim, your martial realm can rise even higher than it is now.”

Geukak Soma had already achieved great completion in the Blood Ang Finger and the Demon Extreme Berserk Palm. With the recent changes in his life and the increase in his internal energy, his martial arts had risen further, and through the two days of training, that realm had become solid.

And now Geom Mugeuk was saying he could go even higher from this state. Since he had now reached the eleventh star, it meant he could reach the great completion of the twelfth star.

“Once the Blood Ang Finger and the Demon Extreme Berserk Palm are further perfected, they will be reborn as completely different martial arts.”

If it had been someone else saying it, he might not know, but if it was Geom Mugeuk, he could believe it. After all, he had experienced life and death together with him more than anyone else. He had experienced Geukak Soma’s martial arts countless times, closer than anyone.

“Become stronger, Soma-nim.”

Geukak Soma had always felt it. There was a clear reason and target behind Geom Mugeuk’s endless pursuit of strength.

“Are the enemies we must face that strong?”

Geom Mugeuk paused briefly before answering.

“They are strong.”

Geukak Soma quietly nodded. If so, no further words were needed.

“I will become stronger.”

If only to protect the Young Cult Leader.

And he was not the only one growing stronger. Recently, not only he but the other Demon Venerables as well were each growing stronger in their own ways.

That was why he had a premonition. The Eight Demon Venerables of this generation would become the strongest Eight Demon Venerables in history.

Just then, Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong arrived with Yeojeong.

Upon entering the room, they were first surprised to see Geom Mugeuk circulating his qi, and surprised again to see Geukak Soma standing beside him. They had not expected to see Geukak Soma here.

“I greet the Demon Venerable.”

“Unit Leader Jin, welcome.”

Compared to Jin Hagun, who greeted him awkwardly, Jin Haryeong greeted him with a bright expression.

“It has been a long time!”

“Miss Jin, it is good to see you.”

Through this greeting, Jin Hagun could see just how much he and his younger sister had changed.

The fact that he had greeted first was one thing, but Jin Haryeong greeting someone in such a bright voice was truly unthinkable.

Jin Hagun’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk, who was circulating his qi. Yes, this expression was fitting.

Everything was divided into before meeting Geom Mugeuk and after meeting Geom Mugeuk.

Because after coming to know Geom Mugeuk, everything had changed. His life, of course, and even his younger sister, who was now asking Geukak Soma with gestures whether Geom Mugeuk had been injured.

Jin Haryeong was happy to see Geom Mugeuk after so long. Seeing him circulating his qi at the moment of their reunion, she thought it was just like him. A joke naturally slipped out.

“You called the Demon-Extermination Unit Leader here and you’re circulating qi? How bold of you!”

Jin Hagun shot her a look that said not to behave carelessly toward the Young Cult Leader in front of a Demon Venerable.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk, having completed one Heavenly Cycle of true qi, opened his eyes.

“I wanted to show just how much I trust our Demon-Extermination Unit Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk sprang to his feet. Geukak Soma, standing beside him, was inwardly surprised.

‘The speed at which he completes a Heavenly Cycle is astonishingly fast.’

Of course, a single Heavenly Cycle could not fully replenish Geom Mugeuk’s immense internal energy, but at this speed, it seemed he would truly recover his internal energy perfectly in no time.

Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun exchanged greetings first.

“You arrived early.”

“As it happened, I was not far away.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jin Haryeong. Instead of greeting him, she summarized the situation.

“Who would believe it? That the moment the future successor of the Martial Alliance received word from the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult that the young alliance leader of the Sado Alliance was in danger, he came running here in one breath.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled at her. She seemed more at ease than before. It meant she had grown further.

“As it happened, I wasn’t far away either. I don’t usually come to Guizhou.”

It must be fate telling me to see you. She almost said those words, but stopped herself.

After they exchanged greetings like that, Geom Mugeuk introduced Yeojeong to them.

“This is the owner of Cheonhwaru, and someone I like and respect.”

Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong exchanged courteous greetings with her. The fact that Geom Mugeuk had emphasized that he liked and respected her meant she was someone that important.

Yeojeong quietly looked at the two of them. Geom Mugeuk was curious. What fate was she reading from them?

“Seeing the two of you, I can tell that the future of the orthodox martial world is brighter than ever.”

“You praise us too highly.”

Once those greetings were exchanged, Geukak Soma made to leave. With him there, the two would surely feel uncomfortable.

“Then speak among yourselves.”

As Geukak Soma was about to leave the residence with Yeojeong, Geom Mugeuk made a request of her.

“Tower Mistress, I’m sorry, but please show your skills one more time. These are friends who must have been suffering away from home, unable to eat properly.”

Before she could answer, Jin Hagun declined.

“It is all right. There is no need to do that because of us.”

At that, Yeojeong smiled and said,

“How many chances would I have in my life to serve a meal to the Demon-Extermination Unit Leader? Please do not reject this great honor granted to me.”

Once she put it that way, Jin Hagun could not refuse.

Yeojeong could guess Geom Mugeuk’s intention. He wanted to deepen her connection with Jin Hagun even a little.

Cheonhwaru was spreading throughout the Central Plains, regardless of orthodox or unorthodox factions. A connection with the Demon-Extermination Unit Leader would be of unconditional benefit to her. Even more so if it was a connection he was bestowing upon her like this.

As Yeojeong left, she looked at Geom Mugeuk and greeted him with her eyes.

‘Thank you, Young Cult Leader.’

Thus Geukak Soma and Yeojeong left, and only the three of them remained.

“Tell me in detail what happened.”

“Our dancer friend has many enemies.”

Geom Mugeuk told them everything that had happened regarding Cheonhwaru.

After hearing the story, the expressions of the two stiffened. On the way here, they had even discussed the possibility that Geom Mugeuk had exaggerated the message because he wanted to see them. But after hearing everything, the situation was serious.

“This is not something a mere merchant group would plan. There must be someone behind them.”

Jin Hagun’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk.

“It means they could be targeting you.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk dragged the two of them in as well.

“Or it could be you. What if it is someone who anticipated that I would call you?”

“Am I falling into danger because I met the wrong friend?”

“You’re escaping the danger of being defeated one by one because you met the right friend.”

Now Jin Hagun knew that those words were not empty self-importance. The enemies had targeted everyone, regardless of orthodox, unorthodox, or demonic.

“At present, our cult is investigating the Baekgye Merchant Group in depth. Results will come soon. Then we may be able to find out what they ultimately want.”

Jin Hagun was grateful that Geom Mugeuk had called him. If Bi Sain and Geom Mugeuk had resolved this matter by themselves, and he had only heard about it later, he felt he would somehow have been disappointed.

“Thank you for calling me.”

“Once you hear what I’m about to say, you won’t be grateful.”

What prank was he trying to pull now? Not only Jin Hagun but even Jin Haryeong was curious what Geom Mugeuk was about to say.

And as always, the question was something they had never expected.

“Have you ever carried drinks in a brothel?”

* * *

“It suits you.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Jin Hagun looked at himself reflected in the bronze mirror. He was currently dressed in the clothing worn by the people who worked at Cheonhwaru.

He truly had never dreamed that he would do something like this in his life.

“If you start learning the work now, you’ll be skilled by the beginning of next month.”

Not only him, but the warriors of the Demon-Extermination Unit had also decided to disguise themselves as workers at Cheonhwaru.

The reason Jin Hagun was standing before the bronze mirror in this outfit was because of Geom Mugeuk’s request.

“I want you to protect the courtesans who work here.”

Geom Mugeuk persuaded him seriously.

“We don’t know what will happen that day. It may end anticlimactically, or something beyond our expectations may occur. I don’t know if you know this, but I am someone who always considers the worst.”

What Geom Mugeuk was worried about was the safety of the courtesans.

“If things become serious, no one will be able to pay attention to them. But I cannot entrust this matter to the Faceless Guests either.”

Geom Mugeuk stared at Jin Hagun and said,

“You and the Demon-Extermination Unit must protect them that day.”

He had called the Demon-Extermination Unit Leader and the Demon-Extermination Unit all the way here to ask them to protect courtesans, so Jin Hagun could have felt displeased. He could have felt as though he had been pushed aside from the important work.

But Jin Hagun nodded readily and answered.

“I will protect them.”

At that answer, Jin Haryeong was inwardly surprised. She sensed no resentment, no feeling of, “I don’t like it, but since it is Geom Mugeuk’s request, I will do it.”

He truly seemed to accept the request willingly.

Even Geom Mugeuk looked surprised at his answer, which was too willing.

“You’re not saying you dislike it? If anything, I’d say, ‘I will save Sain, so you carry the drinks.’ That’s what I would have done.”

At that, Jin Hagun stared intently at Geom Mugeuk and asked,

“Isn’t this the more important task, which is why you are entrusting it to me?”

Geom Mugeuk’s clear, deep eyes curved into a pleased smile.

“Thank you for understanding my heart.”

At that, Jin Haryeong cut in and shouted,

“Don’t be fooled! He just doesn’t want to deal with drunken nuisances, so he’s leaving it to you, Orabeoni!”

Geom Mugeuk played along with her joke, pretending to gasp in shock.

Of course, she knew too. She knew that Geom Mugeuk was sincerely worried about their safety.

What surprised her was her brother’s attitude, and there was a reason Jin Hagun had readily accepted Geom Mugeuk’s request.

“In the operation just before coming here, I saved children and women.”

The look of relief that had risen on the child’s face then kept coming back to him.

“At that moment, I had this thought. Punishing evil is important, but saving their lives is even more important than that.”

If someone heard it, they might say it was far too late a realization.

In truth, Jin Hagun had saved countless people until now and carried out countless acts of chivalry.

“At some point, righteousness became a matter of course.”

Saving people as a matter of course, killing as a matter of course. He was the Demon-Extermination Unit Leader, so wasn’t it only natural? He was someone who would become the Alliance Leader in the future, so he could not rest.

“Surprisingly, even righteousness accumulates fatigue.”

He had felt that, but Jin Hagun had denied it and simply run forward.

In the midst of running without rest like that, he had seen the relief blooming on that child’s face right before his eyes. He had felt the child’s hand gripping his tightly.

It was the moment when chivalry became tangible and embedded itself in Jin Hagun’s heart.

No, perhaps he was consciously engraving that moment into his heart. In order to endure this deep fatigue.

“When the chance to kill a hundred evildoers and the chance to save a child arise at the same time, I will go save the child. But what then? What about the innocent lives those hundred evildoers will kill in the future?”

He wanted to ask Geom Mugeuk. What would you do? Is my thinking right?

That unchanging gaze of Geom Mugeuk looking at him. Yes, it was because he had known Geom Mugeuk would look at him with eyes like that that he could confide such worries.

And because he knew Geom Mugeuk was someone who would give not a complicated answer, but one this clear.

“Do not worry and go save the child. I will go kill the evildoers. I will take on the villain’s role.”

Jin Hagun smiled faintly. Even if he said that, how could Jin Hagun not know that he, too, was the kind of person who would go save the child?

“You will become a truly splendid Alliance Leader. An Alliance Leader who takes the hands of those who must be protected. That path will truly be difficult and exhausting. A path of regretting again and again because of the hundred people you spared. When it becomes so hard you cannot endure it, come find me. I will play with you to your heart’s content. So when that time comes, you must not treat me coldly! You can’t say, ‘That fellow was all right when he was young, but now that I look at him, he’s just a demonic bastard with a silver tongue and too many words!’”

After staring at Geom Mugeuk for a moment, Jin Hagun abruptly said,

“I will. I’ll call you a demonic bastard with a silver tongue!”

“That is too much, Alliance Leader-nim!”

Jin Hagun believed it. Even then, Geom Mugeuk’s words would still shine as they did now. Even if they did not shine, they would touch his heart.

That was why, even after becoming a white-haired Alliance Leader, he would go find him with worries too difficult to ask others about.

—Ah, I am not sure. What do you think?

Jin Hagun’s gaze returned to himself in the bronze mirror as he spoke.

“Someone may be injured or die that day. But among those people, there will be no courtesan of this place.”

At that firm resolve, the gaze of Geom Mugeuk, standing behind Jin Hagun, deepened.

“That day, none of us will be injured or die. My silver-tongued demonic path is far too selfish compared to your righteous path, so I absolutely cannot stand to see such a thing.”

Chapter 540: Will I Be There Then Too?
--------------------------------------
Jin Haryeong also appeared in the mirror.

"We all owe you our lives, yet you're trying to save everyone again."
