If it weren't for Geom Mugeuk, neither she nor her brother would have survived until now. She had heard that the Lord of Cheonhwaru had survived thanks to him as well, and she knew that Bi Sain had also saved his life because of him.

The gazes of the two people inside the mirror turned toward the remaining person.

"Even if you commit a wrong, and someone kills you for it justly, I'll end up living a life full of anger. Maybe not every day, but on the days when I remember you, I'll burst into a rage. Just imagine it. Imagine me going through life taking my temper out on others."

Geom Mugeuk speaking words that hurt people, the complete opposite of how he was now? Jin Haryeong didn't even want to imagine it.

"But losing you to those who scheme and plot in the shadows? And in this place where I called you? I'm not doing this because of you. It's for me, who would suffer for the rest of my life. So don't even dream of getting hurt or dying!"

With that, Geom Mugeuk left the room first.

Jin Hagun turned to his younger sister and spoke calmly.

"This is why we should have come running the moment we received word."

To such a brother, Jin Haryeong had something she wanted to say.

"Don't give your heart too much to the Young Cult Leader."

"Why?"

"Because you'll suffer far more than him."

It was something she said in regard to losing one another. No matter what the Young Cult Leader said, she wasn't worried about him. To her, Geom Mugeuk was that kind of person. But she was worried about her brother.

Jin Hagun looked back at the mirror and straightened his clothes.

"I'm a selfish person too. Since I'm that kind of person…… I suppose I had such worries."

Just pouring out his worries to Geom Mugeuk had lightened his heart considerably. Yes, how could this path have been easy from the very start? Still, it was enough that there was someone who truly understood and supported him as he walked this difficult road.

Jin Haryeong had been inwardly surprised when Jin Hagun poured out his worries. She had thought he carried out his role as Myeolmadaeju because he truly loved it.

If the person he had confided in had been anyone else, she would have been jealous. Because it was a worry he had never once shared with her.

"You don't go giving your heart either."

"I'm not. And it's not as if giving your heart makes it happen."

At that, Jin Hagun suddenly raised his voice sharply.

"What's wrong with you?"

It wasn't the outburst of the Myeolmadaeju, but of her brother.

Of course, it was also a shout without follow-through. He couldn't very well tell her to get along well with the Demonic Cult's Young Cult Leader.

Jin Hagun whirled around and left the room.

Left alone, Jin Haryeong perched on the edge of Geom Mugeuk's bed and spoke.

"How do you give your heart?"

After a brief pause, she added in a low voice.

"I've already given it."

* * *

Jin Hagun and the Myeolmadae were deployed to Cheonhwaru.

"The alcohol is stored in this warehouse. When an order comes down, take it from here. Here is the list of alcohol as well."

Yeojeong took them here and there, teaching them what to do.

As he learned the work from her, Jin Hagun stationed the Myeolmadae martial artists in each room. He also had them draw up plans in advance for where and how to conceal their weapons, and how to evacuate the courtesans in an emergency.

The Myeolmadae martial artists had no complaints about this operation. They were men who already owed Geom Mugeuk debts from past events.

Rather, this kind of work in a pleasure house was fresh. How long had it been since they wore something other than martial uniforms?

The courtesans of the main branch were ones Yeojeong had personally handpicked, so they were not shaken by the recent series of changes.

Rather, the newly arrived Myeolmadae martial artists were the talk of the town among them.

Which martial artist was handsome, who was kind, who supposedly resembled whom.

Several days passed as they adapted to one another.

* * *

"Am I the only one this anxious?"

The person who blocked Jin Haryeong's path was Jin Hagun. He was holding bottles of alcohol in both hands and looked at his sister.

"I'm busy, a guest is waiting!"

Jin Hagun brushed past her and quickly ran off.

That wasn't all. The Myeolmadae martial artists had quickly adapted to the work as well. Among them were some who truly looked like they had worked here for years. There was even a martial artist who had received a love letter from a courtesan.

The Lord of Cheonhwaru also, as if nothing was amiss, looked after the courtesans and remained faithful to her role as the pavilion lord.

In the end, the person Jin Haryeong sought out was Geom Mugeuk.

He had shut himself inside his quarters, doing who knows what.

"Aren't you worried?"

"About what?"

"That right now, the Myeolmadae and the Myeolmadaeju are carrying bottles of alcohol in Cheonhwaru. Hasn't the secret already leaked by now?"

No matter how much this was the main branch of Cheonhwaru, it seemed the secret would leak. No, it must have already leaked.

"Yes, they must know."

"They know? Then doesn't that mean the folks who came from the merchant guild that day are thinking, 'Those guys carrying the alcohol are Myeolmadae martial artists'?"

"Exactly."

"But you're having us carry alcohol so peacefully?"

"They'll know everything. That I'm here, that the Myeolmadae has come. They probably even know that you followed along."

"Then shouldn't we strike Baekgye Merchant Guild first to find out who's behind this?"

"How?"

When he asked this, Jin Haryeong was the one flustered instead.

"Don't you have ways to make them talk?"

"You seem to be expecting some miraculous great technique to make them talk. We're the same as your people. Pulling out fingernails, burning them with fire."

"We don't do that sort of thing!"

"You do too."

Jin Haryeong couldn't strongly refute him. There was no guarantee that somewhere in the Murim Alliance's underground interrogation chamber, a skewer wasn't being heated red-hot.

"Anyway, why don't you do it? Our friend's life is on the line."

Then Geom Mugeuk answered calmly.

"Because our friend's honor is on the line too."

Jin Haryeong flinched.

Baekgye Merchant Guild belonged to the Sado Alliance. Attacking them without clear evidence would be an act of disregarding the Sado Alliance.

"If we strike, Sain should be the one to do it."

Jin Haryeong's face flushed slightly as she nodded in agreement.

"I was short-sighted."

Besides, they had officially invited Bi Sain now.

Then Geom Mugeuk spoke indifferently.

"That's why."

"Why?"

"It feels like they're deliberately dragging us in."

In that instant, Jin Haryeong's eyes widened in shock.

"Anyone can see that the current situation means I need to make contact with Baekgye Merchant Guild or find something out from them, right? Enough that you came here urging me why I don't strike. Do you really think they wouldn't think the same?"

She hadn't thought that far. If Geom Mugeuk's prediction was right? If the enemy was the type to dig a trap in Baekgye Merchant Guild and wait?

"Then aren't we in a really dangerous situation right now?"

Then Geom Mugeuk asked her back.

"Dangerous? For whom? The interior of Cheonhwaru is guarded by the Myeolmadae, the exterior by the Nameless Guest. And I am here, and Lady Soma is here too. You and Lord Jin are here, and Young Alliance Leader Bi will arrive leading the Sado Thirteen Wolves."

It was enough to make one blurt out, 'Is this really dangerous?'

"If you were the enemy, could you pull off some scheme in this situation?"

At Geom Mugeuk's question, Jin Haryeong shook her head.

"I would have fled without looking back."

Right. To defeat their side, one would need to start a war.

"But they're staying put. Not canceling the invitation either. That means they have a dangerous move up their sleeve."

There was one thing Jin Haryeong didn't understand in Geom Mugeuk's words.

"You wish for no one to get hurt or die, right? Then why aren't you avoiding this? You could refuse the invitation and strike Baekgye Merchant Guild together with Young Alliance Leader Bi."

Then unexpected words flowed from Geom Mugeuk.

"Then they wouldn't be able to use the move they've prepared."

Just as she was thinking, 'Wouldn't that be enough then?' she was startled.

"Don't tell me……?"

That 'don't tell me' was correct.

Geom Mugeuk nodded and spoke.

"Yes, I need to find out what that move is."

Jin Haryeong understood. That rather, Geom Mugeuk was trying to draw the opponent in.

"Why?"

"Because even if it isn't used now, it will definitely be used someday."

Words that resolved her doubt were added.

"Will I be there then?"

"!"

Geom Mugeuk was worried that that move would be unleashed where he was not present.

Upon Jin Hagun when he was not there, upon Bi Sain when he was not there, upon someone else when he was not there.

If it was such a powerful move, he intended to step forward and block it while he was here.

Jin Haryeong soon recalled what he had said while looking into the mirror a few days ago.

"None of us will be hurt or die. My gleaming demonic path is far too selfish compared to your righteous path to ever bear witness to such a thing."

Truly, how utterly selfish.

Worrying about others' safety to such a degree.

Worrying not only about the present situation before his eyes, but also about a future that might occur at any unknown time.

"Aren't you tired living like this?"

Geom Mugeuk had an unwavering belief in life.

"If people who don't have to die end up dying because I didn't live this way…… then it would be hard."

Saving those who should not die—that was the very calling of this life.

Geom Mugeuk spoke with a smile.

"Don't worry too much. If we die, they won't be safe either. The revenge of the cult leaders and alliance leaders of Majeongsa who lost their children and disciples would begin."

"Don't smile! It's not funny at all."

Her brother undoubtedly trusted Geom Mugeuk even more than she did. Since Geom Mugeuk had prepared for everything like this, he was probably just carrying alcohol with a light heart, trusting in him.

"What should I do? Should I wash dishes in the kitchen?"

"No. You protect the Lord of Cheonhwaru."

As Jin Hagun had done, Jin Haryeong readily accepted.

"I will. But unlike my brother, I can't promise to guard her to the death. I may not be able to protect her while dying, but I'll definitely protect her until the moment just before I die."

Geom Mugeuk said to her with a smile.

"Retreating only at the brink of death is harder than dying while protecting her, you know?"

* * *

Three days before Bi Sain's arrival.

Geom Mugeuk called everyone to his quarters. Geugak Soma and Jin Hagun, Jin Haryeong, and even Yeojeong.

Geom Mugeuk's desk was piled high with documents. They were the information gathered and sent by Tongcheongak and Eunwol up until now.

The people belonging to Baekgye Merchant Guild and their financial circumstances, their relationships with one another, their relationships with other merchant guilds. It was information that had thoroughly investigated Baekgye Merchant Guild down to the finest detail.

Geom Mugeuk conveyed the most important piece of information to everyone.

"Recently, Baekgye Merchant Guild completed expansion work. They widened the training ground, tore down old buildings, and erected new ones. And Tongcheongak discovered that there were special materials used in that construction."

Tongcheongak had investigated even the construction materials without missing a thing.

"It was a cover-up construction."

At Geugak Soma's guess, Geom Mugeuk nodded and answered.

"Yes. When we analyzed the materials, it seems they installed offensive mechanisms and formations. And of the highest grade at that. They say it will be extremely lethal."

At the mention of lethal mechanisms and formations, everyone's expressions grew grave. Those could not be easily installed, and even if one found a master craftsman capable of making them, it would cost an enormous sum of money.

Jin Haryeong spoke to Geom Mugeuk with a surprised expression.

"Your prediction was right. Baekgye Merchant Guild was a trap."

As Geom Mugeuk slowly nodded, this time Jin Hagun asked.

"What do you intend to do?"

Everyone's attention focused on Geom Mugeuk. He had already made up his mind before calling them.

"Destroying that place is something the Sado Alliance can decide later. For now, let's prepare a proper feast for our friend. Please prepare the finest alcohol and outstanding dishes."

Geom Mugeuk looked around at everyone and added.

"Only when the feast ends well can the real feast begin."

* * *

Deep in the communal cemetery at night, a wolf's howl echoed from afar.

Old tombstones that looked as though they would shatter at the slightest touch heightened the eerie atmosphere even further.

There stood Pyeongwi, Vice Lord of Baekgye Merchant Guild. He had been tense the entire time he waited for someone. And rightly so.

What he had been waiting for appeared in the darkness.

Flying through the night sky, floating in midair, was a palanquin.

The palanquin's atmosphere was unique. It was painted entirely black, as if a chunk of darkness had broken off and was moving on its own.

There were four people around the palanquin wearing black clothes and black veils. They too floated at the four corners of the palanquin and flew alongside it, and the energy they exuded was far from ordinary.

From the lanterns in their hands flowed a bizarre blue light that seemed like it could only come from a ghost's eyes. It felt like a palanquin connecting the underworld and the mortal realm.

Tap, tap-tap.

All living things in the palanquin's path died.

Birds and insects dropped down with pattering sounds, and flowers withered and died in an instant. Even the trees dried up and twisted.

The palanquin stopped in the air before Pyeongwi.

Pyeongwi spoke in a respectful tone.

"Tomorrow is finally the day."

Tomorrow was the day they had invited Bi Sain. Tomorrow night, he would meet Bi Sain personally at Cheonhwaru.

A woman's voice was heard from within the palanquin floating in the air.

"I sense fear from you."

It was a bewitching yet dreamlike voice that shook the listener's soul.

Pyeongwi's pupils trembled. How could he not be afraid? No matter how foolproof the plan was, he had to walk with his own two feet into a place where the Demonic Cult's Young Cult Leader, Geugak Soma, the Sado Alliance's Young Alliance Leader, and the Myeolmadaeju were present.

Again, a sweet woman's voice flowed from the palanquin.

"Do not be afraid."

A desolate wind blew, and clouds veiled the moon.

"The night is always on our side."

Chapter 541: They May Be from the Evil Faction and Seem Heartless and Shameless But
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Pyeongwi entered the Baekgye Merchant Guild.

On the days he came after meeting the palanquin, his whole body ached as if he were dying.

He briefly gazed at the empty training ground under the cold moonlight.

After taking a deep breath, he moved his steps carefully. He had to stay focused. If he made a mistake, it wouldn't merely hurt—he would truly die.

He walked, stepping exactly on the designated spots. The moment he even took a single wrong step, mechanisms and formations that would tear his whole body to pieces were installed.

He passed the training ground and entered the building.

In the corridor filled with an eerie air, he knocked on the wall twice.

A moment later,

Sssssss.

As the formation was dispelled from within, the scenery before his eyes changed. Surprisingly, a formation had been laid in this narrow corridor.

Installing a formation in a narrow indoor space was far more difficult than creating one outside. Just by looking at this, one could tell how high the level of the person who made this formation was.

Passing through the corridor, he entered the innermost room.

There were two people in the dark room.

One person was lying on a bed. The man with a completely sickly complexion was the guild leader, Seo Jeongtae.

In front of him, his wife sat with her back turned.

“Tomorrow is finally the day.”

They were Pyeongwi's words, filled with resolve.

On the other hand, the wife's voice was weary and exhausted.

“I will trust only the Vice-Guild Leader.”

“Yes, Madam.”

Pyeongwi respectfully bowed his head and exited the room.

In front of the door, Pyeongwi took a large, deep breath.

He did not know how he had ended up coming this far.

When he raised his head after being unwittingly swept along by that thing called fate, a black palanquin was floating before his eyes.

* * *

Geom Muggeuk had slept in for the first time in a while.

Until late at night, he had used the Reverse Heaven Secret Technique, saving even sleep, to train in martial arts.

He repeated his training, recalling the bloody battle with the Extreme Evil Soma. He repeated it until he was sick of it.

Because enlightenment always came the very next day after he shouted that he couldn't stand it anymore and stormed out.

Since he had slept his fill, his head was clear and he felt refreshed.

Geom Muggeuk rose from bed, opened the window wide, and took a great deep breath.

“Ah! Great!”

Just as he was about to look up at the endlessly clear, sunny sky, someone was standing in the yard with their back turned.

It was the back of someone unexpected.

Leaning against the window, Geom Muggeuk spoke to him.

“Just how badly do you want to play that you'd come in the morning? The pleasure house doesn't open in the morning.”

Then the man turned toward Geom Muggeuk.

A rugged face that now felt rather welcome, a face that he occasionally even found handsome in a manly way—he was none other than Bisain.

“The sun is already high in the sky, what do you mean, morning! Can a martial artist sleep so obliviously? A hundred assassins could have come and gone.”

Of course, if Bisain had tried to enter the room or revealed even a faint killing intent, the Heavenly Demon Guardian Art would have activated and woken Geom Muggeuk.

“I'm a pacifist, unlike you. Assassins have no reason to come.”

An uncontrollable smile formed on Bisain's savage face.

He had run here personally to warn him of the crisis. And Geom Muggeuk had been preparing today in advance for him here at Cheonhwaru.

“Are you ready to save me?”

Bisain's cold, chilling gaze and Geom Muggeuk's clear, deep gaze intertwined in the air.

“I'm ready to die with you.”

Bisain knew. Those words were not spoken merely to sound good.

“I don't want to die at the same hour as you. So please be sure to save me.”

“Don't we have a reliable mighty warrior as a friend? Ask him.”

Geom Muggeuk leaped and flew out the window.

“By the way, you brought sophisticated clothes that go well with white, right?”

“Just who are you meeting to go this far? Don't tell me it's a woman?”

Since he said sophisticated clothes, not just any clothes, it had to be a woman.

“If it is a woman?”

“I'm not interested in women.”

“If it's a woman, you should start by asking if she's pretty!”

Geom Muggeuk asked with a look of disbelief.

“So? You did bring them, didn't you?”

“...I brought them.”

As Geom Muggeuk looked at him with a strange smile, Bisain changed the subject.

“Where is Commander Jin?”

“He's found a new occupation.”

Geom Muggeuk took Bisain to the back quarters of Cheonhwaru.

Jin Hagun was there in front of the warehouse.

“What are you doing?”

At Bisain's question, Jin Hagun set down the liquor crate he was holding and answered.

“As you can see, I'm sorting liquor.”

“Have you fallen into the Young Cult Leader's trap again?”

Jin Hagun laughed at Bisain's words. This was the only moment he shared common ground with Bisain.

Their common enemy, Geom Muggeuk.

“Seeing as I'm wearing clothes I've never worn in my life and doing things I've never done, perhaps I've fallen into another trap.”

Bisain sympathized with Jin Hagun's self-reproach.

“I seem to have fallen into the same trap, at least when it comes to clothes.”

When Jin Hagun made an expression asking what that meant, Bisain answered.

“There's a reason. Do I just need to move this?”

Bisain went over and began helping move the liquor crates.

Geom Muggeuk raised his voice.

“Why are you two suddenly acting close? You two aren't close, are you? Without me around, you're so awkward you can't even exchange a word, aren't you?”

Bisain pretended not to hear and spoke to Jin Hagun.

“Thank you for coming. If you need me next time, be sure to call me.”

“I will.”

“Even if you're grateful, you should be thanking me!”

Geom Muggeuk shouted, but the two pretended not to hear.

Geom Muggeuk sat in an old chair and watched the two carry the liquor crates.

“Instead of just watching, why don't you help too?”

“Then wouldn't our Young Alliance Leader's friendship toward you be damaged?”

“Excuses.”

Geom Muggeuk was lazing around like that when he suddenly spoke.

“Dangerous mechanisms and formations are spread throughout the Baekgye Merchant Guild. They've laid traps, so don't go there recklessly. They've been confirmed to be very high-level mechanisms and formations. I'm telling you in advance so you can deal with them carefully later.”

Bisain set down the crate and sat on it.

“I can't understand it.”

That the Baekgye Merchant Guild would try to kill him, and that they had spread such mechanisms and formations. They were closer to the Demonic Path Alliance than anyone.

“Could it be them?”

At Bisain's question, Geom Muggeuk nodded.

“It seems so, after all.”

There were too many parts that didn't make sense if this were the Baekgye Merchant Guild's independent action, and above all, it was too reckless.

“As those bastards have done until now, they must have dug into a place that would become the Baekgye Merchant Guild's weakness. Their dark scheme must be hidden somewhere no one could predict.”

Jin Hagun's and Bisain's expressions grew serious. They had already experienced how secretly and tenaciously those bastards dug into people.

“But this case feels a bit different from before. Rough somehow.”

Bisain and Jin Hagun knew well what Geom Muggeuk's words meant. That the substance of that feeling was danger.

Especially in this case, the Cult Leader or Alliance Leader would not intervene; the three of them had to resolve it purely on their own.

“We have to show them that we've grown up now too.”

At Geom Muggeuk's words, Bisain and Jin Hagun nodded.

“By the way, where are the Thirteen Wolves of the Demonic Path?”

At Geom Muggeuk's question, Bisain answered.

“They're looking around inside and outside Cheonhwaru. They're probably in a contest of spirit with your subordinates.”

* * *

The Thirteen Wolves of the Demonic Path were surprised twice upon arriving at Cheonhwaru.

Once outside, and once inside.

First, outside, it was because of the Faceless Guests.

The Thirteen Wolves searched the surroundings first. When the Young Alliance Leader held a drinking party at a pleasure house, naturally they had to inspect the surroundings perfectly.

When they knocked on the door of the nearest house, it opened.

Ten Faceless Guests were waiting there. They had already heard that they were here, but they still had to search.

An inexplicable tension heightened between the searching Thirteen Wolves and the watching Faceless Guests.

“Thank you for your cooperation.”

At Illang's greeting after finishing the search, the Faceless Guests nodded silently.

It wasn't just that house. All houses adjacent to Cheonhwaru were occupied by Faceless Guests.

In the past, they would have driven out the residents, but this time they paid ample money and rented them for a day.

It was surveillance, but if a large fight broke out here, the residents' lives would be in danger, so Geom Muggeuk had asked the Extreme Evil Soma to empty them out entirely.

The Faceless Guests were hiding in nearby houses, carriages, temporary structures, and up in trees. Truly, the outside was guarded like an iron fortress.

That was their surprise outside, and inside there was another surprise.

“It's the Demon Extermination Squad.”

Even without Irang telling him from the side, Illang could tell. Because he knew the person sweeping the yard over there.

Especially since the Thirteen Wolves of the Demonic Path and the Demon Extermination Squad were organizations directly under the successors, they were in competition with each other, knowingly or not. Therefore, they knew plenty about each other.

Those Demon Extermination Squad warriors were disguised as workers of the pleasure house, working here and there: chopping firewood, carrying luggage, delivering liquor.

The Thirteen Wolves felt the gravity of this matter to the bone. Until escorting Bisain here, they hadn't known such an ironclad defense was in place.

“This is the first time.”

At Illang's words, Irang looked at him.

“That Sima Jeong is conducting a joint operation. This is the first time since I joined the alliance.”

That was why this scene was so unfamiliar.

“The Demon Extermination Squad must have already inspected, but still look thoroughly. We do our job.”

“Yes!”

Thus the Thirteen Wolves also investigated the inside of Cheonhwaru thoroughly.

Checking for ambushes, and whether the food ingredients were safe. Truly, they took every precaution for the Young Alliance Leader's safety. They only exchanged glances with the Demon Extermination Squad warriors.

Fortunately, they finished the search without any clashes.

Illang felt that Sima Jeong's atmosphere had truly changed from before, just by this. That the Thirteen Wolves, the Faceless Guests, and the Demon Extermination Squad could be in one place. And so peacefully, at that.

Illang sensed that a new Jianghu was coming.

Because above the head of the Demon Extermination Squad warrior sweeping the yard, from within the tree branches beyond the wall, a white mask was smiling.

* * *

The three successors were in Geom Muggeuk's quarters.

Geom Muggeuk was showing them information received from Tongcheon Pavilion.

“It's data on the Baekgye Merchant Guild. Take a look.”

“Is it okay to just show us classified secrets like this?”

As Jin Hagun saw, they were documents from Tongcheon Pavilion.

“It's not.”

“But?”

“You all have to keep the secret.”

“I've heard that while a secret between two people can be kept, there's no such thing as a secret among three.”

Geom Muggeuk said with a smile.

“I'm not worried about you. If the secret is leaked, I'll be the one to do it first. That I'm this free of secrets with my friends! I wanted to brag.”

Bisain and Jin Hagun nodded with expressions that truly said he would do exactly that. The two dealing with him were in perfect sync.

The playfulness disappeared from Geom Muggeuk's face.

“It's content everyone knows, and it may not be very important, but I thought I should show you anyway so that nothing is missed.”

This was Geom Muggeuk's way. It was the same when he went outside and came back, reporting everything to his father, and to the demonic followers, and to these two here. Making sure there was absolutely no misunderstanding caused by not speaking was Geom Muggeuk's life motto.

Then, while looking through the documents, Bisain found something.

“When was this information obtained?”

“Recently.”

Because after the incident occurred, they had properly investigated them.

“Are all these contents true?”

“If you doubt Tongcheon Pavilion's information, you must never start a war with us in the future.”

That was how accurate Tongcheon Pavilion's information was.

“There's something wrong.”

Bisain held out the paper.

It was the part regarding why the Baekgye Merchant Guild harbored a grudge against Bisain.

The Demonic Path Alliance had misunderstood that the Extreme Evil Soma had massacred their warriors, and had pushed the Baekgye Merchant Guild into the territory he managed and supported them.

Afterward, the misunderstanding on both sides was cleared up, and the Demonic Path Alliance pushed away the Baekgye Merchant Guild while compensating the Extreme Evil Soma for the losses he had suffered over two years.

“At that time, the Baekgye Merchant Guild coughed up all the money they had earned over those two years, and afterward their household declined. Their relationship with the Vice-Guild Leader also worsened. There were also rumors that the guild leader's health had deteriorated. In the end, it is believed that this current grudge began from that revenge. What is wrong with this?”

Then Bisain spoke calmly.

“One might think that since we are of the Demonic Path, we would be merciless and shameless.”

Bisain said something unexpected.

“The money that we compensated back then was put up by the Main Alliance, not the Baekgye Merchant Guild.”

Geom Muggeuk was startled by those words. It was a very important part. Because that was the incident that became the reason for the grudge.

Since Bisain had no reason to lie, if they hadn't suffered losses? Then despite that, they had acted like victims as if they had paid that money?

“Then why did they target you?”

He could now understand the words Bisain had said earlier about them not making sense.

He had previously understood it like this. They were trying to kill the Young Alliance Leader just because they had to cough up money? He had said that didn't make sense.

Geom Muggeuk, who had been lost in deep thought, suddenly flinched. A suspicion arose: could it be.

“What if today's incident wasn't for the purpose of killing you?”

Bisain's and Jin Hagun's eyes also grew sharp.

“Then what is the purpose?”

At Bisain's question, Geom Muggeuk put forth one speculation.

“Destroying Cheonhwaru. What if that was the pure purpose from the beginning?”

Geom Muggeuk had focused on why they were trying to kill Bisain.

But if it wasn't that, and it was to destroy Cheonhwaru?

“When you met me before, you were curious. Just how were they planning to kill me? We even thought they might use a poison that could deceive the Thirteen Wolves of the Demonic Path. But they don't need to.”

Bisain picked up Geom Muggeuk's words.

“Just the fact that they tried to poison me would have gotten the master of Cheonhwaru executed, and Cheonhwaru would have been ruined.”

Why was Bisain the target?

Because he was the fastest and surest way to ruin Cheonhwaru.

Just the fact that there had been an attempt to assassinate the Young Alliance Leader, and with fabricated evidence and witnesses that the master of Cheonhwaru had ordered it, would have been enough.

The Baekgye Merchant Guild would likely not have been revealed as the culprit of the conspiracy either. They probably made it so that there was no fault other than inviting Bisain to Cheonhwaru.

But with the Heavenly Demon Cult intervening in this matter, and with him getting involved, the matter had grown bigger.

Bisain asked with calm eyes.

“Is there anywhere you suspect?”

Geom Muggeuk shook his head. At least one thing was certain.

“It must be someone trying to fill the void left by Cheonhwaru's disappearance.”

It must be someone trying to steal the master of Cheonhwaru's dream of becoming the Empress of the Night.

One trying to become the new ruler of the night.

Then Illang's voice was heard from outside.

“The Vice-Guild Leader of the Baekgye Merchant Guild has arrived.”

Geom Muggeuk looked at the outside scenery growing dark. Now the banquet was beginning, and it was time to get one step closer to the truth.

“Now, change your clothes.”

Chapter 542: No One Leaves Alive Today
--------------------------------------
Geugak Soma stood alone in the rear garden of Cheonhwaru.

Geom Mugeuk went to his side and stood beside him.

“It will be cloudy tonight.”

Indeed, just as Geugak Soma said, even now the moon was hidden behind clouds.

“I have good night vision, so I’ll be fine.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Geugak Soma’s gaze turned to him, to Geom Mugeuk looking at him with a bright face beneath the dark night sky.

“There is something I must tell you.”

Even if no one else needed to know, Geugak Soma did.

“It seems the target those behind this were ultimately aiming for was not the Young Alliance Leader, but Cheonhwaru.”

Surprisingly, Geugak Soma did not look shocked. Perhaps, aside from the master of Cheonhwaru, he was the one who best understood its worth.

“If the Pavilion Master is framed for attempting to assassinate the Young Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance, the dozens of Cheonhwaru establishments throughout the Central Plains will all be forced to close. At that point, those behind this were probably planning to use enormous funds to take over Cheonhwaru, or open new pleasure houses and seize those regions.”

Geugak Soma had once said so before. That the money Cheonhwaru earned was staggering.

Now that it had expanded to a number of pleasure houses incomparable to back then, the current value of Cheonhwaru must be beyond description.

Geugak Soma did not reveal his anger. But Geom Mugeuk could feel it. He was angry. They were people who had tried to kill his woman and steal Cheonhwaru.

“When their source of funds in Shaanxi was cut off, it seems they planned this in order to secure money.”

“And yet, of all people, they were caught by you, Young Cult Leader.”

“That’s thanks to the Dan sisters asking our sect’s small branch for help.”

If she had made a different choice, the outcome would have been completely different from now.

Thinking about it, his bond with the Dan sisters had also been deep. Not only had he been able to save Yeo Jeong, but he had also formed a deep connection with Seo Daeryong.

“Perhaps it was an affinity that Pavilion Master Seo brought to me.”

Geom Mugeuk gave the credit to Seo Daeryong.

“There is something Pavilion Master Seo hangs in his room like a family treasure. Do you know what it is?”

Of course, Geugak Soma sent him a look that said he did not know.

“It is the mask you once gave Pavilion Master Seo as a gift.”

Geugak Soma silently looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“I believe our bonds are connected in that way.”

Just then, from beyond the wall, the sound of people hurrying back and forth could be heard.

“It seems the deputy guildmaster of the Baekgye Merchant Guild has arrived.”

Geom Mugeuk began unwinding the finest Heavenly Silkworm thread wrapped around the hilt of the Black Demon Sword.

“One thing I cannot understand is that, even knowing our sect was involved, they still sent the deputy guildmaster. That is why we must be careful.”

He handed half of the finest Heavenly Silkworm thread to Geugak Soma.

This time, Geugak Soma accepted it without refusing.

The two of them took off their outer garments on the spot and wrapped it around their hearts, stomachs, and other vital parts of their bodies.

“Soma, please be careful.”

“You too, Young Cult Leader.”

The two looked at each other.

“Let us show them whom they tried to lay hands on.”

Within the eyeholes of the mask, Soma’s two eyes smiled coldly.

* * *

Pyeong Wi, the deputy guildmaster of the Baekgye Merchant Guild, entered Cheonhwaru.

A dozen or so subordinates followed behind him. He could have brought more, but in a place swarming with tigers and wolves, having more dogs would make no difference.

“Welcome, Guildmaster Pyeong.”

Yeo Jeong came all the way to the main gate and personally greeted him. Behind Yeo Jeong, Jin Haryeong stayed close, keeping her promise to protect her until the moment before death.

“The guest has already arrived.”

“He is an honored personage. I trust the preparations were made without issue?”

“Do not worry.”

Yeo Jeong led them inside.

In front of Cheonhwaru’s main building stood the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado. Illang examined Pyeong Wi and his subordinates with a sharp gaze.

The merchant guild’s warriors, watching the mood of the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado, took positions at the building entrance and around it.

Illang simply let them do as they pleased. He could have refused to let them in from the start, but he did not.

It was because he judged it better to keep them in sight and watch them.

Moreover, inside this place, warriors of the Demon Annihilation Squad were moving about here and there like workers, maintaining an ironclad defense.

A space for honored guests had been prepared on the first floor of Cheonhwaru.

In the wide space at the center, surrounded in a circle by entertainment rooms, tonight’s banquet had been set. Gorgeous folding screens, fine tables and chairs, and delicacies from mountains and seas prepared by Cheonhwaru’s cooks were laid out until the tables seemed about to break.

Bi Sain sat at the seat of honor.

“I greet the Young Alliance Leader.”

“It has been a long time.”

Pyeong Wi bowed respectfully with a cupped-fist salute. Bi Sain also accepted the greeting lightly.

“I heard you refused when they tried to escort you here.”

“I dislike troublesome things.”

“Allow me to pour you a cup.”

“Receive my drink first.”

After Bi Sain poured wine for him first, he filled his own cup himself. He did not allow Pyeong Wi to lay a hand on the wine bottle at all. The only thing he could do was use poison, after all.

“Thank you for inviting me like this.”

“I have long wished to host you.”

The two toasted and emptied their cups.

“Cheonhwaru is famous for having many beautiful courtesans. Since we have rented out the entire place today, let us bring in all the courtesans and allow you to enjoy yourself!”

The reason Pyeong Wi had asked for a special place to be arranged in this broad space was also because he intended to have every courtesan stand around them on all sides.

Pyeong Wi was about to call for the master of Cheonhwaru when Bi Sain stopped him.

“One moment. Before that, there is something I must say to the deputy guildmaster.”

“Please speak.”

“On the way here, I heard something strange.”

Bi Sain stared straight into Pyeong Wi’s eyes. A face that was frightening even at a glance now looked as if it were glaring, and Pyeong Wi found it truly terrifying.

“It was information that there is a plan to ambush me here at Cheonhwaru.”

“Who would dare scheme such a thing?”

Pyeong Wi’s shocked expression was rather convincing.

“They said it was you.”

Pyeong Wi asked with a confident face,

“Who did?”

At that moment, a voice came from behind.

“I did.”

Geom Mugeuk strode over and took a seat.

Then, without even exchanging greetings, he went straight to the point.

“A great banquet will be held here today. It will be a festival attended by my friends and everyone here. But it will not be a banquet for you.”

Pyeong Wi’s expression hardened. But he was not afraid. He had already anticipated such a situation.

“Today’s banquet will become one for our guild.”

At his words, filled with conviction, Bi Sain asked,

“Let me ask one thing. When you withdrew from Guizhou in the past, why did you spread the rumor that you had paid the money?”

For a moment, Pyeong Wi’s expression twitched.

“Did you say rumor?”

His words were polite, but they were clearly questioning.

“That money was paid by our Baekgye Merchant Guild.”

After saying this, perhaps because anger rose within him, he raised his voice and added,

“Did we not pay it?”

His eyes showed that he truly believed so.

“Because of that matter, the fortunes of our guild declined, and the guildmaster even fell ill from resentment. Yet you, in your position as Young Alliance Leader, dare lie so shamelessly.”

It was clear that he had resolved himself to die.

Bi Sain neither became angry nor made excuses. He only stared at him. To Pyeong Wi, it might have seemed as if he were acknowledging his words, but to Geom Mugeuk, he saw the image of his blunt friend, who simply did not bother with excuses.

“My friend may be of the evil sects, so you might think him cold-blooded and shameless, but at the very least, he is not the petty sort who claims to have given something he never gave.”

Of course, Pyeong Wi did not believe those words.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Bi Sain and said,

“I think I know where they burrowed in.”

Bi Sain understood what Geom Mugeuk meant.

It meant there was someone within the Baekgye Merchant Guild who had embezzled the money. Someone in a position capable of deceiving even this deputy guildmaster. And someone capable of using this man.

“Who is there who could deceive even you?”

Just as Pyeong Wi was about to swallow back the words, What nonsense is this! he flinched in surprise. The image of one person who could currently do such a thing had come to mind.

—I trust only you, Deputy Guildmaster.

The back of the guildmaster’s wife as she kept vigil by the sickbed.

He also recalled her expression after the guildmaster fell ill, as if her soul had been taken by something. And the uncharacteristic venom with which she had begged for revenge.

‘What absurd thoughts!’

Soon, Pyeong Wi glared coldly at the two of them.

“You shameless scoundrels! You remain filthy to the very end!”

Unlike him, who had grown agitated, Geom Mugeuk remained calm.

“Very well. As you say, what did you trust in to come to a place crawling with such wicked men?”

“At least not your consciences.”

“What did the person who sent you say? That if you made the Young Alliance Leader drunk like this, they would come save you?”

“They will not save me.”

Pyeong Wi spoke with a meaningful look in his eyes.

“Because they said no one would leave this place alive today.”

It was at that very moment, as Geom Mugeuk’s and Bi Sain’s gazes met.

Sssssssssss!

From far away came the mournful sound of wind.

In the darkness, a black palanquin was flying toward them, cutting through the air.

As if gliding along an invisible road of silk, the palanquin flew smoothly without making a sound.

Saaak.

Wherever the palanquin passed, flowers and trees withered, and insects and birds fell. The sight was as though all life nearby vanished and the world turned black and white.

From the lanterns held in the hands of the four black-clad figures flying with it, standing on all sides of the palanquin, a blue light like ghost fire flowed out.

The Faceless Guests who had been hiding around Cheonhwaru revealed themselves from every direction.

On the roof of Cheonhwaru, Geugak Soma watched the scene with his arms crossed.

Geugak Soma gave no order to stop it, nor any order to attack.

The Faceless Guests watched with cold gazes as the palanquin passed.

A suffocating, dreadful energy flowed out from within the palanquin.

The gazes of the Faceless Guests turned again toward the place from which the palanquin had flown. The palanquin was not the only thing that had come here today.

At the end of the road, within the darkness, something was densely gathered.

Just as the sinister energy seemed to grow stronger little by little,

They all opened their eyes at once.

What could be seen in the darkness were countless pupils.

Every pupil had a white rim around it. It gave off a truly bizarre feeling.

Geugak Soma could tell. He knew what martial art produced eyes like that when cultivated.

Dark Heaven Night Divine Art.

It was a martial art that could only be used at night, allowing one to display strength far beyond their original abilities. In exchange, once one cultivated it, one could never again see sunlight.

They allowed the palanquin through, but they did not allow those people to pass.

The Faceless Guests all descended and lined up to block them from coming closer.

Some of the Faceless Guests crouched on tree branches, while others stood atop the wall and looked down at them.

The smiling eyes within their masks held no fear whatsoever. It did not matter who the opponent was, or where they had come from. Whether their pupils had white rims or yellow rims, fear was something the other side ought to feel.

Because the place they came from was the Valley of Villains.

The palanquin descended into the front courtyard of Cheonhwaru.

The lantern light in the hands of the four black-clad figures bewitched the minds of those who looked upon it.

“Kraaagh!”

A warrior of the merchant guild near the palanquin, staring at the light as if entranced, staggered toward it before vomiting blood and collapsing. The one with the weakest inner strength among them had been affected first.

They retreated from the palanquin.

Instead, the one standing before it was Jin Hagun, who had been carrying wine bottles.

Jin Hagun stared at the palanquin with sunken eyes. The subordinates of the Demon Annihilation Squad deployed around the palanquin, encircling it. The aura of the four standing at the corners of the palanquin was far from ordinary.

Whoooooosh.

A fierce wind blew in and opened all the doors on the first floor of Cheonhwaru at once.

The moment Geom Mugeuk saw the palanquin, he knew who the opponent was.

The Sixth King among the Twelve Zodiac Kings.

Dark King Heuk Sarin.

Among the Twelve Zodiac Kings, she was known to be the weakest by day and the strongest by night.

Though she was a woman, she was said to use the power of darkness, and when night fell, no one dared challenge her—a true ruler of the night.

Her martial arts were like treasure buried deep in darkness, known to no one.

At last, a woman’s voice came from within the palanquin.

“All those who will rule the future of murim have gathered here.”

It was an exceedingly bewitching voice, one that shook a person’s very soul. One of the merchant company’s warriors, who had retreated at the sound of her voice, began walking toward the palanquin.

Jin Hagun subdued the acupoints of the man as he passed by his side, then flung him backward.

Then he sent a voice transmission to his subordinates.

—There is an energy in her voice that controls the mind. All of you, steady your hearts and raise your internal energy to resist it.

Geom Mugeuk answered her words.

“Isn’t the future murim what you people are trying to dominate? It seems to me you’re working even harder than we are.”

A woman’s laughter came from within the palanquin. It was clear and gentle, a laugh that made the listener’s heart feel pleasantly at ease. But her laughter shook the mind even more powerfully than her words had.

“As expected, Young Cult Leader, you recognize me at a glance.”

“Who are you?”

Geom Mugeuk asked her, pretending not to know.

“I am a sinful soul who cannot live in the light.”

“Then you’ve met the right man. Was I not once the Pavilion Master of Hwangcheon Pavilion? I’m quite good at handling sinners. Would you like to receive a consultation? In a month, you might even repent and turn over a new leaf.”

“As expected, Young Cult Leader, you are as delightful as the rumors say.”

“I could have lived even more delightfully if only you people didn’t exist.”

The woman laughed softly.

“I am well aware that you have been deeply involved in our affairs, Young Cult Leader. Thanks to you, many plans have been changed.”

After my return, so much had truly changed. We had grown stronger, and they had grown weaker.

I could not know what effect this change might have had on Hwa Mugi. Why he remained silent. Whether he would appear at that time in the future, as fate had originally dictated, or whether he would emerge earlier. Or perhaps even later. It was truly impossible to know.

“Your plans will continue to change. As you can see, we’re younger and more vigorous than you are, are we not? If we keep blocking you again and again, you people will grow old and die first.”

Beside him was Bisain, and far off in the courtyard was Jin Hagun; the three of them looked at one another.

The woman spoke again in that same soft, bewitching voice.

“What a pity. That I will no longer be able to see this wonderful friendship.”

And that confidence was not something she was speaking of herself.

“There is someone here who very much wished to see our Young Cult Leader.”

For an instant, Geom Mugeuk was startled. It was truly unexpected.

The door of the palanquin began to open slowly.

The inside of the palanquin, veiled by bead curtains, was dark. Even with Divine Eye Art, which could see through any curtain, he could not see inside. In other words, some special art of darkness was being used within.

In a silence so still it seemed no one even breathed, everyone’s gaze focused there.

Clank.

With a metallic sound, a man’s hand emerged and parted the bead curtain.

From the blackened, dead skin on the back of his hand, poison energy was rising, and as if to suppress that energy, chains flowing with a blue light were wrapped tightly around it.

Through the parted curtain, the man’s face appeared.

His face was even more horrifying. A face charred black and twisted, with only his intense eyes shining coldly.

“It’s been a long time.”

Chapter 543: This Frail and Young Me
------------------------------------
“You were alive.”

Geom Mugeuk recognized who it was, even though the other’s appearance had changed greatly.

He was none other than Tu Wang, returned alive from hell.

Now Geom Mugeuk could understand why they had dared to proceed with today’s banquet even though they knew he was here.

Why the movements behind the scenes this time had felt different from before. Why they had been so rough.

It was because of this man’s desire for revenge—because of Tu Wang.

It was now clear why Bisain was the one used as bait in the Lord of the Cheonhwa Tower’s trap. It was because the chief of the Sado Alliance had driven him to the brink of death.

Tu Wang slowly descended from the palanquin.

Clank. Clank.

Wrapped tightly around his chest was a chain glowing with a blue light. The chain radiating a cold gleam was no ordinary metal. It gave the impression that it would display far greater power than the armor or gauntlets he had worn before.

A woman’s voice came from inside the palanquin.

“When we saved this man using the Anlu Guihuanshu, the deadly poison had spread throughout his entire body to a state where not even the Dara Immortal could have saved him. Your Poison King’s poison truly was severe.”

Indeed, even now, toxic qi that had yet to be fully removed flowed from Tu Wang’s breath.

If the Poison King learned of this, he would be furious. A fine piece of leverage had been found.

“But we decided to save him. It required tremendous effort and enormous investment. He had to undergo two great rituals and six procedures.”

At the memory, Tu Wang’s expression twisted. He had endured such agony that the words *just kill me* had nearly escaped him.

He had endured all that pain for this very moment today.

“As you can see, he was worth the investment.”

Tu Wang’s energy was tremendous.

He gave off a feeling of strength greater than any enemy he had ever met until now. If the Tu Wang of the past had felt like a mere brawler, the current Tu Wang felt as if he had transcended that stage.

It felt as if he were looking at a fighting spirit that had transcended victory and defeat, even life and death itself.

Death resided in his two eyes.

There was a white ring around his pupils. Geom Mugeuk knew what that meant.

‘He has even mastered the Dark Heaven Evil God Technique!’

It meant that Tu Wang, already strong, having received all manner of great rituals and mastered the Dark Heaven Evil God Technique on top of that, had become far stronger than before.

On top of that, he was emitting the Poison King’s toxic qi.

While he himself was invulnerable to all poisons and unaffected by the toxic qi, if Bisain or Jin Hajeon fought him, they would not be able to endure it simply because of that qi. It was truly as if Tu Wang and the Poison King were attacking together.

Tu Wang truly possessed the confidence that he could destroy everyone here single-handedly.

“Just how strong have you become?”

“As much as I have given my life.”

Having mastered the Dark Heaven Evil God Technique, he had reached a state where he could no longer see the light of day.

It was solely for this revenge.

Tu Wang slowly shifted his steps. With each step he took, everyone’s gaze was drawn. The fighting spirit Tu Wang currently emanated was intense enough to face everyone here and then some.

He had been looking around as if seeing the world for the first time, but then—

Shwaaaaak!

Puh-puh-puh-puhk!

With a wave of his hand, the martial artists gathered there from the Baike Merchant Group all burst at the torso and died.

It was a move that allowed no time to block or defend. From this single move alone, one could tell how much stronger he had become. It was truly fast and powerful.

The one who let out a shriek at that sight was Vice Lord Pyeong Wi. Seeing those he had brought all die in an instant, he was aghast.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to him in a low voice.

“You didn’t know it would turn out this way?”

Of course, Pyeong Wi had come prepared to die. But he hadn’t known they would kill his subordinates first. If they were to die, it should be last. He should see them take revenge first!

“You were deceived by them. It wasn’t the Sado Alliance—they used you. To create today’s gathering.”

“Don’t make me laugh!”

With his subordinates dead, Pyeong Wi instead believed even less. Like a man possessed, he leaped and prostrated himself before the palanquin.

“Kill them as promised!”

To him who had rushed into her embrace like this, the Dark King was cruel.

“The young lord’s words are correct. You were used by us.”

In that instant, Pyeong Wi’s face went completely rigid. The truth was delivered to him, shocked and bewildered.

“To be precise, it was the Lord’s wife who used you.”

In that instant, Pyeong Wi’s eyes went wide. The worry he had dared not voice was becoming reality.

The very moment he learned the truth.

“Uwaaaaah!”

He let out a miserable scream. Before anyone knew it, Tu Wang was behind him, grabbing his neck.

He didn’t break his neck. It would have been better if he had.

The moment the toxic qi flowing from Tu Wang’s hand invaded his body, he writhed in terrible agony and perished on the spot.

Thud.

Tu Wang threw his corpse before Geom Mugeuk.

And he laid the responsibility for that death at Geom Mugeuk’s feet.

“This is all because of you.”

Of course, Geom Mugeuk was not one to accept it meekly.

“With so many eyes watching, do you really think you can force this nonsense on me?”

“If not for you, I wouldn’t be here. If not for you, I wouldn’t have this toxic qi in my body.”

He blamed Geom Mugeuk, saying that by revealing his existence hidden within the Sado Alliance, things had turned out this way. Because Geom Mugeuk had drawn in the chief of the Sado Alliance and the Poison King.

“Why is it my fault? It’s your fault for hiding in a place you shouldn’t have hidden in the first place. Why is it my fault? It’s because of your petty heart that couldn’t let me go with a ‘good work’ after using me.”

He had tried to make Geom Mugeuk feel guilty, but couldn’t even break even.

“And why are you doing this to me? If you want revenge, go to the chief of the Sado Alliance or the Poison King. Why? Are you scared of the two of them? Is that why you’re tormenting weak, young me?”

At the words *weak and young,* Bisain and Jin Hajeon let out wry smiles. Truly, this was Geom Mugeuk—able to make one laugh even in such a terrifying situation.

Of course, to Tu Wang, those words touched a sore spot.

“Shut up!”

Clank.

A metallic sound came from Tu Wang’s hand as he clenched his fist. Tu Wang, whose expression had seemed like it wouldn’t waver no matter the situation, was stiff.

“The chief of the Sado Alliance will soon die by my hand as well.”

Tu Wang intended to kill everyone here and then kill the chief of the Sado Alliance as well. He had felt it. This body, forcibly maintained by great rituals, could not survive for long. Before he died, he would kill them all. The organization had likely saved him because they wanted that too.

Then the Dark King spoke from the palanquin.

“As expected, the rumors that the young lord’s tongue is sharper than his sword are true.”

Geom Mugeuk could tell that the Dark King had stepped forward to calm the agitated Tu Wang. She was definitely the more troublesome one to deal with.

“Truth, my foot! Baseless rumors.”

Geom Mugeuk turned to Bisain and asked.

“Isn’t my sword sharper than my mouth?”

At that, Bisain shook his head firmly.

Geom Mugeuk made an incredulous face.

“Why are you trying to be funny at such a serious occasion?”

Bisain’s eyes said it. *Isn’t that you?*

This time, Geom Mugeuk looked at Jin Hajeon.

“I trust you will speak the truth.”

“I don’t want to have a war of words with you.”

He spoke so calmly that it sounded like the truth.

Laughter flowed from inside the palanquin. Both Geom Mugeuk and the Dark King were composed.

“Shall we see directly which is sharper?”

The moment those words ended, the palanquin door opened again.

Shwaaaa.

A hand parted the hanging curtain. It was a bony, wrinkled hand.

The person who revealed his face was an old man. There was no way the Dark King, Tu Wang, and this old man could all have been inside the palanquin, so this was the moment one could be certain that the space inside was no ordinary space.

The old man’s eyes were incomparably sharp.

His old dao robe was covered in bizarre patterns, and the unfolded fan he held was also covered in indecipherable patterns.

A bizarre, murderous aura emanated from his entire body.

The first to recognize him was Jin Hajeon.

“Tian Guai!”

He was a public enemy of the martial world who had thrown the martial world into chaos by mastering all manner of sorcery and formations. His sorcery was truly vicious and cruel, causing countless people to lose their lives to him.

Now they could know who had created the formation installed at the Baike Merchant Group.

Tian Guai looked at Jin Hajeon and spoke.

“You tried so hard to catch me, so try catching me now.”

The Demon Slaughter Squad martial artists simultaneously stepped before Jin Hajeon and pointed their swords.

But Tian Guai was not frightened in the slightest.

Another figure revealed itself from the palanquin. This time, it was not a person. Something leaped out.

Grrrr.

Surprisingly, what came out of the palanquin was a black dog as large as an adult human. It was no ordinary dog. Its build was larger than a wolf’s, and it had huge fangs.

Black energy wafted from its large body like a haze. It was truly a hellhound worthy of guarding the gates of hell.

It was not just one dog. Dozens of hellhounds came out in succession. The sight was truly bizarre; it felt as if this palanquin were connected to hell.

The last person to come out was recognized by Bisain.

“Quan Ren!”

He was an absolute master of the evil sect who called himself a dog butcher while wielding dark sorcery among the sorcerous arts. He was someone who rarely revealed himself to the outside world, yet he had shown himself here today. The hellhounds he summoned were fearsome creatures known to bite and kill even masters.

The hellhounds looked up at one spot and growled lowly.

That spot was the roof where the Extreme Evil Soma stood.

The Extreme Evil Soma silently looked down at the dozens of hellhounds growling at him.

The four black-clothed figures who had flown in with the palanquin stepped before Bisain holding lanterns.

The Sado Thirteen Wolves simultaneously drew their swords and pointed them at the black-clothed figures. Even though they were facing forces they could not handle, the four showed no fear.

Then Bisain saw it. Their shadows stretched long and connected to the palanquin. It was a bizarre sight that made them seem as one body with the palanquin.

Tian Guai for Jin Hajeon, the dog butcher for the Extreme Evil Soma, and the four black-clothed guards who had escorted the palanquin for Bisain.

And Tu Wang for Geom Mugeuk.

The problem was that the Dark King still remained inside the palanquin. It was impossible to know how she would intervene in the fight.

Jin Haryeong retreated as far back as possible with Yeo Jeong. This was not a fight for her to step forward in. She knew it was a fight where she would have to run, carrying Yeo Jeong on her back if necessary.

‘Orabeoni! Be careful!’

The last person she looked at was Geom Mugeuk. As always, she asked again.

‘Please take care of our orabeoni! I will protect the Tower Lord until the moment of my death… No, even if I die, I will protect her.’

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes, meeting hers, turned upward.

The Extreme Evil Soma stood on the roof. Still with arms crossed, he watched the situation below. It was impossible to know what he was thinking, but the most trustworthy person in today’s fight was the Extreme Evil Soma.

Geom Mugeuk and the Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes met.

His two eyes behind the mask were smiling. From that abyssal smile, Geom Mugeuk could tell.

That he had already become the true Extreme Evil Soma.

Then that was enough. Without worrying about the rear, he would focus on Tu Wang before him.

The first to move was Tu Wang.

“Shall you taste my fist?”

Tu Wang extended his fist forward. Along the chain wrapped around his fist, blue energy flowed coldly. He was full of confidence and composure.

‘He knows I have mastered the Guhwa Demonic Art.’

Does he have the confidence to endure the Guhwa Demonic Art that has not achieved great success?

“Then let us have a small taste.”

Geom Mugeuk did not draw his sword but extended his fist.

Fist against fist.

Meaning they would have a contest of strength.

Huaaaaaang.

Tu Wang charged at Geom Mugeuk and threw a punch.

Geom Mugeuk threw a punch without dodging.

What met Tu Wang’s fist, imbued with his Linked Cloud-Storm Fist, was the Thunderbolt Asura Fist learned from the Fist Demon.

The moment fist met fist, an ear-splitting explosion rang out.

Kwa-ah-ang!

Geom Mugeuk’s fist struck the chain wrapped around Tu Wang’s fist.

It was an attack infused with powerful qi, but Tu Wang didn’t budge.

With their fists against each other, Geom Mugeuk and Tu Wang glared at each other.

Of course, it wasn’t an attack using full power, but the same was likely true for Tu Wang.

Tu Wang twisted his body and threw another punch.

The second strike was far faster and more powerful than before.

The second fist collided with fist. An even greater roar burst out than before, but Tu Wang’s fist and chain did not break.

Once again, the two men’s fists met in midair. The sensation of the cold chain was transmitted to the end of Geom Mugeuk’s fist. That sensation spoke thus: *I will never break!*

Tu Wang’s third punch flew in again. Even faster and stronger.

Chae-aeng!

It was not the sound of fist striking fist.

Geom Mugeuk had leaped back, and in his hand, the Black Demon Sword was already drawn.

He could not withstand Tu Wang’s fist technique with the yet-incomplete Thunderbolt Asura Fist.

Having exchanged fists, he felt it through his body. That Tu Wang had become incomparably stronger than before.

“You’ve become a monster.”

Geom Mugeuk turned to Bisain and spoke.

“My friend, if I ever told you to trust only in me, I retract those words.”

Geom Mugeuk did not lose his composure until the very end. And he gave everyone these words of encouragement.

“Today, each of us must survive on our own.”

Chapter 544: I Am Someone Who Lives with Anxiety
------------------------------------------------
At the words that he had become a monster, the Battle King laughed at first.

Even he thought the expression was fitting.

But soon, an unbearable rage surged up within him.

Originally, he was the man who should have become the leader of the Sado Alliance. Yet now, he was spewing poison from his entire body and being called a monster.

‘And whose fault is all this!’

An aura in which fighting spirit and killing intent mingled erupted from his body with overwhelming force.

Whoooooosh!

At the energy that flew toward them as if it would pierce through body and mind alike, the martial artists of both the Murim Alliance and the Sado Alliance drew up their internal energy and steadied their hearts.

As if he had not yet shown the full extent of his skill, the Battle King revealed an even stronger presence.

For now, as if they intended to watch the battle between the Battle King and Geom Mugeuk, the other enemies did not move.

He did not know when the Dark King would order them to attack, but Geom Mugeuk focused only on the Battle King.

If he followed his heart, he wanted to use the Ninefire Demonic Art immediately. But inside Cheonhwaru, there were far more martial artists on their side than enemies. Outside the wall were the Faceless Guests as well. If he used it carelessly, their own people could be swept up in it.

If he used anything, it would have to be the Human-Extinction Form or the Darkness Flash, something aimed at a single person.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the palanquin behind the Battle King.

That cautious Dark King had surely anticipated that he would use the Ninefire Demonic Art. That was why he had to be as careful as possible. There was no telling what preparations they had made.

Hiding those thoughts, Geom Mugeuk showed ease.

“Doesn’t hanging metal off those fists make you slower?”

“It’ll hurt more instead. Every time I look at these chains, I’ll remember you. You, reduced to a handful of bloody pulp on these fists.”

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“After I kill you, I’m thinking of taking those chains as spoils.”

To remember me? Naturally, that was what the Battle King thought. Of course, Geom Mugeuk did not live up to that expectation.

“We can’t hold ourselves back when we see chains made with ten-thousand-year cold iron.”

It was something he had once said about Gowol’s chains.

“I’m going to melt them down and make daggers with these friends as a keepsake this time. Ah, I suppose I’ll think of you now and then when I peel fruit.”

An even deeper killing intent flowed through the Battle King’s eyes.

“You’ll have to melt these chains in the fires of hell!”

Shwaaaaaak!

When the Battle King’s fist struck the air, a strand of fist aura was unleashed.

Kwaaaang!

The aura, stretching out like a beam of light, slammed straight into Geom Mugeuk.

Whoooooom!

A whirlwind raged around Geom Mugeuk, and dust burst up in a cloud.

Rumble, rumble.

Far behind where Geom Mugeuk had been standing, a wall collapsed outright.

Then, farther back, the wall of the building behind it collapsed, and again the force pierced through the inner building and brought down the wall of the rear courtyard. It was a terrifying power that seemed as though it would have pierced on endlessly if there had been more buildings behind it.

Fist wind swept through the surroundings, fiercely whipping the hems of the watching people’s clothes.

Everyone’s gazes turned toward Geom Mugeuk. Bi Sain, Jin Hagun, Jin Haryeong, and Yeo Jeong all looked at him with eyes full of worry. That last move had been so powerful that the thought he might have suffered a grave injury arose naturally.

Only the Extreme Evil Soma stared at the place where Geom Mugeuk stood with an unchanged gaze.

The rising dust settled.

Geom Mugeuk was standing there with his arms crossed before him.

He had blocked it without taking even a single step back.

The Battle King had expected this scene—Geom Mugeuk suddenly coughing up blood.

Instead of blood, Geom Mugeuk spat out an irritating remark.

“Those chains—what’s so impressive about them that we’d have to go all the way to hell to melt them? Artisan Gwak of our cult’s iron workshop would melt them down just fine.”

The Battle King’s expression turned grave.

‘His internal energy overwhelms mine?’

When he had come back to life this time, his internal energy had also improved greatly in the course of the treatment. On top of that, the Dark Heaven Night Divine Art was being displayed as well, so blocking that last move in place should only have been possible for someone on the level of the Demon Cult Leader or the Alliance Leader.

In the Battle King’s two eyes, the desire to defeat Geom Mugeuk swelled into madness.

“Looks like I’m not the only monster here.”

At the Battle King’s words, Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“It wouldn’t be any fun if there were only ugly monsters like you, would it? I’m the peerless handsome man of the monster world, the peerless beautiful monster.”

He calls that talking? The Battle King had truly never seen such a powerful expert make people so furious with words.

At that very moment!

Whoosh!

Geom Mugeuk crossed the space in an instant with the Nether King Step. In a fight against a formidable enemy, when Geom Mugeuk made jokes to provoke his opponent, it was always to unsettle their mind.

Geom Mugeuk’s sword, which had rushed in instantly, thrust toward his face in a surprise attack.

Shiiiiik!

The Battle King twisted his body to avoid the sword and threw a punch. The Battle King was certainly strong.

Whoooom!

Geom Mugeuk’s sword grazed the Battle King’s ear, and the Battle King’s fist grazed Geom Mugeuk’s jaw.

Skaaaak!

The Black Demon Sword immediately changed direction and tried to strike the Battle King’s neck, while the Battle King’s fist flew toward his side.

Geom Mugeuk did not dodge and sent his sword forward as it was. The one who gave up his attack and evaded was the Battle King.

Leaping back, he asked with a startled expression,

“Were you prepared for mutual destruction?”

Though it had only been his side, if he had taken that power head-on, it meant he would not have survived.

“No. I knew you’d dodge.”

“Why were you so sure?”

“If you didn’t have that much attachment to life, would you have come back looking like that?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s provocation, the Battle King surged with killing intent and charged.

Chang-chang-chang-chang!

The Battle King blocked the Black Demon Sword with his fists. The chains wrapped around his fists were not cut even by the Black Demon Sword.

Every time sword and fist collided, sparks flew with sharp metallic sounds.

They were two men whose experience in battle could be said to have reached the extreme.

They were a volcano and a sea, each trying to swallow the other whole.

If the Battle King’s fists were exploding lava, then Geom Mugeuk’s sword became a tidal wave that crashed down upon him.

The two, who had been exchanging sword and fist in one spot, charged forward together as they fought.

They fought their way into Cheonhwaru. From the first floor to the second, from the second to the third. Only the sounds of their battle could be heard. They were not visible, but the sounds were so intense that it felt as vivid as if they were fighting right before one’s eyes. The higher they climbed, the faster the sounds of sword and fist colliding became.

Bang!

The two broke through the roof and soared into the night sky.

Chang-chang-chang-chang-chang-chang-chang-chang!

Sword and fist embroidered the sky.

It looked as though dozens of people were fighting at once.

To the eyes of those watching, dozens of swords and dozens of fists appeared in the night sky at the same time.

The lines created by sword and fist were truly art.

A wonder beyond admiration.

The gazes of the Faceless Guests were also turned toward the sky, and the eyes in the darkness looked up as well.

At least in this moment, everyone watched not as enemy or ally, but as a single martial artist.

The emotion that arose in Jin Haryeong’s heart was this:

‘Beautiful.’

Jin Hagun felt regret. He wanted to show this scene to every martial artist of the Murim Alliance. Originally, he should have said, These are the enemies you face, but—

He wanted to tell them this: This is the path you must walk. This is what you train to unfold. That was how perfect the battle between the two appeared in Jin Hagun’s eyes.

Chang-chang-chang-chang-chang-chang-chang-chang!

As sword and fist collided, brilliant sparks bloomed.

Thus, new stars appeared and vanished in the night sky.

Bi Sain felt two conflicting emotions.

First, he was glad. Glad that a Geom Mugeuk this strong was his friend.

At the same time, he was afraid. He was looking at the future Demon Cult Leader. He was already this strong now, so how strong would he be once he became the Demon Cult Leader? To match this cult leader, how hard would Bi Sain have to work?

He was sincerely glad, and yet his chest grew heavy at the same time.

Shwaaaaaaak.

The two fell back down to the ground and fought.

As the wall collapsed with a rumble, the Faceless Guests confronting one another outside and the eyes in the darkness came into view.

Gradually, the number of martial artists in the darkness increased, so the number of eyes became countless, but the Faceless Guests showed no sign of fear. As the standoff dragged on, they left only a few people in front while the rest sat perched on the wall or squatted on tree branches.

Thus, as the two exchanged sword and fist, they passed between the Faceless Guests and the eyes in the darkness.

Thud!

Geom Mugeuk was shoved backward, sliding all the way back into Cheonhwaru.

The gazes of the Faceless Guests all turned at once toward the inside of Cheonhwaru. There, Geom Mugeuk was grimacing as he rubbed his chest.

This time, the Faceless Guests looked in the opposite direction.

Though he had driven a blow into Geom Mugeuk’s chest, the Battle King did not rejoice. Blood was streaming down from the deep cut on his arm.

The Battle King strode once more between them and walked into Cheonhwaru.

The Faceless Guests watched him pass with strange gazes.

Thus the two stood facing each other again.

“You! What the hell are you?”

No matter that he was the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult, no matter that he was a genius of martial arts—

The Battle King glared at Geom Mugeuk with an expression that said he could not possibly understand.

Still wearing a pained expression, Geom Mugeuk rubbed his chest and said,

“If they made you, who were dying, this strong, it must have cost an enormous amount of money.”

For a moment, it seemed unclear why he was saying that all of a sudden.

“I want to send you back alive again.”

“What nonsense is that?”

The Battle King still did not understand what he meant.

“If I send you back half-dead, your organization won’t be able to do anything else while they’re busy bringing you back to life again, will they?”

“You lunatic! You get hit and still spout nonsense!”

Just then, as blazing rage exploded in the Battle King’s eyes—

The Dark King’s voice came from the palanquin. She had stepped in to calm the Battle King’s excitement.

“I heard this many times. That the Young Cult Leader was far stronger than we expected. I thought it was an excuse for failure, but it seems it was true.”

Bi Sain and Jin Hagun both agreed with her words. Geom Mugeuk grew stronger every time they met him. Realistically, there was no way to understand how that was possible.

Having confirmed Geom Mugeuk’s ability firsthand, the Dark King introduced a change into this evenly matched fight.

“Shall we begin the hunt?”

It was an order given to the Dog Seller.

If a chaotic melee broke out on all sides, the one whose concentration would be shaken would naturally be Geom Mugeuk. The Battle King would not care in the slightest about the lives of his comrades, but Geom Mugeuk would care more than anyone.

Sure enough, Geom Mugeuk looked at everyone. His eyes said, Hold out until I come save you! but the words that came from his mouth were the exact opposite.

“Hurry up and kill them, then come save me.”

A faint smile formed at the corners of Bi Sain’s mouth. Yes, even in this urgent moment, I must learn from that composure.

Bi Sain said to the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance lined up before him,

“You heard him. Let us dispose of them quickly and go help the Young Cult Leader.”

“Yes!”

The Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance answered vigorously.

The four black-masked figures standing before them spread out to the left and right, creating distance. They displayed an unusual lightness skill, as though gliding over ice.

It was the same for Jin Hagun.

“Did everyone hear that?”

At his powerful question, the Demon Annihilation Squad answered resoundingly, as if refusing to lose to the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance.

The Heavenly Monster faced them alone, yet for some reason his expression was relaxed. It was clear that something had been prepared.

The hellhounds looked up at the Extreme Evil Soma and growled. Red light blazed and rose from both their eyes.

Shuk!

At that very moment, the Blood Calamity Finger was unleashed in a way no one had expected.

The finger wind was not headed for the hellhounds.

Thud!

The place where the Blood Calamity Finger struck, stretching out as fast as light, was the Dog Seller.

A hole was pierced through the forehead of the Dog Seller, who failed to dodge in time.

The Dog Seller’s slowly toppling body vanished with a puff, turning into black smoke.

The Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes seemed to have anticipated this situation.

“As expected.”

The weakness of sorcery that summoned beasts like these was that the opponent would aim for the one controlling them.

The Dog Seller had also anticipated that the Extreme Evil Soma would aim for him and had put forth an illusion.

The Extreme Evil Soma could tell where he was hiding. Since he was facing the Extreme Evil Soma, he would not be able to control these creatures delicately from far away.

The Extreme Evil Soma’s gaze turned toward the hellhounds.

He had hidden by becoming one of those hellhounds.

From the two eyes behind the Extreme Evil Soma’s mask, pure white demonic energy poured out.

The air around them began to grow heavy and stifling.

The Extreme Evil Soma’s unique demonic energy, the Laughing Evil Heart, had been unleashed.

Graaaah!

A nearby hellhound grew excited and leapt up toward the roof where the Extreme Evil Soma stood.

Thud!

The Blood Calamity Finger pierced through its body, but it continued to charge.

As the hellhound lunged to tear out his throat, he seized it by the scruff of the neck and slammed it repeatedly into the floor.

Bang! Bang! Bang!

Sssssss.

The hellhound began to vanish into black smoke.

This, too, was not a real living dog, but sorcery created by the Dog Seller.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk’s and the Extreme Evil Soma’s gazes met.

The Extreme Evil Soma leapt down into the middle of the hellhounds and said,

“Do not worry. I am damn good at dogfights!”

From all sides, the hellhounds charged at the Extreme Evil Soma in unison.

As the Extreme Evil Soma’s battle began, the four black-clad men holding lanterns, dragging long shadows connected to the palanquin, flew toward Bi Sain.

Jin Hagun and the Demon Annihilation Squad did not see that sight.

At the very moment they were about to attack the Heavenly Monster—

All of them disappeared from that place.

Geom Mugeuk could tell. They had all entered the formation created by the Heavenly Monster.

As everyone entered battle like that, the Dark King’s enchanting voice came from the palanquin.

“I can feel the Young Cult Leader’s heart trembling with fear that he might lose his friends.”

How could Geom Mugeuk not know she was trying to shake him? Even so, Geom Mugeuk readily admitted it.

“You saw correctly. I am a man who lives with the anxiety that a friend might die in a place like this.”

“Then if we take your friend hostage, we can kill the Young Cult Leader easily.”

Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

“That is not true either.”

“Why is that?”

“Because I am selfish. Truly selfish, so I want to save all my friends. For my own sake. Because I want to become someone who saved them all.”

“……”
