“So it won’t work. Because I know all too well that it is my weakness.”

The Dark King fell silent for a moment, then soon spoke calmly.

“It is not easy to understand a person like the Young Cult Leader.”

“Then I will teach you one thing.”

Geom Mugeuk brought up something she had not expected at all.

“Because of my anxiety that I might lose someone I must not lose, I also underwent a Grand Art. Every day, at that.”

He could feel both the Battle King and the Dark King inside the palanquin being startled.

“My Grand Art will be no less formidable than yours.”

“What Grand Art is it?”

Because of the ability he had shown earlier, and because of his status as the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, the thought that he truly might have undergone some sort of Grand Art would occur to them.

It had been a conversation begun to shake Geom Mugeuk, yet instead, the two of them were now thrown into confusion.

Geom Mugeuk did not tell them and drew the Black Demon Sword again. It was something he had done every day, from his life before his regression until now.

“Taste my Grand Art for yourselves.”

The name of that Grand Art was effort.

Chapter 545: If You Live With Me You’ll End Up Swinging at Air Too.
-------------------------------------------------------------------
Geom Mugik had one conviction that never changed.

If you worked as hard as I did, then yes, take this victory!

Having done the very best he could, he was prepared to accept the result as well.

Of course, it wasn't as though he could afford to lose and die, but this mindset had positive effects on Geom Mugik in many ways.

Because no matter how difficult the fight, he could approach it with a light heart and overflowing confidence.

As he clashed with Geom Mugik once more, Tuwang could see that Geom Mugik's eyes were clearer and brimming with more vitality than ever.

How could such a madman exist! These damnable demonic cult bastards! Amid all those thoughts, his final conclusion was this:

'I must kill him today, no matter what!'

If he was this strong now, what if he met this Geom Mugik again next year? What about the year after? When he reached his thirties, forties, and fifties? He would become an existence beyond imagination.

It seemed Amwang, inside the carriage, felt the same way.

Sssss.

Black energy flowing out from the carriage seemed to form a curtain along Tuwang's body before being absorbed into him. It was clear Amwang had infused him with some power.

"Young Cult Leader, let us see your great art."

As expected, Tuwang regained his vitality. He was as fresh as if he were fighting for the first time.

Riding his momentum, he displayed his unique martial art. It was precisely the Yeonhwan Pungun Gwon he had shown when fighting the Sado Maengju.

Yeonhwan Pungun Gwon, Third Form: Seomjeon Nanmu.

Shushushushushushushuk!

Dozens of fists fast as light flew toward Geom Mugik.

Sasasasasa.

Using the Amyeongbo, Myeongwangbo, and Jeommyeolbo in succession, an opening was created in a space that seemed impossible to escape.

Missed fists overturned the earth and sent debris flying in all directions.

At the end of that final opening was Tuwang.

"I've been waiting!"

It was a move made believing and predicting that Geom Mugik would definitely dodge. Geom Mugik had escaped exactly where he predicted, and there he unleashed a hidden move.

Yeonhwan Pungun Gwon, Fourth Form: Jeoksae Geumryeong.

A chain-shaped red gangqi erupted from Tuwang's palm.

Chwarurururuk!

Perhaps because it was wrapped in chains? The Jeoksae Geumryeong coiled around Geom Mugik even more powerfully.

The very moment Tuwang was about to unleash the Yeonhwan Pungun Gwon, Fifth Form: Pungun Gaebyeok without hesitation!

Geom Mugik abruptly asked.

"Are you ready?"

From this angle where only Tuwang and the carriage were ahead, this exact position where not a single person on his side would be hurt even if he swept everything away.

It was the moment Geom Mugik had been waiting for amidst the chaotic battle with Tuwang. A move already unleashed before being bound by the Jeoksae Geumryeong.

Sssssss.

The evil spirit appearing before Geom Mugik began to split rapidly.

The evil spirits finally revealed themselves.

He couldn't know what preparations they had made, but now was the time to use the Guhwamagong.

Earlier in the hall, there had been too many of his own people, so he couldn't use the Daemyeolsik.

But with Jinha's forces and the Demon Annihilation Corps gone, an opportunity had arisen.

From this angle where he now stood, there were no Bisain or Mumyeongaks. The sound of dogs barking could be heard from far away in the opposite direction.

'I'll blow away not only Tuwang but the palanquin too!'

Just what countermeasures have you prepared?

Kwakwakwakwakwakwakwakwak!

The Daemyeolsik charged toward them, sweeping everything away.

At that very moment!

An astonishing thing happened.

Shuuuk!

Black holes simultaneously formed beneath Tuwang and the palanquin, and they disappeared as if sucked down into the holes.

Kwakwakwakwakwakwakwak!

The Daemyeolsik passed right over them. It disappeared without a trace all the way to the far rear wall.

A black circle formed where everything had been swept away, and the palanquin rose up.

Fortunately, the Jeoksae Geumryeong came undone, but Geom Mugik stared at the palanquin with a surprised expression.

Seureureuk.

The palanquin door opened, and between the curtains, a hand wrapped in chains revealed itself.

Tuwang walked out from inside the palanquin.

Geom Mugik realized one thing.

"It was also you who saved this man before."

Then Amwang's answer came from the palanquin.

"That's right. The Amno Gwihwansul is my unique secret technique."

Earlier, when Tuwang first got out of the carriage, she had mentioned the Amno Gwihwansul. At the time, he hadn't thought it would be her martial art.

When the Daemyeolsik is unleashed, the target is crushed by demonic energy and cannot escape that place.

But with the Guhwamagong of the Seventh Star, it was impossible to stop the Amno Gwihwansul from rescuing its target.

Previously, the user had disappeared leaving behind everything on his body, but today's Tuwang not only had chains on his fists and body but was even wearing his clothes intact. Because Amwang unleashed the Amno Gwihwansul from nearby, it was different from that time. It meant the technique was operating more powerfully.

"Have you no pride, fleeing cowardly in the middle of a fight?"

Geom Mugik provoked Tuwang.

Tuwang did not assert his pride in this regard.

"Your Guhwamagong is what's cowardly."

He was the one who had faced the Daemyeolsik directly just moments ago. The momentum of those evil spirits surging toward him was still vivid. Could he really have blocked that with his bare body?

"It's merely the realm of the Seventh Star. Surely it's worth enduring once?"

"I'll pass."

He was crazed for battle, crazed for victory, and crazed for revenge, but he refused only when it came to the Guhwamagong.

Right then, Geom Mugik unleashed the Inmyeolsik by surprise.

Saaaak.

In an instant, evil spirits appeared in all four directions around Tuwang and swung their swords.

The swords of four evil spirits from east, west, south, and north struck down at him.

Shwiik! Shwik! Shwiik! Shwik!

But the opponent was fast too. This time as well, a black hole formed on the floor and sucked him in.

Therefore, the four evil spirits' swords cut through empty air.

Geom Mugik saw it. The evil spirits facing him disappearing with stiffened expressions. He could feel that their pride was hurt.

In his eyes, he held a different feeling toward the disappearing evil spirits instead of apology.

'If you come out and live with me from now on, you'll make plenty of wasted moves too. It happens. Don't be ashamed. Your master doesn't bother with pride in matters like this.'

Tuwang walked out of the palanquin again, frowning.

"I said I'll pass."

"I'll keep going. If you keep getting out of that carriage, eventually your pride will be hurt and you'll clash with me head-on. You're a fighter who doesn't back down from a fight, aren't you? It's merely the Seventh Star!"

Amwang stepped in and blocked the provocation.

"Why don't you try it. Let's see whose internal energy runs out first. Ah, but before that, all your friends will die."

At those words, Geom Mugik reined in his aggressive momentum.

"Don't try to trigger my anxiety by talking about my friends."

A low laugh flowed from the palanquin.

She was right. He couldn't drag out time recklessly like this. He had to eliminate them as quickly as possible and save everyone.

He clashed head-on with Tuwang again. Having absorbed the black energy, Tuwang was faster and stronger. They had already been evenly matched, and now he had grown stronger.

Chaengchaengchaengchaengchaeng!

Amid the battle where sword and fist met, sending sparks flying, Geom Mugik ultimately failed to block one fist.

Pueeeoek!

Geom Mugik, struck in the chest, flew far away and collapsed on the ground. The gazes of the Mumyeongaks watching from the walls and trees all turned toward Geom Mugik at once. They looked at each other and exchanged glances.

The Mumyeongaks jumped down all at once and surrounded Geom Mugik in a circle. They were ones who never moved without Geukak Soma's order, but today was an exception.

Tuwang looked at them with eyes full of scorn and unleashed killing intent.

"I'll kill you all!"

But the Mumyeongaks didn't move a single step.

Right then, Geom Mugik's face appeared between the white masks.

"I'm fine, so go back to your positions. Lord Soma will be here soon."

Then a gentle, enchanting voice of Amwang came from inside the carriage.

"Geukak Soma will definitely not arrive in time."

Amid the Mumyeongaks' watch, Geom Mugik showed absolute faith.

"Want to bet? On whether he'll come or not?"

* * *

Geukak Soma was literally fighting dogs.

Swaeeeek!

Two hellhounds were swept away and disappeared by the Mageuk Gwangpokjang.

But the hellhounds in the back scattered in all directions and dodged. They weren't ordinary dogs. They moved like martial arts experts.

The vicious murderous intent they exuded while growling aroused an unpleasant fear.

But Geukak Soma's gaze looking at them was only indifferent and cold.

Shuk shuk shuk!

Hyeorangji was fired in succession at the hellhound flying toward Geukak Soma's nape.

Shuk shuk shuk!

Puk puk puk!

Only after three holes appeared in its head did it turn into black smoke and disappear. Another demon dog charged through that smoke.

Puk!

Geukak Soma jumped up, striking its jaw with a fist.

The demon dogs targeting its legs and back flew forward through the air. Other demon dogs charged from all directions.

Geukak Soma grabbed the nape of the one that had leaped into the air and fell to the ground together.

Kwang!

When he slammed it into the ground with internal energy, it turned to smoke and disappeared.

More demon dogs charged there again.

Hwiik, hwiik.

He twisted his body to dodge the charging demon dog. Twisting his body the other way to dodge again, another demon dog brushed past Geukak Soma's side.

In a situation where dozens of demon dogs charged from all sides, ordinary experts would have their hands and feet tangled from visual fear alone, but Geukak Soma's movements were calm to the point of elegance.

Hwik.

Geukak Soma's figure quickly flew toward the one isolated on the left.

Shuk! Puk!

If it deviated even slightly from the center of the forehead, it wouldn't die in one hit. Only when consecutive Hyeorangji pierced through its head did it finally turn to black smoke and disappear. This one wasn't it either.

They knew no fear. From the eyes of those slowly approaching in a siege, red killing intent flowed, and from their bodies, the unique black energy of dark sorcery rippled.

Catching them one by one, Geukak Soma was only thinking of finding the dog handler. The faster he killed him, the more he could reduce his internal energy consumption.

But he didn't easily reveal his identity.

The dog handler was definitely among these demon dogs. At a glance they seemed to move randomly, but the demon dogs had their own order. The dog handler was definitely giving commands among them.

Shwiik.

Geukak Soma charged toward the one furthest away this time. The one judged to be in the safest position in this fight. Then demon dogs charged from all directions.

Geukak Soma broke through the demon dogs with a protective aura and pounced on the target.

Pung!

The thing disappeared into smoke from the Mageuk Gwangpokjang, but the remaining demon dogs were still there. It wasn't the dog handler.

Right then.

Kwak!

A demon dog rushing from behind bit Geukak Soma's arm.

But its fangs didn't sink in. He had deliberately offered the spot wrapped in Geukpum Cheonjamsa.

Shuk! Peoeng!

When the Hyeorangji hit precisely the center of its forehead, the demon dog biting his arm turned to smoke and disappeared.

Dodging the demon dogs charging again from all sides, Geukak Soma flew toward the interior of Cheonhwaru. Once inside, he entered one of the brothels on this floor.

He was trying to lure them inside to fight because fighting in an open place was disadvantageous.

But the demon dogs didn't follow him in.

As if time was on their side, they gathered in front of Cheonhwaru and looked up at Geukak Soma.

Standing by the window, Geukak Soma looked down at the demon dogs with sunken eyes.

Then one of the demon dogs howled long like a wolf. Then all the other demon dogs howled together. Geukak Soma understood. They were trying to lure him out using the one that had howled first.

Watching the demon dogs howl, he suddenly thought of Geom Mugik making dog barking sounds with Dokwang in the training ground long ago. He needed to hurry and go help Geom Mugik.

Just then a thought flashed through Geukak Soma's mind.

Geukak Soma jumped lightly down.

The demon dogs surrounding him in a semicircle growled as if they would charge at any moment.

Swaaaaaaa.

Geukak Soma began emitting the Soageoksim again.

Originally, the Soageoksim was demonic energy that awakened the fundamental violence of existence. When exposed to the Soageoksim, one's heart beat faster and one wanted to kill someone.

When the fight first began, emitting the Soageoksim was about revealing his inherent demonic energy before battle rather than any special purpose. Because of the Soageoksim, the demon dogs had become excited, making the fight a bit more favorable at times.

But now there was a clear reason for emitting the Soageoksim.

The demon dogs exposed to the Soageoksim gradually began to get excited. The demon dog closest to him couldn't endure and charged toward Geukak Soma.

Shwaaaaaak!

Kwaaaang!

After blowing them apart in midair with the Mageuk Gwangpok Palm, Geugak Soma unleashed his evil intent even more fiercely.

Before that extreme evil intent, the demon hounds went berserk. They charged in even more madly, growing ever more ferocious. At last, they even began tearing into the demon hounds beside them, fighting among themselves.

Bang!

Geugak Soma hurled the one that had lunged at him into the one charging in behind it. Even in the midst of it, he continued raising his evil intent, his gaze fixed on the demon hounds.

Whoooooosh.

The reason Geugak Soma was doing this, despite consuming his internal energy, was to find the dog dealer.

As he thought of Geom Mugeuk and the Poison King, a thought suddenly occurred to him.

Like those two that day, wasn’t the dog dealer also merely shaped like a dog, and ultimately a person imitating one?

If so?

Before an extreme evil intent, he would surely react differently. A dog and a human could not be the same.

When his evil intent reached its peak, one of them entered Geugak Soma’s eyes.

‘That one!’

He felt that one’s reaction was different. It was an instinctive choice.

Shwaaaak.

Geugak Soma charged toward it.

The demon hounds desperately threw themselves in his path.

Demon hounds pounced on Geugak Soma from every direction.

They piled on him again and again, blocking him with all their might. Every demon hound rushed in, until Geugak Soma’s figure could no longer be seen.

The eyes of the demon hound Geugak Soma had targeted were not a dog’s eyes.

Geugak Soma’s premonition had been correct. This demon hound was the dog dealer.

‘He may be holding out with protective qi, but he won’t be able to withstand those numbers.’

Just as a satisfied look flashed in his eyes, the dog dealer flinched in surprise.

An arm was sticking out from between the piled-up demon hounds.

And its finger was pointing at him.

Shuuuuuk!

The Blood Calamity Finger shot toward the dog dealer like a streak of light.

Several demon hounds guarding him nearby threw themselves forward at once. They were the demon hounds prepared for just such a contingency.

‘Not a chance!’

At the very moment a triumphant gleam crossed the dog dealer’s eyes—

Chwaaaaaak!

The Blood Calamity Finger split into five streams.

The divided streams of the Blood Calamity Finger swerved in wide arcs around the demon hounds blocking the way.

Thud! Thud! Thud! Thud! Thud!

All of them struck the dog dealer’s face behind them.

A brief silence flowed.

Boom!

The demon hound beside the arm Geugak Soma had extended vanished into black smoke.

Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom!

Starting with that one, the demon hounds began bursting one after another and disappearing. In the place where black smoke rose, Geugak Soma’s white mask was smiling.

When the smoke had completely settled, the dog dealer had returned to his original human body.

Beyond the dog dealer’s corpse, its face riddled with holes, Geugak Soma was launching himself toward the place where Geom Mugeuk was fighting.

‘Young Cult Leader, I’m coming!’

Chapter 546: That Palanquin Is Mine Now.
----------------------------------------
Each time the lanterns swayed, blade-like sword qi split the night sky.

The four men in black fought with lanterns in one hand and swords in the other. Even so, they held their own against the Sado Thirteen Lang and Bi Sain.

Shweeeek! Kwaaang!

Bi Sain flung himself aside, avoiding the sword qi that came flying in. The stone lantern behind him was sliced through and crumbled apart.

Sasasasasasak!

Sword qi poured down in succession toward Bi Sain.

The ones who blocked the incoming sword qi were First Lang, Second Lang, and Third Lang.

Boom! Booom! Booom!

Sword qi collided with sword qi and dissolved in midair.

The next barrage was blocked as another of the Sado Thirteen Lang changed places with them as if taking turns.

The bastards persistently targeted only Bi Sain, and the Sado Thirteen Lang blocked the incoming attacks with perfect coordination.

A battle of four against fourteen. The reason such a tilted fight was possible lay in their internal energy.

They were truly pouring out sword qi without rest to an astonishing degree.

At first, Bi Sain had expected this fight to end quickly. If they used internal energy so recklessly, their dantians would be emptied in no time.

But their internal energy did not run dry.

Their attacks were not the only troublesome thing. When the Sado Thirteen Lang counterattacked, the four men in black avoided the sword qi along strange trajectories.

Their feet were floating slightly above the ground, so it was accurate to say they were flying. Their lightness skill, as though they were gliding over ice, was unlike anything he had ever experienced.

And that was not all. The black-blue light spreading from the lanterns contained an aura of darkness that slowed and dragged down not only a person’s mind, but even their movements.

“Back!”

At Bi Sain’s command, the Sado Thirteen Lang retreated another three zhang.

At a glance, it looked as if they were being pushed back by the enemy’s momentum, but that was not the case.

It was Bi Sain’s deliberate retreat.

As he fought, Bi Sain was focusing on one fact.

These men were not normal martial artists. He had realized that the reason they could display such astonishing movements and pour out sword qi endlessly lay in those shadow-like cords connected to the palanquin.

‘Let’s see how much farther they stretch!’

To resolve that question, Bi Sain had been repeatedly falling back.

Amazingly, even though they had completely left Cheonhwaru and were fighting outside, those cords continued to lengthen.

In any case, the longer the cords grew, the better. If they lengthened, opportunities to cut them would increase, and surely the internal energy being transmitted to them would diminish rather than grow.

But as if to prove that this was a miscalculation, rougher and more powerful sword qi surged from the four men in black.

Fortunately, the Sado Thirteen Lang were elites among elites. Even though their opponents were launching attacks beyond common sense, they did not panic and dealt with them skillfully.

—First Lang! We have to cut those!

—I believe the same.

—I’ll cut the one attached to the leftmost bastard! Focus your attacks on the other three!

—Yes, Young Alliance Leader!

Bi Sain and the Sado Thirteen Lang, who had only been evading until now, launched a counterattack all at once.

Swish! Swish! Swish! Swish! Swish! Swish!

The moment the Sado Thirteen Lang fired sword qi in unison to cover him, Bi Sain kicked off the ground and rushed toward the leftmost man in black.

Bi Sain avoided the sword qi the man in black sent flying with his footwork and thrust out his sword.

Shweeek!

The man in black slid away swiftly and dodged.

Bi Sain passed him, twisted his body, and brought his sword down. His target was not the man in black, but the black cord connected to his body.

At that moment, the black cord undulated and avoided the attack.

Bi Sain persistently chased after the cord, and the man in black attacked Bi Sain fiercely.

The other men in black could not help him. That was because the Sado Thirteen Lang were pouring onto them the attacks they had held back until now.

Paaak!

Blood sprayed from Bi Sain’s shoulder. In return, he made sure to claim the price for throwing himself in despite knowing he would be injured.

Chwaaaak!

The cord of the leftmost man in black was severed. Like a snake cut in two, the cord thrashed violently. Black smoke gushed from the severed cord.

Within the smoke, Bi Sain and the man in black crossed paths.

Shweeeeek!

Pwaaaak!

Blood spurted out from within the smoke.

It had burst from the chest of the man in black. With his cord cut, the man in black was no match for Bi Sain.

As the man in black collapsed and died, the lantern he had been holding went out.

The remaining three men in black, having lost their comrade, let out a ghastly cry. Then ghostly wailing resounded from the lanterns they held.

Swaaaaaaak!

The deathly energy created by dark sorcery stretched out from the lanterns and swept over them.

First Lang shouted.

“Deploy the Ghost-Annihilating Formation!”

At once, the Sado Thirteen Lang gathered around Bi Sain and unfolded a sword formation.

The powerful energy released from the completed sword formation blocked the deathly energy that came flying in.

When the outstretched evil aura was blocked, the ghostly wailing of the lanterns sounded even more sorrowful.

Bi Sain did not miss this momentary opportunity when their attack was halted.

Bi Sain, First Lang, Second Lang, and Third Lang launched themselves forward at the same time.

First Lang kicked off the ground and charged in, aiming directly for the second man in black.

Second Lang aimed for the black cord, and Third Lang aimed at the place where the man in black would dodge.

He avoided Third Lang, but Bi Sain was waiting where he dodged.

Slice! Pwaaaak!

Blood gushed from the neck where the head had fallen, and the body collapsed. Drenched in blood, they launched themselves forward again.

Having been together for so long, they knew one another’s minds from a single glance. They possessed the years that could overcome infinite internal energy.

They did not let go of the advantage they had seized.

“Thirteen Flying Swords!”

At First Lang’s shout, the Sado Thirteen Lang fired sword qi in unison.

Thirteen strands of sword qi flew toward the third man in black as fast as light.

Sasasasasak.

The man in black slipped through the sword qi like an acrobat. It was truly an unbelievable, dazzling movement.

At that very moment, someone shouted.

“Over here, you bastard!”

When the third man in black turned, First Lang was already bringing his sword down on the cord connected to him.

Papapapa!

The severed cord spewed black smoke like blood and thrashed madly.

When the man in black turned his head again, he saw twelve streaks of sword qi flying into his eyes. This time, he could not dodge.

Pukpukpukpukpukpukpukpukpuk!

As the sword qi pierced through his body all at once, the severed cord thrashed one last time, and soon the flame of the lantern in his hand went out as well.

The fourth man in black could not help him.

That was because Bi Sain had rushed in to stop him, and a contest of internal energy had begun.

The two men’s palms were pressed together in midair.

Swaaaaaaak!

The black cord connecting the last man in black to the palanquin swelled.

A tremendous amount of internal energy entered the man in black’s body and pressed Bi Sain back.

Bi Sain gritted his teeth and endured. The opponent’s internal energy was monstrous.

Bi Sain looked at First Lang.

Even without words, First Lang could tell what that gaze was saying.

‘This far!’

Without hesitation, First Lang brought his sword down and cut off the man in black’s arm.

Slice!

“Aaaagh!”

Blood spurted from the severed arm.

The Sado Thirteen Lang rushed in and hacked him to pieces.

First Lang looked over the corpses strewn across the ground and said coldly.

“These bastards. What did they take us for?”

And so the last of the lanterns went out.

Bi Sain asked everyone.

“Are you all right?”

“We are all right.”

Bone was visible in the shoulder of Eleventh Lang, who had been injured by sword qi.

“I was just scratched a little.”

“If that’s a little scratch, I won’t even be able to mention mine.”

At Bi Sain’s words, Eleventh Lang smiled awkwardly.

First Lang treated Bi Sain’s shoulder, and Twelfth Lang treated Eleventh Lang’s shoulder. Fortunately, neither injury was on the side they used to wield a sword.

Bi Sain said to everyone.

“The secret lies in the palanquin those cords were connected to.”

No matter how much dark sorcery they had employed, the internal energy they used had been real internal energy.

Someone inside the palanquin had sent that much internal energy?

That was absolutely impossible. The four men in black had quite literally poured out internal energy without limit as they attacked. This was not internal energy a single person could release.

Besides, was that palanquin not currently fighting Geom Mugeuk?

Bi Sain, who had been lost in thought for a moment, made his decision.

“We will not go to aid the Young Cult Leader.”

Bi Sain spoke as he walked into the darkness.

“We will uncover the secret of that palanquin. It must be somewhere not far from here.”

The Sado Thirteen Lang silently followed behind him.

* * *

“Stake that palanquin in a wager.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, the Dark King’s laughter came from within the palanquin.

“A wager is only made when both sides have something to stake, isn’t it?”

“That’s right.”

“The Young Cult Leader will soon be dead, so what can you stake?”

“Why act like that just because I took one hit? When fighting, you can get hit once or twice.”

Geom Mugeuk was still relaxed.

“I’ll stake my mouth. How about it? If you carry this mouth around, you’ll never be bored.”

“Instead, I’ll develop pent-up rage.”

Leisure answered leisure. A low laugh seemed to come from the palanquin, and then a calm voice flowed out.

“Young Cult Leader.”

After a brief pause, the Dark King added,

“You have lost. Today, you will not save anyone. Not even yourself.”

As soon as those words ended, the Fighting King walked forward.

Geom Mugeuk said to the Faceless Guests who had blocked the way for him.

“Everyone, return to your positions.”

But none of the Faceless Guests moved. They had judged that the Young Cult Leader was at a disadvantage.

The one enraged by that sight was the Fighting King.

“You disgusting things!”

They were unpleasant just to look at, and now they were standing in his way.

“I’ll send every last one of you off!”

The Fighting King thrust his fist toward the Faceless Guests and Geom Mugeuk.

Papapapapapapapapapa!

He had unleashed Lightning Wild Dance of the Chain Wind-Cloud Fist, filled with extreme internal energy.

Dozens of fists flew toward the Faceless Guests.

The Faceless Guests who stood before Geom Mugeuk did not dodge and unleashed Evil Shadow Finger all at once.

Shukshukshukshukshukshukshuk!

But their Evil Shadow Finger could not break the Fighting King’s Lightning Wild Dance.

Papapapapapapapak.

The fist-shaped astral energy shattered the finger winds and poured toward them.

To the very end, the Faceless Guests did not dodge. It was their will to protect the Young Cult Leader, even if they had to block it with their bodies. Because their masks were smiling, the sight truly made them look like madmen.

Kwaang! Kwaaang! Kwaang! Kwaaang!

Ear-splitting explosions followed one after another, and the earth shook.

The Faceless Guests stared in astonishment at the sight unfolding before their eyes. Under normal circumstances, their bodies should have been blown to pieces.

Sssst! Ssssst! Ssst! Ssssst!

The fist-shaped astral energy that had flown in was melting before their eyes. It was truly melting like ice.

Something had been erected before them.

A single wall that had blocked that terrifying attack.

It was the Great Demonic Wall of the Guhwa Demonic Art.

Before the eyes of the Faceless Guests, every fist unleashed by Lightning Wild Dance melted away.

“That damned Guhwa Demonic Art!”

At the Fighting King’s sunken words, Geom Mugeuk replied.

“I want to make wagers with you people and get along, but you keep making me put up walls.”

The Faceless Guests all cupped their fists and bowed their heads, paying respect to the Guhwa Demonic Art.

Then they turned back and looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk looked at them and said with a smile.

“Thank you for protecting me.”

The Faceless Guests were moved. The Young Cult Leader had unfolded for them the Great Demonic Wall that he had not used even while he himself was being beaten. They were even more overwhelmed by the fact that the martial art which had saved their lives was the Guhwa Demonic Art.

Now, when Geom Mugeuk went to the Valley of Villains to meet the Extreme Evil Little Demon, their smiles were sure to brighten even more.

“Now trust me and go up.”

Without hesitation, the Faceless Guests launched themselves up onto the trees and the walls. Even as they did, the eyes in the darkness confronting them ahead still held their positions.

The Fighting King and the Dark King said nothing. They looked shocked by the power of the Great Demonic Wall.

Earlier, both the Great Annihilation Form and the Human Annihilation Form could only be avoided by using the Dark Path Return Art. Yet upon seeing this Great Demonic Wall, they were overwhelmed by its majesty.

The Dark King’s calm voice came from the palanquin.

“Now I understand why the Guhwa Demonic Art is called the greatest demonic art of all time.”

“Don’t be afraid. It’s only at the seventh star for now. Let’s have a proper match.”

Geom Mugeuk provoked the Fighting King again. It would be good if he clashed with the Guhwa Demonic Art.

Just then, the cords hanging long from the palanquin began to thrash, then soon went limp. The other cords were the same.

How could Geom Mugeuk not know what that meant?

“That sounds like bad news.”

As a faint smile formed on Geom Mugeuk’s lips, the Dark King’s voice sank coldly.

“Then I suppose I must deliver bad news to the Young Cult Leader as well.”

As an unknowable incantation began to flow from within the palanquin, the air around them changed.

The Faceless Guests crouched on the tree branches all looked around at once. Something invisible had begun to gather from every direction.

It was darkness. Darkness granted life gathered like storm clouds. It was a visible darkness, and darkness within darkness.

It was a sight neither Geom Mugeuk nor the Faceless Guests had ever seen before.

From the palanquin, the low incantation that tickled the ears continued.

Far away, in the darkness, the eyes all widened at once and began to stir with excitement.

The gathered darkness formed a single shape in the air above the palanquin. It was darkness, yet transparent; darkness, yet gleaming.

What that darkness created was the figure of a woman. Within the darkness, delicate light like the tip of a painter’s brush flowed, shaping her features.

The woman so formed hovered in midair.

The Faceless Guests and the eyes within the darkness all stared at the woman made of darkness.

Darkness was shining within darkness.

“All life shall return to the dark!”

The voice of the Dark King came from the woman made of darkness. If the earlier voice had been soft and alluring, the voice now was deep and mysterious.

The moment her words ended—

Saaaaaaa.

The woman descended onto the body of the Fighting King. Then the Fighting King and the darkness merged into one.

Over the Fighting King’s body, the woman’s form appeared superimposed like a transparent shadow. When the Fighting King raised his hand, the woman of darkness raised hers in the same way. They were two, yet one; one, yet two.

It was none other than the Dark King’s signature ultimate technique, the Dark Descent Art.

The Fighting King had returned far stronger than before, and now even the Dark King had descended upon him. He revealed a force incomparably stronger than when the Fighting King had absorbed the black energy earlier.

“It was already a close fight with him alone. What will you do now? We’ll be fighting as two, you know.”

As her voice flowed out while she was overlaid with the Fighting King, the sight was truly grotesque.

At that very moment, a deep voice came flying through the darkness.

“There are two of us as well.”

Someone crossed the night sky with robes fluttering and landed beside Geom Mugeuk.

A white mask that suited the darkness perfectly—he was none other than Geugak Soma.

“You’ve come?”

“Am I not too late?”

“You arrived at the perfect time.”

At Geugak Soma’s appearance, the Faceless Guests all cupped their fists and paid their respects. In their eyes, one could feel joy and a loyalty deeper than anything.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Geugak Soma. The two eyes within the mask holes shone clear and bright.

“I even made a bet that Soma-nim wouldn’t make it in time.”

“I dislike dogs that bite people.”

At Geugak Soma’s words, Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly.

He had always fought difficult battles with Soma. And he had a premonition that tonight’s battle would be harder than any battle he had fought with him before.

Even so, he was not afraid at all. Because he would be fighting together with his Demon Venerable, with whom no words were needed.

“I won the bet. That palanquin is mine now.”

While joined with the Fighting King, the Dark King spoke softly.

“Nothing will change tonight.”

Over the Fighting King’s fierce face, a deep and beautiful darkness was rippling.

“Let’s see about that. I happen to be rather good at changing things.”

Geom Mugeuk walked forward side by side with Geugak Soma.

As they went, he drew the Black Demon Sword and let it hang down, and Geugak Soma lightly flicked the blade with his finger.

Ting!

A clear metallic ring echoed through the darkness.

Chapter 547: Where There Is Light, There Is Also Darkness.
----------------------------------------------------------
Jin Haryeong and Yeo Jeong were hiding in a secret chamber of Cheonhwaru.

When the fighting began in earnest, the two of them had managed to slip into Cheonhwaru’s secret chamber amid the chaos.

Sounds of battle came from above. Things breaking, shattering, being smashed to pieces.

She was terribly curious and worried about what was happening outside, but Jin Haryeong remained hidden without moving an inch.

If it had been the old her, she would not have chosen to hide like this. She would have thought to help her older brother fight while protecting Yeo Jeong.

Because honor had mattered more than anything else. And because she would have been afraid that when she opened that door later and went out, she might find her brother’s corpse. Because she would not have been able to endure the terror of surviving alone.

But the Jin Haryeong of now had changed from back then. A promise mattered more than honor, and the person she had to protect mattered. Her faith in her older brother had grown beyond comparison as well.

All of these changes had been greatly influenced by her relationship with Geom Mugeuk.

Even the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult thinks and acts like that. Who am I to do otherwise?

She could imagine Geom Mugeuk’s reaction when he heard that she had hidden here.

—You did really well. You made the wisest choice!

And yet he would surely tease her too.

—Ah, I should’ve said I’d protect the Pavilion Master! Do you know how many times I almost died?

At the thought of that reaction, a smile formed at her lips before she knew it.

‘Honestly, you turn me into a madwoman who smiles even in a situation like this.’

Just then, Yeo Jeong spoke to her from behind.

“Miss Jin.”

“Yes, Pavilion Master.”

When Jin Haryeong turned around, Yeo Jeong’s expression was utterly calm. So much so that Jin Haryeong almost thought she might be the one being protected.

“If a moment ever comes later when you must choose, abandon me without hesitation.”

Jin Haryeong could tell. Yeo Jeong meant it sincerely.

“Yes, I will.”

Had she answered too easily? Yeo Jeong’s expression showed that she did not believe her. So Jin Haryeong said to her,

“I promised him. That I would protect you, Pavilion Master, no matter what.”

Yeo Jeong smiled and said,

“The person you made that promise to would understand you even if you survived alone, Miss Jin. He would probably comfort you more warmly than anyone.”

Jin Haryeong thought inwardly.

‘No. He would spit words of resentment at me. He would say vicious things, telling me he died because of me. Enough to make my affection run cold. So that I’d feel something other than guilt. Because he’s the sort of person who would think doing that was for my sake.’

She now knew that Geom Mugeuk was that kind of person. That was why. The reason she absolutely had to protect Yeo Jeong.

Jin Haryeong changed the subject.

“I wonder if my older brother is fighting well.”

She had lived believing that if someone had to die, it should be her, not her older brother. That was how important her brother was to the orthodox martial world.

“You don’t know, do you? How straightforward and inflexible my brother is. That’s why it’s almost surprising he became close to the young cult leader.”

“Then you don’t need to worry.”

“Pardon?”

Yeo Jeong smiled as she said to her,

“People who are straightforward are strong in a crisis.”

* * *

That place was a complicated maze. The walls were made of thick steel, and the paths were intricate.

The Blood Path Maze Formation.

A deadly ultimate formation from which, once trapped, one could never emerge alive.

The one leading Jin Hagun and the martial artists of the Demon Extermination Squad through that ultimate formation was Jeong Gyeong, a martial artist of the squad.

“This way.”

The martial artists of the Demon Extermination Squad were strong in martial arts as a matter of course, and each also possessed their own specialty outside of martial arts. Some were well versed in poison, some specialized in researching sorcery, and some were specialized in tracking arts.

Jeong Gyeong’s specialty was breaking formations.

Given the nature of the Demon Extermination Squad, they often encountered enemies who used evil arts or formations. To prepare for such times, they had all trained and studied how to break formations together.

But Jeong Gyeong, who was walking at the front, was tense.

He had read and reread every book on formations he could find. He had studied hard enough to memorize them all, but the Blood Path Maze Formation was truly dangerous.

The Blood Path Maze Formation was not merely a formation that made one lose their way.

Each time one took the wrong path and chose a death gate instead of a life gate, a terrifying price had to be paid.

Moreover, no matter how much one usually researched and studied, formations developed and changed endlessly. Those who sought to break them could not keep pace with that rate of change.

And so today, in this place, he had to make not only precise calculations but many adaptations and new choices as well.

Thinking that everyone’s lives rested in his hands, Jeong Gyeong felt both afraid and excited.

“This door.”

The place they entered after opening the door was a room filled on all sides with bronze mirrors. Dozens of small mirrors were attached everywhere, enough to make one’s head spin.

Once they had all stepped inside,

Srrrrk.

A mechanism on the platform in front activated.

Sand began to pour down onto one side of a balance scale whose arms had been tilted. Then it slowly began to descend.

Seeing it, Jeong Gyeong shouted,

“We must find the life gate before that scale touches the floor! It is one of the bronze mirrors.”

There was not much time given. How were they supposed to discover which of those many mirrors was the life gate in such a short time? Jeong Gyeong was flustered as well, for this was his first time encountering this method.

The Demon Extermination Squad martial artists rushed over, touching the mirrors and examining them for anything different.

Even as they did, the scale sank lower and lower.

Just before the scale touched the floor,

Jin Hagun hurled himself forward and shoved one of the mirrors with all his might.

“Here!”

The moment he pushed the mirror hard,

Everyone braced themselves tensely for what might happen next. They did not know whether poisonous smoke would gush out or hidden weapons would pour down.

At that very moment, the falling sand stopped, and the scale began to rise back to its original position.

The mirror Jin Hagun had chosen had been the life gate.

With a clack, an exit door opened in the wall.

Everyone sighed in relief and swept a hand over their chests.

“How did you know it was that bronze mirror?”

At Jeong Gyeong’s question, Jin Hagun gave an answer none of them had expected.

“I just guessed.”

“Pardon?”

Everyone stared at him in surprise.

Then Gwanghyo of the Demon Extermination Squad laughed and said,

“That’s even better. Isn’t luck what we need most right now?”

In truth, Jin Hagun’s words were half right and half wrong. At the last moment, Jin Hagun had noticed that his reflection did not appear in that mirror.

Still, since he had chosen it in an instant without checking all the other mirrors, it was no different from a guess. But that instinctive choice had been right.

After leaving the mirror room, they continued searching for the path.

In that way, they steadily made their way through the maze until, in the end, Jeong Gyeong mistakenly chose a death gate. He had made his choice through precise inference and calculation, but there had been a trap within the process.

Clank!

The floor tilted like a slope.

Kururururung.

From somewhere unseen above, there came the sound of something rolling toward them.

“Dodge!”

At Jeong Gyeong’s shout, everyone was about to throw themselves to the opposite side.

But at that instant, Jin Hagun hurled himself toward the direction from which something was rolling.

“We stop it before it gains more speed!”

This was the method to break the trap that he had thought of in that instant.

The Demon Extermination Squad martial artists followed after him.

Gururururung!

What came rolling down was a giant iron ball.

Jin Hagun’s choice was correct. If they had left it alone, it would have reached an uncontrollable speed and crushed them. But now—

Gritting hard!

Jin Hagun blocked the iron ball with his back and raised his internal energy. The Demon Extermination Squad martial artists who had followed him joined him.

Kurururururung.

As those clinging to it drew up their internal energy with all their might, its speed gradually decreased, and at last the giant iron ball stopped.

Then the floor that had tilted returned to its original position.

“Phew!”

Jin Hagun let out a breath of relief.

“Are you all right?”

At his subordinates’ question, Jin Hagun nodded.

“I’m fine.”

“If not for your choice, Captain, we would have been in serious trouble.”

They would not all have died to this trap, but several could have been injured or killed.

It was truly not easy to think of running in the opposite direction in that instant.

For that reason, the subordinates of the Demon Extermination Squad looked at Jin Hagun with renewed eyes. He was strong in righteousness and profound in martial arts, but they had not had many chances to see him display this sort of quick thinking and wisdom.

Jin Hagun was showing strength in a crisis. Once he resolved to get everyone out alive, that sense of responsibility was producing astonishing concentration.

Jeong Gyeong came over and lowered his head with a shamefaced expression.

“I made a mistake. I apologize.”

“It’s all right. If not for you, we would not even have made it this far.”

He was not simply saying it to comfort him. If not for Jeong Gyeong, they would have faced this kind of crisis any number of times already.

“It will become harder and harder to find the life gates. It’s all right if you make a mistake. Aren’t there so many methods of breaking through behind you?”

When Jeong Gyeong turned around, his comrades were looking at him. It meant that even if he made a mistake, they would all break through together.

Jin Hagun said to everyone,

“Let’s break out and tell the bastard who made this who we are.”

“Yes!”

The Demon Extermination Squad martial artists answered thunderously.

They began walking once more toward the next choice between life and death.

* * *

At the sudden downpour of sunlight, the Fighting King closed his eyes.

It was not ordinary sunlight. It was scorching sunlight that seemed as if it would dry up the entire world.

He narrowed his eyes and shielded them from the sun with his hand. His palm grew hot.

The Fighting King was bewildered by the sudden situation.

The place where he stood was parched earth, cracked all over.

And what hung in the sky was truly the sun. A blazing midday sun in a sky without a single cloud.

“What is this?”

Ahead of him stood Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma.

The very moment the sound of Geugak Soma flicking Geom Mugeuk’s sword rang out as the two of them walked toward him,

Their surroundings had suddenly changed like this.

Unlike him, who was confused, the Dark King knew what sort of situation this was.

“It is the Space-Time Transposition Art.”

The Dark King recognized the Space-Time Transposition Art at a glance. Surprise flashed through the eyes of the woman wavering as she overlapped with the Fighting King.

“It is Cult Leader Pungcheon’s unique secret art. How astonishing. To think that greedy old man passed this on to the young cult leader.”

What the Dark King was curious about was this.

“If you knew how to use the Space-Time Transposition Art, why did you not use it from the beginning?”

There was only one reason.

“Because my friends were fighting there with me.”

If he had dragged in only the Fighting King, the Dark King might have killed Bisa-in or Jin Hagun.

But he could not bring the Dark King in as well. It was possible to bring someone nearby into the Space-Time Transposition Art, but not someone riding in a palanquin and invisible to the eye.

Even if he could have brought her, it was a method he would not have chosen. If he had to face the Dark King as well, it would take a long time, and who knew what variables might arise in the fights of his friends or Geugak Soma outside?

He had intended to endure as long as possible and use it once at the very end.

Earlier, however, he had seen the cord connected to the palanquin snap, confirming that Bisa-in was safe.

Geugak Soma was safe as well, and Jin Hagun had entered the formation anyway.

Finally, the moment the Dark King descended into the Fighting King’s body,

He judged that it was time to use the Space-Time Transposition Art. But his opponent was an absolute master of dark sorcery. He absolutely could not let his guard down.

Even right now, the Dark King’s emotions were closer to surprise and admiration than agitation or unease.

“Because of your friends. So the anxiety that your friends might die was true.”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“At the same time, that anxiety is also my strength.”

The Fighting King, who had been listening, said,

“Do you think I can’t kill you just because it isn’t night?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“I don’t think you can. The Dark Heaven Night Divine Art won’t be able to exert its full power.”

Indeed, the white rims that had been in the Fighting King’s pupils had grown so thin that they could hardly be seen.

“Now that the two of us have joined forces, it does not matter even if it is not night.”

“If that were really true, you would have attacked in broad daylight from the start. You wouldn’t have caused this commotion in the dead of night like rats.”

At the word “rats,” the Fighting King shouted in fury.

“Shut your mouth!”

The Dark King restrained the agitated Fighting King.

“Endure it. The more you speak with the young cult leader, the more you will lose.”

Then the Dark King spoke in a calm tone.

“The young cult leader is right. If we fight here, we lose.”

She was grasping the situation precisely.

The Fighting King glared at them fearsomely like a wounded beast, but the Dark King overlapping him had calm eyes.

Her gaze moved back and forth between Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma.

At that moment, the two men’s eyes resembled each other.

That was why she could tell, even though they had not yet fought. How fantastically in sync these two would be in battle.

“Knowing that, why are you so relaxed?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, the Dark King answered,

“Because where there is light, darkness is always there as well.”

He had the feeling that the Dark King, wavering from within the Fighting King’s body, was smiling.

It was right then.

Suddenly, the surroundings began to darken. This world was one Geom Mugeuk had created, a place that should never be able to grow dark.

Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma turned to look behind them. They could tell where the darkness had begun.

Both of their gazes turned toward the sky.

The moon was covering the sun.

It was a solar eclipse.

Chapter 548: Our Demon Venerable Has a Sentimental Side.
--------------------------------------------------------
The scenery around them began to lose its light.

When the moon completely covered the sun, a thin ring shone for a moment, then even that vanished.

And so the world was plunged into utter darkness.

Snap!

Geom Mugeuk flicked his finger again.

The space they occupied changed into another place.

Whooooosh.

A sandstorm blew across it—a desert beyond the frontier, with not a single place for a shadow to fall. The sun should have been illuminating the world more fiercely than anywhere else.

And yet it was still dark. Even in this space, the eclipse continued.

“It’s no use, Young Cult Leader. Once the eclipse has begun, you cannot dispel my darkness.”

No matter how much time passed, the eclipse would not end. Not unless that Dark King ended it.

Geom Mugeuk silently looked up at the sun, now completely obscured. It was as though the moon had been embedded in it. As expected, the Dark King’s dark sorcery was astonishingly formidable.

“Since you invited me here, you should have been prepared for at least this much.”

As darkness descended, the white rims around the Fighting King’s pupils had thickened once more. The Dark Heaven Night Divine Art had begun to manifest again. Once again, the initiative had passed to the Fighting King and the Dark King.

It was then that Geugak Soma opened his mouth for the first time.

“You’re the ones who should have been prepared.”

He meant: had they truly been prepared when they chose to lay hands on the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult?

The gazes of the Fighting King and the Dark King turned toward Geugak Soma. The Dark King’s mysterious voice rang out through the darkness.

“You certainly have the right to say such a thing.”

After all, she had not expected him to dispose of the dog butcher so quickly.

That was why the Dark King needed to break Geugak Soma’s momentum.

“You will become the first Demon Venerable to die because of the Young Cult Leader.”

Geugak Soma’s eyes smiled. Die for the Young Cult Leader?

“As reasons for my death go, that would be the best.”

At Geugak Soma’s words, a smile formed at the corner of Geom Mugeuk’s lips.

‘You already died once because of me. I won’t let you die again.’

And at that moment, Geom Mugeuk grasped one fact.

“It seems you cannot escape this place with that Dark Road Return Art of yours. If you could, you would have left this place instead of needing to cover the sun.”

The Dark King said nothing. It was a silence of affirmation. Though she could create darkness here, anything beyond that was impossible. The Space-Time Transposition Art allowed no one to enter or leave without permission.

Thoom! Thud!

Just then, the Fighting King slammed his fists together, producing a metallic sound. His eyes brimmed with killing intent.

“Even if I could leave, I have no intention of doing so.”

His gaze said that he would see this through to the end here.

Geom Mugeuk instantly broke that momentum.

“Is that your will? Or the will of the woman controlling you?”

The Fighting King’s face twitched.

“Of course it is my will!”

The Fighting King surged forward, throwing a punch. In a flashing instant, he was already right before Geom Mugeuk’s nose, his fist flying.

Whoooom!

The fist aimed at Geom Mugeuk’s face grazed past it by a hair’s breadth.

The sword he had swung in counterattack while evading changed direction.

Clang!

The Fighting King’s second fist was so fast that Geom Mugeuk abandoned his attack and struck it aside with his sword.

The Fighting King’s consecutive attacks sliced through empty air.

When he stamped down and unleashed a powerful punch, the surrounding sand erupted upward.

And in the very instant everyone’s vision was obscured—

Shwoook!

Geugak Soma’s first Blood Calamity Finger shot forth.

Thud!

When the dust settled, the Fighting King’s expression was twisted.

Drip.

Blood was flowing down from his shoulder.

Just now, the Blood Calamity Finger had brushed past Geom Mugeuk’s shoulder and struck his own.

It had been an attack that flew in at an utterly unexpected moment, from an utterly unexpected direction.

Geugak Soma was supposed to be protecting the Young Cult Leader, and yet in a situation with no visibility, he had launched such a dangerous attack? It was truly an attack that caught them off guard.

Then black smoke gathered over the wound on his shoulder. The flowing blood stopped.

The Dark King’s voice came.

“It seems our Demon Venerable is not worried about the Young Cult Leader’s safety.”

Her words were meant to restrain Geugak Soma’s audacious attacks, but Geom Mugeuk instead used them against her.

“You seem rather unconcerned about the safety of the one accompanying you.”

“What do you mean?”

“Merely descending into someone’s body must have a severe ill effect on that person. And now you’re even treating him with the energy of darkness? Are you not simply treating him as a tool to use and discard?”

In truth, he did not know the exact details—what sort of ill effects the Dark King’s descent had on the host, or what consequences that sort of treatment would bring.

But he had this thought: how could such an abnormal state possibly be good for a person? And so he spoke from conjecture.

Darkness had fallen, and the initiative in the fight had passed to them. He had to shake them somehow.

“He was revived at far too great a price to be used as a mere tool.”

The Dark King responded calmly, but her opponent was Geom Mugeuk.

“If you win this costly fight, then he’ll have paid his worth. Killing me, the one who has interfered with you until now, seems like more than enough of a role.”

After a brief pause, the Dark King said,

“As expected. There is no matching the Young Cult Leader in words.”

“That is true. But at least in this situation, did you not lose not a battle of words, but a battle of truth?”

Geom Mugeuk shifted his gaze from the Dark King beyond the shadows to the Fighting King’s eyes.

“Of course, you do not seem to care much.”

The Fighting King was staring at Geom Mugeuk with a terrifying gaze. How could he not know this was an attempt to sow discord and shake him?

Discord was something one suffered even while knowing it for what it was. Once heard, it began as an unpleasant feeling in a corner of the heart.

“So long as I can kill you, I don’t care!”

The Fighting King launched himself forward again. His punch, now laden with his fury, came in even stronger than before.

Clangclangclangclangclang!

Sword and chains collided, sending sparks flying. In the darkness, those sparks were a beautiful spectacle.

But Geom Mugeuk’s expression was hard. The Fighting King with the Dark King descended into him was truly strong. Every time he clashed with the Fighting King’s fists, he genuinely worried that the Black Demon Sword might break.

Unable to endure any longer, Geom Mugeuk escaped the attack with the Dark Shadow Step.

The Fighting King stuck to him like a shadow.

Shuk! Shuk! Shuk!

Blood Calamity Fingers flew in at just the right moment, striking the Fighting King’s shoulder.

With a thud, the shaken Fighting King kicked off the sand and flew toward Geugak Soma.

“I’ll kill you first!”

Swish! Swish! Swish!

Blood Calamity Fingers cut through the air.

The Fighting King twisted his body to avoid them. One of the three Blood Calamity Fingers struck him, but it did not pierce a hole through his body. The Dark King’s dark energy had become protective astral qi and shielded him.

Just as the Fighting King’s fist was about to strike Geugak Soma’s chest—

Kaaang!

Sparks burst before Geugak Soma.

Geom Mugeuk had cleaved through the air with Flicker Step and struck down to cut off the Fighting King’s arm.

It was fortunate that he stopped his attack and let the sword strike down onto his fist; had he continued attacking Geugak Soma, his arm would have been severed.

Shiiik!

Geom Mugeuk’s sword struck repeatedly at his arm.

Clang! Claang! Clang!

The Fighting King pulled back his arm and blocked the attacks with his fists.

He had expected the final move to aim for his shoulder or neck.

Slice! Spurt!

Geom Mugeuk’s sword flew in along an unexpected trajectory and cut the Fighting King’s waist.

Spurt!

Blood sprayed from the Fighting King’s waist. It had clearly struck true.

Yet the Fighting King counterattacked immediately, throwing a fist.

Whooooong!

Geom Mugeuk avoided the first punch.

The second punch flew in, but this time, a succession of Blood Calamity Fingers blocked it.

The Fighting King retreated and cursed Geugak Soma.

“You cowardly bastard!”

There was no way Geom Mugeuk would let an insult against Geugak Soma pass.

“Say that to your waist. You cowardly bastard of a waist!”

Black energy was billowing from the wound on his waist. Once again, the dark energy had sealed his injury.

“You’re going to be in serious trouble at this rate. Even if you win this fight, what will you do about the side effects? Are you planning to leave, ‘I killed the Young Cult Leader and Geugak Soma,’ as your last words?”

The Fighting King did not care at all about Geom Mugeuk’s words.

“I do not fear death.”

“I know. I know that is the kind of person you are. But that and dying as a fool are different matters. Why is it only your life? If that woman is clinging to your body, she should fairly put her own life on the line too. Why are you the only one shortening your lifespan as you fight?”

Then the Dark King’s calm voice flowed out.

“Do not fall for his discord.”

Geom Mugeuk spoke again.

“You’re fighting while panting for breath and nearly losing an arm, yet that calm voice sounds a little too selfish, does it not?”

In the end, the Fighting King could not endure it and raised his voice.

“Shut up!”

Though Geom Mugeuk had spoken as if he cared for him, in the end, it was mockery: You are being used right now. Originally, he had hoped the Dark King would reveal emotion, but this time too, the Fighting King was quicker.

The very moment he grew enraged—

Geom Mugeuk’s sword cleaved through the air like lightning. He had provoked them precisely to use this one move.

Whoosh.

Darkness descended upon darkness.

A single pure-white sword line cut through that darkness.

Ninefold Demonic Art, Fourth Form: Dark Flash.

Kaaang!

With a metallic sound that seemed to tear the ears, the Fighting King’s figure appeared.

The Fighting King’s hand, thrust out before his neck, was trembling.

Crack.

A fissure appeared in the chain wrapped around his fist. But it did not shatter. Black energy rose from between the cracks.

Astonishingly, he had blocked Dark Flash. At the seventh-star level, Geom Mugeuk still could not cut down one who bore the combined strength of the Fighting King and the Dark King.

“To use a technique that draws on darkness against me?”

Because the moment Dark Flash was unleashed, the surroundings had turned pitch-black.

“Who knows? If you let your guard down, perhaps your neck might be sliced clean off.”

He had needed to try it once to confirm. And the result confirmed, as expected, that darkness was on their side.

Compared to the composed Dark King, the Fighting King’s killing intent grew even more intense. He had blocked it, but the thought had occurred to him: if the Dark King had not descended into him, that single move might have severed his neck.

“You will have no more chances to use the Ninefold Demonic Art!”

The Fighting King fiercely pressed Geom Mugeuk.

A fist wind raged from his fists. Sand whirled around them in a vortex.

The Fighting King attacked Geom Mugeuk without pause. He did not spare his internal energy. As attacks laden with extreme internal energy continued, Geom Mugeuk could not meet them head-on and could only dodge frantically.

In this fight, Geom Mugeuk was experiencing his limits. These were not merely attacks being poured out at random. They were the attacks of the Fighting King with the Dark King descended into him. And with what seemed like nearly limitless internal energy.

It felt as though he were fighting beneath the surface of a lake frozen solid with thick ice. Each time he felt he might suffocate and die, a breathing hole opened in the ice.

Shuk!

The instant the Fighting King flinched from a Blood Calamity Finger was the instant Geom Mugeuk escaped crisis.

The Fighting King twisted his body to avoid the Blood Calamity Finger that flew past Geom Mugeuk. This time, he had no choice but to dodge. The Blood Calamity Finger had flown straight for his eye. If Geom Mugeuk had turned his head even slightly to the side, the back of his skull would have been pierced.

The Fighting King cursed him as a madman, but that attack had been born solely from trust.

Geugak Soma trusted his own skill, and he trusted Geom Mugeuk. Without that trust, sending Blood Calamity Fingers so narrowly as he had just done would have been madness.

There was no choice. Unless he attacked this boldly, they could not face the Fighting King and the Dark King.

That was why Geugak Soma did not waste his attacks. He conserved his internal energy and attacked only at precisely the necessary moments.

Had it been before his skills had improved, he would actually have attacked even more than now. Back then, he would not have been able to unleash Blood Calamity Fingers with such precision.

Each time, the Fighting King aimed for Geugak Soma, but Geom Mugeuk did not allow it.

As they charged together and Geom Mugeuk thrust his sword—

Puk!

The Black Demon Sword sank into the Fighting King’s body. Judging by the skill he had shown until now, the Fighting King should not have been pierced by such an attack.

Whoooosh!

The instant Geom Mugeuk pulled out his sword, something pitch-black followed it out from the Fighting King’s abdomen and spread over him like a wrapping cloth.

In that moment, Geom Mugeuk found himself standing in pitch-black darkness where nothing could be seen. It was as though he had been trapped inside the belly of a whale.

Then the Dark King’s voice came.

“It is the Dark Curtain.”

The Dark King, who had been waiting for her chance all this time, had finally unleashed her hidden trump card.

“Until I release you, you can never escape. When you emerge from this place, you will see Geugak Soma’s corpse.”

Her voice vanished into the darkness.

Geugak Soma was struggling to tear through the curtain of darkness.

The black curtain, blooming like a flower bud, would not tear—not under the Blood Calamity Finger, nor under the Demonic Extreme Berserk Palm.

The Battle King watched the sight at his leisure. By now, his expression said that victory was completely in his grasp.

“You will never be able to tear it unless I release it.”

There was ease in the Dark King’s voice. If they killed Geugak Soma, they would naturally be able to kill Geom Mugeuk as well.

“You use the word never far too lightly when it concerns the Young Cult Master.”

The Battle King thrust his fist toward Geugak Soma. The powerful energy carried in that fist sent Geugak Soma sliding backward. He stopped only when his back struck the curtain of darkness behind him.

Geugak Soma sensed that he could no longer hold out. The internal energy he had continued to expend while dealing with the dog butcher was nearly exhausted.

“That internal energy of yours—where in the world was it passed down from?”

At Geugak Soma’s question, the Dark King answered meaningfully.

“You can learn that secret only by dying.”

As he strode forward, the Battle King spoke this time.

“I’ll tell you the secret!”

Leaning against the dark curtain, Geugak Soma said,

“Please come out quickly, Young Cult Master!”

The Dark King laughed sweetly.

“Our Demon Venerable has a more sentimental side than he appears to.”

The Battle King thrust out his fist to deliver the final blow.

Shiiiiing!

It was at that very moment.

Puk!

The Black Demon Sword pierced through the curtain of darkness behind him and, before the fist could reach Geugak Soma, stabbed through the Battle King’s shoulder first.

Slice!

It was such an utterly unexpected strike that he had no way to defend against it.

In that instant, the Dark King and the Battle King screamed at the same time and retreated. The Battle King’s shoulder had been properly pierced, and the Dark King had suffered internal injuries as the dark sorcery she had cast was broken.

Riiiiip.

The curtain of darkness began to tear in a long line.

A flash of shock crossed the Dark King’s eyes.

“How did you break the Curtain of Darkness!”

For the first time, the voice that had always remained calm trembled.

She did not know that, in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes, a bluish method for breaking it had been visible. It was the effect of the Blood-Eye Essence and the Divine Eye Art combining.

Originally, the Blood-Eye Essence could see through the methods to break the Blood Cult’s sorcery, and her dark sorcery, too, was deeply connected to the Blood Cult’s martial arts if one traced it back to its source. That was why she had been able to cause an eclipse here.

“I happen to hate feeling stifled.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward Geugak Soma. The two looked at each other and smiled. Geugak Soma had truly believed that Geom Mugeuk would come out. It was because of that belief that he had taken the Battle King’s attack with his back turned.

‘I should be of greater strength to you.’

How could Geom Mugeuk fail to read his heart?

“Do you know this? I like you all the more, Lord Soma, because you have that sentimental side.”

At those words, Soma’s eyes behind the mask smiled.

On the other hand, irritation surged all the way to the top of the Battle King’s head.

“You damned tenacious bastards! Even so, nothing changes!”

He was still full of vigor. Though the Dark King had suffered internal injuries, it was clear that her internal energy was still supporting him. However, perhaps because of those internal injuries, the black energy was unable to fully heal the wound on his shoulder.

“Let’s go.”

Geugak Soma turned toward the Battle King first. Even with his internal energy at rock bottom, Geugak Soma showed no fear at all. Rather, his eyes shone even clearer and more intensely.

Walking side by side with Geugak Soma toward the Battle King, Geom Mugeuk said,

“That’s right. Since when did we ever fight because we had an abundance of internal energy?”

Chapter 549: He Would Have Done the Same
----------------------------------------
“Where are you now?”

When Bi Sa-in returned empty-handed from investigating the outskirts, not only Geom Mu-geuk and the Geugak Soma, but even the Fighting King and the woman inside the palanquin had vanished. Seeing that the palanquin itself remained, he thought they must be fighting within the dark sorcery.

The fact that they had not emerged despite so much time passing meant a difficult battle was still ongoing.

Moreover, Jin Ha-gun and the Myeolma Unit, who had fallen into the formation, had yet to come out. Jin Ha-ryeong and the Cheonhwa Tower Master were nowhere to be seen either.

“We have to uncover the secret of that palanquin.”

Those who had fought him while connected to that palanquin had truly unleashed internal energy without end. There had to be some secret hidden within it related to internal energy.

Even so, they could not simply destroy the palanquin at random. The aura emanating from it was far from ordinary, and the disappearance of Geom Mu-geuk and the Geugak Soma might very well be because of that palanquin.

“How about expanding the search area a little farther?”

Bi Sa-in shook his head. He did not think that monstrous internal energy could have been sent from far away.

“It’s definitely somewhere nearby.”

Then Bi Sa-in’s gaze turned toward one place.

One place they had not searched.

The place they had been unable to search.

It was where the warriors stood in the darkness, only their eyes revealed.

The only place nearby they had not searched was right there.

As Bi Sa-in stared at them quietly, a strange gleam passed through his eyes.

“Don’t those fellows seem suspicious?”

They were confronting the Faceless Guests, showing only their eyes. Strange eyes with white rims around the pupils.

“I heard that those who cultivate the Dark Heaven Night God Art have eyes like that.”

At Il-rang’s words, Bi Sa-in nodded. He knew that as well.

“That’s not why I said it.”

Il-rang looked at Bi Sa-in with an expression that asked, Then why?

“Aren’t those fellows gathered too tightly in one place?”

Indeed, they were. Even the Faceless Guests confronting them seemed to be scattered here and there at random, but in truth they were naturally spread out in preparation for the enemy’s attack.

By contrast, those men were lurking in the darkness, huddled closely together. Everyone had been so focused on those bizarre eyes that they had missed it, but Bi Sa-in had pointed out precisely that.

“You’re right. If concealed weapons were poured into them, the damage would be great.”

Not only that, something about them felt strange. They subtly pricked at Bi Sa-in’s instincts.

“Let’s take a look behind them.”

Moving stealthily, Bi Sa-in kept enough distance not to be detected and approached their rear.

Both Bi Sa-in and the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path were shocked.

It was different from how it had appeared from the front. No matter how far back they went, there were still more of them. The eyes gleaming at the front were only a small portion of the whole.

Because of the distance, and because these men did not reveal the slightest trace of their presence, they had not realized from the front that there were this many of them.

Like a fish in the dark depths luring prey with a glowing protrusion, their true body lay elsewhere.

“Could they be troops hidden to strike us if the situation turns urgent?”

When Il-rang whispered the question, Bi Sa-in shook his head.

“If that were the case, they would have hidden them better.”

They were too easy to spot for an ambush, and too tightly clustered to be an attacking formation.

“Normally, when a force of this size moves, there should be people posted on the outer perimeter. But there isn’t a single one.”

Quite literally, they were gathered in one place. They looked like one enormous mass.

As they stared at them in silence, Chil-rang whispered.

“The way their eyes blink is strange.”

Chil-rang had always had a sharp eye and knew the bizarre martial arts of the unorthodox factions well.

“There’s a fixed pattern to their blinking.”

At his words, everyone focused on the blinking of those eyes.

Indeed, once they looked at it as something strange, it was strange.

They closed their eyes, then opened them.

Once closed, they did not open for a long time; once opened, they did not close. The sight was truly bizarre, and there was a definite pattern to it.

Just then, the moon that had been hidden behind clouds briefly revealed itself.

Because the Dark King had entered the Space-Time Transposition Art, moonlight had finally appeared outside.

At that moment, Chil-rang discovered something.

“Look over there.”

There was something cutting through the air. A certain aura, rising like a heat haze, extended from the darkness where the eyes were gathered to the palanquin. Beneath the moonlight, it was a flow of energy visible only if one looked very closely.

“It’s them!”

The secret was not far away.

They were transmitting internal energy to the palanquin. Those with their eyes closed were replenishing their internal energy, and those with their eyes open were transmitting it through some sinister grand art of their own.

Now he knew for certain why they had not come out of the darkness.

Once he learned the secret, Bi Sa-in grew anxious. Right now, Geom Mu-geuk and the Geugak Soma must be in the worst battle possible, facing that endlessly supplied internal energy with their own internal energy emptied.

“Wipe them all out!”

At Bi Sa-in’s command, the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path drew their swords in unison and prepared to attack.

“We strike with sword qi at the same time.”

After approaching as close as possible, Bi Sa-in launched himself toward them.

Shiiik!

Bi Sa-in’s sword emitted sword qi.

The Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path following behind him also unleashed sword qi all at once.

Because the enemy was clustered together, they intended to sweep them all away with sword qi in a single blow.

Whooooosh!

Black smoke suddenly surged up around them.

Thud! Thud! Thudthudthudthud!

The incoming sword qi was blocked by the pitch-black smoke. Just as it had become protective energy guarding the Fighting King’s body, it was guarding these people as well.

As if declaring that what it received would be returned.

This time, sword qi flew out from within the smoke.

Shwikshwikshwikshwikshwik!

Papapapapak!

The Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path scattered left and right to evade it.

“Close combat!”

Bi Sa-in took the lead and rushed toward them. He instinctively understood that a battle exchanging sword qi from afar would ultimately become a contest of internal energy, and that they would be at a disadvantage.

The black-clad warriors at the front thrust out their swords in unison. They had not been standing clumped together at random. They had formed a formation capable of blocking an attack if someone came at them.

Clang!

The internal energy carried in their swords was tremendous. At the unexpected power, Bi Sa-in nearly lost his grip on his sword.

“Everyone, be careful!”

As he knocked aside the incoming blades, Bi Sa-in could tell. Just as in the earlier fight against the four black-clad men, the internal energy contained in these men’s swords was not that of a single person.

Shwaaak.

The enemies split to the left and right, and the black-clad warriors behind them launched sword qi.

Bi Sa-in threw himself aside and dodged the sword qi. The black-clad men on that side also poured out sword qi.

Bi Sa-in accepted the danger and charged toward them.

Shiiik!

Exploiting their opening, Bi Sa-in drove his sword into the chest of the black-clad man standing before him.

Puk!

The eyes of the one whose heart had been pierced instantly turned pure white, and his body blackened as he collapsed. He was no ordinary human.

Whiririririk!

As he fell, the group of black-clad men began spinning all at once. The fallen corpse was swept away somewhere, and as they revolved, they scattered sword qi.

Bi Sa-in threw himself aside to evade the sword qi.

When he had first given the order to attack, he had thought they could wipe them all out in one go.

But they could not. The enemy was not easy. Above all, the internal energy contained in their attacks was tremendous.

“Fine. If you’re going to use internal energy, use it all over here!”

However, the flow of internal energy running through the darkness toward the palanquin still continued.

Papapapak!

While running to avoid the sword qi, he looked at the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path. They too were unable to break through easily.

“Back!”

At Bi Sa-in’s command, the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path retreated for a moment.

Bi Sa-in drew up his internal energy and unleashed the Hegemon Heaven-Shaking Sword Art, the exclusive martial art of the Evil Path Alliance Leader.

First Sword: Hundred Heavens Form.

A wave of energy emitting a pure white radiance swept toward them.

The Hundred Heavens Form pierced through the smoke protecting them and swept straight over them.

But at the very moment the energy crashed down, the single mass split in half in an instant.

Pubeobeobeobeok!

Three or four black-clad men were caught in the energy and sent flying, but as if nothing had happened, they merged together again.

From within the darkness, all of their eyes stared at Bi Sa-in at once.

They looked together, then blinked at the same time.

The sight was truly grotesque.

Their movements were not like a sword formation or a combined attack art.

Bi Sa-in could feel it. They were connected like a single organism.

This time, they attacked first.

Like the legs of an octopus, more than ten branches shot out from the large mass and thrust their swords all at once.

Clangclangclangclangclang!

Caught in the sudden attack, Sipil-rang, whose shoulder had been injured, was driven into a corner.

The nearby Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Path tried to help him, but the black-clad men clung to them viciously.

“Careful!”

Just as Sibi-rang, who was closest to him, was about to throw himself toward him despite his injuries—

Shukshukshukshukshukshukshukshukshukshuk!
